### [1] [Not Every Mention Is a Claim: Rethinking Object Hallucination Detection in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23610)
*Hsin-Ling Hsu,Neeraja Kirtane,Kuan-Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NAME-Bench（25,497条对象提及，含concrete/hallucinated/non-assertive三类及四种非断言子类型），揭示现有幻觉检测器将"提及"误判为"断言"，误报率14%–80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM对象幻觉评估默认每个对象提及都是"该对象存在"的断言，但如"a bench for people to sit on"仅提及people而未断言其存在（非断言性提及），将其计入幻觉会高估使用此类语言的模型的错误率。

Method: 构建NAME-Bench：基于五个VLM对COCO图像生成的caption，抽取25,497条对象提及，逐条标注为concrete（断言且存在）、hallucinated（断言且不存在）、non-assertive，后者细分为depictive、purpose、negation、hedged四类；随后评测现有幻觉检测器，并将非断言提及作为负样本加入检测器训练进行校正实验。

Result: 所有被评估的检测器均将非断言提及判为幻觉，误报率14%–80%；以非断言提及为负样本训练可显著校正LLaVA-1.5；但在四个较新VLM上，同等校正需5–10倍数据，且校正后对真实幻觉的召回下降。

Conclusion: 当前检测器混淆了"提及对象"与"断言对象存在"，且该混淆在较新VLM上更难消除；幻觉评估应区分caption所断言的内容与其仅提及的内容。

Abstract: Object hallucination in vision-language models (VLMs) is usually measured by checking each object a caption names against the image. Most benchmarks and detectors alike treat every object mention as a claim that the object is present. This assumption fails when a caption names an object without asserting it, as in "a bench for people to sit on", which mentions people without claiming that any are in the image. We call such mentions non-assertive. Treating them as hallucinations inflates the error rates attributed to models that use such language. To separate them from real hallucinations, we introduce NAME-Bench (Non-Assertive Mention Evaluation Benchmark), 25,497 object mentions from captions that five VLMs generate for COCO images. Each mention is labelled concrete (asserted and present), hallucinated (asserted and absent), or non-assertive, and non-assertive mentions are further divided into four subtypes: depictive, purpose, negation and hedged. All the hallucination detectors that we evaluate flag non-assertive mentions as hallucinations, at rates between 14% and 80%. Adding non-assertive mentions to detector training as negatives largely corrects this on LLaVA-1.5. On the four newer VLMs, a comparable correction takes five to ten times more data, and the detectors then catch fewer real hallucinations. These findings show that current detectors conflate mentioning an object with asserting it, and that this conflation is harder to undo on the four newer VLMs than on LLaVA-1.5. Hallucination evaluation should therefore separate what a caption asserts from what it merely mentions.

</details>


### [2] [Execution Before Memory: Diagnosing Memory Failures in Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23542)
*Weiyang Jin,Jiwen Yu,Sihan Xu,Yuwei Niu,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出当前视频世界模型的记忆评测把“场景搭建、指令执行、末帧呈现”揉成一个分数，导致低分被误读为“遗忘”。作者提出 MIST 评测将“变化是否被执行”与“应留下的记忆”解耦，定位瓶颈在于模型不会执行指令而非遗忘；并据此提出 M3-Agent（Make the Missing Move），递归改写输入指令细节以促成执行。相对基线，MIST reveal 分数提升三倍以上，WRBench 的 T 提升 26.1%，MBench-T 的 U 提升 52.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆评测将三项要素合并为单一指标，分数低时易被归因为“遗忘”；且当指令中的变化根本未被执行时，隐状态转移不会发生，此时评测实际上并未检验记忆能力。因此需要把“指令执行”与“记忆痕迹”分离，以准确定位瓶颈。

Method: 提出 MIST 评测协议：模型先在变化保持隐藏的情况下搭建场景，之后才被要求呈现该变化，并严格控制此前的信息泄漏，从而将执行与记忆解耦。基于定位到的“能搭场景但不执行指令”瓶颈，提出 M3-Agent，通过递归修订输入指令的细节，使冻结模型成功执行所要求的指令。

Result: MIST reveal 分数相对基线提升三倍以上；WRBench 指标 T 提升 26.1%，MBench-T 指标 U 提升 52.7%；该缺口在通用记忆评测中同样存在。

Conclusion: 瓶颈位于执行侧而非记忆侧：一旦“动作”被明确表述，冻结模型即可展现出相应能力。M3-Agent 从执行侧切入解决问题，并为后续工作设立了更严格的基线。

Abstract: A memory test for a video world model should say whether the scene was set, whether the asked change was carried out, and whether the last frame shows the result. Current tests fold these into one number, so a low score is easy to read as forgetting. When the change is not carried out, the hidden state transition cannot appear, so the test is not really a test of memory. We introduce MIST, an evaluation that separates the change from the memory it should leave behind. The model first sets the scene while the change stays hidden, and only later is asked to show it, with leakage before that point kept under control. This separation locates the bottleneck: the model sets the scene but does not carry out the instructed change. The same gap appears in general memory evaluations. On that basis we propose M3-Agent (Make the Missing Move), which recursively revises the details of the input instruction so that the model successfully carries out the requested instruction. Relative to the baseline, it more than triples the MIST reveal score. On the benchmarks, WRBench's score T rises by 26.1% and MBench-T's score U by 52.7%. The method meets the problem from the execution side: it shows what the frozen model can do once the move is stated, and it sets a stricter baseline.

</details>


### [3] [Revealing the Impact of Visual Text Style on Attribute-based Descriptions Produced by Large Visual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27553)
*Xiaomeng Wang,Martha Larson,Zhengyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉文本的字体风格（功能性 vs. 装饰性）是否会"泄漏"到大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）对概念属性的描述中，发现即使在正确识别概念的情况下，文本风格仍显著影响其属性级描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类阅读时词义独立于书写风格，而LVLM在多模态渲染文本理解中是否也保持这种风格无关性尚不清楚。作者希望检验文本视觉风格是否不应影响、但实际会影响模型对概念属性的语义推断。

Method: 构建/筛选以图像形式呈现的单词（视觉文本），并区分功能性风格（面向可读性，如黑色无衬线体）与装饰性风格（面向展示，如彩色手写体）。实验仅考察LVLM能正确识别该词所指概念的样本，进而比较不同风格下模型对概念属性的描述差异。

Result: 实验表明，即便概念被正确识别，文本风格仍会影响模型基于属性的概念描述，即存在非平凡的风格泄漏（style leakage）进入语义推断。

Conclusion: LVLM的语义推断并非风格无关，提示需在基于LVLM的多媒体系统中引入风格感知的评估与缓解措施。

Abstract: When the visual style of text is considered, a wide variety can be observed in font, color, and size. However, when a word is read, its meaning is independent of the style in which it has been written or rendered. In this paper, we investigate whether, and how, the style in which a word is visualized in an image impacts the description that a Large Visual Language Model (LVLM) provides for the concept to which that word refers. Specifically, we investigate how functional text styles (readability-oriented, e.g., black sans-serif) versus decorative styles (display-oriented, e.g., colored cursive/script) affect LVLMs' descriptions of a concept in terms of the attributes of that concept. Our experiments study the situation in which the LVLM is able to correctly identify the concept referred to by a visual text, i.e., by a word or words rendered as an image, and in which the visual text style should not influence the attribute-based description that the LVLM produces. Our experimental results reveal that even when the concept is correctly identified, text style influences the model's attribute-based descriptions of the concept. Our findings demonstrate non-trivial style leakage from text style into semantic inference and motivate style-aware evaluation and mitigation for LVLM-based multimedia systems.

</details>


### [4] [Enhancing RAG-based Medical VQA with Gated Multimodal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23588)
*Daniela Jimenez-Pano,Hugo Jair Escalante,Delia Irazú Hernández-Farías*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态RAG框架，通过门控多模态单元学习图像与问题的联合查询表示以改进医学VQA的证据检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型（Med-LVLMs）在医学视觉问答（MVQA）中表现良好，但仍会产生事实不一致的回答。RAG可引入外部临床证据缓解该问题，但现有检索通常仅由单一模态引导，忽略了医学图像与临床问题的联合语义。

Method: 构建多模态RAG框架，利用Gated Multimodal Unit学习联合查询表示，使证据检索同时利用图像与问题两个模态的信息；同时保持底层Med-LVLM参数冻结。

Result: 在基准数据集上评估，多模态查询表示稳定优于参考Med-LVLM，并与当前最先进方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 多模态查询表示可提升医学VQA中证据检索的有效性，具有进一步研究与应用潜力。

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (Med-LVLMs) have shown strong performance in Medical Visual Question Answering (MVQA), yet they may still produce factually inconsistent responses. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) can mitigate this issue by incorporating external clinical evidence; however, retrieval is often guided by a single modality, potentially overlooking the joint semantics of medical images and clinical questions. We introduce a multimodal RAG framework that learns a joint query representation through a Gated Multimodal Unit, enabling evidence retrieval to leverage information from both modalities while keeping the underlying Med-LVLM frozen. We evaluate the proposed approach on benchmark datasets. Results show that the multimodal query representation consistently outperforms a reference Med-LVLM and remains competitive with state-of-the-art methods. Our findings demonstrate the potential of multimodal query representations for more effective evidence retrieval in Medical VQA.

</details>


### [5] [TAN: A Size- and Form-Adaptive Masked-Convolution Encoder for Tissue Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23475)
*Jan Herschel,Anselm von Gladiss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TAN（Tissue-Aware Network），一种仅0.41M参数、无需预训练的紧凑卷积编码器，直接在超像素的原始形状与范围上运算并排除背景，使嵌入对填充值严格不变。在BACH上其精度与303M参数的UNI基础模型无显著差异（81.1% vs 80.7–82.6%），参数少约740倍、FLOPs少约1190倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织图将组织学图像表示为贴合组织形状的超像素图，避免矩形切块破坏同质组织区域。但现有编码器需将超像素裁剪为包围盒后填充/缩放/掩码，导致背景或填充值渗入节点特征。在医学影像中，预测应取决于组织而非任意预处理选择：BACH上仅改变掩码区域填充值，冻结编码器精度可波动达6个百分点，超过前四名编码器在均值填充下的差距，且最优填充因数据集而异，无法一次性固定。

Method: 构建TAN（Tissue-Aware Network）紧凑卷积编码器（0.41M参数），对每个超像素按其原生形状与范围进行处理，并在所有操作中排除背景，从而在构造上使嵌入对填充值完全不变（精确不变性）。实验中采用相同掩码与相同图分类器进行公平比较；并设计消融——使用TAN架构但按标准方式处理填充包围盒裁剪，以分离掩码机制与架构的贡献。

Result: 在相同掩码与相同图分类器下，TAN在BACH上精度与303M参数的UNI无显著差异（81.1% vs. 80.7–82.6%，依填充而异），参数少约740倍、FLOPs少约1190倍且无预训练。在BRACS上，TAN接近同协议训练的ResNet18，但落后于组织病理基础模型。消融显示：掩码处理使精度相近（BACH名义上更高、BRACS更低，均在噪声范围内），而FLOPs不到一半。

Conclusion: 以组织感知的原生形状编码替代填充包围盒处理，可从根本上消除填充值对节点特征的泄漏，在极小参数量与计算量下取得与大型基础模型相当的性能；掩码机制在保持精度的同时显著降低计算成本，是一种更稳健、高效的超像素嵌入方案，但其在BRACS等更难数据集上仍不及组织病理基础模型，存在泛化性局限。

Abstract: Tissue graphs represent histology images as graphs over tissue-conforming superpixels, which keeps homogeneous tissue regions intact instead of cutting them into rectangular patches. To embed superpixels, current encoders crop them to bounding boxes and then pad, resize, or mask them, so background or fill values can leak into the node features. This matters in practice, and especially in medical imaging, where a prediction should depend on the tissue and not on an arbitrary preprocessing choice: on BACH, changing only the fill of the masked region shifts the accuracy of frozen encoders by up to 6 percentage points, larger than the differences among the four best encoders under mean fill, and the preferred fill differs between datasets, so it cannot be fixed once and for all. We present TAN (Tissue-Aware Network), a compact convolutional encoder (0.41M parameters) that processes each superpixel at its native shape and extent and excludes background from every operation, which makes its embeddings exactly invariant to the fill by construction. Under identical masks and an identical graph classifier, TAN's accuracy on BACH is not significantly different from that of the 303M-parameter foundation model UNI (81.1% vs. 80.7-82.6%, depending on the fill), with ~740x fewer parameters, ~1,190x fewer FLOPs, and no pretraining. On BRACS, TAN is close to a ResNet18 trained under the same protocol and trails the histopathology foundation models. An ablation with TAN's architecture but standard processing of fill-padded crops shows that masking keeps accuracy similar (nominally higher on BACH, lower on BRACS, within noise) at less than half the FLOPs.

</details>


### [6] [FlashReFocus: Interactive Image Refocusing in Seconds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23297)
*Ching-Heng Cheng,Chia-Ming Lee,Ming-Ching Chang,Xin Li,Yu-Lun Liu,Chih-Chung Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashReFocus 将单图重对焦拆分为"全清晰恢复+模糊渲染"两个子问题：扩散模型每张图只运行一次恢复全清晰图像，后续编辑由轻量渲染网络以约0.5秒完成，比 GenRefocus 快148.3×，且零样本渲染保真度最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式重对焦方法每次编辑都需重跑大型扩散模型，而用户交互中会尝试大量焦点与模糊强度组合，导致效率低下；作者观察到恢复全清晰图像是病态问题需生成先验，而渲染模糊主要由深度与焦平面决定，二者性质不同，因此应解耦处理。

Method: 提出 FlashReFocus：每张照片仅运行一次扩散。1）单步扩散去模糊器配合解码器精修模块，高效恢复细节并在保真度与感知质量间平衡；2）轻量渲染器以显式散焦图为条件，采用 defocus-bias attention 使相近散焦像素共享信息而不受其他深度干扰，保证对焦内容不变，并支持通过编辑散焦图实现移轴与双焦平面；3）构建 3CReal 三相机89对基准用于跨相机泛化测试。

Result: 在 EBB! 与 3CReal 上的零样本 bokeh 渲染取得最佳感知保真度；重对焦速度比 GenRefocus 快 148.3×，后续每次编辑约 0.5 秒，快 462×。

Conclusion: 通过将病态复原交由一次性扩散先验、将可控模糊交由轻量条件渲染网络，FlashReFocus 在保持编辑质量的同时实现了交互级实时重对焦，并具备跨相机泛化能力。

Abstract: Single-image refocusing changes the focal plane and depth of field of a photograph after capture. It is inherently interactive: users try many focal points and blur strengths on one image, so each edit should be fast and change only the blur. Recent generative methods, however, rerun large diffusion models for every edit. We observe that the two sub-problems differ in kind: recovering the all-in-focus image is ill-posed and needs a generative prior, whereas rendering blur is largely determined by depth and the focal plane. We therefore propose FlashReFocus, which runs diffusion once per photograph and renders every edit with a lightweight network. To address the ill-posed restoration, a one-step diffusion deblurrer with a decoder refiner recovers fine detail efficiently while balancing fidelity and perceptual quality. For precise control, the renderer is conditioned on an explicit defocus map and uses defocus-bias attention, which lets pixels at similar defocus share information without interference from pixels at other depths; in-focus content stays unchanged, and editing the map yields tilt-shift and two focal planes. To further test generalization to unseen cameras, we collect 3CReal, a three-camera benchmark of 89 pairs. FlashReFocus achieves the best perceptual fidelity for zero-shot bokeh rendering on EBB! and 3CReal, refocuses 148.3x faster than GenRefocus, and takes about half a second per further edit, 462x faster.

</details>


### [7] [Three LiDARs, Twenty-Five Datasets: Cross-Source Generalization in Tree-Species Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22606)
*Jae Joong Lee,Bedrich Benes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出多分辨率多视角ConvNeXtV2模型，将FOR-species20K官方隐藏测试准确率从79.5%提升至85.0%（提交时排名第一）；随后批判性分析指出该分数反映的是分布内性能而非源数据集不相关泛化能力，源-物种单元留出下物种均值从82%降至36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FOR-species20K是最大的公开LiDAR树种分类基准（25个独立采集数据集、33个物种），但现有排行榜分数缺乏对其实际含义的审视：官方随机划分使同一源数据集同时出现在训练与评估中，隐藏测试为物种均衡，且公开集呈长尾分布，因此分数可能高估真实泛化能力。

Method: 采用多分辨率、多视角ConvNeXtV2架构提升分类精度；诊断层面使用logistic探针从折外模型概率中恢复源数据集身份，并利用几何特征做源识别；进一步设计源-物种单元（source-species-cell）留出协议，在5566棵树、8个合格物种上评估（这些物种在其他源中保持相同LiDAR类型的训练支持），并做计数匹配重训练与两种替代骨干网络的对照实验，另在PureForest上做外部验证。

Result: 隐藏测试准确率由79.5%提升至85.0%；logistic探针以69%准确率恢复源身份，几何特征在两种广泛共享物种内源识别率超过90%；33个物种中18个仅出现于单一源；源-物种单元留出下无加权物种均值从82%降至36%，树加权准确率从95%降至60%；计数匹配重训练与替代骨干实验表明该差距不能仅由训练支持减少解释；PureForest上的分组划分效应较小但一致。

Conclusion: 85%的排行榜分数是强分布内性能，但不能作为源数据集不相关泛化的估计；应引入均衡指标与源感知（source-aware）评估协议，以避免数据集偏置带来的乐观偏差。

Abstract: FOR-species20K is the largest public LiDAR benchmark for tree-species classification, pooling 25 independently collected terrestrial, mobile, and airborne laser-scanning datasets across 33 species. We introduce a multi-resolution, multi-view ConvNeXtV2 model that improves official hidden-test accuracy from 79.5% to 85.0%, ranking first at the time of submission. We then examine what this score measures. Because the hidden test is species-balanced, its overall accuracy is more comparable to a macro metric than to accuracy under the public set's long-tailed distribution. Moreover, the official random split places every source dataset in both training and evaluation. A logistic probe recovers source identity from out-of-fold model probabilities with 69% accuracy, while geometric features identify the source above 90% within two widely shared species; 18 of the 33 species occur in only one source. Under a source-species-cell holdout, evaluated on 5,566 trees from eight eligible species that retain the same LiDAR-type training support in other sources, the unweighted species mean falls from 82% to 36%, and tree-weighted accuracy falls from 95% to 60%. Count-matched retraining and experiments with two alternative backbones indicate that this gap is not explained by reduced training support alone, while an external PureForest experiment shows a smaller but consistent grouped-split effect. These results establish the 85% leaderboard score as strong in-distribution performance, but not as an estimate of source-disjoint generalization, motivating balanced metrics and source-aware evaluation. The code is available at: https://github.com/JaeLee18/FOR20K-lidar-tree-species

</details>


### [8] [STRIDE: Evaluating Spatiotemporal Reasoning in Driving Edge Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22795)
*Lieqi Liu,Rui gao,Jia-Chen Gu,Wenbo Hu,Zhaobin Mo,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Nejib Ammar,Wei Wang,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出STRIDE——一个面向自动驾驶长尾安全场景的以对象为中心的时空推理基准，包含六类任务（空间感知、空间理解、时序记忆、时序外推、轨迹预测、场景上下文意识），并配套选择题与开放式VQA两种评测形式；12个模型中最优者GPT-6-Astra仅达43.4%平均准确率（随机基线约20%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶仅靠感知周围对象不足，若无法理解对象随时间如何运动与交互，会导致危险决策；评测VLM需检验其能否将过去空间观测与未来后果关联。现有基准多以基础感知题考察边缘案例，或在简单场景中测时序理解，复杂时空推理仍未被充分探索。

Method: 构建STRIDE基准，聚焦长尾、安全关键的驾驶场景与异常对象行为及复杂交通交互；设六类任务族，将探查单项时空判断的选择题与要求详细场景解释及驾驶决策论证的开放式VQA配对；参考答案由结构化数据集标注经自动化流程生成，辅以有限的语言模型辅助与人工校验以保证标注质量；评测12个专有、开放权重及驾驶专用模型。

Result: 最优模型GPT-6-Astra平均选择题准确率仅43.4%，随机选择基线约20%；分析显示，描述驾驶场景的强表现未必转化为准确的安全关键判断，且模型重建对象运动比回忆过去位置更困难。

Conclusion: STRIDE揭示了被聚合VQA分数所掩盖的时空驾驶能力缺陷，说明需要更细粒度的对象中心时空推理评测。

Abstract: In autonomous driving, perceiving surrounding objects is not enough: failing to understand how they move and interact over time can lead to dangerous decisions. Evaluating vision-language models (VLMs) therefore requires testing whether they connect past spatial observations to future consequences. Yet existing benchmarks often assess edge cases through basic perception questions or test temporal understanding in simple scenarios, leaving complex spatiotemporal reasoning underexplored. We introduce STRIDE (SpatioTemporal Reasoning In Driving Edge Cases), an object-centric benchmark for long-tail, safety-critical driving scenarios. STRIDE covers six task families: spatial perception, spatial understanding, temporal memory, temporal extrapolation, trajectory prediction, and scene-context awareness. It pairs multiple-choice questions probing individual spatiotemporal judgments with related free-form visual question answering (VQA) requiring detailed scene explanations and justifications of driving decisions. We prioritize scenes with unusual object behaviors and complex traffic interactions, generate reference answers from structured dataset annotations through automated procedures, and use limited language-model assistance and human verification to ensure annotation quality. Across 12 proprietary, open-weight, and driving-specialized models, even the best performer, GPT-6-Astra, achieves only 43.4% average multiple-choice accuracy, versus an approximately 20% random-choice baseline. Further analysis reveals that strong performance in describing driving scenes does not necessarily translate to accurate safety-critical judgments, and that models struggle more with reconstructing object motion than recalling past positions. STRIDE thus exposes spatiotemporal driving limitations that aggregate VQA scores can conceal.

</details>


### [9] [COMET: Decoupled Distillation, Routing, and Capacity Control for Task-Agnostic Continual Vision--Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22907)
*Seungyoon Woo,Keonhee Park,Gunhee Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出COMET（Collaborative Mixture-of-Expert Transfer）框架，用于视觉语言模型（VLM）的任务无关持续学习（task-agnostic continual learning）：以冻结教师提供特征参考、三重亲和蒸馏保持多模态几何、学生感知跨专家蒸馏对齐新旧专家，并以上下文bandit控制容量、重构路由器实现测试时稀疏专家选择，在X-TAIL基准上验证各组件均带来增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP类VLM虽具零样本识别能力，但在开放世界部署中常需任务无关持续学习（任务顺序到达、旧数据不可得、任务身份未知、骨干不可重训）。作者指出该设定下VLM面临稳定性-可塑性权衡之外的三重耦合压力：(1) 模态一致性漂移；(2) 表征空间干扰；(3) 单任务容量不足，现有方法难以同时应对。

Method: 针对三重压力分别设计组件：(1) 冻结教师仅在共享图文池上提供特征参考；(2) Tri-Affinity Distillation 同时保持图像-图像、文本-文本、图像-文本三种几何关系以抑制模态一致性漂移；(3) Student-Aware Cross-Expert Distillation 将新专家与兼容的旧专家对齐，使路由错误可优雅退化；(4) 容量与推理解耦：上下文bandit依据训练后信号决定保留或合并整任务专家，重构路由器在测试时无需教师语义即可选择稀疏专家。

Result: 在X-TAIL基准上的消融表明，所提出的每个组件均提升持续学习性能，验证了三重压力诊断与对应组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 持续视觉语言适配可在"特征参考、专家塑造、路由、容量控制"四者结构性分离的前提下，同时实现多模态几何保持、累积专家协同与容量联合分配，为VLM的任务无关持续学习提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP retain zero-shot recognition ability, but their open-world use often requires task-agnostic continual learning: tasks arrive sequentially, past data are unavailable, task identity is unknown, and the backbone cannot be retrained. We argue that this setting fails VLMs through three coupled pressures beyond the stability-plasticity tradeoff: (1) modal-consistency drift, (2) representation-space interference, and (3) per-task capacity shortfall. We propose COMET (Collaborative Mixture-of-Expert Transfer) to resolve these three issues respectively as follows. A frozen teacher provides only a feature reference on a shared image-text pool; Tri-Affinity Distillation preserves image-image, text-text, and image-text geometry; and Student-Aware Cross-Expert Distillation aligns new experts with compatible prior experts so routing errors degrade gracefully. We design COMET to keep capacity and inference separate: a contextual bandit keeps or merges full per-task experts using post-training signals, while a reconstruction router selects sparse experts at test time without teacher semantics. On the X-TAIL benchmark, we show that each proposed component improves continual learning performance. COMET shows that continual vision-language adaptation can preserve multimodal geometry, coordinate accumulated experts, and allocate capacity jointly when feature reference, expert shaping, routing, and capacity control are separated by construction.

</details>


### [10] [HAPACT: A Benchmark For Human-Centric Physical Impact Localization in Movies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22895)
*Youngrae Kim,Yejin Jang,Eunho Kim,Youjin Sung,Kun-Woo Song,Sangpil Kim,Sang Ho Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出以人为中心的冲击定位新任务（视频内时间定位+人体上空间定位），并构建HAPACT数据集与基准（2.8K+视频-查询对、20K+细粒度标注帧），评估显示现有方法仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频人体物理推理研究多集中于接触、压力、姿态与运动等日常交互，对冲击（impact）这类短时、强力的突发物理事件研究不足，而此类事件对理解突发物理事件如何作用于人体具有重要意义。

Method: 提出human-centric impact localization任务；基于电影片段构建HAPACT数据集，采用以观众感知为基准的标注、文本条件定位、帧级时间监督与基于SMPL-X的空间监督；评测中采用视频时刻检索模型做时间定位、人-场景接触模型做空间定位，并提出将空间定位重构为查询引导的身体区域选择的文本条件基线。

Result: 基准结果显示，现有方法在物理冲击定位上仍具挑战性；所提文本条件基线为空间定位提供了有效参考。

Conclusion: HAPACT可作为专门基准，推动视频中物理冲击推理研究。

Abstract: Physical reasoning about humans in videos has largely focused on everyday interactions, where contact, pressure, pose, and motion support embodied perception and contact-aware applications. Still, short and forceful properties like impact events remain relatively underexplored, despite their relevance to broader understanding of how abrupt physical events affect the human body. To fill this gap, we introduce human-centric impact localization, a new task that localizes target physical impacts temporally within a video and spatially on the human body. We further present HAPACT, a HumAn-centric imPACT dataset and benchmark built from movie clips, containing over 2.8K video-query pairs with more than 20K fine-grained human-annotated frames. We built HAPACT around viewer-grounded annotation, text-conditioned localization, and supervision at frame-level temporal and SMPL-X-based spatial granularity. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our benchmark, we conduct extensive evaluations of existing methods, employing video moment retrieval models for temporal localization and human-scene contact models for spatial localization. For the spatial task, we propose a text-conditioned baseline by reformulating localization as query-guided body-region selection. Benchmark results show that physical impact localization remains challenging for existing methods, motivating HAPACT as a dedicated benchmark to support physical impact reasoning in videos.

</details>


### [11] [Persistent Planar Memory for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23795)
*Yuze He,Bin Tan,Zelin Gao,Yujun Shen,Yong-Jin Liu,Nan Xue,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PPMem以平面图元作为视频世界模型的持久3D记忆，通过扩散Transformer联合生成RGB视频与平面几何，实现高紧凑、显式、跨视角一致的场景记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模生成与探索环境的视频世界模型需要持久化的3D记忆。逐像素点云存储冗余大，而平面图元仅需7参数即可紧凑表达室内外场景的主导结构元素（墙、地板、立面），是自然候选方案。

Method: 在视频扩散Transformer上增加平面预测头与Depth Token Fusion（DTF）模块；每个自回归块联合输出RGB视频与平面几何，通过合并程序融合进持久平面记忆，并以其渲染深度作为后续块的生成条件。

Result: 记忆比逐像素点云紧凑约三个数量级（约10万图元、整场景5MB）；具备几何显式性（米制深度、法线、可导出表面）、跨视角一致性及自适应精化能力。在DL3DV与RealEstate10K上视频质量优于既有3D-grounded世界模型，同时输出米制深度、法线与可导出平面几何，并在大视角变化下维持数百帧连贯性。

Conclusion: 平面图元可作为一种高效、显式且一致的持久3D记忆表示，为长时程、大视角变化的视频世界模型提供可行范式。

Abstract: Video world models that generate and explore environments at scale need a persistent 3D memory of what they have produced. Plane primitives offer a natural candidate: a compact set of oriented planes captures the dominant structural elements (e.g., walls, floors, facades) of indoor and outdoor scenes, while requiring only 7 parameters per primitive. We present PPMem, a video world model that adopts plane primitives as its persistent 3D memory. PPMem augments a video diffusion transformer with a planar prediction head and a Depth Token Fusion (DTF) module: each autoregressive chunk jointly produces RGB video and planar geometry, which merges into a persistent plane memory that conditions subsequent chunks via rendered depth. The resulting memory is three orders of magnitude more compact than per-pixel point clouds (~100K primitives, 5MB for a full scene), geometrically explicit (metric depth, normals, exportable surfaces), cross-view consistent (jointly predicted by the shared backbone), and adaptive (refined by the merging procedure as generation proceeds). On DL3DV and RealEstate10K, PPMem outperforms prior 3D-grounded world models in video quality while jointly producing metric-scale depth, surface normals, and exportable planar geometry, sustaining coherence across hundreds of frames under large viewpoint changes. Project page: https://hyzcluster.github.io/ppmem.

</details>


### [12] [A Paired RGB-Sonar Dataset for Fish Monitoring with Consumer Sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23759)
*Mourad Derbedrosian,Michael Hobley,Pietro Perona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布HaCKRS数据集，将消费级前视声呐（Garmin LiveScope）记录与俯视RGB视频时间对齐配对，含逾10.5万帧对、约3.3小时、四种量程与朝向配置，提供帧级元数据、声呐几何与坐标信息、同步数据及三种水平配置的平面配准，旨在支撑鱼类检测、跟踪与计数研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下光学成像在黑暗与浑浊水体中失效，而成像声呐可实现观测；但以往用于生态监测的成像声呐系统通常价格昂贵，可及性受限。消费级前视声呐有望降低鱼类监测门槛，然而其图像噪声大，给标注与自动化方法开发带来困难，亟需配对的参考数据与标定信息。

Method: 构建HaCKRS数据集：同步采集Garmin LiveScope成像声呐记录与锦鲤（koi carp）俯视RGB视频，形成时间匹配的帧对；覆盖四种量程与朝向配置；提供帧级元数据、声呐几何与坐标系信息、全部配置的同步数据，以及三种水平配置的平面配准。数据集定位为配对观测与标定资源，不含任务特定的鱼类标注。

Result: 数据集包含超过105,000个时间对齐帧对，累计约3.3小时记录，覆盖四种量程与朝向配置，并附带上述元数据、几何/坐标、同步与配准信息。

Conclusion: HaCKRS可作为标注基础与基准资源，支持鱼类检测、跟踪与计数方法的开发，并促进消费级声呐用于生态监测可行性的研究。

Abstract: Imaging sonar enables underwater observation in conditions where optical imaging is ineffective due to darkness and turbid water. However, imaging-sonar systems previously used for ecological monitoring have typically been expensive, limiting their accessibility. Consumer forward-looking sonar has the potential to make fish monitoring more accessible, but its noisy imagery makes annotation and the development of automated methods challenging. We introduce the Huntington and Caltech Koi RGB and Sonar dataset (HaCKRS), which pairs Garmin LiveScope imaging-sonar recordings with top-down RGB video of koi carp. The dataset contains more than 105,000 temporally matched frame pairs, spanning approximately 3.3 hours of recordings across four range and orientation configurations. We provide frame-wise metadata, sonar geometry and coordinate information, synchronization data for all four configurations, and planar registration for the three horizontal configurations. The release focuses on paired observations and calibration rather than task-specific fish annotations. We intend HaCKRS to support annotation and the development of fish detection, tracking, and counting methods, and investigations into the potential of consumer sonar for ecological monitoring.

</details>


### [13] [Beyond Multi-Stage Pipelines: A Label-Correlation Ensemble for Multi-Label Retinal Fundus Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23747)
*Md Asif Uddin,Jannatul Ferdous,Asif Islam,Zobaer Mahmud Zisan,Promit Ghosh,Shah Ahafik Arman,Niloy Farhan,Rafeed Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在统一39目标标签空间上控制变量比较三种多病种眼底分类架构，发现分阶段级联无优势、标签相关性建模收益最大、扁平与标签相关性模型融合最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜眼底图常含多种疾病，公开数据集异构且严重长尾。临床分诊提示采用"筛查门控—疾病组路由—组内分类器"的分阶段设计，但其层级复杂度是否带来实际收益尚不明确，需受控验证。

Method: 基于RFMiD 1.0、RFMiD 2.0与ODIR-5K构建含12,652张图像、39个目标的统一标签空间，比较三种架构：(i) 8个模型的三阶段门控级联；(ii) 39路独立sigmoid输出的扁平模型；(iii) 用39个可学习标签查询和两层Transformer解码器替代线性头的标签相关性模型。三者共享同一Swin-V2-B主干、预处理与训练协议，差异仅归于架构。

Result: 级联无一致优势：两个粗粒度门控丢弃34.2%的真实实例，而细粒度分类器仅丢失6.7%，门控硬零使级联macro-AUC降至0.747。标签相关性建模带来最大单项提升，检出率由0.621升至0.682（TTA后0.689）。四视图TTA下扁平与标签相关性模型融合在8项指标中6项最优（完全匹配0.694、micro-F1 0.678、macro-F1 0.456、macro-AUC 0.972）。在定义匹配的子类上，macro-/micro-F1持平或超过四项近期研究，低支持病理上尤为显著。另发现级联与连续输出模型集成有害，且需按来源负采样以消除导致伪AUC=1.000的数据集捷径。

Conclusion: 分阶段级联的层级复杂度未能获得回报，误差主要源于粗粒度门控的硬零抑制；标签相关性建模与扁平模型的连续输出融合是更优选择，级联与连续模型集成反而有害；数据集层面需按来源负采样以避免捷径评估。

Abstract: Retinal fundus images frequently carry more than one disease, and public fundus datasets are heterogeneous and severely long-tailed. Clinical triage suggests a staged design, in which a screening gate, a disease-group router and group-specific classifiers operate in sequence, but it is unclear whether this hierarchy earns its complexity. We present a controlled comparison of three architectures on a unified 39-target label space built from RFMiD 1.0, RFMiD 2.0 and ODIR-5K (12,652 images): (i) a three-stage gated cascade of eight models, (ii) a flat model with 39 independent sigmoid outputs, and (iii) a label-correlation model that replaces the flat linear head with 39 learned label queries and a two-layer transformer decoder. All three share one Swin-V2-B backbone, one preprocessing pipeline and one training protocol, so differences are attributable to the architecture. Staging offers no consistent advantage: tracing every missed disease instance to the stage that lost it shows that the two coarse gates discard 34.2% of true instances while the fine-grained classifiers lose only 6.7%, and the gates' hard zeros depress the cascade's macro-AUC to 0.747. Label-correlation modeling gives the largest single gain, raising the detection rate from 0.621 to 0.682 (0.689 with test-time augmentation). Blending the flat and label-correlation models under four-view test-time augmentation is the best configuration on six of eight metrics (exact match 0.694, micro-F1 0.678, macro-F1 0.456, macro-AUC 0.972). On definition-matched class subsets it matches or exceeds four recent studies on macro- and micro-F1, most clearly on low-support pathologies. We also show that ensembling a gated cascade with continuous-output models is harmful, and that per-source negative sampling is needed to remove a dataset shortcut that otherwise yields a spurious AUC of 1.000.

</details>


### [14] [Material and Defocus Cues for Occluded Object Localization in Single Hyperspectral Images: An Exploratory Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23733)
*Ali Zia,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种融合NMF材质分组、散度光学线索与图拉普拉斯传播的高光谱遮挡目标检测流程，用36个场景的定性例子说明材质与光学线索的互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分遮挡下可见区域纹理弱或外观相似导致目标检测困难，高光谱提供材质相关光谱与波长相关光学模糊作为互补证据。

Method: 非负矩阵分解(NMF)材质分组 + 基于散度的光学线索 + 图拉普拉斯传播；传播得分用于区域提取与边界框定位，推理阶段无需额外深度传感器。

Result: 用两套相机系统采集30个室内和6个室外高光谱场景，覆盖不同材质、深度布局与部分遮挡，给出定性示例：材质信息在光学线索弱时辅助分离，光学线索可区分材质相近的表面；材质与深度均相同的目标仍难处理。

Conclusion: 这是一项探索性研究，建立了传感原理与数学表述，但尚未给出系统定量评估，需补充量化评价与实现细节。

Abstract: Partial occlusion makes object detection difficult when visible regions have weak texture or similar appearance. Hyperspectral imaging provides material-dependent spectra and wavelength-dependent optical blur, which offer complementary evidence for separating objects. We investigate a pipeline that combines nonnegative-matrix-factorization-based material grouping, divergence-based optical cues, and graph Laplacian propagation. The propagated scores support region extraction and bounding-box localization without requiring a separate depth sensor at inference. A collection of 30 indoor and six outdoor hyperspectral scenes acquired using two camera systems provides qualitative examples spanning different materials, depth arrangements, and partial occlusions. The examples illustrate how material information can support separation when optical cues are weak, and how optical cues can distinguish some surfaces with similar material properties. Objects sharing both material and depth remain challenging. This exploratory study establishes the sensing rationale and mathematical formulation, while identifying the quantitative evaluation and implementation details needed to assess detection performance systematically.

</details>


### [15] [Agentic Physics-Grounded Video Generation via Memory-Augmented In-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23677)
*Xikang Guan,Shaocong Yang,Yanqi Bao,Can Wang,Zheng Gu,Zhengqing Chen,Kai Chen,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysRepair：生成-诊断-修复范式的智能体视频生成框架，通过时空因果约束定位并局部重生成物理失效状态，结合经验增强的上下文学习实现自适应修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在合成前用仿真或精炼提示施加物理引导，存在三方面局限：限制动态多样性、无法捕捉仅在生成后才出现的错误、且不一致时需反复重新生成。作者因此提出"生成-诊断-修复"范式，保留原始生成能力并在事后选择性修复，围绕两个关键问题：修什么（what to repair）、怎么修（how to repair）。

Method: 提出PhysRepair智能体框架，含两个核心组件：(1) 时空因果约束（Spatiotemporal Causal Constraints）：识别第一个无效物理状态及其因果受影响状态，生成修复规格以引导物理合理的局部重生成，同时保留未受影响内容；(2) 经验增强的上下文学习（Experience-augmented In-context Learning）：将当前修复上下文与历史修复经验结合，使智能体能跨不同物理失效自适应调整修复决策，并累积已完成的轨迹供未来使用。

Result: 在Physics-IQ Verified数据集上，覆盖固体力学、流体动力学、光学、热力学、磁学等类别，PhysRepair相较现有方法一致地提升了生成视频的物理合理性。

Conclusion: 事后修复范式可在不牺牲生成多样性的前提下提升物理合理性；因果定位+局部重生成+经验复用构成有效的修复闭环。论文未给出定量指标细节，未来可关注修复效率与跨域泛化。

Abstract: Physical plausibility is essential for realistic video generation. Existing methods impose physical guidance before synthesis via simulations or refined prompts, but this can limit dynamic diversity, miss errors that emerge only after generation, and require repeated regeneration when inconsistencies remain. We therefore study a generate-diagnose-repair paradigm that preserves the original generation capability and selectively repairs physical inconsistencies afterward, raising two key questions: what to repair and how to repair? To address them, we introduce PhysRepair, an agentic video generation framework that combines causal reasoning over the observed physical failure with prior repair experience to enable localized and adaptive correction. Specifically, Spatiotemporal Causal Constraints identify the first invalid physical state and its causally affected states, producing a repair specification that guides physically plausible local regeneration while preserving unaffected content. Experience-augmented In-context Learning combines the ongoing repair context with relevant experience from previous repairs, allowing the agent to adapt its repair decisions across diverse physical failures and accumulate completed trajectories for future cases. Experiments on Physics-IQ Verified across solid mechanics, fluid dynamics, optics, thermodynamics, and magnetism demonstrate that PhysRepair consistently improves the physical plausibility of generated videos over existing methods. Our project page is available at https://xxkkxxx.github.io/PhysRepair-Website/.

</details>


### [16] [Learning to Discriminate Scene Structures Makes Self-Supervised Depth Learning Scalable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23600)
*Mengtan Zhang,Yuchuan Cui,Yu Ma,Zizhan Guo,Wei Ye,Rui Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MaSS框架，通过将结构表征通路与上下文特征解耦并用场景结构判别目标引导，使自监督单目深度估计可扩展到多样场景，首次实现混合域训练稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督单目深度估计方法在多样场景上训练时会退化：自监督下模型倾向于编码数据集特定的结构偏置，而非可迁移的场景几何，导致跨域优化冲突。作者希望让自监督深度学习可扩展到多样数据。

Method: MaSS引入一条专门的结构表征通路，显式地与上下文特征解耦；该通路受一种新的场景结构判别（scene-structure discrimination）目标引导，以学习泛化的、具备布局感知能力的结构表征用于深度解码。

Result: 在四个多样数据集上（单独训练与联合训练）显著优于现有自监督方法，并首次在混合域训练下获得稳定一致的性能提升；在另外八个数据集上的零样本评估显示出显著泛化能力，尤其在完全分布外场景上表现突出。

Conclusion: 将结构表征与上下文特征解耦可缓解跨域优化冲突，是实现自监督深度估计跨场景可扩展性的有效途径；代码已开源。

Abstract: Self-supervised monocular depth estimation is appealing for its potential to scale with unlabeled data, yet existing methods often degrade when trained on diverse scenes. This study finds that, under self-supervision, models tend to encode dataset-specific structural biases rather than transferable scene geometry, leading to cross-domain optimization conflicts. To address this issue, this study proposes the MaSS framework, aiming to make self-supervised depth learning scalable to diverse data. MaSS introduces a dedicated structural representation pathway explicitly decoupled from contextual features, which is guided by a novel scene-structure discrimination objective to learn generalized, layout-aware structural representations for depth decoding. Trained and evaluated on four diverse datasets, both individually and jointly, MaSS substantially outperforms prior self-supervised methods and is the first to consistently benefit from mixed-domain training. Zero-shot evaluation on eight additional datasets further demonstrates its remarkable generalizability, particularly on those entirely out-of-distribution scenes. The source code is publicly available at https://mias.group/MaSS.

</details>


### [17] [When Evidence Vanishes: Distilling Visual-Evidence Dependency in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23469)
*Qizhen Lan,Yiling Ma,Ruiqi Chen,Mengchen Fan,Xi Xiao,Lin Zhao,Liang He,Baocheng Geng,Pulin Che,Tianyang Wang,Xiaoqian Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出证据条件依赖蒸馏（ECDD），在医学VLM知识蒸馏中不仅迁移教师的答案偏好，还迁移教师答案对视觉证据的依赖（证据缺失时答案支持度应下降）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑医学视觉语言模型可能不依赖图像、仅凭问题措辞或先验即答对临床问题；标准KD只复制教师在原图上的答案偏好，未检验该偏好是否以问题所需视觉证据为条件，即缺失"医学视觉证据依赖"这一监督目标。

Method: 将训练图像与证据水平匹配的对照样本配对，蒸馏教师在一对样本间答案margin的下降量作为依赖信号；采用下界目标，仅惩罚正依赖不足（shortfall），并保留原图监督以维持准确率；公式可统一适用于查询临床区域、手术器械或整图内容三类证据。

Result: 在三个医学VQA设定下，ECDD原图准确率与蒸馏及对齐基线相当，同时答案支持度对证据移除更敏感；DME主指标下，正例对目标掩码相对随机掩码的margin响应由KD的0.786升至2.023，原图准确率仅差0.11个百分点。

Conclusion: 医学VLM蒸馏应同时迁移教师的答案偏好与其对视觉证据的依赖，ECDD为此提供了可行且可推广的框架。

Abstract: Compact medical vision-language models (VLMs) can answer clinical questions correctly without reliably using the image, relying instead on question wording or learned priors. Standard knowledge distillation copies a teacher's answer preference on the original image, but it leaves unchecked whether that preference is conditional on the visual evidence required by the question. We study this missing target as medical visual evidence dependency: support for the correct answer should decrease when the matched evidence is absent. We introduce Evidence-Conditioned Dependency Distillation (ECDD), which pairs each training image with a control matched to the evidence level and distills the teacher's drop in answer margin across the pair. A lower bound objective transfers positive dependency by penalizing only shortfalls, while original-image supervision preserves task accuracy. The same formulation applies when the evidence is a queried clinical region, a surgical instrument, or whole-image visual content. Across three medical VQA settings, ECDD keeps original-image accuracy competitive with distillation and alignment baselines while making answer support more sensitive to evidence removal. Under the DME headline objective, for example, the positive-case margin response to target rather than random masking rises from 0.786 with KD to 2.023 with ECDD, while original-image accuracy differs by 0.11 points. These results suggest that medical VLM distillation should transfer both the teacher's answer preference and its dependence on visual evidence.

</details>


### [18] [ShadowCast: On-the-Fly Context-Aware Shadow Augmentation for Remote Sensing Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23440)
*Yechan Kim,Dongho Yoon,Jongmin Joo,JongHyun Park,Moongu Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShadowCast将阴影作为训练期数据增广：从分割标签提取地物掩膜、探测亮度估计投影方向并合成阴影注入训练，简单无需网络与元数据，在多基准多架构上稳定提升遥感语义分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高位目标（建筑、树木）投射的阴影会改变遥感图像的光谱表现并模糊目标边界，降低语义分割性能。现有方法多把阴影视为待检测与去除的退化，需要专用网络和阴影专项标注，成本高。

Method: 提出ShadowCast，采取相反思路：训练时合成阴影并注入。具体地，直接从分割标签中提取建筑、树木等高架目标的掩膜；通过探测这些目标周边亮度，估计出图像特定的投影阴影方向线索；沿推断方向投影合成阴影。全过程无需学习网络，也无需成像元数据。

Result: 在Vaihingen、Potsdam、LoveDA三个常用遥感基准上，ShadowCast在多种先进分割架构中一致优于基线；与标准增广和正则化技术结合时还能带来额外提升。

Conclusion: 随机阴影注入是一种简单且互补的遥感分割增广策略，可替代“检测—去除”式的阴影处理范式。

Abstract: Shadows cast by elevated objects alter the spectral appearance and obscure the boundaries of objects in remote sensing images, thereby degrading semantic segmentation performance. Most existing approaches handle shadows as degradations to be detected and removed, requiring additional costs from dedicated networks and shadow-specific annotations. We propose ShadowCast, which takes the opposite approach: it synthesizes shadows and injects them during training for remote sensing segmentation. It extracts masks of elevated objects, such as buildings and trees, directly from the segmentation labels and estimates an image-specific cast-shadow direction cue by probing the brightness around them. It then projects synthetic shadows along the inferred cast-shadow direction, requiring no learned network and no acquisition metadata. Experiments on three widely used remote sensing benchmarks, Vaihingen, Potsdam, and LoveDA, show that ShadowCast consistently outperforms the baseline across diverse state-of-the-art segmentation architectures. Moreover, it brings additional performance gains when combined with standard augmentation and regularization techniques, indicating that stochastic shadow injection serves as a simple and complementary augmentation strategy for remote sensing segmentation.

</details>


### [19] [Towards Reconstructing Geographically Diverse Architecture with 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22514)
*Nicky Kriplani,Yiwen Zhang,Angelina Wang,Hadar Averbuch-Elor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ArchWorld 基准（覆盖广、带丰富元数据的三维建筑地标），系统分析 3D 基础模型（3DFM）的误差与地理差异，揭示差异源于数据代表性、场景规模、区域建筑特征与背景干扰，并提出可同时提升性能、缩小地理差距的干预方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网规模的真实地标照片推动了三维视觉进展，但单次前馈估计场景结构的 3DFM 仍面临挑战；现有工作缺乏对模型失败模式（尤其是地理相关差异）的深入理解。

Method: 构建 ArchWorld 基准（高地理覆盖、丰富元数据的三维建筑地标），据此对现有 3DFM 做系统误差分析，考察模型误差的地理差异及其与训练数据代表性、场景规模、区域建筑特征、背景干扰的关系，并据此设计干预方案。

Result: 模型性能随地理区域变化，且与训练数据代表性的趋势仅部分吻合；地理差异不仅来自数据代表性与场景规模，也来自区域特有建筑特征和背景干扰导致的内在难度差异。

Conclusion: 基于失效模式洞察提出的干预方案在提升 3DFM 性能的同时减小了地理差异，表明误差分析可指导模型改进与公平性提升。

Abstract: Internet-scale photo collections of real-world landmarks have driven progress in 3D computer vision yet remain highly challenging for modern 3D foundation models (3DFMs) that estimate scene structure in a single feed-forward pass. In this work, we introduce ArchWorld, a benchmark of 3D architectural landmarks with high geographic coverage and rich metadata. We use this benchmark to conduct a thorough error analysis of current 3DFMs to understand where they fall short and how they can be improved. We examine socially relevant disparities in model error and find that model performance varies by geographic region; however, unlike typical fairness regimes, the trends only partially match training data representation. Our investigation reveals that the observed geographic disparities reflect not only differences in data representation and scene size but also differences in region-specific architectural features and background distractors that make scenes from different regions inherently vary in difficulty. Leveraging insights from these model failings, we introduce an intervention scheme that improves 3DFM performance while simultaneously reducing geographic disparities.

</details>


### [20] [KFS$^4$LAM: Adaptive KeyFrame Selection with Structure-Aware Mapping for Endoscopic Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22505)
*Xinwei Ju,Zhehua Mao,Tianyi Song,Danail Stoyanov,Sophia Bano,Francisco Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KFS⁴LAM，一种面向内窥镜的Gaussian Splatting SLAM框架，将运动引导的自适应关键帧选择与结构监督建图、受限时间形变相结合，在C3VD系列数据集上提升定位、建图与渲染性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gaussian-Splatting SLAM虽能提供稠密可渲染的三维场景表示，但其定位、重建与渲染性能高度依赖保留用于建图的观测。现有系统多采用固定时间间隔或简单的位姿/重叠度准则选取关键帧；在结肠镜场景中，摄像机运动不均匀、解剖结构重复、镜面高光与视觉伪影会导致这些准则失效，产生冗余关键帧或漏掉信息量大的视角。

Method: 1) 运动引导的自适应关键帧选择：以累积运动量驱动选取，并用互补线索抑制冗余或不可靠候选；2) 密度感知建图结合解剖轮廓（结肠皱襞）监督，强化结构信息丰富区域；3) 受限时间形变建模局部组织动态，避免无约束的几何漂移。

Result: 在C3VD与C3VD-v2上与Gaussian-SLAM基线对比并开展跨骨干与组件消融。整体在轨迹、渲染与深度指标上表现优异，在更难的C3VD-v2上增益明显：与SplaTAM匹配比较中，旋转相对位姿误差降低20.3%，深度RMSE降低38.4%，PSNR提升0.78 dB。跨骨干实验表明框架不依赖单一SLAM架构。

Conclusion: 结构感知的关键帧选择与建图监督可有效应对内窥镜场景的退化因素，且该框架具备跨骨干的通用性，代码与可视化结果已公开。

Abstract: Gaussian-splatting Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) augments conventional SLAM with a dense, renderable 3D scene representation, enabling high-quality reconstruction and novel-view synthesis. However, its localization, reconstruction, and rendering performance depends strongly on which observations are retained for mapping. Existing systems commonly rely on fixed temporal intervals or simple pose- and overlap-based criteria for keyframe selection. In colonoscopy, non-uniform camera motion, repetitive anatomy, specularities, and visual artifacts can make these criteria unreliable, leading to either redundant keyframes or missed informative viewpoints. We present KFS$^4$LAM (KeyFrame Selection with Structure-aware mapping for endoscopic Gaussian Splatting SLAM), which couples motion-guided adaptive keyframe selection with structurally supervised mapping and restricted temporal deformation. Accumulated motion guides adaptive keyframe selection, with complementary cues suppressing redundant or unreliable candidates. Density-aware mapping with anatomical contour supervision (colon folds) emphasizes structurally informative regions, while restricted temporal deformation accommodates local tissue dynamics without unconstrained geometric drift. We evaluate KFS$^4$LAM against Gaussian-SLAM baselines on C3VD and C3VD-v2, together with cross-backbone and component ablations. KFS$^4$LAM achieves strong overall performance across trajectory, rendering, and depth metrics, with pronounced gains on more challenging C3VD-v2 dataset. In the matched comparison with SplaTAM on C3VD-v2, it reduces rotational relative pose error by 20.3% and depth RMSE by 38.4%, while improving PSNR by 0.78 dB. Cross-backbone experiments further show that the framework is not tied to a single underlying SLAM architecture. Code and visualization results are available at https://xinweiju.github.io/KFSLAM/.

</details>


### [21] [Frame-Consistent Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting from Sparse Unposed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23039)
*Viruthshaan Vinasirajan,Ranga Rodrigo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出两阶段前馈高斯框架（冻结VGGT位姿估计 + 冻结TokenGS重建）质量下降的主因并非位姿噪声，而是坐标帧不一致与平移尺度失配两类可确定性修正的集成失败；直接修正后无需额外训练即可接近COLMAP参考质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈高斯模型使新视角合成近乎实时，两阶段（分别专精位姿估计与高质量重建）比单一联合训练网络更有优势。但VGGT-X、3R-GS等已有两阶段工作将质量下降归因于位姿估计噪声并引入学习式精修。作者质疑这一归因，希望通过逐相机分析定位真正的失败来源。

Method: 在DL3DV与MipNeRF360共107个场景上做逐相机分析，以COLMAP位姿为参考，测量对齐后的旋转误差与平移尺度偏移；并在140个DL3DV场景上进行受控消融，区分位姿误差与两类集成失败（坐标帧不一致、平移尺度失配），采用确定性校正替代学习式精修。管线为冻结VGGT驱动冻结TokenGS。

Result: 对齐后旋转误差很小（中位数1.6°），而平移尺度存在大幅且数据集一致的偏移因子。消融表明主要瓶颈是坐标帧不一致与平移尺度失配。修正后，在全视角位姿估计设置下达到22.47 PSNR，与COLMAP参考仅差0.51 dB，且无额外训练；在仅用4张上下文图像的稀疏无位姿协议下，同一管线仍达到全视角质量，形成完全无需COLMAP与精修步骤的流程。

Conclusion: 两阶段前馈高斯的性能损失主要源于坐标帧与平移尺度层面的集成失败，而非位姿精度问题，可通过确定性校正解决；由此得到一个无需COLMAP、无需训练精修的即插即用流程，为两阶段框架的性能归因与工程集成提供了新视角。

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian models have made novel view synthesis almost instantaneous. Feed-forward methods predict Gaussians directly, either in two stages or in a single merged pass. Using two separately trained models is attractive because each can specialize in its own objective: one for accurate pose estimation and the other for high-quality reconstruction, rather than forcing a single jointly trained network to compromise between the two. A natural instantiation pairs a frozen pose estimator, VGGT, with a frozen reconstruction backbone, TokenGS. Prior work, such as VGGT-X and 3R-GS, also adopts a two-stage design, but attributes the resulting quality degradation to noisy pose estimates and introduces learned refinement. We show that this is not the primary source of the problem. Through per-camera analysis across 107 scenes from two datasets, DL3DV and MipNeRF360, using COLMAP poses as the reference, we find that the rotation error after alignment is small (median 1.6 degrees), whereas the translation scale is offset by a large, dataset-consistent factor. Controlled ablations over 140 DL3DV scenes show that the primary obstacles are two integration failures rather than pose error: a coordinate-frame inconsistency and a translation-scale mismatch, both of which can be corrected deterministically. With these fixes, a frozen VGGT driving a frozen TokenGS achieves 22.47 PSNR, within 0.51 dB of the COLMAP reference, with no additional training, when poses are estimated from all scene views. In the sparse setting, where reconstruction uses only four context images under the standard pose-free protocol, the same pipeline matches this all-view quality, yielding a fully COLMAP-free pipeline without any refinement step.

</details>


### [22] [Reliability-Consistent Appearance Modeling for RGB-Infrared Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23125)
*Bingyang Wang,Jiazuo Yu,Lu Zhang,Lihe Zhang,Huchuan Lu,You He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RCA-MOT，保留RGB、红外与融合三路外观表示，并用无需训练的分支级可靠性估计在身份匹配边界判定与外观记忆更新中动态调控各模态证据贡献，在VT-MOT与UniRTL上刷新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-红外多目标跟踪方法多将两种模态证据压缩为单一描述子，当某一模态临时退化时无法单独调节其贡献，不可靠证据会污染关联匹配与外观记忆。

Method: 框架同时维护RGB专属、红外专属与融合三种身份表示作为可共同访问的外观状态；对每个检测用简单的免训练目标场景估计器产出分支级外观可靠性。该可靠性承担两种时序角色：相对可靠性用于调节不同身份分支，绝对可靠性用于校准ReID接受边界附近的候选，同一分支级可靠性还控制匹配描述子对外观历史记忆的更新强度。各组件刻意保持简单，以聚焦可靠性可控的外观组织。

Result: 在VT-MOT与UniRTL上取得新的SOTA，HOTA较此前最优分别提升5.402/6.075个点，IDF1分别提升11.549/12.789个点；事后分析进一步验证可靠性是有信息量的控制信号。

Conclusion: 保留跨模态互补性并使表示后的证据保持可控，是提升跨模态MOT鲁棒性的有效途径，且增益主要来自可靠性驱动的外观组织而非复杂融合或学习式可靠性估计。

Abstract: Recent RGB-infrared multi-object tracking (MOT) methods have substantially improved cross-modal representation learning and multimodal information integration. However, most existing methods compress RGB and infrared evidence into a unified descriptor. When one modality is temporarily degraded, the tracker can no longer regulate its contribution separately; unreliable evidence may distort association and contaminate appearance memory. We therefore propose RCA-MOT, a reliability-consistent appearance modeling framework that preserves cross-modal complementarity while retaining post-representation evidence controllability. RGB-specific, infrared-specific, and fused identity representations are maintained as jointly accessible appearance states. For each detection, a simple training-free target-scene estimator produces branch-wise appearance reliability used across the two temporal roles of appearance: relative reliability regulates different identity branches, absolute reliability calibrates borderline candidates near the ReID acceptance boundary, and the same branch-wise reliability controls how strongly matched descriptors update historical appearance memory. The individual components are intentionally simple, focusing evaluation on reliability-controllable appearance organization rather than elaborate fusion or learned reliability-estimation modules. RCA-MOT sets new SOTA results on VT-MOT and UniRTL, improving the previous best HOTA by 5.402/6.075 points and IDF1 by 11.549/12.789 points, respectively, while post-hoc analyses further validate reliability as an informative control signal. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [23] [Direct Conditional Parameterization for N-Dimensional Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22778)
*Zhongpai Gao,Benjamin Planche,Van Nguyen Nguyen,Meng Zheng,Anwesa Choudhuri,Arun Innanje,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出直接条件参数化（dGS/dBS），用显式逐效应参数替代由协方差推导的条件读取代价，在五个基准上质量持平或更优，切片快6.9–7.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N维splatting将3DGS推广到视角方向与时间维度，但现有N-DGS与UBS在每个前向渲染查询中都要对逐基元做矩阵求逆与回归乘积才能恢复条件均值、不透明度与协方差，计算开销大。

Method: 直接条件参数化：不再从联合协方差推导条件切片，而是显式定义逐效应参数——用Cholesky精度因子表示不透明度，用带可学习逐维耦合的因子化"查询到位置"回归算子表示条件均值；该因子化在保持有效联合协方差的同时引入空间尺度与耦合先验，并可同时用于Gaussian核与Beta核，分别得到dGS与dBS。

Result: 在五个静态与动态基准上质量持平或超越协方差推导版本，主MCMC评估最高提升+1.26 dB；生产切片比即时协方差条件化快6.9–7.7倍；完整渲染在四个已训练场景上提速1.25–1.91倍。

Conclusion: 直接条件化是一种结构化、核无关的N维splatting替代方案，兼得质量与效率。

Abstract: N-dimensional splatting extends 3D Gaussian Splatting with view direction and time to model view-dependent and dynamic effects. Each primitive is lifted into a joint distribution over position and conditioning variables, then conditionally sliced at each rendering query. N-DGS and UBS share this step across Gaussian and Beta kernels: recovering the conditional mean, opacity, and covariance requires per-primitive matrix inversions and regression products in every forward pass. We introduce direct conditional parameterization, replacing this covariance-derived readout with explicit per-effect parameters: a Cholesky precision factor for opacity and a factorized query-to-position regression operator with learnable per-dimension coupling. Unrestricted conditional coordinates recover a valid joint covariance; our factorization adds spatial-scale and coupling priors. Applied to both kernels, the parameterization yields dGS (Gaussian) and dBS (Beta). Across five static and dynamic benchmarks, they match or exceed their covariance-derived counterparts on quality, with gains up to +1.26 dB in the main MCMC evaluation. Production slicing is 6.9-7.7 times faster than on-the-fly covariance-derived conditioning; complete-render profiling gives 1.25-1.91 times speedups on four trained scenes. Direct conditioning provides a structured, kernel-agnostic alternative for N-dimensional splatting. Project page: https://gaozhongpai.github.io/dgs/.

</details>


### [24] [HilbertGen-3D:Hilbert--Multifractal Conditioning for Topology-Aware 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22720)
*Ziyi Zhang,Shixuan Li,Yorie Nakahira,Paul Bogdan,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HilbertGen-3D：基于Hilbert-Multifractal Conditioning的拓扑感知条件扩散点云生成框架，通过全局Hilbert-多重分形结构条件提升生成质量与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式点云生成方法过度依赖局部体素/块特征，忽视全局结构组织，且对如何利用全局条件提升生成质量研究不足。

Method: 提出HMC：计算多尺度体素测度，经3D Hilbert曲线序列化以显式建模空间依赖，并由尺度相关配分函数估计广义分形维数；将信号编码为全局嵌入与多尺度token，通过高效瓶颈融合模块注入扩散去噪器，形成两个条件变体。

Result: 在ShapeNet的Chair、Airplane、Car类别上，相对TopoDiT-3D，1-NNA偏离50%的绝对偏差最多降低8.90个百分点，覆盖率最多提升5.88个百分点。

Conclusion: 将局部密度线索与全局Hilbert-多重分形结构相结合，是一种简单有效的高保真、多样性3D生成方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D generative modeling underpins game asset creation, medical imaging, and VR/AR environmental design, where geometric structure directly influences perception and decision-making. Diffusion models represent a powerful paradigm for point cloud generation. However, existing approaches rely heavily on local voxel or patch features and often miss global structural organization, and much less work has studied how global conditioning can be used to improve generation quality. In this paper, we introduce HilbertGen-3D, a novel topology-aware conditional diffusion framework based on Hilbert-Multifractal Conditioning (HMC). HMC computes multi-scale voxel measures, serializes them via a 3D Hilbert curve to expose structured spatial dependencies, and estimates the generalized fractal dimensions from scale-dependent partition functions. We encode these signals as global embeddings and multi-scale tokens, and inject them into a diffusion denoiser through an efficient bottleneck fusion module. Across the Chair, Airplane, and Car categories of ShapeNet, our two conditioning variants reduce the absolute deviation of 1-Nearest Neighbor Accuracy (1-NNA) from 50% by up to 8.90 percentage points and increase coverage by up to 5.88 percentage points relative to TopoDiT-3D. This shows that combining local density cues with global Hilbert-multifractal structure provides a simple and effective recipe for high-fidelity and diverse 3D generation.

</details>


### [25] [Deep Learning for Deepfake Image Detection: Comparative Model Evaluation and Gradient-Based Visual Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22676)
*Saad Korchi,Abderrahim Waga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究比较了从零训练的紧凑卷积网络与ImageNet预训练MobileNetV2两阶段迁移学习在14万张真实/伪造人脸数据集上的深度伪造检测性能，验证准确率分别为84.15%与95.90%，并用Grad-CAM与Flask原型进行可解释性可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成人脸图像使真实与生成内容的区分对数字取证与在线信任日益重要，需要评估可解释的深度学习工作流，并厘清预训练表征与从头训练在小规模场景下的实际价值差异。

Method: 在平衡的140k Real and Fake Faces数据集（14万张）上，采用两种对比分类策略：从零训练的紧凑卷积模型（含数据增强）与经两阶段迁移学习微调的ImageNet预训练MobileNetV2；使用Grad-CAM可视化与预测相关的图像区域，并构建基于Flask的原型用于并排检查。

Result: 在记录的验证协议下，增强的从零模型达到84.15%准确率（损失0.3625），微调预训练模型达到95.90%准确率（损失0.1074）；结果为验证集结果，而非独立留出测试集估计。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持预训练视觉表征在该设定下的实用价值，同时强调需将验证性能与数据集来源、解释方法局限及评估范围结合解读。

Abstract: Synthetic facial imagery has made the distinction between authentic and generated content increasingly important for digital forensics and online trust. This study examines an explainable deep-learning workflow for image-level deepfake detection using two contrasting classification strategies: a compact convolutional model trained from scratch and an ImageNet-pretrained MobileNetV2 model adapted through two-stage transfer learning. Experiments were conducted with the balanced 140k Real and Fake Faces dataset, comprising 140,000 facial images. Under the recorded validation protocol, the augmented from-scratch model reached 84.15% accuracy (loss 0.3625), compared with 95.90% accuracy (loss 0.1074) for the fine-tuned pretrained model. Gradient-weighted class activation mapping (Grad-CAM) was used to visualize image regions associated with predictions, and a Flask-based prototype was developed for side-by-side inspection. These observations support the practical value of pretrained visual representations in the studied setting while underscoring the need to interpret validation performance alongside dataset provenance, explanation limitations, and evaluation scope. The reported results are validation results, not held-out test estimates.

</details>


### [26] [Stuck on Shuffle: On Attribute-Object Binding in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22602)
*Netanel Y. Tamir,Michael Green,Issar Tzachor,Or Shimshi,Dvir Samuel,Or Patashnik,Rami Ben-Ari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SABER基准评测跨模态属性-对象绑定，并仅用合成文本监督微调MLLM嵌入器，实现SOTA绑定检索且保持通用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准对属性-对象绑定的覆盖有限（仅部分模态配对、简单两对象交换、小属性集），无法判断模型在多种模态配对与复杂场景下的绑定鲁棒性。

Method: 构建SABER受控基准，覆盖全部四种图文模态配对、多样属性类型与最多十对象的场景；评估CLIP式双编码器与MLLM嵌入器；并仅使用合成的属性-绑定文本（含精确控制的困难负样本）微调MLLM嵌入式检索模型。

Result: 当前模型（双编码器与MLLM嵌入器）在SABER上均出现显著绑定失败；仅文本监督微调后，收益迁移至跨模态与图-图检索，SABER达SOTA，并在否定与实例检索基准上提升，通用嵌入性能保持。

Conclusion: 文本是高效、可扩展的绑定监督来源，无需图像监督即可显著改善组合式检索能力，并带来更广泛的检索收益。

Abstract: Retrieval is a fundamental task in multimodal systems: given an image or text query, the goal is to find the most relevant images or texts in a collection, often using a shared embedding space. Reliable retrieval requires these embeddings to capture not only which concepts are present, but also how they are composed. One key compositional challenge is attribute--object binding: encoding which attributes belong to which objects. Existing benchmarks provide a limited view of this capability, typically covering a subset of modality pairings, simple two-object swaps, or small attribute sets. Consequently, it remains unclear how robustly current models preserve attribute--object bindings across different modality pairings and more complex settings. We therefore introduce SABER, a controlled benchmark for evaluating attribute--object binding across all four image and text modality pairings, spanning diverse attribute types and scenes with up to ten objects. Evaluating models on SABER reveals substantial binding failures in both CLIP-style dual encoders and recent MLLM-based embedders. These findings motivate us to ask how binding can be improved efficiently. While multimodal supervision may be effective, collecting suitable images is difficult and costly. Text, on the other hand, is well suited to binding supervision: it is cheap to generate, supports a broader range of attributes and enables precisely controlled hard negatives. We thus finetune an MLLM-based embedder exclusively on synthetic attribute-binding text. Despite the absence of image supervision, the gains transfer to cross-modal and image--image retrieval while preserving general multimodal embedding performance. Our model achieves SOTA results on SABER while also improving on negation and instance-retrieval benchmarks, demonstrating broader benefits of our binding-focused supervision.

</details>


### [27] [ESC: Escaping 2D Diffusion Ambiguity with Geometric Constraints for Consistent Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23732)
*Ge Ya Luo,Daniel Severo,Brian Karrer,Christopher Pal,Adriana Romero-Soriano,Michal Drozdzal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出联合生成目标视图及其深度图的扩散框架，并引入逐像素深度一致性约束，提升大幅相机变化下单目新视图合成的几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从2D扩散模型生成3D一致的新视图仍是计算机视觉的基础难题；在剧烈相机变化（极端视角偏移）下，现有方法难以保证几何对齐与空间保真度。

Method: 以单张源图像、源深度、目标相机射线为条件驱动扩散管线，联合生成目标视图和目标深度图，促使模型在3D空间中推理；并提出逐像素深度一致性约束，在生成视图间验证并强制3D一致性。

Result: 构建了几何一致视图合成的稳健管线，在视觉精度与空间对齐方面优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为从单视图理解复杂环境的稳健3D世界模型奠定基础。

Abstract: Generating 3D-consistent novel views from 2D diffusion models remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. In this work, we introduce a framework that enhances geometric alignment for monocular view synthesis under drastic camera changes by jointly generating a target view and its corresponding depth map. By conditioning the diffusion pipeline on the single source image, source depth, and target camera rays, our approach forces the model to inherently reason in 3D space even under extreme viewpoint shifts. To guarantee spatial fidelity, we propose a novel per-pixel depth consistency constraint, which verifies and enforces 3D consistency across generated views. Our framework establishes a robust pipeline for geometrically consistent view synthesis, yielding superior visual accuracy and spatial alignment compared to current state-of-the-art methods. With this work, we pave the road towards robust 3D world models capable of understanding complex environments from a single view image.

</details>


### [28] [FedRAPP: Federated Test-Time Prompt Tuning for Vision-Language Models with Reliability-Aware Prompt Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23705)
*Syed Md. Ahnaf Hasan,Md Akil Raihan Iftee,Sajib Mistry,Amin Ahsan Ali,AKM Mahbubur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文探索无源联邦测试时提示微调（SF-FTTPT），分析异构客户端分布下传统联邦聚合的失效原因，并提出FedRAPP个性化聚合框架，在九个CLIP基准及ImageNet变体上稳定优于标准聚合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦提示学习方法主要面向有标签、训练分布相对稳定的监督场景，测试阶段的无源联邦提示微调几乎未被研究。测试时客户端可能收到无标签目标样本，其分布既跨客户端异构（常为非IID），又偏离源训练分布，导致本地自适应提示朝不同方向漂移，削弱联邦聚合效果。

Method: 提出FedRAPP个性化聚合框架，包含三部分：(1) 可靠性感知的客户端评估；(2) 服务器端指数滑动平均（EMA）提示记忆；(3) 相似度引导的同伴聚合。该框架选择性地融合来自可靠且兼容客户端的信息，同时保留本地专门化。

Result: 在九个CLIP基准数据集及ImageNet变体上的实验表明，在异构客户端分布下FedRAPP持续优于标准联邦聚合基线，并保持仅提示微调的高效性优势。

Conclusion: 传统联邦聚合在异构客户端分布下难以有效合并日益专门化的本地提示；通过可靠性评估、服务器端EMA提示记忆与相似度引导的同伴聚合进行个性化融合，可在分布偏移下实现更稳健的无源联邦测试时自适应。

Abstract: Federated prompt learning enables vision-language models like CLIP to collaboratively adapt lightweight soft prompts across distributed clients without sharing raw data. However, existing federated prompt learning methods are designed primarily for supervised settings with labeled client data and assumed relatively stable training distributions. At test time, source-free federated prompt tuning remains largely unexplored. Clients may receive unlabeled target samples with heterogeneous, often non-IID distributions that differ substantially across clients and from the source training distribution, causing locally adapted prompts to drift in different directions and reducing the effectiveness of federated aggregation. In this work, we explore Source-Free Federated Test-Time Prompt Tuning (SF-FTTPT), where distributed clients collaboratively adapt soft prompts on unlabeled test streams without access to source data. Our study reveals that conventional federated aggregation methods struggle under heterogeneous client distributions, as locally adapted prompts become increasingly specialized and difficult to combine effectively. To address this challenge, we propose FedRAPP, a personalized aggregation framework that combines reliability-aware client assessment, server-side exponential moving-average prompt memory, and similarity-guided peer aggregation. FedRAPP selectively incorporates information from reliable and compatible clients while preserving local specialization, enabling more robust adaptation under distribution shift. Extensive experiments across nine CLIP benchmark datasets and ImageNet variants demonstrate that FedRAPP consistently outperforms standard federated aggregation baselines under heterogeneous client distributions while maintaining the efficiency advantages of prompt-only adaptation.

</details>


### [29] [TriSAR-3D and PaintFusion: A Novel Trimodal LiDAR-RGB-Thermal Dataset and Method for 3D Person Detection in Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22509)
*Markus Kramer,Jan Wachenfeld,Oskar von Stryk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TriSAR-3D救援场景三模态人员检测数据集与PaintFusion融合检测流程，PaintFusion以65.4 AP@50优于LiDAR-only与BEVFusion等方法且延迟更低，并揭示训练数据姿态多样性对检测性能的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三模态3D检测研究多面向自动驾驶场景，行人多为直立、近路姿态，其性能能否迁移到救援场景（人员可能躺卧、部分遮挡、室内外非结构化环境）尚不明确，缺乏针对该差距的系统性评估数据与基准。

Method: 构建TriSAR-3D多模态数据集（128线高分辨率LiDAR、RGB相机、热成像相机），覆盖室内外、不同时段与多种姿态；提出PaintFusion三模态检测流程，基于PointPainting式多模态证据并用简单MLP进行融合；与CenterPoint、BEVFusion、Tri-BEVFusion等基线对比，并开展姿态类别泛化实验。

Result: PaintFusion达到65.4 AP@50，优于CenterPoint（53.4）、BEVFusion（60.5）与Tri-BEVFusion（60.7）；单帧延迟51.6 ms，显著快于BEVFusion的101 ms；从训练集中移除躺卧样本后，各检测器的躺卧姿态AP@50几乎降至零。

Conclusion: 数据集多样性（尤其姿态多样性）对3D人员检测性能影响显著；简单高效的PointPainting式MLP融合可在性能与速度上同时占优，但检测器对未见姿态的泛化能力仍然薄弱。

Abstract: Reliable 3D person detection is important for rescue robotics to give first responders situational awareness. Yet most multimodal 3D detection research targets automotive scenarios in which pedestrians mostly appear in a narrow range of upright, near-road poses. It is therefore unclear how much of the reported performance transfers to rescue settings, as a person may be lying, partially occluded, or in unstructured indoor and outdoor environments. To investigate this gap, we introduce TriSAR-3D, a multimodal person detection dataset recorded with a high-resolution 128-channel LiDAR, an RGB camera, and a thermal camera. It captures varied poses in indoor as well as outdoor locations at different times of day to study their influence on 3D object detector performance. Furthermore, we propose a novel trimodal 3D detection pipeline, PaintFusion, that fuses PointPainting-based evidence using a simple MLP. We show that the derived method reaches 65.4 AP@50 and thereby outperforms strong LiDAR-only (CenterPoint, 53.4), RGB+LiDAR (BEVFusion, 60.5), and adapted trimodal (Tri-BEVFusion, 60.7) baselines on the dataset. At the same time, it is substantially faster than other fusion-based methods such as BEVFusion, with a per-frame latency of 51.6 ms compared to 101 ms. Additionally, we test how the performance of different 3D object detectors generalizes to unseen pose categories. Removing lying instances from training data collapses the lying-pose AP@50 to near zero across all evaluated detectors. The results indicate that dataset diversity exerts a large effect on 3D object detection performance. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/MarkusKramer1/3D-Trimodal-People-Detection.

</details>


### [30] [Star Light, Star Bright: Can Multimodal Models Parse an Astronomy Visual Question Answering Dataset Right?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22474)
*J. P. Naiman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建天文图像专用VQA数据集，测试低成本LMMs在领域VQA任务上的表现，并与人类历史文献标注对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图形对科学交流至关重要，VQA可检验大型多模态模型（LMMs）的图形理解能力，但低成本LMMs在天文等专业领域图像上的表现尚不清楚。

Method: 构建领域专用VQA数据集：2001张图（等高线图、NASA SkyView真实望远镜图像、基于高斯混合模型着色但共享真实图像坐标轴与角尺度的"假"天空图像各占三分之一），共47357个问题；每张图提供原始版本与模拟历史文献页面老化的退化版本；测试三个低成本专有LMMs，并与104张来自历史期刊、经人工验证且具天体测量标签的图像进行对比。

Result: 低成本LMMs在专业领域问题上常无法达到高准确率；在角尺度（angular extent）估计上，模型与人类标注的中位误差相近。

Conclusion: 低成本LMMs在领域专用天文VQA任务上仍不可靠，需专门数据集与评测推动改进；代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Figures are essential for scientific communication, and the field of Visual Question Answering (VQA) aims to test the figure understanding capabilities of large multimodal models (LMMs). Here, we investigate the accuracy of several low-cost LMMs on domain-specific VQA tasks for astronomical images of the sky. As instrument for this task we build a domain-specific VQA dataset comprising 2001 partially-synthetic figures divided evenly between contour plots, real telescope images drawn from NASA's SkyView, and "fake" sky images which share the axes and angular scales of the real images but draw their colors from a gaussian mixture model, alongside 47357 questions. Each figure is released as pristine and with realistic page-aging degradations mimicking the historical astronomical corpus. Testing three low-cost proprietary LMMs, we find models frequently fail to answer domain-specific questions at high accuracy. An initial comparison with 104 human-verified, astrometrically tagged figures from historical journal publications shows similar median errors in angular extent. All code is available on GitHub: https://github.com/jnaiman/SkyImagesWASP2026 and dataset is available on HuggingFace: https://huggingface.co/datasets/ReadingTimeMachine/astro_sky_image_vqa.

</details>


### [31] [VTrajEmbed: Aligning Text with Video Object Trajectories via Spatiotemporal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22842)
*Ruizhi Chen,Zining Fan,Yudi Guo,Jiang Liu,Zhiyu Lin,Zhongjiang He,Shouqian Shi,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VTrajEmbed嵌入模型，将视频分解为目标轨迹并逐轨迹与文本对齐，实现对象级grounding；包含Slow-Fast轨迹编码、掩码引导对比学习与Text2Traj-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型擅长全局检索，但其单一全局表示将多个对象及其时空动态纠缠在一起，难以进行直接的对象级grounding；文本与视频对象轨迹的细粒度对齐是多模态理解的核心挑战。

Method: 1) Slow-Fast轨迹编码：双分支架构，稠密采样低分辨率帧捕获高频运动线索，稀疏选取高分辨率帧获取场景上下文，二者由共享LLM主干聚合；2) 掩码引导对比学习：在关系掩码下耦合组内损失与批次级双向损失，排除未验证关系并避免确认匹配沦为假负例；3) Text2Traj-Bench：模型辅助构建、人工校验查询—轨迹关系的轨迹级文本—视频基准。

Result: VTrajEmbed-5B在文本到轨迹及轨迹到文本检索的R@1上分别较Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B提升15.48与18.05个百分点；在五个全局检索基准上平均R@1达49.48%，而Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B为42.86%。

Conclusion: 以对象轨迹为基本单元进行嵌入与对齐，可在保持甚至超越全局检索性能的同时显著提升对象级轨迹grounding能力，验证了轨迹级分解表示的有效性。

Abstract: Fine-grained alignment between text and video object trajectories is a core challenge in multimodal understanding. Existing embedding models excel at global retrieval, yet their single global representation entangles multiple objects and their spatiotemporal dynamics, limiting direct object-level grounding. To address this issue, we propose VTrajEmbed, an embedding model that decomposes a video into object trajectories and aligns each trajectory embedding with text for object-level grounding through retrieval. Our main contributions: (1)Slow-Fast Trajectory Encoding, a dual-branch architecture that establishes trajectory representations by densely sampling low-resolution frames for high-frequency motion cues and sparsely selecting high-resolution frames for scene context, aggregated by a shared LLM backbone; (2)Mask-Guided Contrastive Learning, a training objective built upon these representations that couples an intra-group loss and a batch-level bidirectional loss under a relation mask, excluding unverified relations and preventing confirmed matches from becoming false negatives; (3)Text2Traj-Bench, a trajectory-level text-video benchmark with model-assisted construction and human-verified query--trajectory relations. Experimental results show that VTrajEmbed-5B improves text-to-trajectory and trajectory-to-text R@1 over Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B by 15.48 and 18.05 percentage points, respectively, and achieves 49.48% average R@1 across five global retrieval benchmarks, compared with 42.86% for Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B.

</details>


### [32] [MedSTP: Preserving Subspace and Transition Evidence for Medical VQA Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22751)
*Mengchen Fan,Qizhen Lan,Xi Xiao,Lin Zhao,Liang He,Tianyang Wang,Xiaoqian Jiang,Pulin Che,Baocheng Geng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的“子空间-转移”视觉token剪枝方法（Subspace-Transition Token Pruning），在医学VQA上兼顾剪枝效率与边界、纹理证据保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在VQA中需将长视觉token序列送入语言模型，剪枝可提升效率，但医学图像答案常依赖局部边界与纹理证据，直接剪枝风险高。作者将免训练的预解码剪枝建模为“子空间稳定的证据保留”问题。

Method: 要求保留token尽量贴近由全量token诱导的主导视觉证据子空间。方法在PCA主成分空间中估计每个token的谱支持度，构建紧凑表示子空间；并引入有界的token网格转移先验（bounded token-grid transition prior），以强化边界与纹理敏感token。

Result: 在闭合式SLAKE、OmniMedVQA基准及同时提供闭合式与开放式评估的RadImageNet-VQA上，采用Lingshu-7B与Qwen3-VL-8B骨干，方法在闭合式与开放式评估中均取得稳定且较强的性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明该方法是一种适用于医学领域VQA的有效免训练剪枝框架。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) for visual question answering (VQA) often pass long visual-token sequences to the language model, making visual-token pruning attractive but risky for medical images, where answers may depend on localized boundary and texture evidence. We study training free pre-decoding pruning and treat pruning as a subspace stable evidence preservation problem. The retained tokens should remain close to the dominant visual evidence subspace induced by the full token set. To this end, we propose \emph{Subspace-Transition Token Pruning} (\method{}), which estimates each token's spectral support from principal component space and constructs a compact representation subspace. To better reflect medical image structure, \method{} further uses a bounded token-grid transition prior to promote boundary and texture sensitive tokens. We evaluate \method{} on the closed-ended SLAKE and OmniMedVQA benchmarks and on RadImageNet-VQA, which provides both closed-ended and open-ended evaluations, using Lingshu-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B backbones. \method{} delivers consistently strong performance on both the closed-ended and open-ended RadImageNet-VQA evaluations. These results show that \method{} is an effective training free pruning framework for medical domain VQA.

</details>


### [33] [Look, Reason, Draw a Region: Recursive Refinement for Spatial Context Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23302)
*Wajeeha Tahir,Anna Tegon,Lionnus Kesting,Gamze İslamoğlu,Kristóf Karacs,Luca Benini,Thorir Mar Ingolfsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Spatial-TRM：冻结视觉语言特征+凸区域解码器的紧凑递归模型，仅11.2M可训练参数，在RoboSpatial-Home上空间上下文预测准确率29.51%（较同参数量单步模型+9.02pp，相对提升44.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间上下文预测需要在场景中识别相对物体而言的空白区域，依赖视觉定位与空间推理；而大视觉语言模型在空间关系与物理约束上仍显不足。递归推理在离散/抽象推理任务上表现优异，作者据此希望将其引入自然图像的空间上下文预测，并借助权重共享在少量可训练参数下获得更高精度。

Method: 设计Spatial-TRM：以冻结的视觉语言特征为输入，配凸区域解码器（convex region decoder），可训练参数仅11.2M。递归推理器采用权重绑定（weight-tied）结构，支持六种空间关系、三种参考坐标系与开放词表锚点物体。以相同参数量的分步训练单步模型作为对照，验证重复精化的作用。

Result: 在RoboSpatial-Home基准上取得29.51%上下文准确率，较同参数量独立训练的单步模型提升9.02个百分点（相对44.0%）。计入冻结编码器后总参数877.8M，约为RoboPoint报告13B规模的1/15。

Conclusion: 在该基准上，重复迭代精化可在固定可训练参数量的条件下，提升基于预训练视觉语言特征的空间上下文预测性能。

Abstract: Spatial context prediction requires visual grounding and spatial reasoning to identify vacant regions relative to objects in a scene. Spatial relationships and physical constraints remain challenging for large vision-language models (VLMs). Recursive reasoning has demonstrated strong performance on discrete and abstract reasoning tasks, motivating its use for improving spatial context prediction in natural images. Weight sharing enables higher accuracy with a small trainable parameter count. We introduce Spatial-TRM, a compact recursive model with frozen vision-language features, a convex region decoder, and just 11.2M trainable parameters. This weight-tied reasoner supports six spatial relationships, three reference frames, and open-vocabulary anchor objects. On the RoboSpatial-Home benchmark, Spatial-TRM achieves 29.51% context accuracy, a gain of 9.02 percentage points (44.0% relative) over a separately trained single-step model with the same parameter count. Including the frozen encoders, the model has 877.8M total parameters, approximately $15\times$ fewer than the reported 13B size of RoboPoint. These results show that repeated refinement improves spatial context prediction from pretrained vision-language features at a fixed trainable parameter count on this benchmark.

</details>


### [34] [SCALE: Improving Guidance-Free Visual Generation via Self-Contrastive Alignment for Likelihood Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23263)
*Jiwan Hur,Dong-Jae Lee,Dongyeun Lee,Gyojin Han,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自对比引导（SCG）及其训练框架SCALE，通过对比模型在真实样本与自生成样本上的预测来构建直接基于模型的引导信号，并将其融入标准似然目标，实现单次前向的无引导采样，性能超越CFG及CFG模拟类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFG是视觉生成提升样本质量的关键，但带来约两倍采样开销；现有无引导（guidance-free）方法虽通过单次推理近似CFG将成本减半，但其性能上限受CFG约束并继承其局限，缺乏超越CFG的独立引导信号。

Method: 1) 提出自对比引导SCG：对比模型对真实样本与自生成样本的预测，得到一个直接、基于模型的对比信号，将生成推向真实数据流形；2) 通过重参数化将SCG整合进标准似然目标，构建SCALE训练框架；3) 训练后的模型可在单次前向传播中直接输出SCG增强的预测，无需额外引导。

Result: 在扩散模型与自回归模型上开展类条件生成与文生图实验，SCALE持续优于模拟CFG的无引导基线；与DPO结合后性能进一步提升，表明自对比信号与CFG及偏好微调的收益互补。

Conclusion: 自对比信号提供了一种不依赖CFG上限的独立引导来源，可在无额外采样开销下提升生成质量，且与CFG和偏好优化方法正交互补，为高效、可扩展的引导式生成训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is essential for improving sample quality in visual generation, but it incurs substantial sampling overhead. Existing guidance-free approaches halve this cost by approximating the CFG with a single model inference, but their performance is bounded by the CFG and inherits its limitations. In this paper, we propose self-contrastive guidance (SCG), which contrasts the model's prediction on real and self-generated samples to obtain a direct, model-based signal that pushes generation toward the true data manifold. Integrating SCG into the standard likelihood objective via reparameterization yields Self-Contrastive Alignment for Likelihood Estimation (SCALE), a guidance-free training framework that produces SCG-enhanced predictions in a single forward pass. Across class-conditional and text-to-image generation on diffusion and autoregressive models, SCALE consistently outperforms CFG-mimicking guidance-free baselines and improves further when combined with DPO, demonstrating that the self-contrastive signal yields gains complementary to both CFG and preference-based fine-tuning.

</details>


### [35] [Can the Parts Fool the Test? Counterfactual Pairing Cycles for Relational OOD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23248)
*Yibo Zhou,Yirong Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"反事实配对循环"评测原语：复用同一对图像与同一对描述、仅交换配对关系，使图像、文本及加性捷径在有限样本中精确抵消，用于评估视觉-语言模型的"关系性分布外"能力并可作为训练损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系性OOD错误（描述本身自然、物体常见，但关系错误）难以干净评测。现有评测的负样本在描述风格、模板、长度、物体频率或图像统计上存在差异，模型无需检验图文配对即可解题，导致评测被捷径污染。

Method: 构造counterfactual pairing cycles：在比较的两侧复用同两张图像与同两条描述，仅改变图文配对方式（blocked design）。理论上使仅图像、仅文本及加性组件捷径在有限样本中完全抵消；同一对比同时导出关系性outlier exposure的训练损失与双线性视觉-语言打分器的诊断工具；在合成对照、GQA、COCO上验证。

Result: cycles能区分粗粒度图文匹配与方向性角色绑定失败，并识别在全局特征中仍显著的几何尺寸关系；在受捷径污染的COCO outlier-exposure压力测试中，按近似完美捷径验证AUROC选出的模型仍保留可观关系信号但弱于cycle training，后者将CycleAcc从0.882提升至0.927。

Conclusion: 测试模型是否理解配对，应保持各组成部分不变、仅改变配对，并只询问耦合关系；cycles提供了原理干净、可作评测与训练的统一工具。
