Abstract: Relational out-of-distribution errors are easy to describe and hard to evaluate cleanly. An image may be natural, a caption may be fluent, and all mentioned objects may be common, yet the caption may describe the wrong relation in the image. Many evaluations intended to test this behavior can be solved without checking the pairing at all, because negatives differ in caption style, template, length, object frequency, or image statistics. We propose counterfactual pairing cycles, a blocked evaluation primitive that reuses the same two images and the same two captions on both sides of the comparison, changing only which image is paired with which caption. This makes image-only, text-only, and additive component shortcuts cancel exactly in finite samples. The same contrast also gives a training loss for relational outlier exposure and a diagnostic for bilinear vision-language scores. Across synthetic controls, GQA, and COCO, cycles separate coarse image-caption matching from directional role-binding failures, while also identifying geometric size relations that remain strongly visible in global features. In a shortcut-contaminated COCO outlier-exposure stress test, a model selected by a near-perfect shortcut validation AUROC retains substantial relational signal but remains below cycle training on exact cycles; cycle training improves CycleAcc from 0.882 to 0.927. The main lesson is simple: to test whether a model understands a pairing, the benchmark should keep the parts fixed, change the pairing, and ask only about coupling. The official implementation is available online.

</details>


### [36] [Knowledge-Guided Distributional Prompt Learning with Visual Hierarchy for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23172)
*Jiyul Ham,Seongho Yoon,Yonggon Jung,Sangjae Lee,Jun-Geol Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HiDist，一个以正常性为参照的层次化分布提示框架，通过将异常提示分布参数化为对正常分布的偏差，并分别特化为全局/局部表示，统一提升零样本异常检测与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本异常检测方法未显式结合“以正常性为参照的分布建模”与“层次特定的提示特化”，难以在建模相对正常性的异常语义的同时兼顾图像级检测与细粒度定位。

Method: 用对角高斯分布建模每个提示token，通过蒙特卡洛采样生成随机提示变体；异常提示分布不独立学习，而是参数化为学习到的正常参照的偏差；所得分布分别特化为全局表示（图像级检测）与局部表示（像素级定位），并由适配的全局视觉表示引导层次对齐。

Result: 在七个工业与医学数据集上，于检测与定位任务均表现出强跨数据集性能；消融与表示分析验证了分布建模与层次特定表示学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 以正常性为参照的层次化分布提示学习能有效提升零样本异常检测的检测与定位能力，且异常-正常依赖建模与层次特化是关键设计。

Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD) aims to detect anomalies in previously unseen classes using auxiliary images, with applications such as industrial visual inspection and medical imaging. Recent vision-language-model-based approaches improve ZSAD by aligning textual and visual representations, while distributional prompting further models prompt variability for unseen categories. However, existing methods do not explicitly combine normality-referenced distributional modeling with hierarchy-specific prompt specialization, limiting their ability to model anomalous semantics relative to normality while addressing both image-level detection and fine-grained localization. We propose Hierarchical Knowledge-Guided Distributional Prompt Learning (HiDist), a normality-referenced hierarchical distributional prompt framework for ZSAD. HiDist models each prompt token with a diagonal Gaussian distribution and generates stochastic prompt variations through Monte Carlo sampling. Rather than learning normal and anomalous distributions independently, anomalous prompt distributions are parameterized as deviations from learned normal references, explicitly encoding their dependency. The resulting distributions are specialized into global and local representations for image-level detection and pixel-level localization, respectively, while an adapted global visual representation guides hierarchical alignment. Experiments on seven industrial and medical datasets demonstrate strong cross-dataset performance across anomaly detection and localization tasks. Extensive ablation and representation analyses further support the effectiveness of the proposed distributional modeling and hierarchy-specific representation learning.

</details>


### [37] [TriFold: Folding irregular 3D Gaussians into structured latents for 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23050)
*Yun Wang,Chiyu Wang,Fan Jiang,Ziyu Zhu,Zhaoxu Sun,Guangchi Fang,Mu Xu,Dapeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriFold 是一种原生 3D Gaussian VAE，将高斯场景编码为固定布局 triplane 潜变量并联合重建几何与外观，并结合潜空间 flow-matching 实现条件式场景级 3DGS 生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生 3D Gaussian 场景的紧凑生成式表示学习困难：基元数量差异大、空间密度非均匀，且空间相邻基元的几何与外观仍可能显著不同，导致难以学习结构规整的潜表示。

Method: 核心思路是在潜空间施加结构规整性，同时保留底层高斯的空间自适应性。编码端采用逐 cell 的可学习 query，将投影高斯聚合为每 cell 多个 slot，缓解单特征池化的信息损失；解码端先由 occupancy 预测识别活跃区域，再通过 query-based set prediction 与候选选择重建基元数可变的高斯集合。基于该表示训练潜空间 flow-matching 模型，实现点云条件与单图条件的场景级 3DGS 生成（含 amodal 生成）。

Result: 实验表明 TriFold 实现高保真重建，并能仅凭提供场景部分观测的单张图像生成高质量场景，验证了其对原生 3D Gaussian 表示学习（重建与生成）的有效性。

Conclusion: 固定布局 triplane 潜空间与空间自适应的集合式高斯重建可有效弥合规整潜表示与原生 3DGS 之间的鸿沟，为场景级 3DGS 的条件生成提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Recent progress in 3D generation has motivated growing interest in compact generative representations of complex 3D scenes. For native 3D Gaussian scenes, however, learning such representations remains challenging due to widely varying primitive counts, nonuniform spatial density, and differences in geometry and appearance even among spatially neighboring primitives. In this work, we introduce TriFold, a native 3D Gaussian VAE that encodes Gaussian scenes into a fixed-layout triplane latent and jointly reconstructs their geometry and appearance. The key insight is to impose structural regularity on the latent space while preserving the spatial adaptivity of the underlying Gaussians. During encoding, we use cell-wise learned queries to aggregate projected Gaussians into multiple slots per cell, reducing the information loss caused by single-feature pooling. During decoding, occupancy prediction identifies active regions, where query-based set prediction and candidate selection reconstruct local Gaussian sets with spatially varying primitive counts. Building on this representation, we train a latent flow-matching model for conditional scene-level 3DGS generation, enabling point-cloud-conditioned synthesis and single-image-conditioned amodal generation. Experiments demonstrate that TriFold achieves high-fidelity reconstruction and generation from a single image that provides only a partial observation of the scene, validating its effectiveness for native 3D Gaussian representation learning. https://fantasy-amap.github.io/TriFold

</details>


### [38] [Semantic-Critical Regions for Video-to-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23021)
*Yun Fang,Wei Guo,Zhuonan Liang,Heng Wang,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCR-V2A框架，通过显式定位并编码"语义关键区域"（SCRs）为细粒度视觉引导，提升视频到音频生成的质量与分布匹配，且可训练参数少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实视频中的声音事件往往与局部、细粒度的视觉线索相关（物体运动、部件动态、接触、交互）。现有V2A方法仅用全局视觉表示的隐式条件或基于时空线索的显式控制，难以捕捉细微的"发声/声音指示"交互，视觉条件对声音语义与时序动态的刻画不够精确。

Method: 将含声音相关视觉证据的区域定义为语义关键区域（SCRs）；先通过声音相关的视觉定位识别SCRs，再经基于适配器的双视觉编码将其转为控制表示——同时捕获全局视觉上下文与细粒度声音相关证据；以该表示对下游音频生成模型进行条件控制。

Result: 在域内与域外基准上，SCR-V2A在分布匹配与音频质量方面表现强，跨模态对齐具有竞争力，且所需可训练参数很少。

Conclusion: 优先利用细粒度、与声音相关的视觉区域对V2A生成具有显著价值，SCR定位与双编码条件是一种高效、参数友好的思路。

Abstract: In real-world videos, sound events are often associated with localized and fine-grained visual cues, such as object motion, object-part dynamics, contact events, and interactions between objects. Existing video-to-audio (V2A) methods employ implicit conditioning based on global visual representations or explicit control derived from temporal and spatial cues. However, subtle sound-producing and sound-indicative interactions remain insufficiently captured, leaving room for more precise visual conditioning of sound-event semantics and temporal dynamics. Motivated by this observation, we refer to regions containing such sound-relevant visual evidence as Semantic-Critical Regions (SCRs) and introduce SCR-V2A, a V2A generation framework that explicitly localizes and encodes SCRs as fine-grained visual guidance for audio generation. Specifically, we first identify SCRs through sound-related visual localization. We then transform the localized regions into a control representation through adapter-based dual visual encoding, which jointly captures global visual context and fine-grained sound-relevant evidence. The representation is used to condition a downstream audio generation model. Experiments on both in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks demonstrate that SCR-V2A achieves strong performance in distribution matching and audio quality while remaining competitive in cross-modal alignment and requiring few trainable parameters. These results highlight the value of prioritizing fine-grained, sound-relevant visual regions for V2A generation.

</details>


### [39] [How Murky Before a VLM Knows It Cannot See? Accuracy, Confidence and Abstention on a Physical Visibility Axis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22981)
*Mochamad Rizal Fauzan,Irgi Surya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以米为单位的物理可见度轴（雾按Koschmieder模型、水下按吸收谱缩放，V从250 m扫至1.95 m）测评五个2–5B开源VLM，发现准确率在窄可见度带内跌至随机水平，而四个模型在干净图像上自信时其内部与言语化置信度仍维持高位，形成至少0.79个数量级（约6.2倍可见度）的"盲区"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM鲁棒性研究用无物理单位的"level 1–5"名义严重度分级，且很少考察模型置信度是否随可见性丧失而同步下降；作者希望把退化锚定到可度量的物理可见度（米），并检验准确率、置信度与弃答行为是否一致。

Method: 在1000张COCO场景、五个2–5B小型开源VLM上做带框物体识别任务。用单目度量深度图施加雾（Koschmieder模型）与水下（纯水吸收谱缩放），可见度V取8个对数间隔档（250 m至1.95 m）。测量准确率、token概率内部置信度、言语化置信度及显式弃答选项；并设空白图像、打乱图像对照、先验匹配答案集、200张图像预实验，与ImageNet-C雾等级1–5对照。

Result: 准确率在窄带内由近干净值降至随机：雾中半程可见度V_acc为12.1–15.9 m，水下为20.5–38.6 m。四个干净图像上自信的模型在V=1.95 m雾中准确率已达随机，但平均内部置信度仍为0.68–0.84、言语化置信度0.61–1.00，盲区宽度W=log10(V_acc/V_aware)≥0.79个数量级（可见度6.2倍）。另一模型全程欠自信且内部置信度随准确率变化。显式弃答在指明原因时跟随准确率（W为−0.21至−0.06），但某模型用泛化"I don't know"时滞后（W达0.18）。ImageNet-C雾等级1–5仅对应55–110 m物理可见度，落在失效带之外，模型准确率变化在5个百分点以内。

Conclusion: 名义严重度分级（如ImageNet-C 1–5）远未触及物理失效带，掩盖了VLM的置信度盲区；结论为描述性，仅一次1000张图像抽样、依赖假定尺度的深度、模型规模限于2–5B。

Abstract: Robustness studies of vision-language models (VLMs) grade degradation on nominal severity scales ("level 1-5") that carry no physical units, and they rarely ask whether a model's confidence follows the loss of visibility. We measure accuracy, internal (token-probability) confidence, verbalized confidence and an explicit abstain option on a visibility axis in metres: fog follows the Koschmieder model, an underwater arm scales the absorption spectrum of pure water, both are applied with a monocular metric depth map, and the visibility range V is swept over eight log-spaced levels from 250 m to 1.95 m. On 1000 COCO scenes and five small open VLMs (2-5 B parameters), accuracy on a boxed-object identification task falls from near its clean value to chance within a narrow band (half-way visibility V_acc of 12.1-15.9 m in fog and 20.5-38.6 m under water) while, for the four models that are confident on clean images, the confidence stays high: at V=1.95 m in fog their accuracy is at chance but their mean internal confidence is 0.68-0.84 and their verbalized confidence 0.61-1.00, a blind-spot width W=log10(V_acc/V_aware) of at least 0.79 decades, i.e. a factor of 6.2 in visibility. The remaining model is under-confident throughout and its internal confidence follows accuracy. An explicit abstain option follows accuracy when it names the reason (W from -0.21 to -0.06 in fog) but, for one model, lags with a generic "I don't know" (W up to 0.18). ImageNet-C fog severities 1-5 correspond to only 55-110 m of physical visibility, outside the failure band, and leave the accuracy of the models within 5 percentage points of its clean value. Blank-image and shuffled-image controls, a prior-matched answer set and a 200-image pilot that preceded the study are reported. Findings are descriptive: one draw of 1000 images, depth of assumed scale, and models of 2-5 B parameters.

</details>


### [40] [Query-adaptive Recurrent Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22957)
*Bavesh Balaji,Pranav Gopinath,Pouya Sadeghi,Sirisha Rambhatla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QART（查询自适应循环分词器），一个与主干无关的模块，使视觉表示既可被查询引导、又可自适应调整token预算：从冻结编码器特征出发循环构建紧凑表示，以查询相关视觉支撑集上的重建质量控制token分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉与视觉-语言模型大多独立于查询编码图像，并为每个输入分配固定的视觉token预算；但图像中值得提取的信息及其所需token数实际上取决于文本查询，因此需要在内容与容量两方面都实现查询自适应。

Method: QART为backbone-agnostic模块：以冻结编码器特征为起点，循环地构建紧凑视觉表示，并依据"查询相关视觉支撑集上的重建质量"来控制何时停止分配额外token——即不断分配token直至查询相关视觉特征以足够保真度被表示。

Result: 在Visual7W与VQAv2上分别达到81.96%与76.37%准确率，仅需约29个视觉token，相比稠密VLM输入减少20–25倍token量；CORE上P@1为92.0%，PODS上PR-AUC为36.3%，较SteerViT在PODS上提升8.4%。

Conclusion: 视觉表示在内容与容量上均无需固定，可按每个查询的需求自适应；QART以极少token实现有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Visual information worth extracting from an image and the token budget it warrants depend on the text query. Yet modern vision and vision-language models largely encode images independently of the query and allocate a fixed visual token budget to every input. We introduce the Query-Adaptive Recurrent Tokenizer (QART), a backbone-agnostic module that makes visual representations both query-steerable and budget-adaptive. QART steers the visual representation toward query-relevant evidence while adapting its token budget to the amount of information required. Its key principle is simple. Additional tokens are allocated until the visual features relevant to the query are represented with sufficient fidelity. Starting from frozen encoder features, QART recurrently constructs a compact representation and uses reconstruction quality over the query-relevant visual support to control token allocation. On Visual7W and VQAv2, QART achieves 81.96% and 76.37% accuracy using only about 29 visual tokens, reducing token counts by 20-25x relative to dense VLM inputs. It further reaches 92.0% P@1 on CORE and 36.3% PR-AUC on PODS, outperforming SteerViT on PODS by 8.4%. Together, these results show that visual representations need not be fixed in either content or capacity and can instead adapt to what each query requires.

</details>


### [41] [DICL-Qwen3VL: Decoupled Confidence Calibration for Interpretable Workwear Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22929)
*Meng Wang,Wu Zeng,Mei Li,Chao He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解耦可解释VLM框架DICL-Qwen3VL，用于室内工地着装合规检测：LoRA微调Qwen3-VL定位与识别工作服，将视觉观测置信度与合规推理置信度解耦并分别标定，再用GRPO联合优化检测精度、输出有效性与置信度校准；在ICSWD和WICWD上mAP@50分别达0.730与0.753，优于所评估的LoRA-VLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内工地存在弱光与遮挡，着装线索不清；工作服样式多样使合规判定本身存在歧义。现有VLM虽能定位与语义推理，但预测缺乏显式支撑证据，且单一置信度无法区分"看不清"的视觉不确定性与"判不准"的合规歧义，不利于工业安全监控中的人工复核。

Method: 1) 用LoRA适配Qwen3-VL，定位可见上半身着装并判别是否为工作服；2) 解耦校准：将视觉观测与合规推理分离，各自输出置信度；视觉置信度以token熵及对图像扰动的敏感性作为监督信号，使置信度与生成观测及其对图像内容的依赖相绑定；3) 以GRPO强化学习联合优化检测精度、输出有效性与置信度校准。

Result: 在自建室内工地工作服数据集ICSWD与网络采集数据集WICWD上，mAP@50分别为0.730与0.753，超过所评估的LoRA适配VLM基线（含Qwen3-VL）；框架同时输出显式证据与分离置信度，支持人工复核。WICWD已开源。

Conclusion: 将检测、证据生成与视觉/推理双置信度解耦建模，可提升弱光遮挡与样式多样场景下的工作服合规判定可解释性与可靠性，为工业安全监控提供可直接落地的人工复核支持；但结论主要基于两项自建工作服数据集，跨场景泛化性尚待验证。

Abstract: Poor illumination and occlusion obscure clothing cues in indoor work sites, while diverse clothing styles complicate workwear compliance judgments. Although vision-language models (VLMs) support localization and semantic reasoning, their predictions often lack explicit supporting evidence, and a single confidence score cannot distinguish visual uncertainty from ambiguity in compliance judgments. We propose DICL-Qwen3VL, a decoupled interpretable VLM that integrates structured workwear detection, evidence generation, and separate visual and reasoning confidence estimates. First, low-rank adaptation (LoRA) adapts Qwen3-VL to localize visible upper-body clothing and identify workwear. Second, decoupled calibration separates visual observations from compliance reasoning and assigns a confidence score to each. Token entropy and sensitivity to image perturbations provide supervision for visual confidence, linking confidence estimation to the generated observations and their dependence on image content. Finally, group relative policy optimization (GRPO) jointly optimizes detection accuracy, output validity, and confidence calibration. Experiments on the company-collected Indoor Construction Site Workwear Dataset (ICSWD) and the web-collected Indoor Construction Workwear Dataset (WICWD) yield mAP@50 values of 0.730 and 0.753, respectively, outperforming the evaluated LoRA-adapted VLM baselines, including Qwen3-VL. The framework also provides explicit evidence and separate confidence scores to support manual review in industrial safety monitoring. WICWD is publicly available at https://github.com/DaErwen1/WICWD.

</details>


### [42] [From Adult Renal Masses to Pediatric Wilms Tumors: Evaluating Cross-Domain Generalization of Kidney Lesion Segmentation on MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23568)
*Sarah de Boer,Myrthe A. D. Buser,Hartmut Häntze,Jawed Nawabi,Lisa C. Adams,Alida F. W. van der Steeg,Marry M. van den Heuvel-Eibrink,Annemieke S. Littooij,Keno K. Bressem,Mathias Prokop,Alessa Hering*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在成人肾肿瘤MRI上训练的肿瘤分割模型可迁移至儿童Wilms瘤，中位DSC达0.85，接近儿童专用nnU-Net的0.90，但未达到非劣效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童肿瘤分割模型常受限于小样本、单中心数据，而成人肾癌等疾病拥有大规模数据集。本文旨在探究在成人肾病灶上训练的分割模型能否泛化到儿童Wilms瘤，以评估跨人群迁移的可行性。

Method: 基于成人肾脏MRI单序列（T1或T2加权）图像训练分割模型；在留出的成人测试集与儿童Wilms瘤队列上分别评估，并与儿童专用nnU-Net对比；评价指标为Dice相似系数（DSC）与95百分位Hausdorff距离。

Result: 成人测试集上肾病灶中位DSC为0.86；儿童队列中，成人训练模型中位DSC为0.85，儿童专用模型为0.90。尽管年龄、解剖结构与肿瘤类型存在差异，成人训练模型在儿童Wilms瘤上表现有前景，但非劣效性未能得到证明。

Conclusion: 成人数据训练的模型对儿童Wilms瘤具有可迁移性，但与儿童专用模型仍有差距，凸显儿童训练数据的价值；未来可探索微调（fine-tuning）策略以进一步缩小差距。

Abstract: Deep learning segmentation models for pediatric oncology are often trained on small, single-center cohorts, whereas much larger datasets exist for adult diseases, such as kidney cancer. Whether models trained on adult renal lesions generalize to pediatric Wilms tumors remains unclear. We trained a segmentation model on single-sequence T1- or T2-weighted adult kidney MRI and evaluated it on a held-out adult test set and a pediatric Wilms tumor cohort, where it was compared with a pediatric-specific nnU-Net. Segmentation performance was quantified using the Dice similarity coefficient (DSC) and 95th-percentile Hausdorff distance. On the adult test set, the model achieved a median DSC of 0.86 for renal lesions. On the pediatric cohort, the adult-trained model achieved a median DSC of 0.85 compared with 0.90 for the pediatric-specific model. Despite differences in age, anatomy, and tumor type, the adult-trained model showed promising performance on pediatric Wilms tumors, with only a modest difference in median DSC compared with the pediatric-specific model. However, non-inferiority could not be shown, highlighting the value of pediatric training data. In future work, a fine-tuning strategy could be explored.

</details>


### [43] [LiveSync: Iterative Self-Training for Lip-Sync Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23558)
*Huaishao Luo,Huan Zhang,Guanbo Bao,Yinglu Liu,Youzheng Wu,Xiaodong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveSync：基于迭代自训练的音频驱动唇同步框架，用同一掩码模型生成伪配对并自我精炼，无需增加推理阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然视频缺少“身份与姿态匹配、仅发音动作不同”的配对，掩码式训练的随机参考输入与推理时的对齐自参考存在分布差异（训练-推理不一致）。

Method: 先用真实视频语料加外部生成片段（覆盖稀缺视觉条件）训练掩码模型；冻结该检查点，用独立采样音频对真实帧生成嘴部编辑参考，掩码外像素从源帧复制，经置信度过滤后与对齐掩码输入及原始真实帧目标构成伪配对；伪配对与真实数据混合，从当前权重继续精炼同一模型，不新增推理阶段。框架以单次GAN模型实现，亦可迁移至扩散模型。

Result: 在HDTF上取得领先的同步性能；在AIGC-LipSync子集上与基线相比具竞争力，且推理延迟低。

Conclusion: 自训练式伪配对精炼可有效缩小掩码训练与对齐自推理间的差距，在保持低延迟的前提下提升唇同步质量。

Abstract: Audio-driven lip synchronization aims to match target speech through localized mouth and jaw articulation while preserving subject identity, head pose, and surrounding facial context. Naturally recorded videos provide synchronized reconstruction targets but rarely offer pairs that are matched in identity and pose yet differ primarily in speech-related articulation. Mask-based training enables reconstruction from these videos, but random-reference inputs differ from the aligned self-reference used at inference. We present LiveSync, an iterative self-training framework in which a single mask-based model generates pseudo pairs and is refined for deployment. The model is first trained on a curated real-video corpus augmented with externally generated clips covering under-represented visual conditions. A frozen checkpoint then uses independently sampled audio to generate mouth-edited references from real-video frames, copying pixels outside the edit mask from each source frame. After confidence filtering, these references are paired with aligned masked inputs and original real-frame targets. The retained pseudo pairs are mixed with real training data to refine the same model from its current weights, updating training supervision without adding inference stages. Although we instantiate the framework with a single-pass GAN-based model, the self-training strategy could in principle also be adapted to diffusion-based models. Experimental results show that LiveSync achieves leading synchronization performance on HDTF and competitive performance compared with the baselines on the AIGC-LipSync subset, while maintaining low inference latency.

</details>


### [44] [PreciseEdit: Strong Yet Stable Semantic Transport for One-Step Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23535)
*Jingyi Wang,Xiao Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PreciseEdit 是一个模型无关、免训练、免反演的一步式图像编辑框架，通过在可靠方向与可靠空间位置施加强语义传输，兼顾编辑强度与背景/结构保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一步式 T2I 编辑方法通过条件预测间的简单向量运算构建全局传输场，强传输虽能驱动目标语义，却可能沿不可靠方向或作用于非编辑区域，导致编辑区结构扭曲与背景漂移；而全局平滑或统一削弱传输场又使大语义变化过于保守。作者由此提出核心问题：如何在保持强语义传输的同时提升传输稳定性。

Method: 核心洞察是"强语义传输本身并不天然不稳定，关键在于是否沿可靠方向、在可靠空间位置施加"。据此提出 PreciseEdit：模型无关、免训练、免反演的一步式编辑框架；并提出双噪声尺度角色分解策略——高噪声尺度产生用于实际编辑的强目标导向传输，低噪声尺度作为探测信号，用于判断方向一致性与空间结构。

Result: 在 PIE-Bench 上的大量实验表明，PreciseEdit 在多种编辑任务上取得 SOTA 性能与有效性。

Conclusion: 通过从"语义强度 vs. 传输稳定性"的张力角度重新审视一步式编辑，并以双噪声尺度角色分解实现方向与空间位置的双重可靠性约束，可在不牺牲大语义变化的前提下提升编辑精度与背景保真度，且方法通用、无需训练与反演。

Abstract: Recent one-step text-to-image (T2I) models enable efficient image synthesis, opening up new possibilities for interactive Web-based image editing. However, existing one-step editing methods often construct a global transport field through simple vector operations between conditional predictions. While such strong transport can effectively drive the image toward the target semantics, it may be released along unreliable directions or over non-edited regions, leading to structural distortions within the editing region and background drift elsewhere. More importantly, stabilizing the editing process through global smoothing or uniformly weakening the transport field often makes large semantic changes overly conservative. In this work, we revisit one-step image editing from the perspective of the tension between semantic strength and transport stability, and identify a key insight: strong semantic transport is not inherently unstable. What matters is whether it is applied along reliable directions and at reliable spatial locations. Motivated by this insight, we introduce PreciseEdit, a model-agnostic, training-free and inversion-free one-step editing framework that strengthens target-semantic alignment while preserving background fidelity and structural consistency. We further propose a dual-noise-scale role decomposition strategy: a higher-noise scale produces the strong target-directed transport used for actual editing, whereas a lower-noise scale jointly serves as a probe for directional agreement and spatial structure. Extensive experiments on PIE-Bench demonstrate that PreciseEdit achieves state-of-the-art performance and effectiveness on various editing tasks.

</details>


### [45] [TriINR: Unsupervised Atmospheric Turbulence Mitigation with Triple Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23454)
*Yuka Ogino,Rikuto Otsuka,Yuho Shoji,Takuya Ogawa,Takahiro Toizumi,Atsushi Ito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TriINR，将清晰图像、倾斜（tilt）与模糊（blur）分别建模为三个隐式神经表示（INR），其中tilt与blur为时空INR，实现与帧数无关的退化表示和低内存优化，并引入大气湍流的物理与统计约束提升复原精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气湍流使远距离成像质量下降，退化为时空变化的tilt与blur两个分量。现有无监督湍流缓解方法虽用INR表示清晰图像，但其tilt与blur表示依赖具体帧，优化参数随输入帧数增长，导致表示与内存效率受限。

Method: TriINR为无监督方法，从多帧退化图像恢复清晰图像。将清晰图像、tilt、blur三者分别表示为INR；把tilt与blur建模为时空INR，使退化表示与帧数无关并实现内存高效优化；进一步将大气湍流的物理与统计特性作为INR约束以提升复原精度。

Result: 在三种退化强度的合成湍流数据上，TriINR在不同湍流强度下复原更稳健，在评估的无监督方法中精度最高；在含额外非理想运动的真实远距离场景中也表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过时空INR解耦表示tilt与blur并结合物理统计约束，TriINR在无监督湍流缓解中实现了帧数无关、内存高效的退化建模，并在合成与真实场景上取得优于或稳健于现有方法的效果。

Abstract: Atmospheric turbulence degrades image quality in long-range imaging. The degradation is generally modeled with two spatio-temporally varying components, tilt and blur. Recent unsupervised turbulence mitigation methods have demonstrated the effectiveness of representing a sharp image as an implicit neural representation (INR). However, their tilt and blur representations remain frame-dependent, and the number of optimization parameters grows with the number of input frames. To address this limitation, we propose TriINR, an unsupervised turbulence mitigation method that recovers a sharp image from multiple degraded frames. TriINR represents the sharp image, tilt, and blur as three implicit neural representations. By modeling tilt and blur as spatio-temporal INRs, TriINR achieves frame-count-independent degradation representation and memory-efficient optimization. We further incorporate physical and statistical properties of atmospheric turbulence as constraints on the INRs to improve restoration accuracy. Experiments on synthesized turbulence data with three degradation strengths show that TriINR restores images more robustly across varying turbulence strengths than prior TM methods and achieves the highest accuracy among the evaluated unsupervised methods. Our method also demonstrates robustness on real long-range scenes containing additional non-ideal motion.

</details>


### [46] [STC-MGNet: Spatial-Temporal-Channel Attention-Enhanced Multimodal Graph Network for Pedestrian Intention Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23356)
*Yuanzhe Li,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出空间-时间-通道注意力增强的多模态图网络 STC-MGNet，用于行人过街意图预测，在 JAADbeh、JAADall、PIE 上分别达到 70%、87%、88% 的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人过街意图预测对自动驾驶中人车交互安全至关重要，但现有方法难以有效捕捉行人姿态序列中的细微时空模式，也难以融合视觉与自车运动等互补信息。

Method: 构建姿态图编码器，引入空间-时间-通道（STC）注意力三分支，分别强化关键骨骼关节、观测帧与特征通道，并通过可学习权重融合；同时设计多阶段跨模态通道调制机制，在连续图编码阶段以模态特定的通道门控自适应精炼姿态表示。

Result: 在 JAADbeh、JAADall、PIE 数据集上准确率分别为 70%、87%、88%，优于多个代表性基线；消融实验验证视觉与自车运动信息的互补贡献。

Conclusion: STC 注意力与跨模态通道调制能有效提升姿态表示的判别性，多模态互补信息对过街意图预测性能有显著增益。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of pedestrian crossing intentions plays a critical role in enhancing the safety of pedestrian-vehicle interactions in autonomous driving. However, effectively capturing subtle spatiotemporal patterns in pedestrian pose sequences and integrating complementary visual and ego-vehicle motion information remain challenging. This paper proposes a spatial-temporal-channel attention-enhanced multimodal graph network (STC-MGNet) for pedestrian crossing intention prediction. STC-MGNet introduces a spatial-temporal-channel (STC) attention-enhanced pose graph encoder to learn discriminative representations from pedestrian pose sequences. Three parallel attention branches selectively emphasize informative skeletal joints, observation frames, and feature channels, with their outputs integrated through learnable fusion weights. To leverage complementary visual and ego-vehicle motion information, a multi-stage cross-modal channel modulation mechanism is introduced to adaptively refine pose representations through modality-specific channel gating at successive graph encoding stages. Experimental results demonstrate that STC-MGNet achieves accuracy rates of 70%, 87%, and 88% on the JAADbeh, JAADall, and PIE datasets, respectively, outperforming several representative baseline methods. Ablation experiments further highlight the complementary contributions of visual and ego-vehicle motion information to prediction performance.

</details>


### [47] [Synthetic Image Oversampling for Rare-Class Representation Learning in Deep Facial Phenotyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23730)
*Miles Liao,Zhanliang Wang,Hongzhuo Chen,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究疾病特异性合成过采样（PDID生成图像增强GestaltMatcher子集）对两种模型识别真实患者的影响，发现总体准确率提升但疾病层面响应不均，需按模型设定评估增强量与受益疾病。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见遗传综合征患者面部图像稀缺且在各病种间分布不均，合成图像可补充训练数据而无需额外采集；但合成图像可能带来伪影或重复已有特征，其价值取决于能否提升对真实患者的识别，因此需明确合适增强量及受益病种。

Method: 使用Phenotypic Disease Image Database的疾病特异性合成图像增强GestaltMatcher Database子集，在保持真实训练集与患者不重叠评估划分不变的条件下，比较图像模型GestaltMatcher-Arc与多模态模型GestaltMML在不同增强水平下的表现；多次重复训练评估总体与病种准确率，并进行探索性嵌入分析考察类内紧凑性与类间分离度变化。

Result: 两种设定下平均识别准确率均提升，但GestaltMatcher-Arc增益有限，GestaltMML在测试各增强水平上持续提升；总体提升伴随病种层面不均衡响应，部分疾病准确率下降；嵌入分析显示真实训练样本较少的疾病类间分离度提升更多，但几何变化与分类性能并不一致对应。

Conclusion: 结果支持在数据有限的表型识别中采用合成过采样，同时强调需针对每种训练设定评估增强量及各病种的受益情况。

Abstract: Recent deep facial phenotyping methods can support the recognition of genetic syndromes, but patient images are often scarce and unevenly distributed across disorders. Synthetic facial images offer a way to supplement the training data without collecting additional patient photographs. Their usefulness, however, depends on whether they improve recognition of real patients: generated images may introduce artifacts or repeat features already represented in the training set. It is therefore necessary to examine both how much synthetic augmentation to use and which disorders benefit. We investigate disease-specific synthetic oversampling using images from the Phenotypic Disease Image Database to augment a subset of the GestaltMatcher Database. We compare increasing augmentation levels in an image-based model, GestaltMatcher-Arc, and a multimodal model, GestaltMML, while keeping the real training and patient-disjoint evaluation partitions fixed. Repeated training runs assess overall and disease-specific accuracy, and an exploratory embedding analysis examines changes in class compactness and separation. Augmentation improves mean recognition accuracy in both training setups, although gains remain modest in GestaltMatcher-Arc and continue across the tested levels in GestaltMML. These aggregate improvements are accompanied by uneven disease-level responses, including reduced accuracy for some disorders. In the embedding analysis, disorders with fewer real training images tend to gain more class separation, but geometric changes do not consistently track classification performance. Together, the findings support synthetic oversampling for limited-data facial phenotyping and emphasize the need to assess its amount and benefit for individual disorders within each training setup.

</details>


### [48] [StopWatch: Goal Grounding for Object-goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23841)
*Xinhao Liu,Yunsheng Tian,Yuheng Qiu,Pieter Abbeel,Xi Chen,Guanya Shi,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 预训练VLM微调得到的物体目标导航策略成功率低，诊断发现主因是目标接地（goal grounding）能力缺失；通过显式目标接地可显著提升成功率并在六个基准上达到SOTA，同时修正了基准标注缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有将预训练VLM微调为导航策略的常见做法，其成功率仍偏低，且不清楚这些策略究竟缺失哪种能力，因此需要系统性诊断失败根因。

Method: 对微调VLM导航策略的失败模式进行诊断，定位到"目标接地"为主要失败来源（探索尚可，但无法发现目标已在视野内、无法估计距离）；据此在策略中显式引入目标接地，并修正三个场景集合、六个基准中的标注缺陷。

Result: 显式目标接地显著提升成功率，在六个基准上取得SOTA；在修正后的干净基准上，使用真值接地时策略性能几乎追平生成示教的专家。

Conclusion: 目标接地是物体目标导航中缺失的关键环节；当前剩余的性能差距主要来自感知而非策略学习，显式接地提供了简单有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Pretrained VLMs bring rich world knowledge and semantic priors to object-goal navigation, and fine-tuning a VLM into a navigation policy has become a common recipe. Despite this progress, success rates remain low, and it is unclear which capability these fine-tuned policies lack. Our diagnosis identifies goal grounding as the dominant failure. These policies explore well, yet often fail to notice that the goal is already in view or to estimate how far away it is. To address this failure, we ground the goal explicitly. Explicit grounding substantially improves success and reaches state-of-the-art results on six benchmarks across three scene collections. We also correct annotation defects in these benchmarks, so that future methods are evaluated more faithfully. Our analysis on the clean benchmarks shows that, with ground-truth grounding, our policy nearly matches the expert that generated its demonstrations, leaving the headroom in perception rather than policy learning. Our work points to goal grounding as a missing piece of object-goal navigation and offers a simple and effective solution to the task.

</details>


### [49] [MTEdit: Packed Trajectories Beat Rollouts for Multi-Turn Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23751)
*Houyuan Chen,Fanzhou Wang,Xiangyu Fan,Songlin Yang,Zhitao Yang,Lvmin Zhang,Pei Yang,Hao Zhao,Lei Yang,Anyi Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MTEdit数据集及其构建流程（以打包轨迹画布PTC生成为核心）与MTEdit-Bench基准，并微调得到Qwen-Image-MTEdit，在多轮图像编辑评测中取得开源模型最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮图像编辑比单轮更契合人机协同创作，但高质量多轮训练数据构建困难：传统序列式rollout会累积误差并在各编辑轮次引入视觉质量漂移（visual-quality drift）。

Method: 设计以packed trajectory canvas（PTC）生成为核心的流水线：在统一视觉上下文中联合生成多个编辑状态，以抑制跨轮视觉质量漂移；数据覆盖step-wise与context-aware两类编辑场景，构建500K条多轮轨迹（最多8轮，平均5.38轮）。同时提出MTEdit-Bench，评估指令跟随、内容一致性、抗视觉质量漂移及历史上下文利用能力。基于MTEdit微调Qwen-Image-Edit-Causal得到Qwen-Image-MTEdit。

Result: 在MTEdit-Bench上评测众多闭源与开源图像编辑模型：Qwen-Image-MTEdit在所评估的开源模型中性能最佳，验证MTEdit对提升多轮图像编辑能力的有效性。

Conclusion: PTC式联合生成缓解多轮编辑的误差累积与质量漂移，所构建的大规模数据集与配套基准可有效提升开源模型的多轮图像编辑能力。

Abstract: Multi-turn image editing aims to modify an image according to a sequence of instructions, making it better aligned with human-AI collaborative creation than single-turn editing. However, constructing high-quality multi-turn training data remains challenging, as conventional sequential rollout accumulates errors and introduces visual-quality drift across editing turns. To address this challenge, we introduce MTEdit, a multi-turn image editing dataset built through a pipeline centered on packed trajectory canvas (PTC) generation. By jointly generating multiple editing states within a unified visual context, this pipeline reduces visual-quality drift across turns. Covering both step-wise and context-aware editing scenarios, MTEdit comprises 500K multi-turn image editing trajectories, with up to 8 turns and an average of 5.38 turns per trajectory, making it one of the most extensive datasets for multi-turn image editing. We further introduce MTEdit-Bench, a benchmark designed to evaluate both scenarios by assessing models' ability to follow editing instructions, maintain content consistency, resist visual-quality drift, and effectively use historical context across turns. Using MTEdit, we fine-tune Qwen-Image-Edit-Causal to obtain Qwen-Image-MTEdit. On MTEdit-Bench, we evaluate a broad range of closed-source and open-source image editing models, including Qwen-Image-MTEdit. Experimental results show that Qwen-Image-MTEdit achieves the best performance among the evaluated open-source models, demonstrating the effectiveness of MTEdit in improving models' multi-turn image editing capabilities.

</details>


### [50] [PCDFusion: Proposal-Context-Detail Bayesian Rendering for Infrared-Visible Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23663)
*Rui Liu,Lin Gu,Hesong Li,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PCDFusion，将红外-可见光图像融合(IVIF)重构为局部贝叶斯后验渲染问题，用后验动作权重控制融合，并采用"Proposal-Context-Detail"三阶段可见光锚定框架，在公开基准上提升了融合质量、目标恢复及下游检测与分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光与热红外观测在物理上互补但各自存在不同形式的不可靠性。现有深度IVIF方法主要改进特征交互与重建，缺乏显式的局部规则来决定何时应以可见光外观为主、何时应引入热响应，导致融合过程缺乏可解释的局部决策依据。

Method: 将IVIF重构为局部贝叶斯后验渲染，融合由后验动作权重而非直接模态混合控制；据此提出PCDFusion——一个以可见光为锚点的框架，通过三个渲染阶段实现该公式化：(1)保留潜在有用的热响应(Proposal)，(2)更新曝光退化区域的可见光内容(Context)，(3)将可靠的带限红外结构渲染进最终亮度分量(Detail)。

Result: 在多个公开IVIF基准上的大量实验表明，PCDFusion在挑战性光照条件下提升了融合质量、目标恢复效果，以及下游检测与分割任务的性能。

Conclusion: 以局部贝叶斯后验渲染为核心的可见光锚定三阶段框架，为IVIF提供了显式的局部融合决策机制，在融合质量与下游任务上均取得一致增益，代码已开源。

Abstract: Infrared-visible image fusion (IVIF) aims to combine visible and thermal infrared observations for human perception and downstream vision tasks. Visible and thermal infrared observations are physically complementary but unreliable in different ways. Existing deep IVIF methods mainly improve feature interaction and reconstruction, but often lack an explicit local rule for deciding when visible appearance should dominate and when thermal responses should contribute. To address this issue, we reformulate IVIF as local Bayesian posterior rendering, where fusion is controlled by posterior action weights rather than direct modality mixing. In this paper, we propose Proposal-Context-Detail Fusion (PCDFusion), a visible-anchored framework that implements this formulation through three rendering stages. PCDFusion preserves potentially useful thermal responses, updates exposure-degraded visible regions, and renders reliable band-limited infrared structures into the final luminance. Extensive experiments on public IVIF benchmarks show that PCDFusion improves fusion quality, target recovery, and downstream detection and segmentation performance under challenging illumination. The code is available at https://github.com/BIT-RuiLiu/PCDFusion.git.

</details>


### [51] [I2V-DETACH: Source Grounding Detachment for Unauthorized Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23287)
*Chanhui Lee,Yeonghwan Song,Yewon Kang,Jeany Son*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 I2V-DETACH 图像免疫方法，通过切断源图像接地实现未授权 Image-to-Video 生成的防护，在多种扩散架构上显著降低源内容保留度并具备强黑盒迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 I2V 免疫方法依赖破坏视觉质量或时间一致性来实现防护，本文重新审视该问题，将其转化为"降低源内容保留"，从输出退化转向削弱源条件内容传播。

Method: 基于 I2V 模型在去噪过程中将源图像线索传播至视频潜变量的机制，采用两种互补手段：(1) 抑制源-视频接地以减少源线索传播；(2) 增强视频内部交互，使去噪偏向视频侧动态、降低对源图依赖；并用源导向状态的辅助视频潜变量初始化以获取信息量充足的优化信号。

Result: 在多种扩散架构上的实验表明，I2V-DETACH 大幅降低源内容保留，优于既有免疫方法，且具有强黑盒迁移性。

Conclusion: 将 I2V 免疫重新定义为抑制源条件内容传播是有效视角，源接地切断可在不显著牺牲视觉质量的前提下提供更强、更可迁移的防护。

Abstract: Recent diffusion models have advanced image-conditioned video generation, but their accessibility raises risks of unauthorized manipulation. Image immunization has emerged as a promising defense for Image-to-Video (I2V) protection, yet existing methods often rely on degrading visual quality or temporal coherence. In this work, we revisit I2V immunization as reducing source-content preservation, shifting the focus from output degradation to weakening source-conditioned content propagation. To this end, we propose I2V-DETACH, an image immunization method that prevents unauthorized I2V generation through source grounding detachment. I2V models preserve source content by propagating source-image cues into video latents during denoising. Accordingly, I2V-DETACH disrupts this process in two complementary ways: it suppresses source-video grounding to reduce source cue propagation, and strengthens intra-video interactions to shift denoising toward video-side dynamics with reduced reliance on the source image. To obtain informative optimization signals, we initialize auxiliary video latents with source-oriented states that remain within the source-conditioned generation regime while deviating from source-preserving trajectories. Extensive experiments across diverse diffusion architectures show that I2V-DETACH substantially reduces source-content preservation, improves over prior immunization methods, and exhibits strong black-box transferability.

</details>


### [52] [Xiaomi-Lens-0 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23279)
*Pei Fu,Yike Liu,Yue Sun,Hanmo Wang,Xiuwen Xi,Ruoceng Zhang,Shaojie Zhang,Chao Jiang,Hang Li,Bingrui Liu,Zhenbo Luo,Heng Qu,Changjian Xu,Ming Xu,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Xiaomi-Lens-0（4B，基于Qwen3.5）让MLLM从自身视觉表征预测掩码，实现细粒度区域理解与"视觉潜变量推理"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM擅长整图问答，但细粒度区域理解薄弱；主因是纯文本监督从不指明哪些区域支撑答案。

Method: 轻量解码器融合视觉编码器patch特征与视觉潜变量（即LM的mask-token隐状态），使掩码监督直达共享主干、无需额外分割编码器；每个潜变量解码为掩码，模型可在推理中交错使用区域特定潜变量进行"视觉潜变量思维"，并以grounded推理轨迹监督思维链所访问的区域。

Result: 在RefCOCO、RefCOCO+、RefCOCOg全部八个划分上，指代理解平均优于Qwen3.5 5.6分，分割cIoU在对比模型中最高；且在全部感知基准上超过Qwen3.5。

Conclusion: 将掩码预测与语言主干统一、以视觉潜变量承载区域信息并结合接地推理监督，可有效提升MLLM的细粒度区域理解与分割能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) answer questions about whole images well, yet they still struggle with fine-grained region understanding. A key reason is that text-only supervision never specifies which regions support an answer. To close this gap, we present Xiaomi-Lens-0, a 4B MLLM built on Qwen3.5 that predicts masks from its own visual representations. Specifically, a lightweight decoder combines vision-encoder patch features with visual latents, the language model's mask-token states, so mask supervision reaches the shared backbone without an additional segmentation encoder. Because each latent decodes to a mask, the model can interleave reasoning with region-specific latents and thus think with visual latents, while grounded reasoning traces supervise the regions it visits during chain-of-thought. As a result, across all eight RefCOCO, RefCOCO+, and RefCOCOg splits, Xiaomi-Lens-0 gains 5.6 points on average over Qwen3.5 in referring comprehension and achieves the highest segmentation cIoU among compared models. Moreover, it surpasses Qwen3.5 across all perception benchmarks. Homepage: \href{https://seerray-lab.github.io/Xiaomi-Lens-0}{this https URL}

</details>


### [53] [RAP: A Dataset for Paper-to-Poster Generation via Declarative Markup from Human-Crafted Exemplars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23230)
*Haonan Han,Yukang Lin,Minliang Lin,Haoxian Tan,Jun-Yan He,Rui Yang,Zhi-Qi Cheng,Jie Hu,Guanglu Wan,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RAP（Reconstruct A Poster）数据集与基准，将"论文转海报"定义为可视化编码任务。RAP 包含 2022–2026 年间 ML 会议的 25K+ 经校验的论文-海报对，并附三级内容分类体系与 150 篇留出测试基准。通过全自动"反渲染"流水线将海报转为带样式、可回填槽位的 HTML/CSS，使每个样本成为可用渲染比对验证的"论文-海报-标记"三元组。语料同时支持推理与训练：免训练的 PosterRAG 多智能体流程检索范例并回填槽位，SFT+RL 训练专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有该任务的基准资源稀少，仅标注布局框，导致评估只能依赖 VLM-as-judge 或人工清单而非客观渲染比对；且现有生成方法需从零设计布局，使人工制作的海报无法作为可检索设计库或可信监督。

Method: 构建 25K+ 论文-海报对语料及 150 篇留出基准，配三级内容分类；设计全自动反渲染流水线，将海报转化为带类型的可回填槽位的样式化 HTML/CSS，形成可渲染验证的三元组；提出免训练多智能体 PosterRAG（检索范例 + 槽位回填，不改布局样式）与 SFT+RL 训练方案。

Result: 数据集规模达 25K+ 已验证样本并含 150 篇留出测试集；每个样本可通过渲染与源海报比对进行客观校验；PosterRAG 与 SFT+RL 模型均可由同一三元组数据支持推理与训练，并提供在线演示。

Conclusion: RAP 将论文转海报重构为可验证的可视化编码任务，通过反渲染实现客观 render-and-compare 评估，同时把人类海报转化为可检索设计库与监督信号，兼顾免训练检索式生成与模型训练两条路线。

Abstract: Making a conference poster is visual coding in miniature: an entire paper must be compressed onto a single page, which exercises summarization, visual design, and code implementation at once, using nothing beyond the paper itself. Yet benchmarks for this task in the context of visual coding remain scarce: existing resources are small and annotated only with layout boxes, so evaluation must fall back on VLM-as-judge or human checklists rather than objective render-and-compare, and the generation methods they support design layouts from scratch, leaving human-crafted posters unusable as either a retrievable design library or as verified supervision. We introduce RAP (Reconstruct A Poster), a dataset and benchmark for paper-to-poster generation as a visual coding task. RAP contains 25K+ verified paper-poster pairs from various machine learning conferences held between 2022 and 2026, annotated with a three-level content taxonomy, plus a held-out benchmark of 150 test papers each paired with its human poster. A fully automated derendering pipeline turns each poster into styled HTML/CSS of typed, refillable slots, so every sample is a paper-poster-markup triplet verifiable by rendering against the source poster. The corpus serves both inference and training: PosterRAG, a training-free multi-agent pipeline, retrieves an exemplar and refills its slots without touching layout or styling, while an SFT+RL recipe trains a dedicated model from the same triplets. An online demo is available at https://reconstruct-a-poster.github.io/.

</details>


### [54] [OmniXRef: A Unified Framework for Multimodal Multi-Reference Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22744)
*Xiaoyan Cong,Tianyu Wang,Hyeonho Jeong,Zhifei Zhang,Yuqian Zhou,Yijun Li,Shaoteng Liu,Zhe Lin,Srinath Sridhar,Soo Ye Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OmniXRef统一框架，支持文本、图像、视频任意组合的多参考视频编辑，并配套数据、基准与评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编辑中的创作意图可经由文本指令、图像参考、视频参考或其组合表达，而现有方法多为任务特定、难以同时接纳可变数量的多模态参考并泛化到新参考。

Method: 设计带参考库（reference-bank）接口的统一输入，并提出R²-RoPE以泛化到更多参考；OmniXRef-Model以单流扩散Transformer在交错序列中联合处理源视频与来自MLLM的多模态上下文；构建OmniXRef-Data（多任务合成流水线），采用课程学习逐步提升任务复杂度与分辨率；提出OmniXRef-Bench评测视频参考属性迁移。

Result: 综合评测显示OmniXRef-Model在多项任务上优于任务特定基线及商业模型；OmniXRef-Bench覆盖Camera、Motion、Content、Style、VFX五类视频参考属性迁移。

Conclusion: 统一的多模态多参考视频编辑框架可行，配套数据构建、课程学习与基准评测可系统支撑视频作为控制模态的编辑研究。

Abstract: Creative intent in video editing can be expressed through text instructions (e.g., "remove the people"), image references (e.g., subject appearance), video references (e.g., camera movement), or a combination of the above. We introduce OmniXRef, a unified framework for multimodal multi-reference video editing that allows users to combine variable numbers of text, image, and video references to control diverse attributes of a source video. We first devise a unified input design with a reference-bank interface and introduce $R^2$-RoPE to support generalization to more references. Then our model, OmniXRef-Model, uses a single-stream diffusion transformer to jointly process the source video and multimodal context from an MLLM in an interleaved sequence. To address the scarcity of training data for video-reference editing, we construct OmniXRef-Data using synthesis pipelines for different editing tasks and combinations of references. We use curriculum learning to progressively increase task complexity and resolution. To support systematic evaluation of video as a control modality, we introduce OmniXRef-Bench, a benchmark for video-reference attribute transfer across Camera, Motion, Content, Style, and Visual Effects (VFX). Comprehensive evaluations show that our OmniXRef-Model outperforms task-specific baselines and commercial models.

</details>


### [55] [Improving OCR Accuracy Through Multi-Classifier Verification of Visually Similar Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22726)
*Nuzhat Khan,Ab Al-Hadi Ab Rahman,Mohamad Anuar Kamaruddin,Muhammad Paend Bakht,Sana Fatima,Farhad Khodadadi,Ibrahim Yousef Alshareef*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出字符级后处理框架CVM，用专门二分类器校验并纠正OCR输出中的易混淆字符（如O/0、Z/2、S/5），不依赖词典，在三种OCR引擎十个字体上提升3.95–14.81个百分点，纠错率87.3%，误纠率仅2.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉相似字符的区分是OCR的长期难题，直接导致关键文本数字化应用出错。现有预处理只改善图像质量、词级后处理只纠正拼写错误，均未解决字符级视觉歧义——这是某些字符对的固有属性，连SOTA模型也受限于此。

Method: 构建Character Verification Model（CVM）：面向易混淆字符对训练专用二分类器，在OCR输出上做字符级校验与纠正。该框架不依赖词典验证，因而适用于序列号、字母数字编码、车牌等非词汇数据；采用模块化架构，可直接嵌入现有OCR流水线，无需修改底层识别模型。

Result: 在Tesseract、EasyOCR、TrOCR三个OCR引擎与10种复杂度从低到高的字体上实验，准确率提升3.95–14.81个百分点；视觉相似字符错误纠正率达87.3%，误纠率仅2.3%。

Conclusion: 字符级视觉歧义可通过针对性的二分类校验有效缓解；CVM作为词典无关的模块化后处理方案，能显著提升OCR精度且低误纠，具备与现有流水线即插即用的实用价值。

Abstract: Distinguishing visually similar characters is a persistent challenge for optical character recognition (OCR) systems, causing errors in critical applications of text digitization. While preprocessing techniques improve image quality and word-level post-processing corrects spelling errors, neither addresses character-level visual ambiguity, an intrinsic property of certain character pairs that limits even state-of-the-art OCR models. We present the Character Verification Model (CVM), a character-level post-processing framework that verifies and corrects ambiguous characters in OCR outputs using specialized binary classifiers trained on visually confusable pairs such as 'O'/'0', 'Z'/'2', and 'S'/'5'. CVM operates independently of dictionary validation, making it suitable for non-lexical data including serial numbers, alphanumeric codes, and license plates. Experiments on three OCR engines (Tesseract, EasyOCR, TrOCR) and ten fonts with low to high complexity demonstrate accuracy improvements of 3.95-14.81 percentage points, with an 87.3% correction rate for visually similar character errors and a false-correction rate of only 2.3%. The modular architecture integrates with existing OCR pipelines without modification to underlying recognition models.

</details>


### [56] [Thinking from Above: Efficient Spatial Reasoning with Bird's-Eye-View Visual Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22700)
*Faizan Siddiqui,Nirav Savaliya,Sahith Reddy Chada,Enna Sachdeva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Cambrian-BEV，用无参数几何 tokenization 将多帧视觉 patch 特征反投影到共享 BEV 坐标系并按空间单元聚合，把 128 帧约 100K 个 DINOv3 patch 特征压缩为固定 27×27=729 个 BEV token，在 VSI-Bench 达 63.48（较同配置帧 token 对照 +8.5，视觉 token 少 12.6×），ReVSI 达 44.68（+4.5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频 MLLM 主要按时间组织观测，而具身空间推理需要按空间组织观测。其视觉表征在相邻帧上局部一致，却缺乏跨视角、跨时间稳定的持久空间上下文。具身智能体天然可获得深度与相机位姿，可用于显式建立空间对应关系。

Method: 在 Cambrian-S 基础上扩展出 Cambrian-BEV：采用无参数几何 tokenization，将每帧视觉 patch 特征借助深度和相机位姿反投影到共享 BEV 坐标框架，对落入同一空间单元的特征取平均，使同一物理位置在不同视角/时间步的观测聚合为同一空间 token。除 BEV tokenization 外不做任何架构修改，视觉编码器为冻结的 DINOv3，仅训练多模态投影器和 LLM。

Result: 该方法既提供持久场景表征，又构成高效压缩机制：128 帧约 100K 个 DINOv3 patch 特征压缩为固定 27×27 网格共 729 个 BEV token，LLM prefill 时间降低 2.9×，峰值内存降低 3.6×。VSI-Bench 上达 63.48，较相同微调的帧 token 对照提升 8.5 分且视觉 token 少 12.6×，其中房间尺寸（+24.5）与相对方向（+34.1）提升尤其显著；ReVSI 上达 44.68，较对应对照提升 4.5。

Conclusion: 以 BEV 空间坐标系替代纯时间序列组织视频观测，可在显著减少视觉 token 与计算/内存开销的同时大幅提升具身空间推理性能，说明显式几何对应与持久空间上下文对视频 MLLM 的空间理解至关重要。

Abstract: Existing video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) organize observations primarily in time, but embodied spatial reasoning requires organizing them in space. Consequently, their visual representations can remain locally consistent across nearby frames while lacking a persistent spatial context that is stable across viewpoints and time. We exploit depth and camera pose, which are naturally available to embodied agents, to explicitly establish this spatial correspondence. We introduce Cambrian-BEV, which extends Cambrian-S with a parameter-free geometric tokenization scheme that back-projects visual patch features from each frame into a shared bird's-eye-view (BEV) coordinate frame and averages features that fall within the same spatial cell. Thus, observations of the same physical location, regardless of viewpoint or time step, are aggregated into a common spatial token. In addition to providing a persistent scene representation, this formulation acts as an efficient compression mechanism: 128 frames containing approximately 100K DINOv3 patch features are consolidated into a fixed 27$\times$27 grid of only 729 BEV tokens, cutting LLM prefill time by $2.9\times$ and peak memory by $3.6\times$. Cambrian-BEV requires no architectural modification beyond BEV tokenization and uses a frozen DINOv3 vision encoder with a trainable multimodal projector and LLM. On VSI-Bench, our model reaches 63.48, $+8.5$ points over an identically fine-tuned frame-token control with $12.6\times$ fewer visual tokens, with particularly large gains on room size ($+24.5$) and relative direction ($+34.1$). On ReVSI, it reaches 44.68, $+4.5$ over the corresponding control.

</details>


### [57] [Seeing Seizures: A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Video and EEG Deep Learning for Automated Rodent Seizure Detection and Severity Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22566)
*Alloy Das,Shreyan Ganguly,Nyzil Massey,Hung Phi Doan,Zhanhong Jiang,Thimmasettappa Thippeswamy,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究基于soman诱导癫痫大鼠模型（20只、601场次、24,131对同步视频-EEG片段），系统评估10种视频架构与7种单通道EEG模型在发作检测及3/5级行为严重程度分级上的表现，揭示跨个体泛化是分级任务的核心难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床前癫痫研究中，同步视频与EEG记录的人工标注是瓶颈，亟需自动化方法替代；同时需要明确现有模型在行为定义严重程度标签上的能力边界。

Method: 构建配对视频-EEG数据集；独立评测10种视频架构与7种单通道EEG模型，分别完成二分类检测、三分类与五分类严重程度分级；设置会话互斥（session-exclusive）与被试互斥（subject-exclusive）两种评估协议；开展时间采样（多帧数量）消融实验；并在RodEpil数据集上进行纯视频二分类检测，采用统一重编码以削弱类别相关的编码线索。

Result: 会话互斥条件下，R(2+1)D-18与GRU的检测macro-F1分别为0.968和0.974；五分类分别降至0.657与0.560。被试互斥评估使五分类macro-F1进一步降至视频0.475、EEG 0.404。多帧时间采样对性能有显著增益，而检测性能在不同视频架构间差异较小。RodEpil纯视频检测的重编码后癫痫类平均F1达0.979。

Conclusion: 现有模型在检测任务上表现稳健，但行为严重程度分级（尤其对未见个体）仍具挑战；研究刻画了现有模型在既定协议下的性能边界，并指出发展专家辅助标注流程的必要性。

Abstract: Manual annotation of synchronized video and EEG recordings is a bottleneck in preclinical seizure research. We present an empirical study of seizure detection and behavioral severity grading using 24,131 paired clips from 601 sessions across 20 rats in a soman-induced epilepsy model. Ten video architectures and seven single-channel EEG models are evaluated independently on binary detection and three- and five-class severity tasks. In the session-exclusive reference comparison, R(2+1)D-18 and GRU achieve detection macro-F1 scores of 0.968 and 0.974, respectively; for five-class grading, the corresponding scores are 0.657 and 0.560. Subject-exclusive evaluation reduces five-class macro-F1 to 0.475 for video and 0.404 for EEG, highlighting the difficulty of grading unseen animals. Temporal-sampling experiments demonstrate the value of multiple frames, while detection performance varies relatively little across the evaluated video architectures. A separate video-only binary-detection evaluation on RodEpil achieves a mean seizure-class F1 of 0.979 after uniform re-encoding to reduce identified class-correlated encoding cues. The study characterizes existing models for behavior-defined severity labels under the evaluated protocol, motivating further investigation of expert-assisted annotation workflows. Our code and github page is available here.

</details>


### [58] [CFC26: Building Evaluations for Deployment in Sonar-Based Fish Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22549)
*Madison Van Horn,Suzanne Stathatos,Sevan Brodjian,Justin Kay,Kai Van Brunt,Erik Young,Sara Beery,Pietro Perona,Michael Hobley*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Caltech Fish Counting 2026 (CFC26)数据集，扩展至9条太平洋鲑鱼河流系统的11个部署地点，并引入模拟部署场景的评估协议（分布内/分布外）与两项新计数指标nMNE、nMANE，用于暴露以往被掩盖的上下游计数方向性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鱼群精确计数对生态监测、保护决策、捕捞限额与河流管理至关重要，但实际部署中依赖专家人工审阅声呐视频，成本高、速度慢、难以规模化；自动计数虽有潜力，却难以在真实部署中兑现。现有评估指标（如帧级检测指标）不能反映真实部署条件与专家工作流程，掩盖了实际性能问题。

Method: 构建CFC26数据集：在CFC22基础上扩展至阿拉斯加至北加州的9条太平洋鲑鱼河系、11个部署地点，覆盖更广地理与生态范围；设计模拟真实部署的评估协议，区分已见河流与未见河流，分别测试分布内与分布外性能；提出两项新计数指标nMNE与nMANE，对齐专家实际操作流程。

Result: 在真实部署导向的评估下，新指标揭示了此前被帧级检测指标掩盖的上下游计数方向性偏差，该偏差若未被发现将对生态产生直接且显著的影响。

Conclusion: 更贴近真实部署的评估协议与指标有助于缩小自动计数系统从研究到落地的差距、推动实际计数性能提升；数据集与代码已在CaltechDATA与GitHub公开。

Abstract: Accurate counts of fish populations are essential for monitoring ecosystems and making decisions for conservation, harvest limits, and river management. In many field deployments, these counts are obtained from sonar videos that experts must inspect manually, making large-scale monitoring slow, expensive, and difficult. Automated counting systems have been proposed as a scalable alternative, but so far this promise has been difficult to realize in deployment. Developing evaluations that better reflect real-world deployment conditions will help close this gap and drive improvements in counting performance in practice. To achieve this, we propose Caltech Fish Counting 2026 (CFC26), a new dataset with improved evaluation protocols that simulate deployment to seen and novel rivers, testing in-distribution and out-of-distribution performance. We expand the CFC22 dataset to eleven deployment locations across nine Pacific salmon river systems from Alaska to Northern California, capturing a broader geographical and ecological range of data. We additionally introduce two new evaluation metrics for counting, nMNE and nMANE, that mirror the operational workflow that experts use and help surface errors that frame-level detection metrics do not capture. Using these metrics, we reveal previously-concealed directional bias in upstream vs downstream counts, which would have direct and significant ecological implications if undetected. The dataset and code are publicly available on CaltechDATA and GitHub.

</details>


### [59] [JAVIS: Joint Audio-Visual Interaction and Streaming for Real-Time Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22499)
*Junseok Ahn,Jaehyun Park,Jeongsoo Choi,Joon Son Chung,Ji-Hoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端到端流式音视频对话智能体框架JAVIS，通过单一共享表示同时驱动语音与人脸生成，并构建334小时合成数据集JAVIS-Syn。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类交流本质上是多模态的，意图不仅通过语言和声音线索传递，也通过视觉信号表达；而现有对话系统主要聚焦文本或语音，忽略了自然交互所必需的视觉反馈。

Method: 在共享LLM之上引入Joint Refined Head，生成统一的音视频联合表示，再分别馈入语音与面部生成的专用流式分支；端到端设计避免中间瓶颈，实现实时生成并保持语音与面部紧密同步。同时构建JAVIS-Syn合成语料（334小时），覆盖情感丰富的多轮对话与助手式交流。

Result: 初始延迟比强基线降低约200倍；在唇部同步与面部表情恰当性上优于基线。

Conclusion: 单一共享表示驱动语音与面部的端到端流式架构可有效实现低延迟、高同步的音视频对话智能体，配套合成数据可缓解表达性音视频对话数据稀缺问题。

Abstract: Human communication is inherently multimodal, conveying intent not only through linguistic and vocal cues but also through visual signals. However, existing conversational systems have mainly focused on text or speech, leaving out the visual feedback that is essential for natural interaction. In this paper, we propose JAVIS, an end-to-end streaming framework for real-time audio-visual conversational agents. Our key idea is to let the voice and the face speak from a single representation. On top of a shared LLM, a Joint Refined Head produces a unified audio-visual representation, which is fed into dedicated streaming branches for speech and face generation. This end-to-end design avoids intermediate bottlenecks, enabling real-time generation while keeping speech and face tightly synchronized. In addition, we present JAVIS-Syn, a 334-hour synthetic corpus designed to address the lack of expressive audio-visual dialogue data, covering emotionally rich multi-turn conversations and assistant-style exchanges. Experiments show that JAVIS achieves approximately 200$\times$ lower initial latency than a strong baseline, while outperforming baselines in lip synchronization and facial expression appropriateness.

</details>


### [60] [SemPurify: Semantics-Aware Data Purification for Black-box Backdoor Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23589)
*Jiawei Yu,Syed Irfan Ali Meerza,Yi Wu,Amir Sadovnik,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个语义感知的数据净化框架 SemPurify，利用T2I模型结合多级语义引导黑盒后门防御中的图像修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后门攻击隐蔽性强（仅在特定触发器激活时改变预测），难以检测。现有基于净化的防御依赖图像变换（如模糊）破坏触发器，但会降低图像质量；即便用扩散模型等高级修复技术，仍会改变视觉或语义信息，损害下游任务效用。

Method: 在黑盒防御设定下构建语义感知净化框架：先对样本进行图像变换以破坏触发器，再将多级语义（低层语义嵌入和高层图像标签）作为条件注入文本到图像（T2I）框架，引导T2I模型对变换后样本进行修复重建。

Result: 在分类模型、CLIP式编码器和生成模型等多种后门场景下进行大量实验，表明SemPurify能可靠去除后门，同时有效保留面向下游任务的语义信息。

Conclusion: 多级语义引导的T2I修复可兼顾后门移除与语义保真，为净化式黑盒后门防御提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks have emerged as a critical security threat in machine learning, where adversaries embed triggers within poisoned training samples to manipulate a model's predictions on targeted inputs while leaving its general behavior unaffected. This stealthy nature makes backdoor attacks difficult to detect, as the model functions normally until activated by a specific malicious trigger. Current purification-based defenses use image transformations (e.g., blurring) to disrupt trigger patterns but often degrade image quality. Even with advanced restoration techniques like diffusion models, these approaches can still alter visual or semantic information, reducing utility for downstream tasks. To address this, we propose the first semantics-aware data purification framework for black-box backdoor defense, SemPurify, which explicitly incorporates multi-level semantics (i.e., low-level semantic embeddings and high-level image tags) within a text-to-image (T2I) framework to guide image restoration during purification. Using this semantic information, the T2I model subsequently refines the transformed samples, helping preserve visual details and semantic information during restoration. Extensive experiments spanning multiple backdoor scenarios, i.e., classification models, CLIP-style encoders, and generative models, demonstrate that SemPurify reliably removes backdoors while effectively preserving semantic information for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [61] [PONDER: Learning to Generate in the Right Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23531)
*Julius Erbach,Dominik Narnhofer,Bernt Schiele,Jan Eric Lenssen,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PONDER，用强化学习策略学习掩码生成模型的填充顺序并允许"重思考"（回填已生成位置重新生成），以FIDgrad等奖励优化，显著降低FID并提升视觉数独准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码生成模型按步填充缺失变量，填充顺序决定后续可用上下文并影响最终样本质量；常用随机顺序或局部置信度贪心策略，缺乏对演化上下文的适应能力且无法修正早前错误预测。

Method: 以冻结生成器的部分样本与特征为条件训练策略网络，选择下一步填充位置及/或重新掩码已填充位置（rethinking）；用组相对策略梯度（GRPO）优化，奖励包括约束满足、人类偏好与分布质量；提出FIDgrad——基于将生成样本加入当前特征分布所引起Fréchet Inception Distance一阶变化的图像级奖励。

Result: 冻结生成器下，学习顺序将MAR-B ImageNet FID从4.09降至2.78，困难视觉数独准确率从3.9%升至56.6%（超过不确定性贪心排序的38.7%）；加入rethinking后进一步达到2.70 FID与96.7%准确率，且推理时增大固定策略的rethinking预算可持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 生成顺序可被学习且对样本质量影响显著；允许修正早期预测带来额外增益，并支持推理期计算扩展。

Abstract: We introduce PONDER, a method that learns in which order an image generator should fill in content and which of its earlier predictions to revise. Masked generative models construct a sample by repeatedly filling in subsets of missing variables. The order of these partial predictions determines the context available in subsequent steps and therefore affects the quality of the final sample. Common strategies are to choose the order randomly or based on local confidence estimates. Conditioned on the partially generated sample and the generator's features, our policy chooses which positions to fill next and/or which previously filled positions to mask for later regeneration, a mechanism we call rethinking. In this way, the generation process is able to adapt to the evolving context and also to revise earlier predictions. We optimize the policy using group-relative policy gradients, with rewards that measure constraint satisfaction, human preference, or distribution quality. For the latter, we introduce FIDgrad, an image-level reward derived from the (first-order) change in Fréchet Inception Distance caused by adding a generated sample to the current feature distribution. With the generators frozen, learned ordering reduces MAR-B ImageNet FID from 4.09 to 2.78 and raises the accuracy on hard visual Sudokus from 3.9\% to 56.6\%, surpassing uncertainty-based greedy ordering (38.7\%). Rethinking further improves these results to 2.70 FID and 96.7\% accuracy, while increasing the rethinking budget of a fixed policy yields continued improvements at inference time.

</details>


### [62] [GeoReason: Bridging Logical Reasoning and Spatial Fidelity in Remote Sensing Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23416)
*Cheng Fan,Han Wu,Tingzhang Luo,Minjing Dong,Yehui Tang,Jianyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoReason 通过"预期先验"将 [SEG] 令牌前置为用户查询条件，并结合定位感知 CoT 与 Latent Patch-to-Token 对齐损失，缓解遥感推理分割中的空间保真退化与目标误识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在"Embedding-as-Mask"范式的 MLLM 遥感推理分割中，杂乱地物背景与极端尺度变化导致空间保真退化与目标误识别。作者定位到关键瓶颈：自回归生成中分割令牌 [SEG] 只在长推理链末端产生，其所依赖的视觉证据已被语言抽象与自回归噪声逐步稀释，形成"顺序性疏离"；对像素足迹极小的遥感目标尤为致命。

Method: 1) Anticipatory Prior：把 [SEG] 令牌直接注入初始用户查询，使其由终端输出变为首要条件，成为持续存在的空间锚点；2) Location-aware Chain-of-Thought：用边界框约束由粗到细的定位推理；3) Latent Patch-to-Token Alignment (PTA) Loss：显式对齐隐层分割令牌与真实遥感纹理特征。

Result: 在 Earthreason 与 RRSIS-D 两个基准上，GeoReason 一致优于现有 SOTA 方法；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 将分割令牌前置为条件并配合定位感知推理与潜空间对齐，可有效保持遥感推理分割的空间完整性，为 MLLM 驱动的细粒度遥感感知提供可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly advanced instruction-driven perception through the "Embedding-as-Mask" paradigm. However, extending this capability to Remote Sensing (RS) reasoning segmentation remains exceptionally challenging under cluttered geographical contexts and extreme scale variations, often leading to Spatial Fidelity Degradation and Target Misidentification. We identify a critical yet underexplored bottleneck: the sequential alienation of the segmentation token [SEG] from its initial visual grounding within the autoregressive generation paradigm. Produced only at the end of long reasoning chains, [SEG] relies on visual evidence that has been progressively diluted by linguistic abstraction and autoregressive noise. This information decay is particularly catastrophic for RS targets with minuscule pixel footprints, where even marginal fidelity loss leads to localization failure. To address these bottlenecks, we propose GeoReason, a novel framework designed to fortify logical deduction and preserve spatial integrity. First, we introduce an Anticipatory Prior mechanism that injects the [SEG] token directly into the initial user query, shifting it from a terminal output to a primary condition. This paradigm shift transforms the token into a persistent spatial anchor, preventing information decay during long-form linguistic inference. Second, we enhance visual reasoning via a Location-aware Chain-of-Thought (CoT) that enforces coarse-to-fine localization using bounding boxes, coupled with a Latent Patch-to-Token Alignment (PTA) Loss that explicitly aligns the latent segmentation token with physical RS textures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GeoReason consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods on the Earthreason and RRSIS-D benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/Fanc0126/GeoReason.

</details>


### [63] [MOSS-Image Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23374)
*MOSI. AI*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出开源图像生成系统 MOSS-Image，通过 5B 单流扩散 Transformer 预训练与意图感知提示增强模块，实现双语长文本渲染与复杂布局生成，并发布 LLTB、RLA 两个基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源图像生成系统已能生成信息密集型图像，而开源权重文生图模型在密集文本与复杂布局上仍明显落后，论文旨在缩小这一差距。

Method: (1) 从零预训练 5B 单流 DiT（single-stream diffusion transformer），采用精心设计的训练配方，支持中英双语长文本渲染与精确布局控制；(2) 设计意图感知的智能体式提示增强（PE）模块，将多样用户请求转译为细化的内容与布局规格；(3) 构建 LongLongTextBench（LLTB，评测长文本中英文渲染）与 RealLayoutArena（RLA，评测端到端富布局生成）。

Result: 在 LLTB 上取得开源权重模型中的 SOTA，平均文本覆盖率 96.3%；在 RLA 端到端系统比较中排名第二，其主干在共享增强提示条件下于生成器中排名第一。

Conclusion: MOSS-Image 证明开源系统可在文本密集与布局一致生成上接近闭源水平；作者将开源模型、训练配方与评测框架以推动社区研究。

Abstract: Recent closed-source image generation systems can produce diverse visual content from simple compositions to information-dense graphics, yet open-weight text-to-image models still struggle with dense text and intricate layouts. To narrow this gap, we introduce MOSS-Image, an open generation system that combines high-fidelity visual synthesis with intent-aware composition. First, we pretrain a 5B single-stream diffusion transformer (DiT) from scratch with a carefully designed recipe, enabling bilingual long-form text rendering and precise layout control. Second, we develop an intention-aware agentic prompt enhancement (PE) module that translates diverse user requests into detailed content and layout specifications. Together, they enable MOSS-Image to generate text-rich and layout-coherent images. We further introduce LongLongTextBench (LLTB) for evaluating long-form English and Chinese text rendering, and RealLayoutArena (RLA) for end-to-end layout-rich generation. MOSS-Image achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-weight models on LLTB, with an average text coverage of 96.3%. On RLA, MOSS-Image ranks second in the end-to-end system comparison, while its backbone ranks first among generators evaluated with shared enhanced prompts. We will release the model, training recipe, and evaluation framework to support further research and promote the text-to-image community.

</details>


### [64] [UniPolar: Joint Image and Polarimetric Restoration under Compound Degradations via Physical Consistency Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23360)
*Yang Jin,Guangyu Guo,Linghao Shen,Binglu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UniPolar 耦合图像-Stokes 框架，从复合退化的彩色偏振滤光阵列（CPFA）观测中联合恢复四个偏振角图像并直接预测 Stokes 差分分量，通过跨域交互与物理一致性学习兼顾视觉保真度与偏振可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏振图像复原方法通常只单独处理器件退化或环境退化，难以适用于二者共存的 CPFA 成像；复合退化会同时扰动图像强度与跨角偏振差异，因此仅提升图像保真度不足以保证 Stokes、DoLP、AoLP 的准确恢复。

Method: UniPolar 采用两条互补流：一流重建四幅偏振角图像，另一流直接预测 Stokes 差分分量。以退化条件驱动的跨域交互（DCCDI）协调两流，其中双域频率交互（DDFI）实现频率选择性证据交换，退化条件调制（DCM）实现条件自适应特征校正；输出端引入图像-Stokes 物理一致性学习（ISPC），对齐图像导出与直接预测的 Stokes 分量，并正则化角度保真度与 DoLP 物理可行性。

Result: 在复合退化基准上，平均图像 PSNR 31.522 dB，DoLP PSNR 24.097 dB，AoLP 平均误差 16.833°。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了同时考虑视觉保真度与偏振可靠性的必要性，为复合退化下基于学习的偏振复原提供了物理约束视角。

Abstract: Polarimetric image restoration aims to recover polarization angle images and reliable polarimetric quantities from degraded observations. Beyond visual fidelity, it also requires preserving the physical relationships underlying reliable polarimetric estimation. However, existing methods typically address either device- or environment-induced degradations in isolation, limiting their applicability to color polarization filter array (CPFA) imaging, where both often coexist. Such compound degradations jointly perturb image intensities and cross angle polarization differences. Consequently, improved image fidelity alone does not necessarily ensure accurate Stokes, DoLP, and AoLP recovery. To address this challenge, we propose UniPolar, a coupled Image Stokes framework for joint image polarimetric restoration from compound-degraded CPFA observations. Specifically, UniPolar employs two complementary streams to reconstruct four polarization-angle images and directly predict Stokes difference components. To coordinate these streams, Degradation Conditioned Cross Domain Interaction combines Dual Domain Frequency Interaction for frequency selective evidence exchange with Degradation Conditioned Modulation for condition adaptive feature correction. Furthermore, at the output level, Image Stokes Physical Consistency Learning aligns image derived and directly predicted Stokes components while regularizing angular fidelity and the physical feasibility of DoLP. On the compound-degradation benchmark, UniPolar achieves average image and DoLP PSNR of 31.522 dB and 24.097 dB, respectively, with an average AoLP error of 16.833 degree. These results underscore the importance of jointly considering visual fidelity and polarimetric reliability, offering a physically grounded perspective on learning based restoration under compound degradations.

</details>


### [65] [Diversity Without Labels: Task-Agnostic Coreset Selection Across Perception and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23642)
*Julia Hindel,Benjamin Ramtoula,Kyuhwan Yeon,Nick Heppert,Paul Newman,Daniele De Martini,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoreDNA是一种无监督、任务无关的核心集估计方法，通过汇聚视觉基础模型神经元激活构建图像级与数据集级分布，利用末层激活统计尾部筛选高质量样本，在感知与动作任务上达到或超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从大规模数据集中筛选高质量子集（核心集）虽有需求但困难：现有方法依赖代理模型训练、大量标注或任务特定定制；无监督方法多局限于成对图像比较或与固定子集的比较，难以兼顾低层视觉结构与高层语义。

Method: 提出CoreDNA：将视觉基础模型的神经元激活汇聚为每张图像和整个数据集的分布，通过比较单图分布与数据集级分布跨层捕获低层视觉结构与高层语义信息；发现高质量样本集中于最后一层激活统计的尾部（并与基于训练的核心集方法进行关联验证），据此设计计算高效的筛选机制，构建任务无关的高质量核心集用于选择性标注与模型训练。

Result: 在图像分类、语义分割、深度估计，以及端到端驾驶与移动操作的模仿学习任务上，CoreDNA与现有最优方法持平或更优，展现出在感知与动作任务上前所未有的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 紧凑的基于激活的表示对数据选择十分有效，CoreDNA可作为无监督、任务无关的核心集估计通用方案。

Abstract: While data collection for model training is relatively easy, curating high-quality datasets from large collections is inherently challenging. Coreset estimation methods address this problem by identifying the best data subsets to train models on. However, existing approaches often require proxy model training, extensive annotations, or task-specific tailoring. Unsupervised alternatives typically exploit geometric properties in foundation model feature spaces, but are limited to pairwise image-to-image comparisons or comparisons against a fixed subset. We introduce CoreDNA, an unsupervised, task-agnostic coreset estimation approach that pools neuron activations from vision foundation models into per-image and per-dataset distributions. By comparing each image's distribution to the dataset-level distribution, CoreDNA can capture both low-level visual structure and high-level semantic information across layers. We find that high-quality samples concentrate in the tails of the last layer's activation statistics, which we validate by relating them to training-based coreset methods. Building on these findings, we develop a computationally efficient selection mechanism to construct task-agnostic, high-quality coresets for selective annotation and model training. Across image classification, semantic segmentation, depth estimation, and imitation learning for end-to-end driving and mobile manipulation, CoreDNA matches or outperforms the state of the art, demonstrating unprecedented generalization across perception and action tasks. These results highlight the effectiveness of compact activation-based representations for data selection.

</details>


### [66] [MLLM Frame Subset Ensembling for Audio-Visual Video QA and MLLM-based Reranking for Ad-hoc Video Search in TRECVID 2025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23214)
*Andreas Goulas,Damianos Galanopoulos,Ioannis Patras,Vasileios Mezaris*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CERTH-ITI团队在TRECVID 2025的AVS与VQA赛道的系统方案综述：AVS采用基于基础模型的两阶段检索-重排框架，VQA采用音视频MLLM微调加推理时缩放与帧子集集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何借助基础模型（VLM、MLLM）在无需训练的条件下提升视频检索的语义匹配精度，并充分释放MLLM在视频问答中的多模态理解潜力。

Method: AVS：两阶段框架。第一阶段用多个VLM对经LLM改写增强的查询与候选镜头编码，输出加权相似度完成初检；第二阶段用MLLM重排模块对Top-N镜头与原始查询做语义对齐评估并生成新相关性分数。VQA：在TRECVID训练集上微调音视频MLLM，并引入推理时缩放；AG任务从不同随机帧子集贪心解码多次、以多数投票聚合；MC任务对候选答案logits做均值池化。

Result: VQA AG任务在3项指标上取得最高分，VQA MC任务取得第二高分。

Conclusion: 基于基础模型的多模型融合与LLM/MLLM驱动的重排可在零训练下显著改善检索排序；微调结合帧子集集成能有效提升开放式与多选视频问答性能。

Abstract: This paper presents the overview of the runs related to the Ad-hoc Video Search (AVS) and Video Question Answering (VQA) tracks of TRECVID 2025 on behalf of the CERTH-ITI team. For the AVS track, we introduce a two-stage framework built on foundation models. In the first stage, multiple vision-language models (VLMs) encode both the input query, augmented through LLM-generated rephrasings, and the candidate video shots, producing weighted similarity scores for initial retrieval. In the second stage, we utilize a Multimodal-LLM(MLLM)-based reranking module that evaluates the semantic alignment between each shot among the top-N highest-ranked ones and the original query, generating updated relevance scores for reordering these shots. This MLLM-driven reranking significantly improves contextual matching and produces more accurate final rankings without requiring any model training. Regarding the VQA track, we fine-tune an audio-visual MLLM model on the provided TRECVID training dataset and we implement an inference-time scaling technique to enhance the multimodal understanding capabilities of the MLLM. For the open-ended Answer Generation (AG) task, we aggregate multiple model responses per question via a majority vote. The responses are generated with greedy sampling from different random frame subsets of the video and they are ranked based on the number of votes. For the Multiple-Choice (MC) task, instead of voting, we use mean pooling on the logits assigned by the fine-tuned model to each candidate response. Through the combination of fine-tuning and frame subset ensembling we achieve the highest score across 3 metrics in the VQA AG task and the second highest in the VQA MC task.

</details>


### [67] [Video Diffusion Avatars: Taming Video Diffusion Models into Controllable 3D Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23772)
*Simon Giebenhain,Tobias Kirschstein,Liam Schoneveld,Davide Davoli,Zhe Chen,Matthias Nießner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VDA 通过微调视频扩散模型，从单张图像生成照片级真实、多视图一致的化身，并实现相机视角、面部表情与上半身姿态的独立精确控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网规模数据训练的视频扩散模型蕴含显式3D渲染与昂贵3D采集难以复现的外观、运动与物理先验；同时3DMM的表达能力（如舌头、口腔内部）存在上限，需要更强的驱动表达空间。

Method: 放弃3DMM，采用学习式表情编码以提升表达力；通过解耦消除其严重的视角偏置（以DISTRACT基准揭示），使其可作为多视图一致化身的驱动信号；相机与上半身姿态通过代理网格渲染联合控制，从而独立调控表情、姿态与视角；采用camera-triplet训练策略提升效率，单张A100仅需34小时、仅用公开多视图数据训练。

Result: 在新视角重演任务上优于现有单图3D化身：BecomingLit上人脸LPIPS降低29%，感知质量JOD提升66%；静态3DGS重建探针验证其生成结果多视图一致性最佳；并呈现物理合理的头发动态、阴影、反射与折射等效果。

Conclusion: 以视频扩散先验替代显式渲染器，可在低训练成本下实现高保真、多视图一致且可控的化身生成，并产生显式渲染难以实现的物理效果。

Abstract: Video diffusion models (VDMs) trained on internet-scale data encode a rich prior on appearance, motion, and physics that is difficult to replicate with explicit 3D rendering and costly 3D data capture. We introduce Video Diffusion Avatars (VDAs), a method that creates photo-realistic, multi-view consistent avatars from a single image by fine-tuning a VDM for precise control over camera viewpoint, facial expression, and upper-body pose. Departing from 3D morphable models (3DMMs), VDAs use a learned expression space that lifts the expressiveness ceiling of 3DMMs, e.g., for the tongue and mouth interior. However, learned expression codes exhibit heavy viewpoint bias, as our DISTRACT benchmark reveals. We show that this bias can be removed through disentanglement, enabling their use as driving signals for multi-view consistent avatars. Camera and upper-body pose are controlled jointly via proxy-mesh renderings, giving independent control over expression, pose, and viewpoint. A camera-triplet training strategy makes the approach efficient: our models train for 34 hours on a single A100 using only public multi-view data. VDAs outperform state-of-the-art single-image 3D avatars on novel-view reenactment, reducing face LPIPS by 29% and improving perceptual quality (JOD) by 66% on BecomingLit. A static 3DGS reconstruction probe further confirms that our generations are the most multi-view consistent. Built on a video prior, VDAs produce effects beyond the reach of explicit renderers, including physically plausible hair dynamics, shadows, reflections, and refractions.

</details>


### [68] [CatchUp: Depth-Aware Re-Admission of Visual Tokens for Efficient Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23324)
*Eunju Park,Uiwon Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 CatchUp，通过深度感知的视觉 token 重新准入与表示追赶机制，解决可恢复路由中过期表示问题，在低 token 预算下显著降低成本并保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM 在多解码层中反复处理数百个视觉 token，计算与显存开销大；现有推理期剪枝方法不可逆地丢弃低分 token，但受查询条件关注的视觉 token 集合会随解码深度变化。可恢复路由虽保留被跳过 token，但其绕过中间变换后以“过期表示”重新准入；作者发现用全 token 参考轨迹中当前深度的 oracle 状态替代过期状态可显著提升 grounding 性能。

Method: CatchUp 为免训练框架：在每一解码区间反复重估查询条件化的视觉相关性，仅将选中的视觉 token 送入计算，其余 token 延后保留待再准入；再准入时，利用语义与空间相关视觉 token 的“参考→当前”转移信号来近似其缺失的表示演化。

Result: 在三个指代表达式 grounding 数据集与六个多模态基准上，CatchUp 在 64/128/192 token 预算下两组基准均取得最高平均相对保留率。64 token 预算下活跃视觉 token 减少 88.9%，六个多模态基准平均保留全模型性能的 92.17%，端到端延迟降低 8.0%。

Conclusion: 深度感知的可恢复 token 路由优于不可逆剪枝，关键在于再准入时对表示演化的补偿；CatchUp 以免训练方式在极低视觉 token 预算下兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) repeatedly process hundreds of visual tokens across decoder layers, incurring substantial computation and memory costs. Existing inference-time visual token pruning methods often irreversibly remove low-ranked tokens, although the set of visual tokens receiving high query-conditioned attention can change across decoder depth. Recoverable routing preserves skipped tokens for later reconsideration, but re-admits them with stale representations after bypassing intermediate transformations. We further find that replacing stale re-admitted states with their current-depth oracle states from a full-token reference trajectory substantially improves grounding performance. We therefore propose CatchUp, a training-free framework for depth-aware visual-token re-admission. CatchUp repeatedly re-evaluates query-conditioned visual relevance, processes only selected visual tokens through each decoder interval, and preserves deferred tokens for later re-admission. Upon re-admission, CatchUp approximates missed representation evolution using reference-to-current transition signals from semantically and spatially related visual tokens.
