Across three referring-expression grounding datasets and six multimodal benchmarks, CatchUp achieves the highest average relative retention in both benchmark groups at 64-, 128-, and 192-token budgets. At a 64-token budget with 88.9% fewer active visual tokens, CatchUp retains 92.17% of the full model's performance on average across six multimodal benchmarks while reducing end-to-end latency by 8.0%. Code is available at https://github.com/pej0918/CatchUp

</details>


### [69] [LonGaussian: Tracking Foundation Priors for Long-Context Feedforward Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23250)
*Zhoufeng Xie,Guanjun Wu,Shuai Zhang,Xinggang Wang,Wenyu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LonGaussian：面向长视频的流式前馈高斯重建框架，通过多帧点轨迹引导的高斯合并与渲染深度一致性过滤，在压缩高斯数量的同时提升新视角渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频重建高质量可渲染3D场景对机器人感知与具身智能至关重要。现有前馈高斯重建方法虽能从少量图像快速预测3D场景，但扩展到长序列存在两个瓶颈：联合处理所有视图导致GPU显存需求激增；逐像素累积的对齐预测冗余表示同一表面，增加存储与渲染开销。

Method: 构建于预训练的流式3D重建骨干网络之上，将长上下文几何特征解码为稠密高斯；在图像已知内参、相机位姿未知的设定下进行流式重建。每个chunk内，利用多帧点轨迹关联同一表面的重复观测，引导学习式合并器（learned merger）整合对应的高斯预测；并引入基于渲染深度的一致性过滤，抑制来自其他chunk、错误遮挡局部重建表面的高斯。

Result: 在DL3DV与RealEstate10K基准上，LonGaussian在提升新视角渲染质量的同时，所用高斯数量显著少于所评估的前馈基线方法。

Conclusion: 以流式前馈方式实现长序列紧凑、全局可渲染的高斯场景重建，兼顾显存与存储效率及渲染质量，为长视频3D重建提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-quality, renderable 3D scenes from long videos is essential for robotic perception and embodied intelligence. Many feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction methods rapidly predict 3D scenes from a small set of images, but extending them to longer sequences presents two bottlenecks: jointly processing all views increases GPU memory requirements, while accumulating pixel-aligned predictions redundantly represents the same surfaces and increases storage and rendering costs. We present LonGaussian, a streaming feed-forward framework that reconstructs compact, globally renderable Gaussian scenes from long image sequences with known intrinsics and unknown camera poses. Built on a pretrained streaming 3D reconstruction backbone, LonGaussian decodes long-context geometric features into dense Gaussians. Within each chunk, multi-frame point tracks link repeated observations of the same surface and guide a learned merger to consolidate the corresponding Gaussian predictions. Rendered-depth consistency filtering further suppresses Gaussians from other chunks that incorrectly occlude locally reconstructed surfaces. On DL3DV and RealEstate10K benchmarks, LonGaussian improves novel-view rendering quality while using substantially fewer Gaussians than the evaluated feed-forward baselines.

</details>


### [70] [Cross-Person Identity Change Under Source-Coupled Multi-Human Generation: A Controlled UniPortrait Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22746)
*Edrees Al-Edreesi,Ghaleb Al-Gaphari,Muneer Alsurori,Mohammed Edrees,Fouad Al-Hamdani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在不引入新保持算法的前提下，刻画 UniPortrait 生成中"非名义目标人物"的身份变化，用配对对比量化源耦合单ID生成(A1)与原生双ID生成(A2)的身份漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人物个性化不仅影响名义目标，也会改变目标之外人物的身份；已有工作常将此类附带变化误读为"成功修复+附带损失"，缺乏对身份变化的系统刻画。

Method: 两项序列研究，119个已接受的双人基线场景、3个固定输出种子；用 InsightFace buffalo 度量基于参考的连续人脸相似度。研究1评估源耦合单ID仅目标生成(A1)；研究2评估原生双ID生成(A2)，采用配对对比（共享基线相似度在配对差分中代数抵消，形成匹配的POST比较），共111个案例、287对可联合测量样本；并用独立身份评估器、盲评人类判断及buffalo内部扰动模型做验证与敏感性分析。

Result: A1对受保护人物(-0.567904)与目标(-0.236904)均为负向平均转变，提示广泛扰动；A2配对对比更正向：受保护人物+0.512918（95%案例簇CI [0.471434,0.550259]），目标+0.158853（[0.118903,0.197523]）。验证与独立评估器及盲评方向一致。

Conclusion: 研究刻画的是完好的原生生成机制，其参考、路由、适配器与token数共同变化，因而不能确立组件因果、类别化身份保持、成功的局部编辑，也不能外推到所评估实现与可测群体之外。

Abstract: Multi-human personalization must account for identity changes in people outside the nominal target of a generative operation. We characterize this behavior in UniPortrait, without introducing a new preservation algorithm. Two sequential studies use 119 accepted two-person baseline scenes and three fixed output seeds, measuring continuous reference-based facial similarity with InsightFace \buffalo. In Study~1, source-coupled single-ID target-only generation (A1) produces negative mean transitions for both the protected person ($-0.567904$) and target ($-0.236904$), indicating broad perturbation rather than established successful repair with collateral loss. In Study~2, native dual-ID generation (A2) yields more positive paired contrasts: protected $+0.512918$ (95\% case-cluster CI $[0.471434,0.550259]$) and target $+0.158853$ ($[0.118903,0.197523]$), across 287 jointly measurable pairs from 111 cases. Shared baseline similarity cancels algebraically in these paired contrasts, leaving a matched POST comparison. Validation designed after the primary result freeze finds directional agreement with an independent identity evaluator and blinded human judgments; within-\buffalo\ nuisance models provide supportive sensitivity evidence. The findings characterize intact native regimes, whose references, routing, adapters, and token counts co-vary. They do not establish component causality, categorical identity preservation, successful localized editing, or generalization beyond the evaluated implementation and measurable cohort.

</details>


### [71] [EgoP$^{2}$C: Internalizing Plan-Perceive-Compose for Spatial Reasoning in Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22681)
*Chaoyu Li,Arka Sadhu,Yuliang Li,Hyo Jin Kim,Yuning Chai,Pooyan Fazli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoP²C 将"规划-感知-组合"流程内化进单个 VLM，通过 Dual-LoRA 教师与 MoE 学生蒸馏，实现无需外部工具的端到端第一视角空间推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂第一视角空间推理需确定所需证据、定向获取观测并多步组合推理；现有 VLM 难以可靠完成该流程，而智能体式系统虽调用外部视觉/几何工具，但仅训练其推理、工具本身不更新，错误观测仍导致错误答案。

Method: 先用独立适配器（Dual-LoRA）分别训练专用 Planner 与 Perception 教师；再在教师监督下用混合专家（MoE）学生微调对应专家；随后用多教师在线策略蒸馏的片段级扩展（MOPD）在当前策略生成的轨迹上训练学生，将不同轨迹片段分配给不同监督者——Planner 监督提问哪些找证据的问题、何时停止收集证据及如何组合最终答案，Perception 监督这些问题的视频落地回答。推理时学生端到端执行该自适应流程，不使用外部空间工具。

Result: 在三个空间推理基准上，基于 Qwen3-VL-8B 与 SenseNova-SI-1.3-8B 的 EgoP²C 学生分别平均超越基座模型 9.2 与 7.5 个百分点；两种骨干下推理 FLOPs 降低 65.8–66.3%，相对共享 Dual-LoRA 教师获得 1.29–1.49× 加速。

Conclusion: 将规划与感知能力内化至单一 VLM 的端到端范式可同时提升第一视角空间推理准确率与计算效率，避免对外部工具的依赖及观测误差传播。

Abstract: Answering complex spatial reasoning questions from egocentric video requires determining what evidence is needed, acquiring targeted observations, and combining them through multiple reasoning steps. Existing vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to perform this process reliably. Agentic systems address this challenge by querying external vision and geometry tools, but training their reasoning leaves these tools unchanged, so inaccurate observations can still lead to incorrect answers. We introduce EgoP$^{2}$C, a framework that internalizes a plan-perceive-compose process within a single VLM. To this end, we first train specialized Planner and Perception teachers using separate adapters (Dual-LoRA), then fine-tune the corresponding experts in a mixture-of-experts (MoE) student under their supervision. We subsequently train the student on trajectories generated by its current policy using a segment-level extension of multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD), assigning different trajectory segments to different supervisors. The Planner teacher supervises which evidence-seeking questions to ask, when to stop gathering evidence, and how to compose the final answer. The Perception teacher supervises the video-grounded answers to those questions. At inference, the student executes this adaptive workflow end-to-end, without external spatial tools. Across three spatial reasoning benchmarks, EgoP$^{2}$C students built from Qwen3-VL-8B and SenseNova-SI-1.3-8B outperform their base models by an average of 9.2 and 7.5 percentage points, respectively. Across both backbones, EgoP$^{2}$C reduces inference FLOPs by 65.8-66.3% and achieves $1.29$-$1.49\times$ speedups over their shared Dual-LoRA teacher.

</details>


### [72] [ProactGen: Learning Proactive Clarification for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22608)
*Zhixiao Wang,Yang Chen,Yifan Xu,Chenbin Li,Yue Chang,Kai Long,Fengming Dong,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProactGen 提出主动澄清式视频生成框架：用强化学习训练的 VLM 提问策略，基于视频草稿向用户追问意图，以低成本草稿降低澄清开销，显著提升生成与用户目标的对齐效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言生成视频虽质量提升，但单次生成代价高，且提示词信息不足导致"生成—检查—修改"的多轮循环，耗时严重。现有视频提示词优化方法只能自动改写，无法补足初始描述中根本不存在的想法与偏好。

Method: 将视觉语言模型训练为提问策略：输入初始提示词与视频草稿，通过强化学习优化，奖励信号为最终生成视频与目标参考视频的对齐程度，从而学会提出对下游生成最有价值的问题。并利用"提问无需高质量草稿"的性质，用更少去噪步数生成草稿以控制澄清成本。

Result: 在目标对齐上，ProactGen 相比直接生成的提升至少为最强基线提升的 1.8 倍；单轮 ProactGen 的对齐效果已超过最强基线四轮细化后的水平，且耗时仅为其 29%。

Conclusion: 主动澄清、以草稿为锚点的提问是提升视频生成意图对齐的高效途径，且澄清可建立在低质量草稿之上，实现低成本、少轮次的交互式生成。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation let users create high-quality videos from natural language descriptions, yet obtaining results that match user intent remains time-consuming: individual generations are computationally expensive, and underspecified prompts force repeated cycles of generation, inspection, and revision. Existing video prompt optimization methods improve generation through automatic rewriting, but cannot supply the ideas and preferences that were not in the initial description. We propose ProactGen, a video generation framework that learns proactive clarification, eliciting user intent through questions grounded in generated video drafts. Specifically, we train a vision-language model as a questioning policy with reinforcement learning: observing the initial prompt and a video draft, it learns to ask the questions most valuable to downstream generation, rewarded by the alignment between the final generated video and a target reference. We further find that effective questioning does not require drafts of final-output quality, and exploit this property by generating drafts with fewer denoising steps, keeping the added cost of clarification low. Experiments show that ProactGen improves target alignment over direct generation by at least 1.8x the gain of the strongest baselines. Moreover, a single round of ProactGen already exceeds the alignment that the strongest baseline reaches after four refinement rounds, in 29% of its time.

</details>


### [73] [BRICE: Brain-inspired Recurrent Illusory Contour Encoder for Point Cloud Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23001)
*Yunlei Wang,Dawei Li,Xue-song Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 受哺乳动物V1区前馈-反馈回路生成错觉轮廓（IC）的启发，提出即插即用的脑启发循环错觉轮廓编码器BRICE，基于预测编码（PC）基础模型，可无缝嵌入主流点云补全基线，在MVP2K、PCN、PlantPCom上提升多种基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流点云补全方法均基于深度学习：一方面重复堆叠注意力模块以提升性能，偏离统一、可解释的网络设计；另一方面几乎所有架构仅依赖前馈连接传播信息，忽视了深层反馈。作者由此寻求更简洁、可解释且具生物合理性的架构。

Method: 借鉴V1区前馈-反馈循环回路产生错觉轮廓（IC）的机制（IC本质是大脑对视觉信息的补全机制），设计脑启发循环错觉轮廓编码器BRICE，以预测编码（PC）为基础模型，通过前馈-反馈循环"想象"不完整点云中缺失的部分；模块为即插即用式，可集成至主流点云补全基线。

Result: 实验表明BRICE能在MVP2K、PCN、PlantPCom等不同3D数据集上增强多种补全基线，验证其通用有效性。

Conclusion: 前馈-反馈循环式脑启发编码器为点云补全提供了简洁、可解释且可迁移的新范式；BRICE的即插即用特性使其具备良好通用性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Mainstream point cloud completion methods are unanimously based on deep learning architectures. On one hand, these methods tend to repetitively stacking attention modules to enhance network performance, which drifts away from the neat design of unified and interpret-able networks; on the other hand, nearly all existing architectures rely solely on feed-forward connections to propagate information, neglecting feedback from deeper layers. The recurrent neural architecture, formed by feedforward-feedback loops, have shown its ability to generate Illusory Contours (ICs) in the V1 brain region of mammals; and ICs are essentially a completion mechanism of the brain for visual information. Motivated by this, we design a Brain-inspired Recurrent Illusory Contour Encoder (BRICE) to "imagine" what is missing in the incomplete 3D point clouds. Building upon the foundation model of Predictive Coding (PC), the plug-and-play nature enables BRICE to be seamlessly integrated into mainstream point cloud completion baselines. Experimental results show that BRICE can enhance various completion baselines on different 3D datasets such as MVP2K, PCN, and PlantPCom. Our code: Https://github.com/wangyunlei-wyl/BRICE.

</details>


### [74] [A100: A Hybrid Vision Foundation Model for Agricultural Plant Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22991)
*Mayur Pratim Das,Ajai Dubey,Aksshay Mathew P,Sana Afreen,Rishi Kumar Soni,Deepak Meena,Pratibha Kumari,Mukesh Saini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出农业视觉基础模型A100，基于300万张无标注图像自监督预训练，覆盖100+作物，在分类、病害检测、表型回归与少标签场景下均优于基线，并计划公开模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业中的作物监测、病害诊断与植物表型分析缺乏专家标注数据，发展中国家尤为突出，限制了精确视觉模型的应用。

Method: 构建混合CNN-Transformer主干：MBConv阶段提取局部特征，Transformer阶段建模全局上下文；在300万张覆盖100余种作物的无标注图像上做自监督预训练，融合DINO自蒸馏、iBOT掩码块重建与KoLeo均匀性正则。

Result: 冻结主干下作物分类准确率95.37%（较ImageNet-12K高2.56%），GroMo25表型基准叶片计数误差降低10.4%；全量微调后32类作物识别99.90%、44类病害检测99.02%；PlantDoc上优于全部基线（64.89% vs CvT-13的63.56%）；仅用1%标注数据（约每类284张）达98.01%，同协议下比FoMo4Wheat与LeafVision高14点以上。

Conclusion: A100以统一主干支持分类与回归任务，具备强标签效率，计划公开发布预训练模型以便农业从业者在有限标注下适配多类植物分析应用。

Abstract: The use of accurate vision models for crop monitoring, disease diagnosis, and plant phenotyping in agriculture is hindered by the lack of expertly annotated data, especially in developing countries. We propose A100, an agricultural vision foundation model that is pre-trained using self-supervised learning on 3 million unlabeled images from fields covering more than 100 crop species. A100 uses a hybrid CNN-Transformer backbone that fuses MBConv stages for local feature extraction with Transformer stages for global contextual representation. A100 uses pre-training with the integration of DINO self-distillation, iBOT masked patch reconstruction, and KoLeo uniformity regularization, resulting in features that are better than ImageNet-12K supervised representations for agricultural applications without any fine-tuning. By utilizing a frozen backbone, the accuracy for crop classification of A100 is 95.37%, which is +2.56% better than ImageNet-12K, and reduces the leaf count error by 10.4% on the GroMo25 phenotyping benchmark, proving that the same backbone can be used for classification and regression tasks. After full fine-tuning, the performance of A100 is 99.90% on 32-class crop identification and 99.02% on 44-class disease detection. On the difficult PlantDoc dataset, A100 outperforms all baselines, such as CvT-13 (64.89% against 63.56%). Remarkably, A100 obtains 98.01% accuracy on plant classification using just 1% of the labelled data (about 284 images per category), showing great label efficiency for use cases with limited labeling. Under an identical protocol, A100 also outperforms the publicly released agricultural foundation models FoMo4Wheat and LeafVision by more than 14 points at 1% labels. We plan to publicly release our pretrained model to allow agricultural professionals to adapt A100 for various plant analysis applications using limited annotations.

</details>


### [75] [Rethinking the design space of Vanilla Pixel Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23854)
*Soumik Mukhopadhyay,Long Mai,Abhinav Shrivastava,Aniruddha Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出MFlow，一种在数据主方向上自适应插值x0预测与速度预测的参数化方法，并以端到端方式与扩散训练联合学习；同时提出轻量卷积模块ConvRefiner，在后处理阶段以对抗损失修复像素扩散Transformer的高频缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散可避免有损潜空间瓶颈与单独训练的自编码器，JiT已证明原生扩散Transformer可直接在像素空间生成，关键在于用x0预测替代u预测。但该参数化选择是否普适、是否随数据表征与模型容量变化，尚缺乏系统研究；此外像素扩散模型在频域统计上存在缺陷。

Method: 1) MFlow：沿数据主方向学习x0预测与u预测之间的自适应插值权重，并与扩散训练端到端优化；2) 分析参数化偏好与数据表征、模型容量的关系；3) ConvRefiner：在冻结扩散骨干的前提下，以对抗损失事后训练的轻量卷积模块，用于补充高频细节。

Result: 参数化偏好随数据表征与模型容量系统性变化，模型容量增大时偏好转向速度预测；MFlow在ImageNet、UCF-101视频及大规模文生图任务上取得有竞争力或更优的生成性能。原生像素扩散Transformer无论采用何种参数化，均相对真实图像存在持续的高频缺失；ConvRefiner仅需少量额外训练即可恢复生成图像的高频统计，同时保持FID不退化。

Conclusion: 参数化不应固定为单一形式，自适应插值的MFlow为像素扩散提供了更优的训练目标选择；像素扩散的高频缺陷可通过轻量后处理模块有效弥补，二者共同提升了像素空间扩散的实用性与图像质量。

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion models have recently re-emerged as a compelling alternative to latent diffusion, avoiding the lossy latent bottleneck and separately trained autoencoder. JiT shows that a vanilla diffusion transformer can generate directly in pixel space by replacing the commonly used velocity prediction ($\boldsymbol{u}$-prediction) with clean image prediction ($\boldsymbol{x_0}$-prediction). In this work, we revisit this choice and introduce MFlow, an adaptive parameterization that learns to interpolate between $\boldsymbol{x_0}$- and $\boldsymbol{u}$-prediction across principal directions of the data, learned end-to-end with diffusion training. We find that the preferred parameterization varies systematically with the data representation and model capacity -- notably, increasing model capacity shifts preference toward velocity prediction. We evaluate MFlow across ImageNet, UCF-101 video, and large-scale text-to-image generation, demonstrating competitive or improved generative performance across diverse settings. In addition, we find that vanilla pixel diffusion transformers, irrespective of parameterization, retain a persistent high-frequency deficit relative to real images. To address this, we introduce ConvRefiner, a lightweight convolutional module trained post-hoc with an adversarial loss while keeping the diffusion backbone frozen. With only a small amount of additional training, ConvRefiner restores the high-frequency statistics of generated images while preserving FID. Project webpage: https://soumik-kanad.github.io/mflow-diffusion/.

</details>


### [76] [UniField++: A Unified Agentic Framework with Loop Reasoning for MRI Field-Strength Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22982)
*Yiyang Lin,Chenhui Wang,Tianliang Yao,Shuaibo Li,Zijun Sun,Yiming Deng,Yixuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniField++ 是一个双智能体框架，统一 64mT→3T 与 3T→7T 等多场强增强任务：VLM 规划器预测幻觉高风险区域并生成修复策略，流匹配增强器沿轨迹循环推理、反复细化高风险区域，从而在跨场强任务上实现解剖学可信的增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI 场强增强旨在让低场扫描器兼具高场图像质量，但现有方法易产生解剖学幻觉：均匀处理使困难脑区检查不足；配对数据稀缺限制了模型学习不同场强间组织对比度与解剖可见性的变化规律。此外，单一场强转换的训练难以泛化。

Method: 提出 UniField++ 双智能体框架。(1) 统一学习：将 64mT→3T、3T→7T 等多种场强转换任务纳入同一框架，使 VLM 规划器与基于流匹配的增强器共享跨任务的场强相关图像特征。(2) 规划器利用放射科医师对易幻觉区域的标注及对应校正方案训练，预测区域风险并制定针对性修复策略。(3) 增强器在规划指导下沿流匹配轨迹进行循环推理，对高风险区域重复处理以实现额外细化。跨转换学习与风险引导细化共同保证跨场强增强的解剖学保真度。

Result: 在评估任务上，相较对比方法平均提升 PSNR 2.74 dB、SSIM 14.05 个百分点；相较消融基线分别提升 3.97 dB 与 13.92 个百分点。在含未见场强的外部队列上仍保持增强质量，并将下游全脑分割 Macro Dice 相较原始低场图像平均提升 0.1457。

Conclusion: 统一多场强转换学习与风险引导的区域细化可有效抑制解剖学幻觉，提升跨场强增强的图像质量与下游分割性能，并展现对未见场强的一定泛化能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: MRI field-strength enhancement seeks to combine the accessibility of low-field scanners with the image quality of high-field scanners. Yet, existing methods remain prone to anatomical hallucinations, as uniform processing leaves difficult brain regions insufficiently examined and scarce paired data limit their learning of how tissue contrast and anatomical visibility change across field strengths. To address these challenges, we propose UniField++, a two-agent framework that unifies enhancement tasks across multiple field-strength transitions, including 64mT-to-3T and 3T-to-7T. Unified learning allows both the VLM-based planner and the flow-matching-based enhancer to draw on field-dependent image characteristics observed across tasks, rather than relying on a single transition. Trained with radiologists' annotations of hallucination-prone regions and corresponding correction plans, the planner predicts regional risk and formulates targeted restoration strategies. Guided by these plans, the enhancer performs loop reasoning along the flow-matching trajectory, repeatedly processing high-risk regions for additional refinement. Together, cross-transition learning and risk-guided refinement support anatomically faithful enhancement across field strengths. Across the evaluated tasks, UniField++ improves PSNR by an average of 2.74 dB and SSIM by 14.05 percentage points over comparison methods, with corresponding gains of 3.97 dB and 13.92 percentage points over the ablation baseline. It also maintains enhancement quality on an external cohort with an unseen field strength and improves downstream whole-brain segmentation Macro Dice by an average of 0.1457 over the original low-field images. Code is available at https://github.com/CUHK-AIM-Group/UniField_plusplus.

</details>


### [77] [ANCHOR: Audio-Visually Grounded Chain-of-Thought Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22964)
*Joel Julin,Souraja Kundu,Liza Dahiya,George Z. Wei,Liuyue Xie,Jingru Yi,Xu Zhang,Hao Yang,Laszlo A. Jeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ANCHOR基准与评估框架，衡量多模态大模型音视频问答中"有据可依的推理"（grounded rationale），发现答案正确不等于推理有感知证据支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估最终答案准确率，无法揭示模型给出的推理理由是否真正对应视觉区域、时间区间和音频事件，答案正确可能掩盖"理由与证据脱钩"的接地失败。

Method: 构建ANCHOR：2,319个视频、8,272个问答对，含结构化推理链、时间证据、空间边界框与显著音频线索；另设224视频/975问答对的人工精修测试集。分别度量答案正确性、幻觉、逻辑偏离与简洁性，并在子集上做直接时空接地测量；用Gemini/GPT生成与人工参考做循环性检验；对三个模型做线索消融。

Result: 12个当代MLLM普遍存在对齐差距：答案表现相近的模型在解释是否被标注证据支持上差异显著。匹配子集（100视频/446问答对）上不同参考来源排序高度稳定（平均Pearson r=0.99），可排除模型参考循环性。消融显示一致模态层级：去除视频使准确率下降38.4–41.8点，去除边界框（3.2–7.7点）与音频（4.2–5.6点）降幅较小、互补且因模型而异。

Conclusion: 仅看答案的评估会掩盖关键接地失败；ANCHOR可作为评估"准确且有证据对齐的音视频推理"的测试平台。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) perform well on audio-visual question answering, but correct answers do not necessarily imply that their stated reasoning is grounded in relevant perceptual evidence. Existing benchmarks mainly evaluate final-answer accuracy, offering limited insight into whether model-generated rationales correctly identify visual regions, temporal intervals, and audio events supporting predictions. We introduce ANCHOR, a benchmark and evaluation framework for measuring grounded-rationale alignment in multimodal reasoning, comprising 2,319 videos and 8,272 question-answer pairs with structured reasoning traces, temporal evidence, spatial bounding boxes, and salient audio cues, including a human-refined test set of 224 videos and 975 question-answer pairs. We separately measure answer correctness, hallucination, logical divergence, and conciseness, with direct spatial and temporal grounding measurements on a focused subset. Across the full benchmark, twelve contemporary MLLMs show a substantial alignment gap: models with comparable answer performance can differ markedly in whether their explanations are supported by annotated evidence. On a matched subset of 100 videos and 446 question-answer pairs, rankings remain highly stable across human-refined, Gemini-generated, and GPT-generated references (mean Pearson r=0.99), providing evidence against model-reference circularity within this subset. Grounding-cue ablations of three MLLMs on the full human-refined test set reveal a consistent modality hierarchy: removing video reduces accuracy by 38.4-41.8 points, while removing bounding boxes (3.2-7.7 points) and audio (4.2-5.6 points) causes smaller, complementary, model-dependent drops. These findings show answer-only evaluation obscures important grounding failures and position ANCHOR as a testbed for evaluating accurate and evidence-aligned audio-visual reasoning.

</details>


### [78] [From GAN-Based Models to GPT-5: A Taxonomy-Guided Study of Human Performance in Detecting Deepfake Face Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23144)
*Zhiqing Guo,Lixin Jia,Luning Sun,Xiaowei Chen,Guofeng Yu,Liejun Wang,Gaobo Yang,David Stillwell,Thorsten Holz,Shujun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨模型深度伪造人脸伪影分类法 DFAT，并基于此构建通用与模型专用训练材料，通过在线实验（n=524）考察五种生成模型下人类的检测表现，发现检测准确率强烈依赖模型，训练后多数条件下显著提升，模型专用训练数值增益更大，五个月随访（n=342）未出现显著遗忘或压力上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式深度伪造已从 GAN 演进到扩散模型与多模态大模型，但关于人类检测深度伪造人脸图像的实证研究滞后；尚不清楚检测表现是否随生成模型而变化，以及模型专用训练是否比通用训练更有效。

Method: 提出 Deepfake Face Artifact Taxonomy（DFAT）——跨模型、人类可识别的视觉伪影分类法；据此构建通用与模型专用训练材料；开展在线实验（n=524），覆盖五种代表性生成模型（三种 GAN、一种扩散模型、一种 MLLM 即 GPT-5），评估检测表现与训练效果；并进行五个月随访测试（n=342）。

Result: 参与者检测准确率强烈依赖生成模型；更新、更先进的模型生成的图像未必更难检测；训练后多数条件下出现显著提升且提升幅度因模型而异；模型专用训练通常比通用训练带来更大的数值增益；五个月随访中检测准确率无显著下降，感知压力无显著上升。

Conclusion: 研究为设计有效、可持续且有针对性的反深度伪造训练方案提供了可操作依据，以应对日益增长的深度伪造威胁。

Abstract: Generative models for deepfakes have rapidly evolved from GAN-based architectures to diffusion models and multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, empirical research on human performance in detecting deepfake face images has not kept pace. It remains unclear if (and how) detection performance varies across generative models and whether model-specific training improves detection more effectively than general-purpose training. In this paper, we propose the \emph{Deepfake Face Artifact Taxonomy} (DFAT), a cross-model taxonomy of human-recognizable visual artifacts in deepfake face images, based on which we build both general-purpose and model-specific training materials. Through an online experiment ($n = 524$), we examined human performance in detecting deepfake face images and evaluated training efficacy across five representative generative models: three GAN-based, one diffusion-based, and one MLLM (GPT-5). Our results show that participants' detection accuracy was strongly model-dependent and that images generated by more recent and advanced models were not necessarily harder to detect. After training, participants achieved significant performance gains across most conditions, and their improvement varied by model. Notably, model-specific training generally yielded greater numerical gains than general-purpose training. In a five-month follow-up test ($n = 342$), we observed no significant decline in detection accuracy and no significant increase in perceived stress. Overall, our findings provide actionable insights for designing effective, sustainable, and targeted training programs to counter the growing threat posed by deepfakes.

</details>


### [79] [Event-STVG: Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding for Long-Form Event Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22444)
*Yizhe Zhang,Xujia Wang,Songqi Tang,Xujie Tang,Leo Jiahao Jiang,Jiacheng Wang,Bowen Qu,JinBo Li,Qin Wang,Ji Qi,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Event-STVG 新任务，将时空视频定位从"定位被描述的目标"推向事件级推理，并构建含 4,016 个事件标注、覆盖足球等 8 个领域的大规模基准；最强 MLLM 在 10 分钟时长下仅 5.4% m-vIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 STVG 基准主要依赖实例特定描述来定位目标，与 MLLM 日益强调的长程推理能力脱节，缺少对重复事件、角色推断与多目标联合定位的考察。

Method: 提出 Event-STVG 任务，统一长程上下文、重复事件推理、事件定位、角色推断和多目标定位；构建覆盖足球及另外 7 个领域、共 4,016 个事件发生的基准；对当前先进 MLLM 进行系统实验评测（指标为 m-vIoU）。

Result: 任务难度显著：最强模型在 10 分钟时间跨度下 m-vIoU 仅 5.4%；跨领域一致出现三类失败模式——同类型重复事件混淆、空间定位瓶颈、难以同时定位全部所需角色。

Conclusion: Event-STVG 为 MLLM 视觉理解建立了新挑战，亟需能在时空上联合推理复杂事件的模型；代码与标注已开源。

Abstract: Spatio-Temporal Video Grounding (STVG) aims to jointly localize a target in space and time given a natural-language query. Despite continued advances, existing STVG benchmarks remain largely centered on localizing targets through instance-specific descriptions, leaving them largely decoupled from long-form reasoning increasingly emphasized in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). In this paper, we introduce Event-STVG, a task that shifts STVG from locating what is described toward event-level reasoning by unifying long-form context, repeated-event reasoning, event localization, role inference, and multi-target grounding. To facilitate research on Event-STVG, we construct a large-scale benchmark comprising 4,016 annotated event occurrences across soccer and seven additional domains. Experiments with state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal substantial difficulty, with the strongest model achieving only 5.4% m-vIoU at the 10-minute horizon. Across domains, we consistently observe confusion among repeated same-type events, spatial grounding bottlenecks, and rare success in jointly grounding all required roles. Event-STVG establishes a new challenge for visual understanding in MLLMs, calling for future models that can jointly reason about complex events across space and time. Code and annotations are available at https://github.com/THU-KEG/EventSTVG

</details>


### [80] [LithoWorld: An Endpoint World Model for Lithography Process-Window Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23408)
*Botong Zhao,Fang Yu,Xinyuan Chen,Yue Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LithoWorld：面向SEM形貌的终点世界模型，用因果预测分支对齐终点表征、条件流策略输出二维校正动作，实现光刻参数恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光刻工艺漂移会使已成形图形偏出工艺窗口，导致SEM形貌不合格；需要依据SEM图像反推校正参数，实现自动化参数恢复。

Method: 构建终点世界模型：内部代表性目标共同决定合格参考外观与校正监督；因果预测分支将当前图像特征与终点表征对齐；条件流策略以动作块形式生成二维校正量；以173组实测SEM按焦距-曝光图组织训练，未测单元保留为未知。

Result: 在73组子集的200个测试对上，合格区域成功率88.5%、内部成功率46.0%，分别超过最强对比方法2.5与6.0个百分点；消融实验区分了目标选择、终点对齐与输出参数化的贡献；定性检索显示可恢复至合格内部而无需精确参考坐标匹配。

Conclusion: 以终点表征对齐替代精确参考匹配的建模方式可有效支撑SEM引导的光刻参数恢复，并具备可分解的模块贡献。

Abstract: Lithography process drift can move a fabricated pattern outside its process window, producing unqualified scanning electron microscopy (SEM) morphology. We present LithoWorld, an endpoint world model for SEM-guided parameter recovery. Interior representative targets jointly determine qualified reference appearance and correction supervision. A causal predictive branch aligns currentimage features with endpoint representations, while a conditional flow policy generates a two-dimensional correction as an action chunk. The model learns from 173 groups of measured SEMs organized as focus-exposure maps, preserving unmeasured cells as unknown. On 200 test pairs from the 73-group subset, LithoWorld achieves 88.5% qualified-region success and 46.0% interior success, exceeding the strongest comparison method by 2.5 and 6.0 percentage points, respectively. Ablations separate the effects of target selection, endpoint alignment, and output parameterization. Qualitative SEM retrieval illustrates recovery to qualified interiors without requiring exact reference-coordinate matching.

</details>


### [81] [VISTA: A Multimodal Egocentric Benchmark for Goal-Oriented Perception and Assistance to Blind and Low-Vision Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22822)
*Noé Mosca,Mohamed Rayan Barhdadi,Muhammad Hamza,Joshua Yeh,Suryodaya Bikram Shahi,Zhiyan Li,Selim-Antoine Lali,Weiyue Li,Hasan Kurban,Erchin Serpedin,Gang Luo,Yue Zhang,Minghan Li,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VISTA——一个多模态第一人称视角基准，用于评估视觉模型对盲人和低视力用户的目标导向辅助能力，包含约1000段Project Aria眼镜录制，支持六种模态输入与十类任务，实验表明现有模型仍难以生成有效的目标导向辅助指引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲人和低视力人群的日常任务受限于视觉能力，需要AI助手提供指导，但现有基准存在两点不足：i) 主要依赖视觉与文本输入，缺乏对穿戴设备中日益可用的凝视、惯性测量、空间轨迹等模态的支持；ii) 评估侧重场景理解与问答，对面向盲低视力用户、覆盖日常任务的目标条件化指导覆盖不足。

Method: 构建VISTA基准：约1000段由Project Aria眼镜采集的第一人称录制，每段提供同步传感器流（其中六种作为模型输入）与三种类型标注，覆盖从物体定位到危险检测的十个任务类别（TC）。在VISTA上对14个代表性视觉-语言模型进行零样本评测，并设计一个融合全部六种模态的轻量级多模态基线模型。

Result: 零样本评测显示，现有模型虽常能描述第一人称场景，但在生成目标导向的辅助指引方面仍存在明显局限；融合六模态的轻量基线为多模态辅助任务提供了参照。

Conclusion: VISTA为评估可提升日常无障碍性的多模态模型提供了基准，揭示了场景描述能力与目标导向辅助能力之间的差距。

Abstract: Blindness and low vision limit many people's ability to complete daily tasks, motivating AI assistants that provide useful guidance. Yet, existing benchmarks do not adequately test whether vision models can provide effective everyday assistance. In particular, i) existing benchmarks primarily rely on visual and textual inputs, with limited support for other modalities that are increasingly available in egocentric wearable devices such as gaze, inertial measurements, spatial trajectories, and others; and ii) existing evaluations emphasize scene understanding and question answering, with limited coverage of goal-conditioned guidance for blind and low-vision users across everyday tasks. To bridge this gap, we introduce VISTA (Visually Impaired Scene and Task Annotations), a multimodal egocentric benchmark for goal-oriented assistance for blind and low-vision users. VISTA contains $\sim$1,000 egocentric recordings captured with Project Aria glasses; each recording provides synchronized sensor streams, six of which serve as model inputs, and three different types of annotation, covering ten task categories (TC), from object localization to hazard detection. We evaluate 14 representative vision-language models zero-shot on VISTA and design a lightweight multimodal model baseline that integrates all six modalities. Experimental results show that, although existing models can often describe egocentric scenes, they remain limited in generating goal-directed assistive guidance. VISTA thus provides a benchmark for evaluating multimodal models that could improve everyday accessibility.

</details>


### [82] [Input- and Feature-Space Correction for Cross-Hospital IHC Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22756)
*Jongwoon Lee,Jhair Gallardo,Christopher Kanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统比较了输入空间与特征空间的无标签校正方法，提出输入空间方法FIX，并发现特征级标准化与FIX组合可显著提升冻结病理基础模型在跨医院IHC分级上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型主要在H&E图像上训练，却被越来越多地作为冻结特征提取器用于IHC分级；跨医院差异会损害分级性能，而针对跨医院IHC分级的校正方法缺乏系统比较。

Method: 在冻结FM编码前对输入图像、编码后对特征分别施加无标签变换，使用UNI与Virchow2两个模型、三种IHC标记、六个有向跨医院迁移任务，共12种设置进行基准评测；提出输入空间方法FIX，并与染色归一化及CORAL等方法对比。

Result: 输入空间中FIX取得最大的macro-F1提升，优于常用染色归一化；特征空间中简单特征级标准化最优，优于CORAL；患者平均的tile级评估下，FIX加特征标准化使macro-F1与QWK分别提升0.181和0.208，序数误差降低0.209。

Conclusion: 输入空间与特征空间变换对冻结病理基础模型的跨医院IHC分级具有互补收益，二者结合可有效缓解域偏移。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (FMs) are trained predominantly on hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) images but are increasingly used as frozen feature extractors for immunohistochemistry (IHC), where cross-hospital differences can impair grading. However, systematic comparisons of correction methods for cross-hospital IHC grading remain limited. To address this gap, we benchmark label-free transformations applied to input images before frozen-FM encoding and to features after frozen-FM encoding using UNI and Virchow2, three IHC markers, and six directed cross-hospital transfers, yielding 12 settings. Among the evaluated input-space methods, our proposed method FIX achieved the largest macro-F1 improvement over no correction, outperforming well-known stain normalization techniques. Among feature-space methods, a simple feature-wise standardization achieved the best improvement over no correction, outperforming CORAL. Under patient-averaged tile-level evaluation, combining FIX with feature-wise standardization improved macro-F1 and quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) by 0.181 and 0.208, respectively, and reduced ordinal error by 0.209 relative to no correction. These results show that input- and feature-space transformations can provide complementary benefits for cross-hospital IHC grading with frozen pathology FMs.

</details>


### [83] [ADA 2025: A Multimodal Dataset for Water-Deficit Stress Detection in Sugar Beets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23609)
*Piper Powell,Robert Franz,Amos Smith,Isaak Ihorst,Joachim Hertzberg,Martin Atzmueller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文公开了ADA 2025数据集：>500 GB、面向水分亏缺胁迫下幼年甜菜植株的四模态（热成像、高光谱、RGB、深度）多时相数据集，并提供环境元数据及RWC、叶片厚度两类真值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML在作物管理中的应用受限于缺乏合适的公开数据集：病害与虫害检测数据相对充足，而水分亏缺胁迫检测几乎无公开数据；现有数据集多为单模态（常仅RGB），无法支撑多模态早期检测；且缺乏精确元数据、植株数量少、采样间隔大。

Method: 构建并公开发布ADA 2025数据集：采集健康与水分亏缺两种状态下的幼年甜菜植株，同步获取热成像、高光谱、RGB与深度四类影像，并配套环境与元数据；以相对含水量（RWC）和叶片厚度作为地面真值来源，并通过示例性结果验证数据有效性。

Result: 数据集规模>500 GB，涵盖多模态、多样本、较小采样间隔与准确元数据；示例性分析显示胁迫与健康植株之间存在可检测的差异，验证了数据集的有效性。

Conclusion: ADA 2025填补了水分亏缺胁迫检测领域多模态公开数据集的空白，为作物胁迫早期检测及多模态方法研究提供基础资源。

Abstract: Research and development into artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) applications in crop management are hindered by a lack of suitable public datasets. Whereas applications in disease and pest detection benefit from a comparatively higher amount of available data, applications like water-deficit stress detection often have very few if any public datasets available. Moreover, most datasets that are available are single-modality and frequently RGB-only, leaving the early detection and enhanced performance offered by other methods, and particularly by multimodal approaches, out of reach. Datasets with accurate metadata, higher numbers of plants, and smaller intervals between data points are also scarce. To address this, we publicly release ADA 2025, a >500 GB comprehensive multimodal dataset of young sugar beet plants under healthy and water-deficit conditions combining thermal, hyperspectral, RGB, and depth imagery along with relevant environmental and metadata and both relative water content (RWC) and leaf thickness as ground truth sources. Exemplary results showcase the dataset's validity in terms of differences detectable between stressed and healthy plants.

</details>


### [84] [A Language-Informed Loss for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23653)
*Suchir Agarwal,Keshigeyan Chandrasegaran,Ziyu Chen,Kyle Sargent,Juan Carlos Niebles,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可逆图像编码器 LILIE，利用语言模型监督构建语言信息损失，替代像素损失用于端到端视觉生成，在 GPIC 上将 gFD 从 66.84 降至 53.01。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期基于像素空间损失的端到端视觉生成已无需学习式图像 tokenizer 即可取得有竞争力的效果，但作者想探究语言信息监督能否为视觉生成提供比像素损失更丰富的训练信号。

Method: LILIE 是一个可逆图像变换（无损编码器），采用归一化流 Transformer，与视觉-语言模型通过下一 token 预测联合训练，使 VLM 能从加噪特征中恢复图像描述；随后用该编码器构造简洁的语言信息损失，用于训练文生图模型 JiT。

Result: 在 GPIC 数据集上，使用 LILIE 损失训练的 JiT 模型相较纯像素基线将 gFD 降低 14 点（66.84 → 53.01）。

Conclusion: 语言信息监督可作为比像素损失更丰富的生成训练信号，且通过可逆无损编码器实现，无需依赖学习式 tokenizer；代码、编码器与生成模型均已开源。

Abstract: End-to-end visual generation with pixel-space loss has recently achieved competitive performance without the need for learned image tokenizers. In this work, we study whether language-informed supervision can provide a richer training signal than pixel loss for visual generation. We introduce LILIE (pronounced "lily"), a Language-Informed and Lossless Image Encoder. LILIE is an invertible image transform trained such that a vision-language model can recover captions from noised features. The encoder uses a normalizing flow transformer trained jointly with a vision-language model via next token prediction. We then use the encoder to extract a simple language-informed loss for text-to-image generation. We train JiT models with LILIE loss, reducing gFD by 14 points (66.84 $\to$ 53.01) against a pixel-only baseline on the GPIC dataset. Our code, encoders, and generative models are available at https://suchiragarwal.com/lilie.

</details>


### [85] [Agentic Image Generation in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23269)
*Yifan Li,Bowen Qin,Kun Zhou,Haochen Zhao,Zikang Liu,Zheng Li,Minsheng Hao,Yuhao Cheng,Haosheng Zou,Jeff Chen,Wayne Xin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"野外图像生成"(Image Generation in the Wild)设定，构建Savanna基准（500条提示、16子类）与自动化困难提示合成管线，并提出Locate-Corroborate-Isolate范式训练Ranger-8B，在检索与生成质量上超越前沿模型驱动的harness框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic图像生成依赖搜索代理从网络检索参考图以扩展生成器知识边界，但真实请求常涉及仅数日前出现的实体：网络视觉证据稀缺、搜索结果被易混淆实体占据、目标极少单独出现，现有基准与方法未刻画该困难场景。

Method: 构建自动化管线：从近期新闻标题合成提示，仅保留真实搜索结果高度混淆的样本；据此构建Savanna基准（500提示/16子类）。提出Locate-Corroborate-Isolate范式：先以文本检索锁定目标身份，再多源核验候选图像，最后裁剪干净参考图供生成器使用；基于15K条遵循该范式的轨迹训练Ranger-8B。

Result: Savanna上，基于前沿模型的harness框架仅约半数提示能检索到正确参考；Ranger-8B取得67.2的搜索分数，超过前沿模型harness；在所有测试生成器上取得最佳生成质量，并在其他图像生成基准上超越先前agentic方法。

Conclusion: 面向时效性强、视觉证据稀缺的野外场景，显式的"定位—核实—分离"检索推理链与相应轨迹训练可显著提升agentic图像生成的检索准确率与生成质量；作者开源管线、基准、训练数据与模型。

Abstract: Agentic image generation pairs an image generator with a search agent that retrieves references from the web, extending generation beyond the generator's training data. Real-world requests, however, often concern entities that appeared only days ago. We call this setting Image Generation in the Wild. In this setting, the web offers limited visual evidence, search results are crowded with easily confused entities, and the target is rarely shown on its own. To study this setting, we build an automated pipeline that synthesizes prompts from recent headlines and keeps only those whose real search results are highly confusing, yielding requests that are hard to solve yet natural to ask. With it, we construct Savanna, a benchmark of 500 prompts across 16 subcategories. On Savanna, even harness frameworks built on the frontier models retrieve a correct reference for only about half of the prompts. We then propose Locate-Corroborate-Isolate, a paradigm in which the agent first pins down the target's identity through text search, then verifies candidate images against multiple sources, and finally crops a clean reference for the generator. Trained on 15K trajectories that follow this paradigm, Ranger-8B achieves a search score of 67.2, outperforming harnesses equipped with frontier models. It also achieves the best generation quality with every generator we test, and surpasses prior agentic methods on other image generation benchmarks. We release the pipeline, benchmark, training data and model at https://github.com/RUCAIBox/Agentic-ImageGen-Wild.

</details>


### [86] [Kingfisher: Fast Personalized Visual Autoregressive Generation via Scale-Aware Prompt-Conditioned Scale Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23238)
*Dinh-Khoi Vo,Akihiro Sugimoto,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需模型微调的 Kingfisher 框架，通过分解、重组视觉自回归模型早期尺度码的内容与风格分量，实现快速个性化图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型个性化依赖主体特定的权重优化、文本嵌入、LoRA 或适配器，准备成本高、速度慢；作者希望探索视觉自回归（VAR）模型中的快速个性化新方向。

Method: 关键观察是早期自回归尺度码的短前缀足以决定物体结构和整体外观，后续尺度主要细化既有生成轨迹。基于此，将早期残差尺度码经提示词条件化的尺度码子空间分解为内容与风格分量；给定两张真实图像，先分解其早期尺度码，再把一图的内容分量与另一图的风格分量重组成紧凑语义前缀，由冻结生成器通过按位自校正预测剩余尺度以完成连贯细化。

Result: 在 CSD-100 上，内容保持能力强，风格与文本对齐在对比方法中最高；配对准备时间相比扩散方法减少约 54 倍，相比 VAR 类替代方法减少约 7.5 倍。

Conclusion: 直接操控冻结视觉自回归生成器的原生尺度空间表示即可实现有效个性化，无需模型微调，兼顾质量与效率。

Abstract: Personalized image generation has been extensively studied in diffusion models, often relying on subject-specific optimization of model weights, textual embeddings, LoRA modules, or adapters. In this work, we explore a different direction for fast personalization in visual autoregressive models. Our key observation is that a short prefix of early autoregressive scale codes is sufficient to strongly determine both object structure and global appearance, while later scales primarily refine the established generation trajectory. Based on this insight, we introduce Kingfisher, a model-tuning-free framework that decomposes early residual scale codes into content- and style-related components through prompt-conditioned scale-code subspaces. Given two real images, Kingfisher first decomposes their early scale codes, then recomposes the content components of one image with the style components of the other into a compact semantic prefix. The frozen generator subsequently predicts the remaining scales through bitwise self-correction for coherent refinement. Experiments on CSD-100 show that Kingfisher achieves strong content preservation and the highest style and text alignment among the evaluated methods, while reducing pair-specific preparation time by approximately 54x compared with diffusion-based methods and 7.5x compared with VAR-based alternatives. These results demonstrate that effective personalization can be achieved by directly manipulating the native scale-space representation of a frozen visual autoregressive generator.

</details>


### [87] [X-Splat: Unified Gaussian Splatting for Joint RGB and X-ray 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23237)
*Wangze Xu,Chu Zhou,Mae Yamaguchi,Linrui Dai,I-Chao Shen,Takashi Ijiri,Imari Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一高斯表示联合重建RGB与X-ray：所有高斯基元共享同一套几何参数，仅表面邻近基元额外携带外观属性；通过干净MVS与X-ray几何代理进行跨模态配准，并用双模态监督加表面深度一致性损失联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB与X-ray联合重建可同时刻画外部外观与内部结构，优于单模态；但现有方法按模态分别训练，存在几何冗余、对齐伪影与耦合松散的问题，限制了跨模态信息发现。

Method: (1) 统一表示：全部高斯基元共享单一几何参数，仅表面邻近基元附加外观属性，减少冗余；(2) 跨模态配准：利用干净的多视图立体与X-ray几何代理进行配准，而非对齐两组已优化的高斯，提升对齐稳定性；(3) 联合优化：双模态监督，并引入由X-ray几何导出的表面深度一致性损失指导RGB重建。

Result: 在InsideOut数据集及自建Flower & Insect数据集上，跨模态对齐更稳健，RGB渲染质量更高且不牺牲X-ray重建，表示比维护两套独立几何更紧凑。

Conclusion: 以几何共享、外观附加的统一高斯表示，配合代理式配准与深度一致性约束，可有效解决跨模态联合重建中的冗余与对齐问题，实现紧凑且高质量的RGB–X-ray联合重建。

Abstract: Jointly reconstructing RGB and X-ray provides new insights by connecting external appearance with internal structure, enabling a more complete understanding of real-world objects than single modality. Yet existing methods train separate models per modality and suffer from redundant geometry, alignment artifacts, and loose coupling that limits cross-modal insight discovery. We propose a unified representation in which all Gaussian primitives share a single set of geometric parameters, with only surface-proximal primitives additionally carrying appearance attributes. We perform cross-modal registration through clean multi-view stereo and X-ray geometric proxies instead of aligning two sets of optimized Gaussians, improving alignment stability. We then jointly optimize the unified representation via supervision from both modalities, complemented by a surface-depth consistency loss derived from X-ray geometry to guide RGB reconstruction. Experiments on the InsideOut dataset and our Flower & Insect dataset show that our framework achieves more robust cross-modal alignment, higher RGB rendering quality without compromising X-ray reconstruction, and a more compact representation than maintaining two independent geometries.

</details>


### [88] [Scene-Designer: Learning Design Intelligence for Agentic 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23121)
*Xuanyu Liu,Hongyan Feng,Miao Pan,Sunlai Chen,Lizhou Liang,Bing Sun,Wenqi Zhang,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Scene-Designer 智能体框架，通过"设计+批判"互补经验学习设计智能，构建 Scene-OTA-35K 与 Scene-OTC-15K 数据集并提出 Critic 引导的 Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 框架 Scene-GRPO，在物理有效性、功能正确性和布局连贯性上取得更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自然语言生成完整、连贯、可用的 3D 室内场景需要"设计智能"——既能设计高质量场景，又能评判其质量。现有方法要么依赖缺乏领域设计经验的通用模型，要么仅从静态场景中学习，无法获得设计与批判能力。

Method: 构建两个数据集：Scene-OTA-35K（Observation–Thought–Action 监督，学习场景设计与高效纠错）与 Scene-OTC-15K（Observation–Thought–Critic 监督，学习跨物理有效性、功能正确性、布局连贯性的场景质量批判）；在此基础上提出 Scene-GRPO——一种 Critic 引导的 GRPO 强化学习框架，用于改进设计决策并减少无效试错修正。

Result: 实验表明 Scene-Designer 在物理有效性、功能正确性和布局连贯性上表现更优；学习得到的 Scene-Critic 在场景批判上显著优于通用模型；测试时的 Critic 引导可进一步提升场景生成质量，说明 Critic 在训练之外仍具指导价值。

Conclusion: 设计与批判两种能力呈现相互增强的趋势，验证了以"批判经验"引导场景设计训练这一范式的有效性。

Abstract: Generating complete, coherent, and usable 3D indoor scenes from natural language requires strong \textbf{design intelligence}: the ability to design high-quality scenes and critique their quality. However, existing approaches either rely on general-purpose models lacking domain-specific design experience or learn from static scenes. In this work, we introduce \textbf{Scene-Designer}, an agentic framework that learns design intelligence through complementary design and critique experiences. We construct two datasets: \textbf{Scene-OTA-35K}, which provides Observation--Thought--Action supervision for learning scene design and efficient error correction, and \textbf{Scene-OTC-15K}, which provides Observation--Thought--Critic supervision for learning scene quality critique across physical validity, functional correctness, and layout coherence. Based on these experiences, we propose \textbf{Scene-GRPO}, a Critic-guided GRPO framework that improves design decisions and reduces unnecessary trial-and-error corrections. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Scene-Designer achieves superior performance in physical validity, functional correctness, and layout coherence, while the learned Scene-Critic significantly outperforms general-purpose models in scene critique. Moreover, test-time Critic guidance further improves scene generation, showing that the Critic can provide useful guidance beyond training. Additional analysis suggests a trend of mutual enhancement between scene design and critique abilities.

</details>


### [89] [FlashControl: One-Step Controllable Generator via Distillation-Friendly Single-Stream Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22537)
*Ngan Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Quan Dao,Dimitris N. Metaxas,Minh Hoai,Cuong Pham,Anh Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DeltaControl（单流多步可控扩散模型，微调部分层替代双分支）与FlashControl（基于步蒸馏的一步可控生成模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型推理慢（需多步采样），已有蒸馏方法可实现一步文生图，但难以推广到可控生成，因ControlNet等双分支结构不利于蒸馏。

Method: 放弃额外条件分支，改为微调原T2I扩散模型中选定子集层，直接支持空间条件生成，得到单流多步的DeltaControl；再以其为教师模型进行步蒸馏，得到单次前向即可完成空间可控生成的FlashControl。

Result: DeltaControl支持空间条件生成且性能与双分支方法相当；FlashControl实现一步空间可控生成。

Conclusion: 单流架构微调比双分支更利于蒸馏，能在保持可控生成质量的同时显著降低采样步数。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a leading approach in visual generative modeling, particularly effective in text-to-image and controllable generation. Their main drawback is slow inference as it requires many sampling steps. While recent distillation methods enable one-step text-to-image generation, extending these techniques to controllable generation is still challenging. Existing controllable models, such as ControlNet, rely on dual-branch design that make distillation difficult. In this work, we adopt a simpler strategy. Instead of adding extra conditional branches, we finetune a selected subset of layers in the original text-to-image diffusion model to directly enable controllable generation. This results in DeltaControl, a single-flow, multi-step diffusion model capable of supporting spatial-condition generation while matching the performance of dual-branch approaches. Building on this teacher model, we further apply step-distillation to obtain FlashControl, a one-step model capable of spatial controllable generation with a single forward pass.

</details>


### [90] [Learning the Shared Latent Structure of Cryo-EM Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22523)
*Nithin Chalapathi,Theo Sternlieb,Ethan Han,Sonya M. Hanson,Pilar Cossio,Aditi S. Krishnapriyan,Ritwik Gupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出EMber系列视觉Transformer，通过自蒸馏结合全局与掩码patch目标，在大规模实验与模拟颗粒图像上预训练，学得可用于多种cryo-EM逆问题的统一蛋白结构表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: cryo-EM二维颗粒图像噪声大，且结构、姿态与成像条件耦合编码；姿态估计、异质性分析与重建等逆问题通常独立求解，但共享蛋白结构与成像模型，因此需要跨逆问题的通用表征。生物相关差异往往空间局域化，而实验数据集判别可能依赖采集特异线索，需受控评估。

Method: 研究带互补全局与掩码patch目标的自蒸馏；构建受控的合成与实验评估以排除采集线索干扰；提出EMber视觉Transformer家族，用214个EMPIAR数据集的超5000万实验颗粒图像及268,000个分子组装的模拟投影进行预训练。

Result: 密集空间特征有利于结构判别；EMber在构象与组成判别上分别较现有方法提升14.85和14.26个百分点；线性探针估计构象群体误差在1.59pp以内；生成的颗粒指派可在更少epoch内得到参考质量的重建。

Conclusion: 将互补全局与掩码patch自蒸馏目标结合，并在大规模实验与模拟数据上预训练，可学得捕获生物学信息、服务多个cryo-EM逆问题的表征。

Abstract: Understanding biomolecular function requires characterizing three-dimensional structure and conformational variability, for which cryogenic electron microscopy (cryo-EM) is a powerful tool. Cryo-EM produces noisy two-dimensional particle images in which molecular structure, particle pose, and imaging conditions are jointly encoded. Recovering biological information from these observations spans inverse problems including pose estimation, heterogeneity analysis, and reconstruction. These tasks are usually addressed independently despite shared protein structure and image formation, motivating a common representation across inverse problems. Since biologically meaningful differences may be spatially localized, we study self-distillation with complementary global and masked patch objectives. Because experimental dataset discrimination can reflect acquisition-specific cues, we develop controlled synthetic and experimental evaluations and find that structural discrimination benefits from dense spatial features. We introduce the EMber family of vision transformers, pre-trained on >50 million experimental particle images from 214 EMPIAR datasets together with simulated projections from 268,000 molecular assemblies. EMber improves upon existing methods at conformational and compositional discrimination by 14.85 and 14.26 percentage points, estimates conformational populations to within 1.59pp error with a linear probe, and produces particle assignments yielding reference-quality reconstructions in fewer epochs. We demonstrate how to learn representations that capture biological information for use across multiple cryo-EM inverse problems.

</details>


### [91] [Sink-Sparse Hybrid Attention for Efficient Inference in T2I/T2V Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22975)
*Bingxin Xing,Yu Zhang,Jiatong Ding,Lei Lu,Ping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Sink-Sparse Hybrid Attention，一种免训练注意力替换方法，通过复用离线标定的静态sink掩码并辅以轻量池化KV分支，加速DiT类T2I/T2V生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT已成为高质量文生图/文生视频的常见骨干，但随token序列增长且去噪步数众多，重复的稠密自注意力成为主要推理瓶颈。作者观察到预训练DiT的稠密注意力中存在可复用的、按层/头特定的高质量（high-mass）结构，可从少量离线校准集识别并跨未来生成复用。

Method: 保持模型权重与采样调度不变，用Sink-Sparse Hybrid Attention替换原注意力：复用静态sink掩码稀疏化注意力，并用轻量池化KV分支补充被稀疏掉的信息，属免训练（train-free）方案。

Result: 在Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B上获得1.54×端到端加速且保持生成质量；在Z-Image-Turbo上获得1.57×端到端加速，GenEval2表现具竞争力。

Conclusion: 可复用的重要性感知稀疏支撑可为T2V与T2I两类DiT提供有效的免训练加速路径。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become a common backbone for high-quality text-to-image and text-to-video generation, but repeated dense self-attention remains a major inference bottleneck as token sequences grow and the same operator is invoked across many denoising steps. Our study suggests that dense attention in pretrained DiTs contains reusable layer/head-specific high-mass structure that can be identified from a small offline calibration set and reused across future generations. This leads to Sink-Sparse Hybrid Attention, a train-free attention replacement that reuses static sink masks and supplements them with a lightweight pooled-KV branch, while leaving model weights and sampling schedules unchanged. On Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B, the method attains 1.54$\times$ end-to-end speedup while maintaining generation quality. On Z-Image-Turbo, it attains 1.57$\times$ end-to-end speedup with competitive GenEval2 performance. These results indicate that reusable importance-aware sparse support can provide an effective train-free acceleration path for both T2V and T2I DiTs.

</details>


### [92] [ODSS-Net: Orthogonally Decoupled Shared-Specific Representations for Multi-Modal Object Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22922)
*Zhifeng Ma,Yuhong Feng,Chongyi Huang,Mingyang Zhou,Minwen Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ODSS-Net，通过正交解耦的共享/特有特征学习与基于样本的专家自适应混合融合，提升多模态目标重识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态ReID方法主要依靠特征对齐与融合缩小模态差距，但对齐会把模态共享的结构信息与模态特有线索纠缠在一起，而常规融合又难以充分利用互补信息。

Method: 框架含三个模块：(1) SSFC抑制模态相关的表观变化，提取模态共享结构信息；(2) OSFL聚合与身份相关的局部信息，并通过几何正交投影去除已对齐的共享分量；(3) SAMoE以共享结构作为路由上下文，自适应精炼并融合各模态特有特征。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验验证了ODSS-Net对多模态目标ReID的有效性。

Conclusion: 共享-特有特征的正交解耦加样本自适应专家融合，是缓解对齐纠缠、充分利用模态互补信息的可行路径。

Abstract: Multi-modal object re-identification (ReID) leverages complementary information from heterogeneous sensors. Cross-modal alignment and fusion remain challenging. Existing methods mainly reduce modality gaps via feature alignment and fusion. However, alignment may entangle modality-shared structural information with modality-specific cues, while conventional fusion may not fully exploit complementary information. To address these issues, we propose ODSS-Net, a framework for orthogonally decoupled shared-specific representation learning and adaptive fusion of complementary modality-specific features. First, a Shared Structural Feature Construction (SSFC) module suppresses modality-dependent appearance variations and extracts modality-shared structural information. Then, an Orthogonal Specific Feature Learning (OSFL) module aggregates identity-relevant local information and removes the shared-aligned component via geometric orthogonal projection. Finally, a Sample Adaptive Mixture of Experts (SAMoE) module uses the shared structure as routing context to adaptively refine and fuse modality-specific features. Experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of ODSS-Net for multi-modal object ReID.

</details>


### [93] [MaskMerge: Structure-aware Token Merging for Efficient Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22921)
*Bavesh Balaji,Letian Jiang,Alexander Wong,David Clausi,Sirisha Rambhatla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MaskMerge：利用粗粒度中间掩码预测约束基于特征相似度的 token 合并，避免跨实例 token 融合，在保持实例边界的同时提升实例分割效率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 token merging 主要依赖特征相似度决定合并对象，该准则在语义分割中有效，但在实例分割中会因同类相邻目标特征高度相似而失效，作者将这一失效模式定义为跨实例 token 融合（cross-instance token fusion），导致实例边界被破坏。

Method: MaskMerge 是一种实例感知的 token 合并方法，使用粗粒度的中间掩码预测对基于特征的合并施加约束，从而在压缩冗余 token 的同时保留实例边界。在实例间视觉相似度高的 PanNuke 与 iSAID 两个实例分割数据集上，与 SOTA 方法 ALGM 对比评估，并在 Cityscapes 上测试，同时验证在 MAE 预训练骨干上的可迁移性。

Result: PanNuke 上 mAP 由 30.1 提升至 32.1，计算量由 229 降至 153 GFLOPs，速度快 1.18 倍；iSAID 上 mAP 由 33.1 提升至 36.2，计算量由 198 降至 182 GFLOPs，速度快 1.23 倍；Cityscapes 上以更少 FLOPs 达到与 ALGM 相当的性能；增益可迁移至 MAE 预训练骨干。

Conclusion: 当仅凭外观相似度无法可靠保持目标身份时，引入实例感知的合并约束可实现更激进的 token 压缩，说明掩码引导的合并准则在实例分割场景下优于纯特征相似度准则。

Abstract: Token merging improves the efficiency of Vision Transformers by combining redundant tokens, but existing methods largely rely on feature similarity to determine which tokens to merge. This criterion is effective for semantic segmentation, yet can fail in instance segmentation, where adjacent objects of the same class often have highly similar features. We identify this failure mode as cross-instance token fusion. We introduce MaskMerge, an instance-aware token merging method that uses coarse intermediate mask predictions to constrain feature-based merging and preserve instance boundaries. We evaluate MaskMerge on two diverse instance segmentation domains characterized by high inter-instance visual similarity: PanNuke and iSAID. Against the SOTA token merging method ALGM, MaskMerge improves mAP from 30.1 to 32.1 on PanNuke while reducing computation from 229 to 153 GFLOPs and running 1.18x faster, and from 33.1 to 36.2 on iSAID while reducing computation from 198 to 182 GFLOPs and running 1.23x faster. Additionally, we also test it on Cityscapes and match ALGM's performance while using fewer FLOPs. The gains also transfer to an MAE pretrained backbone, demonstrating that instance-aware merging enables more aggressive compression when appearance similarity alone cannot reliably preserve object identity.

</details>


### [94] [Variational and Decomposed method for Mitigating Bimodal Bias in Robust Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22845)
*Yihan Zhao,Yuncheng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究VQA中的多模态偏置，发现计数题与非计数题的偏置成因机制不同：计数偏置源于视觉侧并经模态融合传播，非计数偏置主要来自语言偏置并含少量视觉影响；据此提出变分与分解方法VDB分别处理两类偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作多关注语言偏置，近期虽注意到多模态偏置，但其本质仍不清楚。作者希望通过区分计数与非计数问题，厘清多模态偏置的来源机制，从而更精准地消除偏置。

Method: 分别考察计数与非计数问题中的多模态偏置机制。解释性发现：计数信息来源于多模态特征在特定维度上的幅值，故计数偏置源自视觉侧并通过模态融合传播至多模态特征；非计数偏置主要源于语言偏置并含轻微视觉偏置。据此提出VDB（Variational and Decomposed method for mitigating Bimodal Bias）：提取受视觉与语言侧不同比例影响的双模态偏置，引入基于变分自编码器原理的变分偏置模型以学习以语言偏置为主、视觉影响较小的偏置，并构建结合语言与视觉两侧的双模态偏置模型。

Result: 在VQACPv2、VQACPv1与GQAOOD数据集上的大量实验验证了所提主张的合理性及方法有效性，并对VQAv2上的性能折损给出了解释。

Conclusion: 计数与非计数问题的多模态偏置具有不同底层机制与来源，需区别对待；VDB通过变分建模与偏置分解分别抑制两类偏置，实验证明其合理且有效。

Abstract: Visual question answering (VQA) models are known to suffer from the bias issue, which refers to overfitting the regularities of frequent patterns. Previously, researchers only consider the language bias. Recently, the multimodal bias is noticed. However, what multimodal bias is remains unclear. In this paper, we investigate the multimodal bias in counting and non-counting questions. We find that they have distinct underlying mechanism, so their biases come from distinct reasons. We provide valuable interpretations of VQA counting mechanism: \textbf{quantity information stems from the magnitude of the multimodal features in specific dimensions.} Thus, counting bias stems from the vision side and propagates to multimodal features through modality fusion, while the non-counting bias stems from primarily language bias and slightly vision bias. Thus, we propose a method to deal with them differently: Variational and Decomposed method for mitigating Bimodal Bias (VDB). It extracts bimodal bias affected by different proportion of vision and language sides. We introduce variational bias models utilizing the principle of Variational Auto-Encoder to learn mainly language bias with slight vision effect, and a bimodal bias model for bimodal bias combining language and vision sides. Extensive experiments on VQACPv2, VQACPv1, and GQAOOD datasets demonstrate the rationality of our claim and the effectiveness of our method. We also provide explanation for the compromised performance on VQAv2.

</details>


### [95] [Replay Pruning and Few-Bit Integer Inference for Hardware-Efficient Speed-Sign Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22811)
*Se Un Park,Kyungkeon Chung,Huigeol Kim,Junyoung Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向车载相机SoC的限速标志两阶段识别系统：检测器用"channel replay"通道剪枝+单次知识蒸馏恢复，分类器为18k参数全整型五层CNN，整体0.72M参数，在外部相机上达95.2%识别率（90%精度）、94 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载SoC的存储、算力与能耗预算严格，需把检测器与分类器同时压入芯片；现有剪枝/量化方案在精度、部署一致性和硬件加速器适配性上难以兼顾。

Method: 1) 检测器：YOLO族网络（ReLU、hard-sigmoid），采用channel replay——迭代剪枝级联识别待删通道，将删除记录回放到原始任务训练网络（parent）上，再做一次连续知识蒸馏恢复；并提出per-step replay变体。2) 分类器（reader）：五层CNN、18k参数，W4A8整数运算；YOLOv5n/YOLOv8n头部后处理亦全整型。3) 部署：导出的可执行流式模型与运行时逐点一致；在Jetson AGX Orin上比较FP16/INT8及TensorRT显式/隐式形式与DLA。

Result: YOLOv5n上replay剪去60.3%参数且保持parent精度；五组匹配种子的验证mAP50-95优于迭代剪枝与从头训练，per-step replay为最准剪枝方法。YOLOv8n上replay与per-step replay优于其他方法与未剪枝parent。reader以W4A8达12.1KB片上存储且精度等同FP32，流式模型在9,850张测试crop上完全复现INT8预测。Orin上INT8仅在TensorRT隐式形式快于FP16，replay检测器在DLA上最快。系统总体0.72M参数，仅用公开数据训练，在留出车载相机上95.2%识别率、90%精度、94 FPS。

Conclusion: channel replay（尤其per-step变体）是兼顾精度与压缩率的通道剪枝方案；全整型reader与后处理、W4A8及DLA映射共同构成可在车载SoC预算内实时运行的两阶段限速标志识别系统，且具备可复现的部署一致性。

Abstract: Speed-limit sign recognition on an automotive camera system-on-chip (SoC) must fit a detector and a classifier (the reader) into the memory, arithmetic, and energy budgets of the device. In our two-stage recognizer, the detector, a YOLO-family network with ReLU and hard-sigmoid operators, is compressed by channel replay: an iterative pruning cascade identifies the channels to remove, the recorded deletions are replayed on the original task-trained network, the parent, and one continuous knowledge-distillation recovery follows. On a YOLOv5n, replay removes 60.3% of the parameters at the accuracy of the parent and, in validation mAP50-95 over five matched seeds, outperforms iterative pruning and training from scratch, and its per-step variant (per-step replay) is the most accurate pruning method. On a YOLOv8n, replay and per-step replay outperform every other method and the unpruned parent. The reader, a five-layer CNN with 18k parameters, and the post-processing of the YOLOv5n and YOLOv8n heads use integer arithmetic only. With 4-bit weights and 8-bit activations (W4A8), the reader matches FP32 accuracy in 12.1 KB of on-chip memory, and an executable streaming model reproduces the INT8 class predictions of its runtime on all 9,850 test crops. On a Jetson AGX Orin, INT8 is faster than FP16 only in the implicit formulation of TensorRT, and the replay detector runs fastest on the deep-learning accelerator (DLA). Trained on public data only, the combined system of 0.72 M parameters recognizes 95.2% of the signs of a held-out automotive camera at 90% precision and 94 frames per second.

</details>


### [96] [Shift-Reliance Alignment: Ranking Vision-Language Models by the Cues They Trust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22610)
*Faisal Mohammad,Mohamed Younis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Shift-Reliance Alignment (SRA)模型选择指标：用少量目标域标注数据校准一个"线索误导检测器"，估计目标域中线索误导答案的比例（shift），并结合模型在源域上的误差与线索依赖度（reliance）来预测其在目标域的表现，从而在无需运行候选模型于目标域的前提下选出最优VLM，显著降低物体存在性（yes/no）问答中的幻觉误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM选择依赖排行榜给出的单一排名，但不同应用场景（如物体存在性问答）中最优模型并不相同；特别是场景暗示却未包含某物体时，POPE幻觉基准上误差最低的模型未必在该类问题上最优。直接在每个目标域上运行所有候选模型代价高昂，因此需要一个无需在目标域上运行候选模型即可预测其表现的模型选择方法。

Method: 1) 以"线索依赖度"（reliance）刻画模型对场景上下文、图像内文字等特征的依赖：对100张标注源图像进行编辑使线索指向错误答案，并与匹配的对照编辑比较，量化该依赖。2) 定义SRA得分 = 源域标注问题上的误差 + reliance × shift；shift为目标域中线索误导答案的问题比例，由基于650–1,200个标注目标项校准的检测器在无标注目标图像上估计。3) 在目标域候选模型中选择SRA得分最低者。4) 发布CueDial基准，用于控制线索误导频率进行评测。

Result: reliance能预测哪些VLM会被物理贴纸攻击误导（在未参与分数设计的九个模型上Spearman相关系数0.93），以及在未编辑COCO照片上产生幻觉的程度，且该预测超出模型"倾向回答yes"所能解释的部分。预注册测试中，在多数问题具误导性的新图像上，SRA将所选模型误差从按源域误差选择的30.4%降至18.8%（Objects365与OpenImages），但在COCO上效果与预测不符。CueDial上，当叠加文字否认可见物体时，基于模型置信度的选择比最优模型差40个百分点以上，而SRA保持在3个百分点以内。

Conclusion: 模型对线索的依赖度是跨域性能的可迁移预测因子，SRA可在不运行候选模型于目标域的情况下实现高效、可解释的VLM选择，尤其适用于线索频繁误导的场景；但方法的预测在COCO上未成立，说明其适用边界仍需进一步界定。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are usually chosen from leaderboards, which give one ranking for every application. On questions about objects that a scene suggests but does not contain, however, the model with the lowest error on the POPE hallucination benchmark is not the one with the lowest error on such questions. For yes/no questions about object presence, we show that the best model for a target domain can be predicted without running any candidate on that domain, given 650 to 1,200 labeled target items that calibrate a detector. We measure each model's reliance on a cue, a feature such as scene context or text in the image, by editing 100 labeled source images so that the cue suggests the wrong answer, and comparing with a matched control edit. Reliance predicts which VLMs are misled by physical sticker attacks (Spearman .93 on nine models not used to design the score) and which hallucinate on unedited COCO photographs, beyond what their tendency to answer yes explains. Shift-Reliance Alignment (SRA) scores a model by its source error on the labeled source questions plus its reliance multiplied by the shift. The shift is the proportion of target questions on which the cue misleads, and a detector estimates it on the unlabeled target images. SRA selects the model with the lowest score. In pre-registered tests on new images where most questions mislead, it lowered the error of the selected model from 30.4% under selection by source error to 18.8% on Objects365 and OpenImages but, against its prediction, not on COCO. On CueDial, a benchmark we release that varies how often a cue misleads, selection by model confidence falls more than 40 percentage points behind the best model when overlaid text denies a visible object, and SRA stays within 3 points.

</details>


### [97] [CLEAR: Cooperative Latent Flow for Visual Counterfactual Explanations with Accurate Region Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23577)
*Yan Zeng,Changlu Guo,Oskar Kristoffersen,Anders Nymark Christensen,Morten Rieger Hannemose,Anders Bjorholm Dahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLEAR：基于潜空间 Flow Matching 的视觉反事实解释框架，通过分解分类器引导为协同/冲突分量并自适应衰减冲突分量，同时动态约束可编辑区域，兼顾效率与真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉反事实解释需在改变分类器预测的同时保持真实性和决策无关内容。图像空间方法质量高但计算昂贵，潜空间方法高效但保真度低。作者指出该差距源于两点：(1) 潜生成力求从扰动表示恢复自然样本，而分类器引导将样本推向目标类，二者更新方向可能相反；(2) VAE 重建会改动应保持不变的区域。

Method: 提出 CLEAR，潜空间 Flow Matching 框架。利用 Flow Matching 显式瞬时速度作为生成方向参考，将分类器引导分解为协同分量与冲突分量，保留协同分量并按冲突强度自适应衰减冲突分量；分析生成全过程中的冲突频率与强度以揭示引导与生成过程的交互；并动态识别可编辑区域、约束潜变量修改、直接保留区域外的事实内容，以减少 VAE 引入的变化。

Result: 在两个数据集、五个双向任务上，CLEAR 在保持潜空间效率的同时，反事实质量与最强图像空间基线相当或更优，相较最强基线获得 2.5 倍加速。

Conclusion: 将分类器引导与生成方向显式解耦（协同/冲突分解）并结合区域约束，可在潜空间中实现高质量、高效率的反事实解释，缩小潜空间方法与图像空间方法的保真度差距。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations (VCEs) should alter a classifier's prediction while remaining realistic and preserving decision-irrelevant content. Image-space methods provide high-quality counterfactuals but are computationally costly, whereas latent-space methods are more efficient but often less faithful. We identify two sources responsible for this gap. First, latent generation recovers a natural sample from a perturbed representation, whereas classifier guidance pushes it toward a target class, so their update directions may oppose. Second, VAE reconstruction can alter regions that should remain unchanged. We introduce CLEAR, a latent Flow Matching framework for VCEs. CLEAR uses Flow Matching's explicit instantaneous velocity as a reference for the generative direction to decompose classifier guidance into cooperative and conflicting components. It preserves the cooperative component and adaptively attenuates only the conflicting one according to conflict strength. We further analyze conflict frequency and strength throughout generation to reveal how classifier guidance interacts with the generative process. CLEAR also dynamically identifies editable regions, constrains latent modifications, and directly preserves factual content outside them to reduce VAE-induced changes. Across five bidirectional tasks on two datasets, CLEAR retains latent-space efficiency while achieving counterfactual quality comparable to or better than strong image-space baselines, with a 2.5x speedup over the strongest baseline. Project page: https://yan-zen9.github.io/CLEAR/

</details>


### [98] [PanoBench: A Panoptic Occupancy Prediction Benchmark for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23537)
*Yi Feng,Zizhan Guo,Junwu E,Yu Ma,Mengtan Zhang,Jin Wu,Wei Ye,Hanli Wang,Qijun Chen,Danwei Wang,Rui Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PanoBench——以相机为中心、跨合成与真实驾驶场景的全景占用预测基准，统一CarlaOcc、nuScenes、KITTI360的数据接口与评测协议，提供带持久实例身份的多分辨率标注，并建立统一的valid-mask评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景占用统一了3D几何、语义与实例身份，但其发展受限于实例级标注稀缺：现有基准多提供由不完整传感器观测重建的语义标签，而全景数据集则在域覆盖或标注完整性上受限。

Method: 构建相机为中心的全景占用基准，整合PanoBench-CarlaOcc（合成）、PanoBench-nuScenes与PanoBench-KITTI360（真实），通过规范化数据接口与统一评测协议提供多分辨率、带持久实例ID的标注；合成分支由仿真网格导出完整全景几何；真实数据采用新流程：基于几何先验与拓扑约束重建背景，从多帧多视图图像生成前景网格资产，并融合为稀疏全景体素；设计统一valid-mask协议，对时序观测支持下的完整thing实例评测、对stuff区域按体素级评分。

Result: 在观测保真度、几何完整性、空间连续性与时序一致性四方面的分析表明标注优于现有占用基准；在语义占用预测、全景占用预测与4D占用预测任务上评测代表性方法，并研究体素分辨率、合成到真实迁移与存储效率。结果显示体素法与射线法指标具有互补优势，真实世界实例感知预测仍存在持久挑战。

Conclusion: PanoBench为实例感知的全景/4D占用预测提供统一、完整且时序一致的评测平台，揭示了体积与射线类指标的互补性及真实场景实例级预测的关键难点，已公开。

Abstract: Panoptic occupancy unifies 3D geometry, semantics, and instance identities, but its development is limited by scarce instance-aware annotations. Existing benchmarks mainly provide semantic labels reconstructed from incomplete sensor observations, while panoptic datasets have limited domain coverage or annotation completeness. We introduce PanoBench, a camera-centric panoptic occupancy prediction benchmark spanning synthetic and real-world driving scenes. It combines PanoBench-CarlaOcc, PanoBench-nuScenes, and PanoBench-KITTI360 through canonical data interfaces and unified evaluation protocols, providing multi-resolution annotations with persistent instance identities. The synthetic branch derives complete panoptic geometry from simulated meshes. For real-world data, a new pipeline reconstructs backgrounds using geometric priors and topology constraints, generates foreground mesh assets from multi-frame multi-view images, and fuses them into sparse panoptic voxels. A unified valid-mask protocol evaluates complete thing instances supported by temporal observations while scoring stuff regions at the voxel level. Analyses of observation fidelity, geometric completeness, spatial continuity, and temporal consistency demonstrate the benefits of the resulting annotations over existing occupancy benchmarks. We benchmark representative methods on semantic occupancy prediction, panoptic occupancy prediction, and 4D occupancy forecasting, and study voxel resolution, synthetic-to-real transfer, and storage efficiency. These results reveal complementary strengths of volumetric and ray-based metrics and persistent challenges in real-world instance-aware prediction. PanoBench is available at https://mias.group/PanoBench.

</details>


### [99] [ReVision: Reprogramming Image Generators for Dense Visual Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23533)
*Yitong Yang,Yinglin Wang,Xuexin Liu,Wei Chen,Zhaoyang Xu,Jiaxin Lin,Rui Chen,Shuting He,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReVision：基于冻结的多模态理解模型与冻结图像生成器，通过指令条件的瞬态低秩参数生成实现统一稠密视觉感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将多任务编码进共享参数化，联合训练对异构数据混合比例与权重敏感，易产生跨任务干扰。

Method: 冻结多模态理解模型与图像生成器；理解模型将图像与自由形式指令联合编码为多模态表示，可训练参数生成器将其映射为生成器的瞬态低秩更新；每个图像–指令对产生独立的一组预测参数，无需任务特定头、任务标识或存储适配器；统一RGB输出接口覆盖分割、深度与法向预测。

Result: 在多个稠密预测基准上达到与SOTA相当的性能，并支持自然语言灵活指定任务；分析显示任务间存在显著梯度冲突，生成的更新在权重空间呈结构化、任务与输入依赖的组织。

Conclusion: 实例级参数生成可缓解多任务冲突，为统一多任务稠密感知提供有效范式。

Abstract: Recent image generators exhibit rich visual priors that can support dense visual perception. However, existing approaches typically encode multiple perception tasks into a shared parameterization, making joint training sensitive to the mixture and weighting of heterogeneous data and prone to cross-task interference. To address this issue, we introduce ReVision, an instruction-conditioned parameter generation framework built on a frozen multimodal understanding model and a frozen image generator. Given an image and a free-form instruction, the understanding model jointly encodes them into a multimodal image--instruction representation, which a trainable parameter generator maps to transient low-rank updates for the generator. Each image--instruction pair induces a distinct set of transient prediction parameters, enabling instance-specific adaptation while both pretrained backbones remain unchanged. ReVision requires no task-specific heads, explicit task identifiers, or stored adapters. A shared RGB output interface unifies segmentation, depth estimation, and surface-normal prediction. Across diverse dense prediction benchmarks, ReVision achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods while supporting flexible task specification through natural-language instructions. Our analysis further reveals substantial gradient disagreement across tasks, highlighting the conflict introduced when heterogeneous objectives share a single parameterization. Meanwhile, the generated updates exhibit structured task- and input-dependent organization in weight space, further validating instance-level parameter generation for multi-task perception.

</details>


### [100] [Momentum: Persistent Dynamics for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23423)
*Nedko Savov,Stefan Maria Ailuro,Danda Pani Paudel,Yilun Du,Xi Wang,Luc Van Gool*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Momentum，用多台参考相机组成的相机架（rig）视频同步表示不断演化的场景，并通过图结构在相邻 rig 间传播生成，从而在目标视角之外显式维护动态内容；该框架包含 WorldUpdate 与 WorldInteract 两阶段，并配套 CARLA 数据采集与一致性评测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型需刻画超出当前相机视野的场景演化，使内容重新出现时观测一致。现有方法依赖观测历史、静态点云或历史帧构建的图像缓存，代价高且无法更新视野外动态内容——生成的车辆、行人离开视野后被遗忘或回来时物理状态错误。

Method: 用由 8 台相机组成、共同覆盖周围场景的参考相机架（rig）同步视频表示演化中的场景；将多个 rig 连成图，在相邻 rig 间传播视频生成以协调跨位置、跨时间的场景动态。整体为两阶段流水线：WorldUpdate 联合生成参考相机视频，作为场景动态的共享记忆；WorldInteract 将相机控制的视频生成锚定于该演化记忆，使运动内容在目标视野外仍被表示并可一致重现。同时构建 CARLA 数据流水线，采集参考相机架与移动目标相机的同步视频，并给出评测框架，衡量生成世界状态内部一致性与目标观测一致性。

Result: 在生成街景上的实验表明，相比静态图像条件，将目标视频锚定于 Momentum 生成的场景动态，在未见场景中把动态场景一致性提升 126.2%，同时视觉保真度也得到改善。

Conclusion: 以多 rig 同步视频作为可演化的场景记忆、并以图传播方式协调动态，是解决视野外动态建模与重现一致性的有效途径；模型与源码将公开。

Abstract: Video world models must capture how a scene evolves beyond the current camera view to generate consistent observations when its content reappears. Existing approaches rely on observation history, static point clouds, or image caches constructed from past frames. These representations are often expensive and, more importantly, cannot update dynamic scene content outside the field of view: generated vehicles and pedestrians may be forgotten after leaving the field of view or reappear in physically inaccurate states. We represent an evolving scene through synchronized videos from reference-camera rigs, each comprising eight cameras that jointly cover the surrounding scene. We connect these rigs in a graph and propagate video generation between neighboring rigs to coordinate scene dynamics across locations and time. We introduce Momentum, a two-stage pipeline: WorldUpdate jointly generates reference-camera videos as a shared memory of scene dynamics, and WorldInteract grounds camera-controlled video generation in this evolving memory, allowing moving content to remain represented outside the target view and reappear consistently. To support training and evaluation, we develop a CARLA pipeline that captures synchronized videos from reference camera rigs and a moving target camera, together with an evaluation framework measuring consistency within the generated world state and the target observations.Experiments on generated street scenes show that grounding target videos in scene dynamics generated by Momentum improves dynamic scene consistency by 126.2% in unseen scenes over static image conditioning, while also improving visual fidelity. Our models and source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [101] [Can We Use Saliency Maps for the Evaluation of Generative Image Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23411)
*Eleonora Ristori,Luca Bindini,Marco Lippi,Paolo Frasconi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受人类感知启发的生成图像评估框架：为每个生成器训练专用的真伪分类器，并用显著性图分析其决策依据，结合分类精度与显著性信息构造与人类判断一致的复合分数，无需依赖大型预训练主干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常用生成图像评估指标依赖固定的预训练特征提取器，这些特征器针对其他任务优化，在不同数据集与模型族上并不总与人类的真实性感知一致。

Method: 避免使用为无关任务学习的表征，训练生成器专属分类器区分真实图像与该模型生成的样本，并利用显著性图（saliency maps）解释其决策；将分类准确率与显著性信息组合为复合评分，仅使用被评估模型的真实与生成样本。

Result: 在多个基准上，分类器学到的是生成器特有的伪影而非普适的"虚假性"概念，具有高准确率与有限的跨生成器迁移能力；归因图有效凸显各模型输出特有的视觉线索与结构不规则性；复合分数与人类判断对齐。

Conclusion: 生成器专属分类器与显著性分析的结合提供了一种轻量、可解释且与人类感知一致的生成图像评估范式，摆脱了对大型预训练主干网络的依赖。

Abstract: Evaluating generative image models remains challenging, as commonly used metrics depend on fixed pretrained feature extractors optimized for different tasks that do not always align with human realism perception across datasets and model families. To address this limitation, we investigate a human-inspired evaluation framework that avoids representations learned for unrelated tasks. Specifically, we train a generator-specific classifier to distinguish real images from samples produced by a given model and analyze its decisions using saliency maps. Across diverse benchmarks, we observe that these classifiers learn generator-specific artifacts rather than a universal notion of "fakeness", exhibiting strong accuracy and limited cross-generator transfer. This makes saliency analysis particularly informative: the resulting attribution maps highlight visual cues and structural irregularities characteristic of each model's outputs. By combining classification accuracy and saliency information, we obtain a composite score aligned with human judgments, without relying on large pretrained backbones and using only real and generated samples from the evaluated model.

</details>


### [102] [FloodVLM-Pix: Language-Guided Pixel-Level Flood Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23377)
*Chengyang Zhang,Zhihao Wang,Zhiyu Long,Xueqian Wang,Quanming Yao,Wei Wu,Gang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建首个大规模遥感洪水多模态基准（131,200图像块、443,100条文本），并提出语言引导的像素级洪水理解模型FloodVLM-Pix，缓解注意力偏置并提升洪水描述与分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化使洪水更频繁、更具破坏性，亟需准确的洪水理解。但现有视觉语言模型在洪水任务上表现差，存在注意力偏置与洪水量级描述不准确的问题；同时缺少支持语言级理解与像素级分割的大规模基准。

Method: 构建首个支持语言级洪水理解与像素级分割的大规模遥感洪水多模态基准，含131,200个图像块及443,100条关于洪水严重程度与演变的文本描述。基于该基准提出FloodVLM-Pix：包含洪水先验模块（自动蒸馏子区域洪水空间先验并注入模型，缓解注意力偏置）和多时相洪水像素解码器（引入通用-特定特征融合机制，抑制云、山体阴影等干扰）。

Result: 在FloodVL-Pix上的实验表明，相比当前最先进的视觉语言模型与洪水分割模型，FloodVLM-Pix在洪水描述质量和像素级洪水分割性能上均有提升。

Conclusion: 该工作通过新基准与语言引导的像素级模型，统一了语言级洪水理解与像素级分割，验证了先验注入与通用-特定特征融合在缓解注意力偏置、抵抗干扰方面的有效性。

Abstract: As climate change intensifies, floods are becoming more frequent and destructive, making accurate flood understanding an urgent priority. However, existing vision-language models perform poorly on flood-related tasks, suffering from attentional bias and inaccurate descriptions of flood magnitude. To bridge this gap, we construct the first large-scale remote sensing flood multi-modal benchmark that supports language-level flood understanding and pixel-level flood segmentation. The benchmark contains 131,200 flood image patches and corresponding 443,100 textual descriptions for flood severity and evolution. Based on this benchmark, we propose FloodVLM-Pix, a language-guided pixel-level flood understanding model that includes a flood prior module and a multi-temporal flood pixel decoder to achieve accurate flood understanding.
