The flood prior module automatically distills sub-region flood spatial priors and inputs priors into FloodVLM-Pix, solving the attentional bias problem in flood language understanding. The flood pixel decoder introduces a novel general-specific feature fusion mechanism to address performance degradation caused by interference (e.g., clouds or mountain shadows) during flood segmentation. Experiments on FloodVL-Pix demonstrate that FloodVLM-Pix improves both flood description quality and pixel-level flood segmentation performance compared with state-of-the-art VLMs and flood segmentation models. Our code and data will be released at https://github.com/Haomiaoyanmeng/FloodPix.

</details>


### [103] [Learning to Correct Geometry in Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23856)
*Weijie Lyu,Tiancheng Shen,Jason Li,Yujing Wang,Xiaoyuan Wang,Haobo Yuan,Yizhou Zhao,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Video Geometry Corrector (VGC)，一个基于合成配对监督训练的后处理视频到视频模型，用于修正生成视频在相机运动下的几何不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成视频在相机运动下常出现几何不一致：视角变化导致结构扭曲、视差矛盾、平滑运镜退化为镜头切换式转场。作者认为这本质上是数据问题——网络规模视频语料中可见运动多来自静态或弱运动相机拍摄的动态物体，对视角变化引起的表观变化监督稀疏。

Method: 从具多样相机运动与位姿标注的真实视频出发，构建VG-Pairs合成配对监督数据生成流水线：将每个视频片段视为几何一致的目标，用预训练图像到视频修复模型结合轨迹自适应关键帧合成失真对应版本。VGC学习将失真输入映射到几何一致目标，不修改源生成器，并以光流作为辅助线索帮助解耦相机运动与物体运动。

Result: 在位姿条件与提示驱动两类基准上，VGC在多个源生成器上均提升几何一致性；扩展至更大骨干网络后，框架可进一步走向更通用的视频精修。

Conclusion: 几何一致性可作为数据驱动问题，通过合成配对监督的后处理修正器有效缓解，且具备跨生成器的通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Generated videos often lose geometric consistency under camera motion: structures warp under viewpoint change, parallax becomes inconsistent, and smooth camera movement may collapse into shot-like transitions. We argue that this failure is largely a data problem. In web-scale video corpora, most visible motion comes from dynamic objects captured by static or weakly moving cameras, leaving only sparse supervision for viewpoint-induced appearance changes. To address this, we propose Video Geometry Corrector (VGC), a post-hoc video-to-video model trained on synthetic paired supervision. Starting from real videos with diverse camera motion and pose annotations, we construct VG-Pairs with a synthetic paired-supervision data generation pipeline. Each video clip is treated as a geometry-consistent target and paired with a distorted counterpart synthesized by a pretrained image-to-video inpainting model using trajectory-adaptive keyframes. VGC learns to map the distorted input to the geometry-consistent target without modifying the source generator, and uses optical flow as an auxiliary cue to help disentangle camera motion from object motion. Experiments on pose-conditioned and prompt-driven benchmarks show that VGC improves geometric consistency across multiple source generators, and scaling to a larger backbone further extends the framework toward more general video refinement.

</details>


### [104] [The Collection of a Real-World, Large-Scale Dataset for Automated Skill Evaluation in Central Line Dressing Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23789)
*Ummey Tanin,Shannan K. Hamlin,Hsin-Mei Chen,Peizhu,Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文公开了一个大规模中心静脉导管换药（CLDC）技能评估视频数据集：365段注册护士考核录像，附带17个子任务级时序标注与临床表现评分，并给出时空深度学习基准实验，验证了视频自动技能评估的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLABSI是可预防的院内感染，规范执行CLDC是关键；但现有能力评估依赖临床教育者人工观察，耗时、难规模化、一致性与细粒度不足。AI辅助评估需要临床级大规模数据，而此类数据长期缺失。

Method: 在Houston Methodist Hospital 2026年度能力考核中采集365段注册护士操作视频；每段视频配子任务级时序标注与临床表现评估，覆盖17个子任务；详述采集与整理流程（录制配置预试、标注协议设计、采集后处理）；并用时空深度学习模型开展基准实验。

Result: 构建了包含时序标注与评分的CLDC技能评估数据集，并通过时空深度模型基准实验初步证实了基于视频的技能评估可行性。

Conclusion: 该数据集为自动化临床技能评估、反馈生成与AI辅助护理培训研究提供基础，并支持客观、可复现、可规模化的评估方向。

Abstract: Central line-associated bloodstream infections (CLABSIs) remain a major source of preventable morbidity and mortality in healthcare settings. Proper execution of the central line dressing change (CLDC) procedure is essential for infection prevention, yet current approaches to competency assessment rely primarily on manual ob- servation by clinical educators, making evaluation time-intensive, difficult to scale, and limited in consistency and granularity. These challenges highlight the need for AI-assisted skill evaluation methods that can support more objective, reproducible, and scalable assessment of clinical performance. However, progress in this area has been constrained by the lack of broad-scale, clinically grounded datasets for developing and evaluating such methods. In this work, we present the collection process of a large-scale video dataset for CLDC skill evaluation, comprising 365 recordings of registered nurses collected during the 2026 annual competency exam at Houston Methodist Hospital. Each video is paired with subtask-level temporal annotations and clinical performance assessments spanning 17 subtasks of the procedure. We describe the data collection and curation pipeline, including pilot testing of record- ing configurations, annotation protocol design, and post-collection processing. We further establish the feasibility of video-based skill assessment through benchmark experiments with spatiotemporal deep learning models. This dataset provides a foundation for future research on automated clinical skill assessment, feedback generation, and AI-assisted training in nursing education.

</details>


### [105] [An Efficient Approach to Beetle Damage Detection of Banana Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23661)
*Md. Mehedi Hasan Naeem,Ishtiyak Ahmmad Araf,Md. Arefin Haque Mahir,Tushar Kanti Saha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出基于YOLOv8n的轻量级框架，实现香蕉果实实例级定位与表面健康/不健康二分类，用于替代主观、难扩展的人工目检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 香蕉果实表面疤痕损伤（文中以"beetle damage"操作性表述）会降低商品价值，而人工检查主观性强且难以规模化，需要自动化、可部署的检测方案。

Method: 自建孟加拉本地市场图像数据集（2,473张图像、5,827个标注实例），采用80/20划分（1,982训练/491测试）；以预训练YOLOv8n在640×640分辨率、50 epochs、batch size 4下训练，并与YOLOv8s对比。

Result: 测试集上精度81.36%、召回74.30%、F1 77.67%、mAP@0.50 87.04%、mAP@0.50:0.95 59.33%；YOLOv8s的F1与mAP略低但模型体积显著更大；YOLOv8n权重5.96 MB，NVIDIA T4上推理6.54 ms/图。

Conclusion: YOLOv8n可作为实例级香蕉表面检测的紧凑基线，适用于所研究的采集条件；模型并不识别具体甲虫物种或生物学成因。

Abstract: Visible surface damage of banana fruits by Basilepta subcostata (the banana fruit scarring beetle) can reduce their market value. Here, manual inspection of Banana fruits is subjective and difficult to scale. This study presents a lightweight framework based on YOLOv8n deep learning model for locating individual bananas and classifying their visible surface condition as healthy or unhealthy. The term beetle damage in the paper title is used operationally for the locally observed scar-like surface condition; the model does not identify a specific beetle species or biological cause. A custom dataset of 2,473 images containing 5,827 annotated banana instances was prepared from local-market imagery in Bangladesh. The reported experiment used an 80/20 protocol with 1,982 training images and 491 test images. A pretrained YOLOv8n model was trained at 640x640 resolution for 50 epochs with a batch size of 4. On the test partition, it achieved 81.36% precision, 74.30% recall, 77.67% F1-score, 87.04% mAP@0.50 and 59.33% mAP@0.50:0.95. A YOLOv8s comparison gave slightly lower F1 and mAP while using a substantially larger checkpoint. The YOLOv8n checkpoint occupied 5.96 MB and required 6.54 ms/image for inference on an NVIDIA Tesla T4. The results support YOLOv8n as a compact baseline for instance-level banana surface inspection within the studied acquisition conditions.

</details>


### [106] [FlexPart: Text-Controlled Semantics-Aware 3D Part Segmentation at Flexible Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23659)
*Yiqun Zhao,Jiachen Xu,Yihao Zhi,Yunfei Zhao,Zibo Zhao,Xintong Han,Chunchao Guo,Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLEXPART：文本控制的统一3D网格分割框架，通过多粒度数据构建与整体控制提示实现"所求即所得"的分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D资产存在多种有效部件分解，语义感知分割需同时体现用户期望的粒度和想要提取的具体部件。现实痛点：缺乏多粒度训练数据；缺乏整体化控制机制。

Method: 1) 整体控制提示：由语义部件名+位置子句+形状子句构成；2) 用自动化VLM流水线构建大规模多粒度划分数据集及对应提示；3) 模型在原生3D空间生成视角无关的部件身份场，直接为网格面分配离散部件ID；4) 采用随机背景丢弃训练，迫使模型依赖语义条件而非几何先验；5) 推理时VLM规划器从2D渲染自动补全描述子句，用户仅需用日常词汇命名部件。

Result: 规划器补全提示下，mIoU较最强基线提升15%（即使该基线获得真值引导）；在自建FLEXPARTBENCH上各粒度均领先，平均mIoU提升22%；仅提供部件名时，选择性分割IoU较最强点击驱动基线提升35%。

Conclusion: 统一框架验证了"文本控制+多粒度数据"可有效实现灵活、高精度的3D网格部件分割，且降低用户输入成本。

Abstract: A 3D asset admits multiple valid part decompositions, while semantics-aware segmentation should reflect both the desired granularity and the parts a user wants to extract. Ideally, a practical segmentation
  model should strive for a simple principle: what you ask for is what you get. However, realizing this goal is hindered by two core obstacles: the lack of multi-granularity training data and the absence of a
  holistic control mechanism. We propose FLEXPART, a unified framework for text-controlled 3D mesh segmentation. To establish flexible control, we introduce a holistic control prompt comprising semantic part
  names with location and shape clauses. To support this mechanism, we utilize an automated Vision-Language Model (VLM) pipeline to construct a large-scale dataset of multi-granularity partitions and their
  corresponding prompts. Driven by this data, FLEXPART generates a view-independent part-identity field in native 3D space, directly assigning discrete part IDs to mesh faces. To compel the model to respect
  semantic conditions rather than rely on geometric priors, we train the model using randomized background dropping. Furthermore, an inference-time VLM planner can automatically complete descriptive clauses
  from 2D renderings, allowing users to simply name the desired parts in ordinary words. FLEXPART with planner-completed prompts outperforms the strongest evaluated baseline by 15% in mIoU, even when that
  baseline receives ground-truth guidance. On our held-out FLEXPARTBENCH, FLEXPART leads at each granularity and improves average mIoU by 22% over the strongest baseline. With only the requested part names
  supplied by the user, it also improves IoU by 35% over the strongest click-driven baseline on selective segmentation.

</details>


### [107] [Stereo LWIR-RGB Automatic Labeling for Low-Light Camera-Based VRU Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23621)
*Dimitrios Bouzoulas,Pyry Jäppinen,Pejman Habibiroudkenar,Eerik Alamikkotervo,Risto Ojala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于LWIR-RGB双目立体设置的自动化低光照VRU标注流水线，利用LWIR（热成像）作为标签源，无需专用对齐硬件即可生成高质量低光照VRU标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光照是VRU碰撞高发时段，但低光照检测进展受限于大规模数据集缺失和自动标注方法不足；现有自动标注多以RGB检测为标签源，无法用于低光照场景。

Method: 使用LWIR-RGB图像对，从LWIR中提取标签（VRU因体温与背景区分明显）；相较此前依赖复杂硬件对齐的方案，改用LWIR-RGB双目立体设置完成模态对齐，降低硬件复杂度与成本，提升可复现性；在芬兰首都地区采集的数据集上进行端到端评测。

Result: 在人工标注数据集上测试，基于所生成标签训练的模型在F1、mAP@50和LAMR指标上均优于现成基线及在手工标注子集上微调的模型，且结论对两种不同的LWIR检测器均成立。

Conclusion: 低光照VRU自动标注无需专用对齐硬件即可实现，兼具可及性与有效性，为低光照VRU检测的大规模数据构建提供了可行路径。

Abstract: VRU detection in low-light conditions is a key component in ensuring VRU safety during the most collision prone time of the day. Currently, advances in low-light detection are limited due to the lack of large scale datasets and automated labeling methods. Most autolabeling methods use RGB detections as their labels source, which cannot be replicated in low-light conditions. To fill this gap in this paper we present an automated pipeline for low-light VRU labeling. We use LWIR-RGB image pairs and source our labels from LWIR as VRUs differentiate from the background due to their body temperature. Unlike our previous work that relied on a complex hardware setup to align the two modalities, we perform this experiment using a LWIR-RGB stereo setup, lowering hardware complexity and cost, making the pipeline easier to reproduce. We evaluate method end-to-end on a dataset collected in the capital region of Finland. Models trained on our generated labels beat both off-the-shelf baselines and models fine tuned on a hand labeled subset on F1, mAP@50 and LAMR metrics when tested on a manually labeled dataset, and the result holds for two different LWIR detectors. This shows that low-light VRU autolabeling can be made both accessible and effective without specialized alignment hardware.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [The Model Knows Better: Defending Medical Multi-Agent Systems Against Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22594)
*Fardin Ahsan Sakib,Ziwei Zhu,Ozlem Uzuner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Medical multi-agent LLM clinical decision support systems are vulnerable to prompt injection (up to 61% success), and a training-free defense that verifies clinical judgments against patient findings reduces attack success to 4-7% on MedQA while preserving accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Medical multi-agent systems where LLM agents discuss a case are increasingly used for clinical decision support, but prompt injection can manipulate them into wrong decisions. How injection affects these systems and how to protect them remains unclear.

Method: Train-free defense that, following clinical practice, separates patient findings from clinical judgments (diagnoses, recommendations, instructions) and checks each judgment against the findings alone, so the final decision rests only on supported judgments. Motivated by the observation that a model which followed an injection usually rejects it when asked to check the injected text against the patient's findings.

Result: Across two systems, two model families and three benchmarks, a single injected passage succeeds in up to 61% of cases; collaboration does not consistently protect, and existing defenses help only partly, missing injections that resemble ordinary clinical text. The proposed defense outperforms all baselines on every benchmark, lowering attack success on MedQA to 4-7% while maintaining accuracy on unattacked cases, and remains effective with a medical model and against defense-aware and adaptive attacks.

Conclusion: Prompt injection is a serious threat to medical multi-agent decision support, and verifying the clinical judgments behind a decision is an effective, training-free defense robust to adaptive attacks.

Abstract: Medical multi-agent systems, in which LLM agents discuss a case, are increasingly studied for clinical decision support. Prompt injection, text that an attacker adds to a model's input, can manipulate LLMs into wrong decisions. How it affects these systems, and how to protect them, remains unclear. Across two systems, two model families and three benchmarks, a single injected passage succeeds in up to 61% of cases, and collaboration does not consistently protect against it. Existing defenses help only partly: they miss injections that look like ordinary clinical text. Yet when asked to check the injected text against the patient's findings, a model that followed the injection usually rejects it. We therefore propose a training-free defense that verifies the clinical judgments behind the system's decision. Following clinical practice, it separates the patient's findings from clinical judgments, such as diagnoses, recommendations and instructions, and checks each judgment against the findings alone, so the decision rests only on supported judgments. Our defense outperforms all baselines on every benchmark, lowering attack success on MedQA to 4-7% while maintaining accuracy on cases without an attack, and remains effective with a medical model and against defense-aware and adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [109] [Mitigating Demographic Disparities in Explanation-Human Alignment via Rationale Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22586)
*Suryansh Talukdar,Taha Sarwat,Jonathan Rusert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Plausibility-EFG指标，度量仇恨言论检测模型生成的解释在不同人口群体间可信度（解释合理性）的最大差异，发现对抗训练（ADV-RoBERTa）在提升分类性能与解释-人工理由对齐的同时可降低解释差异，且预测层公平与解释层公平是相关但不同的目标，并提出Fair-REF框架以缓解解释差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分类性能并不保证模型对不同人口群体给出同等合理的解释，即解释层面可能存在被忽视的群体间不公平；已有研究多关注预测层公平性，而解释公平性尚缺度量与缓解手段。

Method: 基于HateXplain数据集，将模型生成的token归因（Integrated Gradients与SHAP）与人工标注rationales对比，提出Plausibility-EFG指标，以各人口群体间解释合理性的最大差值量化解释不公平；评估BERT、RoBERTa与对抗训练的ADV-RoBERTa，并开展公平性-性能权衡分析，提出Fair-REF框架。

Result: ADV-RoBERTa在提升分类性能与解释-人工理由对齐度的同时，降低了群体间解释差异；该结论在两种归因方法下均成立，且在训练/验证/测试划分上保持一致。仅减少预测层偏置并不能消除解释差异。更强的对抗正则可进一步降低Plausibility-EFG，但可能导致macro-F1小幅下降。

Conclusion: 解释公平性是仇恨言论审核中的重要目标，与预测公平相关但相异；Fair-REF框架可有效减少模型解释在不同人口群体间的差异，且解释公平与性能之间存在可调节的权衡。

Abstract: We study explanation fairness in hate speech detection. High classification performance does not always mean that a model produces equally plausible explanations for different demographic groups. Using the HateXplain dataset, we introduce Plausibility-EFG, a metric that measures the largest difference in explanation plausibility across demographic groups by comparing model-generated token attributions with human-annotated rationales. We evaluate BERT, RoBERTa, and ADV-RoBERTa, an adversarially trained model, using Integrated Gradients and SHAP. Our results show that ADV-RoBERTa improves classification performance and alignment between explanations and human rationales while reducing explanation disparities across demographic groups. These improvements are observed with both attribution methods and remain consistent across training, validation, and test splits. We also show that reducing prediction-level bias alone does not eliminate disparities in explanations, suggesting that predictive fairness and explanation fairness are related but distinct objectives. Finally, our fairness-performance analysis shows that stronger adversarial regularization can further reduce Plausibility-EFG, although it may cause a small decrease in macro-F1. Overall, this work presents explanation fairness as an important objective in hate speech moderation and introduces Fair-REF, a framework designed to reduce disparities in model explanations across demographic groups.

</details>


### [110] [alkhder at AlexandriaX-2026: Context-Aware English-to-Dialectal Arabic Dialogue Translation with QLoRA-Fine-Tuned NileChat-3B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22525)
*Hasan Alkhder,Maher Pasa,Maria Hanini,Amro Najjar,Ahmet Zengin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 团队 Alkhder 参加 AlexandriaX-2026 子任务1（英阿13种方言对话翻译），用 QLoRA 微调 NileChat-3B-Base，单一适配器覆盖全部方言，并以目标国别、方言、领域、参与者角色、说话人-听话人性别方向及前文语境为条件。推理时自回归翻译、以自身前序输出作为上下文，不使用测试集金标准目标端信息。受限赛道仅用 Alexandria 数据，训练约1小时、私有测试集翻译不足2小时，在两条赛道均取得国别宏平均 spBLEU 20.36、chrF++ 36.64。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英语到多国阿拉伯方言的对话级翻译面临方言多样、语境依赖强、算力与数据受限等问题；本文旨在受限条件下（仅用官方数据、低成本训练）构建一个统一模型完成13种方言的对话轮翻译。

Method: 以 NileChat-3B-Base 为底座，在 Alexandria 平行对话语料上用 QLoRA 微调，训练一整套覆盖所有方言的适配器权重；每轮翻译以目标国家与方言、领域、参与者角色、说话人-听话人性别方向以及前序对话上下文为条件。推理按对话自回归进行，将模型自身先前输出回灌为上下文，不使用任何金标准目标端测试信息。

Result: 受限赛道（仅 Alexandria 数据）训练约1小时，私有测试集翻译耗时不足2小时；在受限与开放两条赛道上均取得国别宏平均 spBLEU 20.36、chrF++ 36.64。

Conclusion: 结果表明，单一参数高效适配器配合方言/国别等元数据条件与自回归上下文回灌，可在极低训练与推理成本下为13种阿拉伯方言提供统一、可复现的对话翻译基线；未来可探索方言专用适配或数据增强以进一步提升质量。

Abstract: This paper describes team Alkhder's submission to Subtask 1 of the AlexandriaX-2026 shared task, which asks systems to translate English dialogue turns into thirteen Arabic dialects. We fine-tune NileChat-3B-Base with QLoRA on the Alexandria parallel dialogue corpus, training one set of adapter weights for all dialects. Each turn is translated conditioned on the target country and dialect, the domain, the participant roles, the speaker-addressee gender direction, and the preceding dialogue context. At inference, conversations are translated autoregressively and the model is fed its own previous outputs back as context without using any gold target-side test information. Under the constrained track, using only Alexandria data, it trains in about one hour and translates the private test set in under two hours. The system reaches a country-macro-averaged spBLEU of 20.36 and chrF++ of 36.64 on both tracks.

</details>


### [111] [Embeddings Are Overpriced? A Frozen 16 MB Vocabulary for Small and Ternary Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22428)
*Sai Kiran Bathula*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文用一份512比特码、16 MB、无训练参数的冻结嵌入表替换小语言模型的常规可学习嵌入表，发现学习到的表只换来0.20 nats验证损失，却在多数下游任务上不占优；配合三值BitNet b1.58主干可将整模型压到46 MB，在笔记本CPU上以1/7内存实现5–6倍于8-bit GGUF的生成速度，且对罕见词与新词扩展显著更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型中词表嵌入是单一最大组成部分（Gemma 3 270M中168M/268M参数），且量化通常保留嵌入为16-bit，使其在模型其余部分压缩后仍占据文件主体。作者要探究该嵌入表究竟"买"到了什么，并探索用无参数冻结码本替代的可行性与代价。

Method: 构建一张冻结的512-bit码表（16 MB、无可训练参数），通过主成分投影与符号二值化从预训练嵌入一次性导出；在相同2.05B token上分别在有/无该表的条件下从头训练相同Gemma结构主干；进一步将主干替换为三值BitNet b1.58，并评测验证损失、LAMBADA/HellaSwag/PIQA/ARC-Easy、罕见词表现、训练后新增1万词的扩展性，以及在笔记本CPU上与llama.cpp 8-bit GGUF的速度/内存对比。

Result: 学习嵌入表以2.6倍可训练参数换来0.20 nats验证损失优势，但LAMBADA、HellaSwag、PIQA上冻结表模型持平或更好，仅ARC-Easy偏向学习表。三值BitNet b1.58版整模型为46 MB文件，在LAMBADA与PIQA上与浮点模型持平，笔记本CPU上以62 MB内存生成615–740 tokens/s，速度为8-bit GGUF的5–6倍、内存为其1/7。冻结表在罕见词上领先1.7–2.3 nats，训练后接受1万新词仅3%代价，而学习表崩溃43%。全部权重、数据与评测答案均开源。

Conclusion: 在小模型中，大参数量的可学习嵌入表带来的收益有限且主要集中在验证损失与部分任务，而冻结二值码本在主流下游任务上不劣、在极端压缩与部署效率、罕见词鲁棒性及词表可扩展性上明显更优，说明该参数预算的性价比应被重新审视。

Abstract: In a small language model the vocabulary is the single largest part. In Gemma 3 270M the embedding table holds 168M of 268M parameters, and because quantisation methods leave the embedding in 16-bit, it is also most of the file once the rest of the model is compressed. We ask what that table actually buys. We replace it with one frozen table of 512-bit codes, 16 MB, with no trainable parameters, derived once from a pretrained embedding by principal-component projection and sign binarisation, and we train the same Gemma-shaped body from scratch on the same 2.05B tokens with and without it. The learned table buys 0.20 nats of validation loss for 2.6 times the trainable parameters, but on LAMBADA, HellaSwag and PIQA the frozen-table model is better or tied; only ARC-Easy favours the learned table. With a ternary BitNet b1.58 body the whole model is a 46 MB file, still ties the float model on LAMBADA and PIQA, and on a laptop CPU generates 615 to 740 tokens per second in 62 MB of memory, five to six times the speed of the float model's 8-bit GGUF in llama.cpp at a seventh of the memory. The frozen table wins by 1.7 to 2.3 nats on rare words, accepts 10,000 new words after training with a 3% cost where the learned table collapses by 43%, and all of it is released with the weights, the data and every evaluation answer.

</details>


### [112] [Disentangling Language Effects on Tool Admission and Post-Retrieval Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23093)
*Aditya Nawal,Manit Baser,Mohan Gurusamy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示跨语言检索场景下的安全评估缺口，提出SLIP（跨语言加工中的安全损失）现象：非英语有害请求显著提高工具准入率，且要求用页面语言作答会提升已准入调用中的危害性，呼吁多语言安全评估分别度量工具准入与响应危害性，并区分拒答与能力失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地跨语言使用检索工具，但安全评估仍以英语为中心；语言究竟影响工具准入、检索后生成，还是两者兼有，尚不清楚。现有评估常用单一危害性标签，无法区分真实安全拒答、以语言困难为由的推诿和不可用回答。

Method: 在受控URL检索设置中，独立改变请求语言、检索页面语言与所需回答语言，覆盖十个模型、七种语言；并在自主检索设置中对四个模型进行验证。另用自动化审计区分拒答、推诿与能力失败。

Result: 非英语有害请求使平均工具准入率从46.9%升至67.4%，且在每个模型上均为正变化；自主检索设置下四个模型均保持该模式。要求以页面语言作答使匹配工具调用上的汇总危害性从50.3%升至57.4%（四个闭源模型，主要为中文和印地语）。仅翻译页面在八个可比推理设置模型中未带来一致的危害上升。

Conclusion: 语言主要作用于工具准入阶段，回答语言要求进一步放大生成侧风险。多语言安全评估应分别测量工具准入与响应危害性，并区分安全拒答与能力失败，避免以单一危害性标签误判安全性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly use retrieval tools across languages, yet their safety evaluation remains English-centric. It remains unclear whether language affects tool admission, post-retrieval generation, or both. We independently vary request, retrieved-page, and required response languages in a controlled URL-retrieval setting across ten models and seven languages. We identify Safety Loss through Interlingual Processing (SLIP). Non-English harmful requests increase mean tool admission from 46.9% to 67.4%, with a positive mean change in every model. This pattern also persists in an autonomous retrieval setup across all four models tested in this setting. Requiring answers in the page language increases pooled harmfulness on matched tool calls from 50.3% to 57.4% across four closed-weight models, mainly in Chinese and Hindi. Translating pages alone produces no consistent harm increase in eight models with comparable inference settings. Automated audits show that a non-harmful response may be a genuine safety refusal, a deflection citing language difficulties, or an unusable answer. Harmfulness labels alone cannot distinguish these outcomes. Multilingual safety evaluations should therefore measure tool admission and response harmfulness separately and distinguish refusal from capability failure.

</details>


### [113] [ParetoChat: An LLM-Based Interface for Interactive Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23062)
*Gokhan Ceyhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 ParetoChat：基于 LLM 的多智能体交互式多目标优化界面，将自然语言偏好转为标量化参数并迭代反馈，在背包/集合划分算例上优于 DESDEO 中的参考点与 NIMBUS 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中，决策者需把定性偏好翻译成数值权重、优先级或参考点，这些参数难以设定和解释，构成人机交互瓶颈。

Method: 构建多智能体架构，分离输入解析、MCDM 专家知识、解的呈现与反馈处理四个模块；智能体将自然语言转为结构化标量化参数，调用优化系统、展示候选解并迭代征求反馈；采用基于 LLM 的决策者模拟代理，与 DESDEO 中参考点法、NIMBUS 法进行基准比较，并应用于历史快递订单分配实例。

Result: 在多目标二元背包与集合划分算例上，ParetoChat 能高效引导搜索至契合决策者偏好的解；快递订单分配案例显示用户可通过自然语言探索目标极值与中间权衡。

Conclusion: LLM 智能体可作为业务相关方与优化工具之间实用的交互层；但需注意其评估依赖 LLM 模拟决策者，真实用户验证仍有待补充。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization models are often deployed in settings where decision makers (DMs) must balance some conflicting criteria. In practice, however, stakeholders must translate their qualitative preferences between different criteria into numerical weights, priorities, or reference points required by optimization methods, which can be difficult to specify and interpret. This paper proposes \emph{ParetoChat}, an LLM (large language model)-based expert-agent interface for interactive multi-objective optimization. The agent translates natural-language user input into structured scalarization parameters, invokes an optimization system, presents candidate solutions, and iteratively elicits feedback. We describe a multi-agent architecture that separates input processing, multi-criteria decision-making (MCDM) expertise, solution presentation, and feedback processing. We then present a simulation study using an LLM-based DM agent and benchmark ParetoChat against interactive methods implemented in DESDEO, including reference-point and NIMBUS methods. The computational experiments on multi-objective binary knapsack and set-partitioning instances show that ParetoChat can guide the search efficiently toward solutions that match the DM's preferences. An application to historical courier-order assignment instances illustrates how users can explore objective extremes and intermediate trade-offs through natural-language requests. The results indicate that LLM agents may provide a practical interaction layer between business stakeholders and optimization tools.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond Surface Cues: Robust LLM-Generated Korean Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22940)
*SungHo Kim,SangKeun Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出韩语LLM文本检测基准KoLing与可解释检测器KoLingProbe，抑制表层捷径并区分"生成"与"改写"两类检测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM文本检测研究高度以英语为中心，韩语严重不足；且现有韩语检测评估依赖表层线索（如长度、标点等捷径），导致性能被高估。

Method: 构建KoLing：5.09万条偏差受控的对抗性基准，压制表层捷径、保留语义内容，并分别评估LLM生成文本与LLM改写文本的检测；提出KoLingProbe：基于理论语言学、建模韩语特有属性、跨多个语言层级的可解释检测器。

Result: KoLingProbe在所有检测任务上显著优于强基线，并对对抗攻击保持稳健；分析表明多层级互补语言特征的整合同时提升性能与可解释性。

Conclusion: 韩语LLM文本检测需要偏差受控的基准与语言学驱动的检测方法；多层级韩语语言特征的融合是实现高性能与可解释性的有效路径。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) generate increasingly fluent text, the need for reliable detection of LLM-generated content has grown substantially. However, existing research remains heavily English-centric, leaving Korean largely underexplored. Moreover, current evaluations of Korean text detection often rely on superficial cues, leading to overly optimistic estimates of detection performance. To address these limitations, we introduce \textsc{KoLing}, a bias-controlled adversarial benchmark comprising 50.9k instances that suppresses superficial shortcuts while preserving semantic content and separately evaluates LLM-generated and LLM-modified text detection. We further propose KoLingProbe, an interpretable detector grounded in theoretical linguistics that models Korean-specific properties across multiple linguistic levels. Experiments show that KoLingProbe substantially outperforms strong baselines on all detection tasks while remaining robust to adversarial attacks. Our analyses further reveal that integrating complementary linguistic features across multiple levels yields both improved performance and interpretability.

</details>


### [115] [Competence-Based Evaluation of Logical Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22894)
*Jize Jiang,Chengxiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出“能力评估”（CBE）范式，通过参数化生成器在固定所需能力下缩放复杂度、并用答案保持的多种表述检验正确性，以诊断LLM推理的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理基准只报告单一准确率数字，属于“基于表现”的评估，难以反映模型在推理上的泛化能力；LLM在语言变化下存在不一致性，亟需能精确衡量推理能力的评估方法。

Method: 提出competence-based evaluation (CBE)：用参数化生成器在保持所需能力不变的前提下缩放问题复杂度，并采用匹配的、答案保持的不同表述形式检验正确性；在基础排序任务上实例化CBE，改变输入规模并变换同一排序规则的表述，评测六个GPT与Qwen模型，并考察Chain-of-thought、GRPO与监督LoRA的作用。

Result: 在有界复杂度范围内模型表现尚可，但即便强模型也未能通过所测问题族上的能力条件；CoT与GRPO能延伸强性能表现却未解决全部失败；监督LoRA的留出迁移很小。

Conclusion: CBE可提供诊断性能力画像与可随未来模型扩展的评估协议，弥补单点准确率评估对推理泛化刻画不足的问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have brought impressive performance across diverse tasks; however, shortcomings remain in logical reasoning, including inconsistencies under linguistic variation. To improve the reasoning capacity of LLMs, we must be able to accurately measure their reasoning capacity. Current benchmarks primarily report single accuracy numbers on selected reasoning tasks. Such a performance-based evaluation methodology says little about a model's generalization capacity in reasoning. To address this limitation, we argue for a different evaluation paradigm, called competence-based evaluation (CBE), which tests whether models reliably apply the same competence across a family of problems. A parameterized generator scales complexity while keeping the required competence fixed, and matched answer-preserving presentations test correctness beyond one formulation. We instantiate CBE on elementary ordering tasks, scaling input size while varying presentations of the same ordering rule. Across six GPT and Qwen models, performance is reasonably good over bounded complexity ranges, but even strong models fail the competence condition across the tested family. Chain-of-thought and GRPO extend strong performance without resolving all tested failures; supervised LoRA shows little held-out transfer. CBE provides diagnostic profiles and an evaluation protocol that can grow with future models.

</details>


### [116] [Self-Evolving Coding Agents via Harness-Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22791)
*Zhepei Wei,Wenlin Yao,Baolin Peng,Danqing Wang,Qianhui Wu,Hao Cheng,Yu Meng,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Self-HPO,一种交替优化 harness 与 policy 的递归自改进框架,由策略自身担任 harness 提议者,在三个代码基准上显著超越单组件基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 代码智能体的性能由策略模型与中介其与环境交互的 harness 共同决定,二者相互强化、需协同演化。仅优化其一为次优:为某检查点调好的 harness 未必针对更新后策略的失效模式,而策略学习又受限于固定 harness 所诱发的轨迹。

Method: Self-HPO 采用递归交替优化:每轮中当前策略先分析自身失败并提出新 harness 候选(H-step);随后在选定 harness 下用强化学习更新策略(P-step);训练中识别出的困难任务构成下一轮 harness 搜索集,使 harness 优化始终对准策略演化的失效模式。

Result: 在三个代码基准上稳定优于 harness-only 与 policy-only 基线,Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 平均解决率由 57.8% 提升至 64.8%,且增益可泛化至分布外场景;两个优化组件分别与各自基座配对时仍保留增益,联合增益超过各自增益之和;同时提升 Pass@1 可靠性并扩展 Pass@k 能力前沿;以更强外部模型(如 Opus 4.8)作 harness 提议者可获得进一步提升。

Conclusion: harness 与 policy 的协同共演化优于单组件优化,且二者存在正向协同效应;框架具备良好的泛化性与可扩展性,可借助更强提议模型进一步增益。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have shown promising potential in coding tasks, and their performance is jointly determined by the policy model and the harness that mediates their interaction with the environment. Indeed, harness and policy are mutually reinforcing and evolving only one component is suboptimal---a harness tuned for one checkpoint may not target the failure modes of an updated policy, while policy learning remains limited to the trajectories elicited by a fixed harness. In light of this, we propose Self-HPO, a recursive self-improving framework for coding agents that alternates harness and policy optimization, using the evolving policy as its own harness proposer. Specifically, in each round, the current policy first analyzes its own failures and proposes new harness candidates (H-step). The policy is then updated through reinforcement learning (RL) under the selected harness (P-step), and hard tasks identified during training form the next harness search set, keeping harness optimization targeted to the policy's evolving failure modes. Across three coding benchmarks, Self-HPO consistently outperforms harness-only and policy-only baselines, improving Qwen3.5-35B-A3B's average resolve rate from 57.8% to 64.8%, with gains generalizing to out-of-distribution settings. Further analysis shows that both optimized components retain gains even when paired with their base counterparts, while their joint gain exceeds the sum of their individual gains. Self-HPO also improves reliability (Pass@1) and expands the capability frontier (Pass@k) over the base agent. Additionally, we show that with a stronger external model (e.g., Opus 4.8) as the harness proposer, the same framework yields a further performance boost.

</details>


### [117] [Trajectory-Guided Steering: Mitigating Spurious Feature Bias in Clinical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22618)
*Fardin Ahsan Sakib,Ziwei Zhu,Ozlem Uzuner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出轨迹引导干预（TGS），仅凭模型在各层间的预测轨迹逐文档决定是否纠正及纠正方向，无需反事实数据与虚假特征先验，可显著修复临床分类错误并降低对性别虚假特征的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在临床分类中依赖虚假特征（如单个性别词替换可改变多达53.5%的性别无关诊断）。现有推理时纠正方法要么需要围绕特定虚假特征构建反事实数据，要么行为不可预测、效果不稳定。

Method: 观察到模型对答案偏好在层间的演化轨迹（prediction trajectory）可指示某文档的纠正是否有益。据此提出Trajectory-Guided Steering (TGS)：仅依据轨迹本身判断是否纠正及纠正方向，不使用反事实数据，也不需已知虚假特征，实现逐文档的自适应选择。

Result: 在i2b2的16个临床分类任务上，TGS修复17.7%的错误，仅破坏1.3%的正确预测，在所有测试超参数下均为净正收益（基线均净负或接近零）；性别替换不一致率显著下降（高血压53.5%→38.0%，糖尿病35.1%→21.5%）；对全部文档统一纠正会使正确正例的破坏率从0.1%升至6.4%，证明逐文档选择必要；方法可迁移至3个模型家族及3个其他临床与生物医学数据集。

Conclusion: 预测轨迹是判断纠正有效性的可靠信号；TGS在无虚假特征先验条件下实现稳健的净正纠正，既修复错误又降低虚假特征依赖，且具备跨模型、跨数据集的泛化性。

Abstract: Large language models rely on spurious features in clinical classification: swapping a single gender token in clinical notes alters gender-irrelevant diagnoses in up to 53.5% of cases. Existing inference-time corrections either need counterfactual data built around the specific spurious feature or behave unpredictably. We observe that how a model's preference between answers evolves across layers, its prediction trajectory, indicates for each document whether a correction is likely to help. We propose Trajectory-Guided Steering (TGS), which decides from the trajectory alone whether to correct each prediction and in which direction, with no counterfactual data and no knowledge of the spurious feature. On 16 clinical classification tasks from i2b2, TGS fixes 17.7% of errors while breaking only 1.3% of correct predictions and stays net-positive across all tested hyperparameter settings; all baselines are net-negative or near zero. The same corrections reduce reliance on the spurious feature: on the most affected diseases, gender-swap inconsistency falls sharply (hypertension 53.5% -> 38.0%, diabetes 35.1% -> 21.5%). Applying the correction to every document instead raises breaks on correct positive predictions from 0.1% to 6.4%, so per-document selection is essential. TGS also transfers to three model families and three other clinical and biomedical datasets.

</details>


### [118] [Broad and Efficient PII Detection with Unified Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22597)
*Andy Hsu,Konstantin Berlin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个141M参数的编码器型PII检测器，基于77个公开数据集、112万条经统一重标注的样本训练，覆盖12类结构化PII；在低FPR工作点上以更高吞吐和更低显存取得领先召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PII检测器趋于使用复杂架构推高部署成本，且多在少量合成数据上微调、覆盖不足；直接合并公开数据集又因分类体系不同、标签对“何为检测”判定不一致，导致训练与评估信号矛盾。因此需要兼顾高召回、低误报、低部署成本的实用方案。

Method: 采用编码器架构（141M参数）；整合77个公开数据集共1.12M样本；按“广义上下文检测”的细化定义将12类结构化PII统一至公共schema并重标注，以消除跨数据集标签冲突；在低FPR工作点下于AI4Privacy与Nemotron-PII基准评测，与更大基线对比。

Result: 在14组对比中，按本文标签有11组、按原始标签有9组取得最高召回，优于更大基线；主要增量覆盖包括多语种披露信息与URL中嵌入的IP地址；吞吐量为基线的2-10倍，峰值GPU显存减少94-97%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，统一重标注的大规模数据整合配合适度规模编码器，即可在显著更低成本下实现领先的PII检测性能，架构复杂度并非性能的必要条件。

Abstract: Personally Identifiable Information (PII) detection models must efficiently find identifiers across diverse contexts while maintaining a low false-positive rate (FPR) on high-volume traffic. Recent detectors increasingly rely on complex architectures that raise deployment cost, yet many are fine-tuned on only a few synthetic datasets that limit their coverage. Combining public datasets is challenging because their taxonomies differ, and even after categories are mapped to a common schema, labels can disagree on what counts as a detection, producing inconsistent training and evaluation signals. To address these problems, we present a practical 141M-parameter encoder-based detector trained on 1.12 million examples from 77 public datasets, relabeled under a detailed definition of broad contextual detection for 12 structured PII categories. Evaluated on the AI4Privacy and Nemotron-PII benchmarks at a low-FPR operating point, our model achieves the highest recall against larger baselines in 11 of 14 comparisons under our labels and 9 of 14 under the original labels. Its largest recall gains include useful additional coverage, such as multilingual disclosures and IP addresses embedded in URLs. It achieves this with 2-10x higher throughput and 94-97% less peak GPU memory than the baselines, delivering leading detection performance at substantially lower cost.

</details>


### [119] [Analyzing Occupational Gender Bias across the LLM Training Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23587)
*Manthan Gadhia,Alexander Hoyle,Karolina Stańczak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文追踪OLMo 3在完整后训练流程中职业性别偏见的演变，发现"后训练减少偏见"的假设并不成立，且模型自身的性别推断呈现稳定的女性偏好偏斜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练被普遍认为能削弱预训练继承的社会偏见，但直接证据仍然混杂；作者希望在完全开放模型的完整流水线上，逐阶段、可溯源地检验这一假设。

Method: 以OLMo 3全开放模型族为对象，对每个训练检查点用合成人物画像（给定职业与教育）生成开放式叙事，设置两种条件：明确给定性别与模型自行推断性别；比较叙事语义多样性、性别归因几率，并与美国劳动力人口统计对比；同时对SFT语料做来源分析以追踪偏见来源。

Result: 给定性别时，后训练提升了男性编码画像的语义多样性，女性编码画像的增益在SFT后较小、DPO与RLVR后不显著；模型自行推断性别时，两类画像受后训练影响相近。模型自身性别假设相对美国劳动力人口统计呈稳定女性偏好偏斜（重男性主导职业除外），且每个后训练阶段都单调提高赋予女性标签的几率；SFT语料仅存在约7.7%的女性词元盈余，不足以解释下游效应规模。

Conclusion: 后训练并不能一致地消减职业性别偏见，反而系统性放大女性归因偏斜且不均衡地影响不同性别的画像叙事；偏见来源无法由SFT语料词元分布充分解释，需在流水线更早阶段与更细粒度上追溯。

Abstract: Post-training is widely assumed to reduce the social biases language models inherit from pretraining, yet direct evidence for this assumption remains mixed. In this paper, we examine how gender disparities evolve across the language modeling pipeline. In particular, we study occupational gender bias in OLMo 3, a fully open language model family, across its post-training stages by prompting each checkpoint to generate open-ended narratives about synthetic personas given their occupation and education. We consider two settings, a gender-given setting in which the profile states the persona's gender explicitly and a gender-assumed setting in which it must be inferred by the model. We find that when gender is stated, post-training increases semantic diversity for male-coded profiles, whereas for female-coded profiles the increase is smaller after SFT and not significant after DPO and RLVR. When gender is assumed by the model, post-training affects narratives about female and male personas similarly. The model's own gender assumptions, however, show a consistent female-preferential skew relative to United States labor-force demographics, except for heavily male-dominated occupations. Every successive post-training stage monotonically increases the odds of attributing a female label over the base model. A provenance analysis of the supervised fine-tuning corpus finds only a modest 7.7% female-token surplus, too small to explain the size of these downstream effects. The project code is available under: https://github.com/manthangadhia/llm-lifecycle-bias.

</details>


### [120] [ConvexLLM: Scaling Convex-Hull Speech Interfaces to General-Purpose Audio Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23539)
*Ming-Hao Hsu,Shujie Liu,Yuxuan Hu,Yuxiang Wang,Yingda Shen,Jinyu Li,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ConvexLLM，用C-Gate接口将音频表示为冻结文本嵌入的连续凸组合，在VoiceBench十一任务上取得开放权重模型最高聚合分75.36，MMAU Test-mini达62.50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音/音频语言模型多用MLP或Q-Former把声学特征映射进解码器输入空间，迫使解码器重新学习如何解释这些表示，可能损害其预训练文本能力。作者希望验证：将声学表示锚定在解码器已有的文本嵌入上，能否既帮助解码器利用声学信息、又更好保留预训练文本能力。

Method: 提出ConvexLLM通用语音与音频语言模型，其C-Gate接口把音频表示为冻结文本嵌入的连续凸组合，而非依赖MLP或Q-Former映射。在与2B参数解码器的对照实验中，将C-Gate与MLP、Q-Former接口在口语语言任务、文本任务以及转写与音频理解任务上进行比较。

Result: ConvexLLM在VoiceBench十一任务聚合分达75.36，为所比较开放权重模型中最高的；MMAU Test-mini准确率62.50%。相对基线优势最大的是口语知识与推理任务，而最强基线在音频理解与转写上更准确。C-Gate在聚合口语语言表现上优于MLP与Q-Former接口，文本任务表现接近MLP，在转写与音频理解上存在依赖任务的权衡。

Conclusion: 结果支持将文本嵌入的凸组合作为一种可行的通用语音与音频语言模型接口；该接口在口语知识与推理上收益显著，但在音频理解与转写上并非全面占优，需按任务权衡。

Abstract: Speech and audio language models connect continuous acoustic representations to decoders pretrained primarily on text data. Most such models use MLP or Q-Former interfaces to map acoustic features into the decoder's input space, requiring the decoder to learn how to interpret the resulting representations. We investigate whether grounding these representations in the decoder's existing text embeddings can help it use acoustic information while better preserving its pretrained text capabilities. We present ConvexLLM, a general-purpose speech and audio language model whose C-Gate interface represents audio as continuous convex combinations of frozen text embeddings. ConvexLLM achieves an eleven-task VoiceBench aggregate of 75.36, the highest among the open-weight models we compare, and an MMAU Test-mini accuracy of 62.50%. Its largest margins over the baselines are on spoken knowledge and reasoning tasks, while the strongest baselines are more accurate on audio understanding and transcription. In a comparison with a 2B-parameter decoder, C-Gate achieves higher aggregate spoken-language performance than MLP and Q-Former interfaces, with text-task performance close to MLP and task-dependent tradeoffs in transcription and audio understanding. These results support convex combinations of text embeddings as a viable interface for general-purpose speech and audio language models.

</details>


### [121] [Truth in Contrast: Calibrated Factual Evidence from Language Model Likelihoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23527)
*Ponhvoan Srey,Jason Lucas,Pittawat Taveekitworachai,Junyoung Lee,Anh Tuan Luu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出对比事实证据(CFE)及其概率形式对比事实后验(CFP)，从判别与校准两个角度研究多语言事实陈述中基于似然的置信度；CFP通过抵消共享误差获得更紧的校准界，并以似然几何中的证据方向实现无标签的跨语言证据对齐，提升低资源语言的事实判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM蕴含大量事实知识，但对其事实性主张的置信度可靠估计仍具挑战；需同时考察判别(区分真伪)与校准(置信度与正确率一致)两方面，且低资源语言下判别普遍偏弱。

Method: 将对比生成似然实例化为CFE，即比较同一主张在竞争性事实与非事实条件下的似然，并导出其概率对应物CFP；分析证明CFP校准依赖两个代理条件分布之间的差异误差（共享误差可抵消，得到更紧校准界）。进一步构造似然几何，诱导出承载证据的方向及正交的证据不变方向，仅在事实证据方向上把目标语言主张与更强的英语证据对齐，无需目标语言标签。

Result: CFP显著改善校准，同时事实判别力与最强基线相当；但判别在低资源语言中普遍较弱，削弱了校准的实际效用；选择性沿证据方向对齐目标语言与英语证据后，判别力与开放式事实生成均有提升。

Conclusion: CFP提供了理论可解释且校准更优的似然置信度度量；似然几何中的证据方向为无标签跨语言事实知识迁移提供有效途径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) encode substantial factual knowledge. Yet, estimating confidence in factual claims reliably remains challenging. We study likelihood-based factual confidence from the complementary perspectives of discrimination and calibration across multilingual factual statements. Specifically, we instantiate contrastive generative likelihoods as Contrastive Factual Evidence (CFE), which compares the likelihood of the same claim under competing factual and non-factual conditions, and derive its probabilistic counterpart, the Contrastive Factual Posterior (CFP). We show analytically that CFP calibration depends on the differential error between the two surrogate conditionals, allowing errors shared across them to cancel and yield tighter calibration bounds. Empirically, CFP substantially improves calibration while maintaining factual discrimination comparable to its strongest competitor. Nonetheless, discrimination is uniformly weak across methods in lower-resource languages, which simultaneously undermines the utility of the measured calibration. To this end, we interpret a likelihood geometry, within which an evidence-bearing direction is induced, along with an orthogonal evidence-invariant direction. In our investigation, we selectively align target-language claims with stronger English evidence only along the factual-evidence direction, improving discrimination and open-ended factual generation without target-language labels.

</details>


### [122] [Where's the Indirect Evidence? Investigating Grammatical Generalization with Influence Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22919)
*William Dai,Zhenghao Herbert Zhou,Maya Viswanathan,Robert Frank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自下而上的方法，用影响函数从BLiMP的15个语法现象中剔除直接证据后，取影响力最高的6万句作为"间接证据"，并证明其显著影响模型学习与预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明语言模型能泛化到训练语料之外，说明存在"间接证据"驱动学习；但先前工作要么只推断其存在而不刻画其性质，要么只检验特定假设，缺乏系统识别间接证据的手段。

Method: 采用Koh & Liang (2017) 的影响函数量化单句训练样本对模型在BLiMP 15个语法现象上预测的影响；用人工构造规则过滤掉某现象的直接证据；将剩余句子中影响力最高的60,000句作为该现象的间接证据。

Result: 在五个模型、全部15个现象上，剔除间接证据后重训练，留出准确率下降幅度均大于剔除规模匹配的随机句组（中位数12个百分点）；间接证据以中位4.7倍于随机组的比例（部分）满足直接证据过滤器的结构条件；按间接证据重叠度对现象聚类，可恢复含多个现象的5个BLiMP类别中的3个。

Conclusion: 影响函数提供了一种有效的自下而上间接证据识别方法：所得间接证据对学习影响显著、与直接证据结构条件部分同构，并能揭示语言现象间的关系，说明该方法具备发现语言学关联的潜力。

Abstract: Language models generalize beyond their training corpus to learn linguistic phenomena (Patil et al., 2024; Misra and Mahowald, 2024), showing that there exists indirect evidence driving learning. Prior work has inferred that indirect evidence exists without identifying its character or considered specific hypotheses about what it might be. We explore an alternative bottom-up method for identifying indirect evidence. We use influence functions (Koh and Liang, 2017) to measure how individual training sentences affect model predictions in 15 grammatical phenomena from BLiMP (Warstadt et al., 2020). We filter out a phenomenon's direct evidence using hand-constructed rules and take the 60,000 most influential remaining sentences as indirect evidence. This indirect evidence substantially affects learning: retraining without it lowers held-out accuracy more than removing a matched random group for every phenomenon across five models (by a median of 12 points). We characterize the indirect evidence by its similarity to our direct evidence filters: it (partially) meets the filters' structural conditions at a median of 4.7 times the rate of a matched random group. Finally, clustering phenomena by indirect-evidence overlap recovers three of the five BLiMP categories that include multiple phenomena, suggesting that this method can reveal linguistic relationships.

</details>


### [123] [Cross-Centaur: Modeling Individual Decision-Making Across Psychological Tasks with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23157)
*Hikaru Asano,Hidezo Suganuma,Ryutaro Mori,Yukino Baba*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出跨任务个体级行为数据集 Cross-Psych-101（591名被试、261,551条有效决策、覆盖12个Bandit/Tradeoff/Structure/Social任务），并训练 Cross-Centaur 模型，利用同一被试的源任务描述与行为预测其目标任务决策，在被试内与被试间、任务内与任务间均优于现成LLM与Centaur。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM行为建模数据（如Centaur所用Psych-101）未将同一被试跨任务关联，模型只能学到群体层面的规律，无法建模个体跨任务的一致性倾向。

Method: 构建 Cross-Psych-101 数据集，将被试在多个任务上的描述与逐试次决策配对；训练 Cross-Centaur，以目标任务描述＋同一被试的源任务描述与源任务行为为输入，预测该被试的目标任务动作；并通过隐状态探针分析检验其是否编码个体级行为倾向。

Result: Cross-Centaur 在留出被试上（任务内与跨任务）均优于现成LLM与Centaur；探针分析显示其隐状态编码了可跨任务迁移的被试级行为倾向。

Conclusion: 跨任务个体级数据能有效提升语言模型对人类决策的预测能力，为个体化行为建模提供了数据与模型基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as computational models of human behavior. Recent models such as Centaur have advanced this goal by fine-tuning LLMs on trial-by-trial decisions from psychological experiments. However, existing large-scale behavioral datasets do not link the same participants across different tasks, so these models learn population-level regularities but are not trained to model individual-level regularities across tasks. We introduce Cross-Psych-101, a dataset of 591 participants and 261{,}551 valid decisions across 12 Bandit, Tradeoff, Structure, and Social tasks. We train Cross-Centaur to predict a participant's target-task actions from a target-task description and the source-task description paired with the same participant's source-task behavior. Cross-Centaur outperforms off-the-shelf LLMs and Centaur on held-out participants, both within and across tasks. Probing analyses suggest that its hidden states encode participant-level behavioral tendencies that transfer across tasks. These results highlight the potential of cross-task individual-level data for predicting human decision-making with language models. Cross-Pysch-101 is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/HikaruAsano/cross-psych-101.

</details>


### [124] [The Reliance Hypothesis: Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness Rises as Language Resource Level Falls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22841)
*Lucas Resck,Jingyi Sun,Qianli Wang,Isabelle Augenstein,Anna Korhonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将思维链（CoT）忠实性与语言资源水平及任务类型（知识密集型/推理密集型）关联：在MMMLU八种语言的平行选择题上，知识密集任务中忠实性随资源水平下降而上升，推理密集任务中则不然，并提出"依赖假设"加以解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT忠实性关系到大模型的监控、解释与安全部署，但研究几乎都局限于英语。低资源语言中模型知识较少，可能改变模型对自身推理的依赖程度，因此有必要考察资源水平如何影响CoT忠实性。

Method: 构建MMMLU八种语言的平行多项选择题，在四个模型、多种忠实性度量下，系统比较资源水平与任务类型（知识密集型 vs. 推理密集型）对CoT忠实性的交互影响，并提出并检验"依赖假设"（Reliance Hypothesis），辅以模型置信度分析。

Result: 知识密集型任务中，忠实性随资源水平降低而上升；推理密集型任务中无此趋势，该差距在四个模型与多种度量下方向一致。置信度可部分解释资源水平的效应，但无法解释知识与推理任务间的差距；语言层面置信度越低越依赖推理，而同一语言内低置信度答案却不然，构成辛普森悖论。

Conclusion: 低资源语言中模型对更多答案依赖推理，而非对单个答案依赖更强，支持依赖假设；CoT监控可能在最少被验证的低资源语言中最具信息量，提示应加强该方向的验证研究。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) faithfulness matters for monitoring, explaining and safely deploying large language models, yet it has been studied mostly in English. Languages differ in level, the amount of text available to learn from, so models know less in lower-resource languages, which may change how much they rely on their reasoning. To our knowledge, we are the first to relate CoT faithfulness to both resource level and task type (knowledge- or reasoning-intensive). On parallel multiple-choice questions from Multilingual Massive Multitask Language Understanding (MMMLU) in eight languages, faithfulness rises as resource level falls on knowledge-intensive tasks but not on reasoning-intensive ones, a gap that points the same way across four models and several faithfulness measures. We propose, and find support for, the Reliance Hypothesis: models rely more on their reasoning when they cannot recall the answer, as in lower-resource languages on knowledge-intensive tasks. Model confidence partly explains the effect of resource level, but not the knowledge-reasoning gap. Languages in which models are less confident rely more on reasoning, yet less confident answers within a language do not, a Simpson's paradox. In lower-resource languages, models rely on their reasoning for more answers, not more strongly for each. CoT monitoring may therefore be most informative in lower-resource languages, where it is least often validated.

</details>


### [125] [DLite: History-free Speculative Decoding for Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23787)
*Jiaming Xu,Hanzhen Wang,Tianlang Zhao,Jiewen Xiao,Chengze Yuan,Jiayi Pan,Yongkang Zhou,Jiancai Ye,Yu Wang,Guohao Dai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DLite：面向智能体负载的无历史投机解码框架，直接复用目标模型上下文隐状态做局部预测，接受长度达2-6，较DSpark最高提升70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码是加速LLM推理的主流技术，但智能体负载具有长且持续演化、多轮交互频繁的特点。此时现有草稿模型接受长度最多下降80%，而保留历史KV缓存又带来大量系统开销。作者指出不断增长的历史上下文是这两类问题的共同根源。

Method: 提出DLite无历史投机解码框架：直接复用目标模型的上下文隐状态，在不显式维护历史上下文的前提下做局部投机预测，将复杂建模问题转化为局部简单预测；并用低开销的轻量顺序头（sequential head）恢复块内因果依赖；同时设计定制的两阶段训练策略以训练这一异构无历史架构。

Result: 在通用、长上下文与智能体负载上的实验表明，DLite取得2-6的接受长度，并比当前最优方法DSpark最高提升70%。

Conclusion: 以"去历史化"重构草稿模型是应对智能体场景下投机解码退化的有效路径，兼顾了接受长度与系统开销。

Abstract: Speculative decoding has become a mainstream technique for accelerating large language model (LLM) inference by using a lightweight draft model to generate candidate tokens that are subsequently verified by the target model. However, agentic workloads introduce long and continuously evolving contexts and frequent multi-turn interactions. Under such workloads, we observe that existing draft models suffer from up to an 80% degradation in acceptance length, while maintaining their historical KV cache introduces substantial system overhead. In this paper, we identify the growing historical context as a common source of both problems and introduce DLite, a history-free speculative decoding framework for agentic AI. DLite directly reuses contextual hidden states from the target model and performs local speculative prediction without explicitly maintaining the historical context, transforming an increasingly complex modeling problem into local simple prediction problem. A lightweight sequential head further restores intra-block causal dependencies with low overhead. To effectively train this heterogeneous history-free architecture, we introduce a tailored two-stage training strategy. Experiments on general-purpose, long-context and agentic workloads show that DLite achieves 2-6 acceptance length and outperforms the state-of-the-art speculative decoding method, DSpark, up to 70%.

</details>


### [126] [Recognizing Study Lineage Is Not Enough: Over-Crediting Same-Trial Evidence in LLM-Based Medical Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23721)
*Mukand Krishna,Anoushka Harit,Chenghao Xiao,Zhongtian Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"出版物计数≠研究计数"这一问题，发现医学LLM在识别试验血缘（同一试验的多篇报告）时表现正确，但在证据聚合时却会重复计入同一试验的第二篇出版物，二者存在行为性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医学证据合成与系统综述中，同一临床试验常产生多篇出版物。若模型将每篇出版物当作独立研究计数，会高估证据量（重复计数/过度计入）。现有评估多关注任务内正确率，未区分"识别共享试验血缘"与"聚合证据时的实际使用"这两个环节，因此需要检验二者是否一致。

Method: 构建14个经来源审核的临床家族（families），覆盖provenance（来源/血缘）、clinical applicability（临床适用性）、result direction（结果方向）和composition（构成）四类；其中provenance家族用于直接测试血缘识别。设置5种模型条件。识别与聚合作为两个独立的无状态调用分别执行，并重复调用以评估同一输入内的稳定性。

Result: 在三个原始provenance家族中，GPT-5.5在识别调用中10/10正确识别独立更新篇目，但在5/5独立聚合调用中对同一试验报告过度计入。选定的Qwen3-4B与Ministral-3-8B条件呈现相同的跨任务反差。加入"研究分组（study-grouping）"指令后，GPT-5.5在后续纯provenance家族中的过度计入从4/5降为0/5。其他模型条件行为混杂，且被拒输出限制了若干比较。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了"被诱导出的血缘识别"与"证据聚合行为"之间的缺口：模型能说出正确血缘关系，却不在聚合中运用。提示层面加入研究分组指令可显著缓解过度计入，但效果因模型条件而异，且需注意输出拒绝带来的评估局限。代码与数据见匿名仓库。

Abstract: Publication count is not study count. A model can identify shared trial lineage correctly in a direct task yet over-credit a second publication from the same trial when aggregating evidence. We evaluate this behavior in medical LLM verification using 14 source-audited clinical families and five model conditions. The families span provenance, clinical applicability, result direction, and composition; the provenance families provide the direct lineage tests. Recognition and aggregation are separate stateless calls, and repeated calls assess within-input stability. Across all three original provenance families, GPT-5.5 identifies the independent update in 10/10 recognition calls per family but over-credits the same-trial report in 5/5 separate aggregation calls per family. Selected Qwen3-4B and Ministral-3-8B conditions show the same cross-task contrast. A study-grouping instruction reduces GPT-5.5 over-credit from 4/5 to 0/5 in each later pure-provenance family. Other model conditions show mixed behavior, and rejected outputs limit several comparisons. The results identify a behavioral gap between elicited lineage recognition and evidence aggregation. The code and data artifacts are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/study-lineage-review-artifact.

</details>


### [127] [GLOBE: Evolving Structured Agentic Memory for E-commerce Product Optimization in Generative Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23709)
*Hyunseo Kim,Suil Choe,Sukkyoung So,Daeyeon Yi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 GLOBE 框架，通过维护"显式、按字段划分的行为手册"作为可复用的智能体记忆，实现电商商品列表优化的持续自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向生成式搜索的商品列表优化需要语言智能体既能有效改写，又能在多品类间保留成功策略、持续自我改进。但现有方法把结构化列表当作自由文本处理，有效策略隐含在提示词、搜索轨迹或改写输出中，无法沉淀为可复用的智能体记忆。

Method: GLOBE 将优化知识存于不断演化的"字段级行为手册"中。每个回合由 Editor 应用相关行为并获得基于排名的反馈，Reflector 将反馈转为行为更新；Moderator 剔除冗余提案，Curator 提交被接受的改动，持续有害的行为被停用，从而把回合反馈转化为持久、按字段划分的优化记忆。

Result: 结构化行为优于单体式提示优化；反馈驱动的演化与审核机制进一步提升效果并降低下行风险；学得的行为在未见品类上仍保持有效。

Conclusion: 智能体记忆的演化能够支撑可解释、可迁移的商品列表优化。

Abstract: Optimizing product listings for generative search requires language agents not only to rewrite effectively but also to continually self-improve by retaining successful strategies across diverse categories. However, existing approaches often treat structured listings as free-form text, leaving effective strategies implicit in prompts, search trajectories, or rewritten outputs rather than accumulating them as a reusable agentic memory. To address this limitation, we introduce GLOBE, an agentic framework that drives continual self-improvement by storing optimization knowledge in an evolving handbook of explicit, field-scoped behaviors. In each episode, an Editor applies relevant behaviors and receives rank-based feedback, which a Reflector converts into behavior updates. Crucially for dependable execution in real-world settings, a Moderator removes redundant proposals, a Curator commits accepted changes, and consistently harmful behaviors are deactivated. This cycle transforms episodic feedback into a persistent, field-scoped optimization memory. Experiments show that structured behaviors outperform monolithic prompt optimization, while feedback-driven evolution and moderation further improve effectiveness and reduce downside risk. The learned behaviors also remain effective on unseen product categories, suggesting that agentic memory evolution supports interpretable and transferable product optimization.

</details>


### [128] [Trajectory-Triggered Sparse Collaborative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23613)
*Amit Ranjan Trivedi,Sruthikesh Surineni,Kyong Ho Lee,Mohit Mittal,Steven Li,Rex Hill*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究稀疏协作解码：由草稿模型生成大部分token，目标模型仅在纠正片段被调用。核心发现是局部不确定性（熵、NLL、logit margin等）几乎无法预测失败（AUC 0.50-0.53），而基于草稿置信度路径的聚合轨迹形状特征可达AUC 0.86；据此在线学习的触发策略在GSM8K上超过最优开环调度5.3-20.0个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密辅助方法（如投机解码）反复用目标模型验证草稿token，使目标计算与生成过程耦合，开销大。作者希望降低目标模型调用频率，转而研究"何时调用目标模型"这一触发时机问题。

Method: 提出稀疏协作解码框架：草稿模型生成大部分token，目标模型仅用于短纠正片段。系统比较两类触发信号——局部不确定性特征（熵、负对数似然、logit margin、top-k概率质量、速度特征）与草稿置信度路径上的聚合轨迹形状特征；后者用仅依赖草稿正确性标签训练的轻量logistic预测器实现在线触发。

Result: 局部不确定性特征的失败预测接近随机（AUC 0.50-0.53），而轨迹形状特征AUC达0.86。分析揭示触发时机权衡：早调用杠杆更大但证据弱，晚调用证据强但纠偏能力弱。在线学习式稀疏协作解码在五个GSM8K草稿-目标组合上较最优开环调度提升5.3-20.0个点；MATH-500上在早期预测与目标能力匹配时提升7.0-13.5点，否则下降；StrategyQA上提升5.0-8.3点。

Conclusion: 局部不确定性不适合作为目标模型调用触发器，轨迹级置信度形状特征是更有效的信号。学习式在线触发能显著优于开环调度，且可迁移到算术以外的推理任务，但收益依赖早期可预测性与目标模型能力的对齐。

Abstract: Dense assistance methods such as speculative decoding repeatedly verify draft tokens with a target model, keeping target computation coupled to generation. We study sparse collaborative decoding, where a draft model generates most tokens and a target model is invoked only for short corrective segments. The key problem is deciding when to call the target. We show that local uncertainty is a weak trigger: entropy, negative log-likelihood, logit margin, top-$k$ mass, and velocity features give near-chance failure prediction, with AUC 0.50-0.53. In contrast, aggregate trajectory-shape features over the draft confidence path reach AUC 0.86 using a lightweight logistic predictor trained only from draft correctness labels. This induces a trigger-timing tradeoff: early calls have more leverage but weaker evidence, while later calls have stronger evidence but less ability to redirect the trajectory. Used online, learned sparse collaborative decoding improves over the best open-loop schedule by +5.3 to +20.0 accuracy points across five GSM8K draft-target pairs. On MATH-500, it gains 7.0-13.5 points when early prediction and target competence align, but loses when either is weak. On StrategyQA, it improves over open-loop by 5.0-8.3 points, showing transfer beyond arithmetics.

</details>


### [129] [AuditKIT: A Unified Evaluation Spine for Benchmarking, Judging, and Comparing Large Language Models and Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22511)
*Shreeyans Arora,Utsav Avaiya,Zera Lyngkhoi,Hem Gosalia,Ram Mohan Rao Kadiyala,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu,Pratinav Seth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 AuditKIT——一个开源评估库，将基准准确率、裁判评估、运行时性能、工具调用行为与检索质量统一记录到单一结果表示中，并通过方向感知的指标契约、配置指纹和可扩展的指标与后端，避免接口、指标语义与轨迹格式差异导致的对比失真与智能体工作流中的隐性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型与智能体评估需综合多类信号，但接口、指标语义和轨迹格式的差异使跨系统对比困难，且易在智能体工作流中掩盖失败；缺失或不可观测的证据亦常被误计为失败。

Method: AuditKIT 采用统一结果表示记录各类测量，引入方向感知的指标契约、配置指纹以及可扩展的指标与后端。面向智能体，提供轮次感知的工具调用匹配，区分并行与串行执行，并将经验证的任务结果与轨迹级诊断分离；缺失或不可观测证据标记为 unscored 而非失败。面向 RAG，融合排序检索指标、裁判式接地性评估与确定性证据校验。

Result: 通过覆盖模型比较、服务运行时、检索、工具调用与互操作性的七个案例研究，展示了答案抽取方式、运行时行为和指标选择如何改变评估结论。

Conclusion: 统一表示与显式指标契约对可靠、可复现的多维评估至关重要；将不可观测证据与失败区分、并分离任务结果与轨迹诊断，可提升智能体与 RAG 评估的准确性。

Abstract: Evaluating language models and agents requires combining benchmark accuracy, judge-based assessment, runtime performance, tool-use behavior, and retrieval quality. Differences in interfaces, metric semantics, and trace formats complicate these comparisons and can hide failures in agentic workflows. We present AuditKIT, a source-available evaluation library that records these measurements in one result representation, with direction-aware metric contracts, configuration fingerprints, and extensible metrics and backends. For agents, it provides turn-aware tool-call matching, separates parallel from sequential execution, and distinguishes verified task outcomes from trajectory-level diagnostics. Missing or unobservable evidence is marked unscored rather than counted as failure. For retrieval-augmented generation, it combines ranked retrieval metrics, judged grounding, and deterministic evidence checks. Seven case studies spanning model comparison, serving runtimes, retrieval, tool calling, and interoperability show how answer extraction, runtime behavior, and metric selection change evaluation outcomes.

</details>


### [130] [From Parallels to Divergence: Language Models' Learning of Action Nominal Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22468)
*Iskar Deng,Shane Steinert-Threlkeld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过"语法工程化"方法构建96种在词序、配列、补语系统与ANC策略上变化的合成语言，从零训练GPT-2-small并用34类语法现象的最小对检验，发现模型学习偏好与类型学分布仅部分一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行动名词化构式（ANC）以名词形式表达事件，其论元标记既可比照小句也可比照普通名词短语。作者想探究不同语法配置如何影响语言模型对ANC及相关构式的学习，并将模型的学习偏好与自然语言的类型学分布相比较，以判断类型学分布能否由通用学习压力解释。

Method: 采用语法工程化的合成语料构建方法，将自然语料的共享语义输入与计算语法相结合；构建96种目标语言，系统改变小句词序、名词短语词序、配列（alignment）、补语系统与ANC策略；对每种语言从零训练GPT-2-small，并用覆盖34种语法现象的目标性最小对进行评估。

Result: 结果呈现类型学平行性：基本小句配置上模型偏好与自然语言分布一致；但在补语系统与ANC策略上出现偏离，且模型还会习得自然语言中罕见或未见过的组配。

Conclusion: 这种部分对齐表明，自然语言的分布可能同时反映通用学习压力与模型学习实验未能完全刻画的功能性需求。

Abstract: Action nominal constructions (ANCs) express events in nominal form, with their argument marking following patterns found in clauses or ordinary noun phrases. In this paper, we investigate how different grammatical configurations affect language models' learning of ANCs and related constructions, and how these learning preferences compare with typological distributions in natural languages. We use a grammar-engineered method for synthetic corpus construction that combines shared semantic input from natural corpora with computational grammars. We construct 96 target languages varying in clause word order, noun-phrase word order, alignment, complement system, and ANC strategy. For each language, we train GPT-2-small models from scratch and evaluate them using targeted minimal pairs covering 34 grammatical phenomena. The results show typological parallels in basic clause configurations but divergences in complement systems and ANC strategies, with models also learning combinations rare or unattested in natural languages. This partial alignment suggests that natural language distributions may reflect both general learning pressures and functional demands not fully captured by model learning experiments.

</details>


### [131] [Causal Edit Serialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22820)
*Louis Robinson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Causal Edit Serialization (CES)框架，用指针token在单一因果token流中表达插入、删除、替换、复制与移动等修订操作，并配合轻量定位头、双索引旋转位置编码与预训练/后训练流程，实现生成与局部化修订的统一接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归语言模型只能顺序生成文本，而修订需要回指输入上下文或已生成token，与因果单向结构不兼容。作者希望在同一因果token流内原生表达修订，避免额外解码器或非因果结构。

Method: 引入特殊指针token并预测源地址，将五类编辑操作显式局部化；设计轻量定位头与双索引旋转位置编码以同时支持地址预测和对已发出指针的条件化，并保持标准因果注意力掩码；预训练混合普通语言建模与由随机损坏、模型自编辑、重复文本构造的序列化修复数据；后训练采用监督学习与强化学习，以语法纠错为验证场景。

Result: 在不同模型规模与编辑基线下，CES模型能完整应用编辑，干净文本bits per byte仅略高于纯自回归模型；监督与强化学习后训练在语法纠错上产生有益修改多于有害修改（参考盲模型评判）；正确修复程序还能在保留原上下文及缓存状态的前提下提升后续文本预测。

Conclusion: CES为生成与局部化修订提供了统一接口，可扩展到迭代精修及其他指针寻址式变换。

Abstract: Standard autoregressive language models generate text sequentially, but revision requires referring back to tokens in the input context or those already generated. Causal Edit Serialization (CES) is introduced as a framework for expressing such revisions within the same causal token stream. Special pointer tokens are paired with predicted source addresses, allowing insertion, deletion, replacement, copying and movement to be localized explicitly. A lightweight localization head and dual-index rotary position encoding support both address prediction and conditioning on previously emitted pointers, while retaining a standard causal attention mask. A pretraining procedure combines ordinary language modeling with serialized repairs derived from random corruptions, model-generated edits and repeated text. Across model scales and editing baselines, CES models demonstrate complete applied edits with modestly higher clean-text bits per byte than clean autoregressive models. Supervised and reinforcement-learning post-training provide a grammatical-error-correction proof of concept, producing more beneficial than harmful changes under reference-blind model judging. Correct repair programs also improve subsequent text prediction while the original context and its cached states are retained. CES provides a common interface for generation and localized revision, with potential extensions to iterative refinement and additional pointer-addressed transformations.

</details>


### [132] [Watermarkability: Measuring and Improving Language-Model Compatibility with Logit-Based Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22810)
*Zewei Deng,Mohamed Seif,Liyan Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"可水印性"(watermarkability)刻画模型与水印所请求token集合的兼容性，并给出两阶段方法降低该代价，在近乎硬性固定偏置基线下显著降低困惑度增量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于logit的水印在每步解码时提升指定token集合的logits，但基模型在该集合上的原生概率质量随上下文、模型与水印规则变化，导致相同logit偏置产生不同的遵循率与困惑度(PPL)增量，缺乏对"模型—水印"兼容性的量化刻画。

Method: 定义watermarkability cost：在目标质量τ下，为把至少τ概率质量分配给请求集合所需的最小KL散度期望。降低该代价的两阶段方法：①在16路词表划分的并集上训练以目标集合为条件的低秩适配器(LoRA)，提升请求集合的概率质量；②用闭式控制器施加达到目标质量所需的最小剩余均匀logit偏置。

Result: 在OPT-125M与Qwen3-8B、6种水印调度共12个模型-调度对上验证：适配前的可水印性代价与近硬固定偏置基线的log-PPL增量强相关(pooled Pearson r=0.929)；各调度下至少99.80%采样token落入对应解码步的请求集合；在相同可靠性下，该方法的平均逐调度prompt条件PPL较固定偏置基线在OPT-125M上降低46.3%，Qwen3-8B上降低5.2%。

Conclusion: 可水印性代价能有效预测水印带来的质量损失，两阶段适配+闭式控制策略可在保持高遵循率的同时显著降低困惑度代价，且对大模型收益较小、对较小模型收益更大。

Abstract: Language-model watermarks can help identify model-generated text, encode provenance information, and detect post-generation edits. A broad class of logit-based watermarks increases the logits of a designated token set at each decoding step. Because a base model's native probability mass on this set varies across contexts, models, and watermark rules, the same logit bias can yield different adherence rates and increases in perplexity (PPL). We introduce watermarkability to characterize the compatibility between a model and the token sets requested by a watermark. At a target mass $τ$, we quantify this property through a watermarkability cost: the expected minimum KL divergence from the base-model distribution required to assign probability at least $τ$ to the requested set. A lower cost means that the model is more watermarkable for that request distribution. To reduce this cost, we propose a two-stage method. In the first stage, we train a target-set-conditioned low-rank adapter on unions drawn from a 16-way vocabulary partition to increase the requested set's probability mass. In the second stage, a closed-form controller applies the smallest remaining uniform logit bias needed to reach the target mass. We evaluate the method on six watermark schedules using OPT-125M and Qwen3-8B. Across the 12 model-schedule pairs, the pre-adaptation watermarkability cost correlates with the log-PPL increase under a near-hard fixed-bias baseline (pooled Pearson $r=0.929$). For every schedule, at least 99.80% of sampled tokens fall in the target set requested at their decoding step. At this reliability, the adapter and controller reduce average per-schedule prompt-conditional PPL by 46.3% on OPT-125M and 5.2% on Qwen3-8B relative to the fixed-bias baseline.

</details>


### [133] [On the Position Bias of Large Language Models: A Comprehensive Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22807)
*Yining Li,Huayang Li,Lemao Liu,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统考察了从十亿级到万亿级参数模型、覆盖五种语言、多选与多文档任务的LLM位置偏见，发现模型规模扩大与标准CoT可显著缓解该问题，具备扩展推理能力的"thinking models"几乎免疫位置偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于LLM位置偏见的研究大多模型覆盖有限、基准以英语为主，导致对前沿大模型及多语言场景下的位置偏见行为认识不足，亟需更全面的实证刻画。

Method: 开展跨模型规模（十亿至万亿参数）、跨任务（多选评测与多文档RAG）、跨语言（五种语言）的全面实证分析，并对比标准CoT提示与具备扩展推理能力的thinking models的表现。

Result: 位置偏见在小规模模型和非英语语言中仍较明显；随模型规模增大及采用标准Chain-of-Thought提示，该问题显著减轻；thinking models几乎不受位置偏见影响。

Conclusion: 扩展推理能力是缓解位置偏见的可行但代价较高的通用路径；该发现对多选评测可信度与RAG可靠性具有实践意义。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that position bias remains a critical vulnerability in large language models (LLMs), undermining both the integrity of multiple-choice evaluations and the reliability of retrieval-augmented generation. However, existing understanding of this phenomenon is largely derived from studies with limited model coverage and English-centric benchmarks, leaving the behavior of frontier models and diverse languages underexplored. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive investigation of position bias across model scales ranging from billion-level to trillion-level parameters, covering both multiple-choice and multi-document tasks in five languages. Our experiments reveal that while position bias remains noticeable in small-scale models and Non-English languages, increasing model scale and employing standard Chain-of-Thought prompting significantly mitigate this issue. Moreover, we demonstrate that emerging \emph{thinking models} with extended reasoning capabilities are nearly immune to position bias, suggesting a costly but general approach toward resolving this vulnerability.

</details>


### [134] [TokenEconomizer: Experience-Driven Black-Box Cost Control for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22732)
*Aaron J. Li,Weijia Xu,Chandan Singh,Zixiao Chen,Jeevana Priya Inala,Charlotte Siska,Acar Tamersoy,Bin Yu,Ion Stoica,Michel Galley,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TokenEconomizer外部控制器，在连续多任务场景下将编码智能体的token成本平均降低18.4-30.9%，任务成功率相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿编码智能体token成本急剧上升，而现有评测多在孤立任务上进行，与开发者真实连续构建、更新、维护大型项目的使用方式不符。在任务序列上评测发现智能体存在随环境复现的token开销，现有方法难以削减。

Method: TokenEconomizer作为黑盒外部控制器，在任务序列中通过三项决策降低环境相关成本：(i) 注入哪些操作性微技能；(ii) 剪枝哪些持续性无关工具；(iii) 何时干预低效失败循环。

Result: 在Claude Code、Codex、GitHub Copilot CLI搭配前沿模型下，平均token成本降低18.4-30.9%，成功率相当；所有仓库与各智能体-模型组合均有效，并泛化到顺序式Web开发、安全审计等领域，成本下降6.4-21.2%。

Conclusion: 自适应黑盒成本控制是降低真实场景下编码智能体token成本的有前景方向。

Abstract: Advances in frontier coding agents have sharply increased the token costs of AI-assisted software engineering. Their token efficiency is typically evaluated on isolated tasks, whereas real-world developers use them to continuously build, update, and maintain large-scale projects. This mismatch motivates us to evaluate agents over sequences of realistic coding tasks, where we find that agents exhibit recurring environment-dependent token costs that existing methods struggle to reduce. To minimize these costs, we introduce TokenEconomizer, an external controller that cuts environment-specific token costs over a sequence of tasks. These cuts come from determining (i) which operational micro-skills to inject, (ii) which persistently irrelevant tools to prune, and (iii) when to intervene in unproductive failure loops. Across Claude Code, Codex, and GitHub Copilot CLI paired with frontier models, TokenEconomizer reduces the average token cost by 18.4-30.9% with comparable task success. Savings hold in every repository under every tested agent-model setting and generalize to diverse domains such as sequential web development and security auditing, where costs fall by 6.4-21.2% depending on task structure. Our work establishes adaptive black-box cost control as a promising direction for reducing token costs of coding agents in realistic settings.

</details>


### [135] [Inductive Learning of Concept Embeddings from Cross-Linguistic Colexification Patterns with Textual Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22638)
*Arne Rubehn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种归纳式概念嵌入框架，将完全与部分共词化网络的结构属性与简短概念描述的语义信息相结合，使未见于训练数据的概念也能获得嵌入表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布语义学以词嵌入为代表已改变语义表示范式，但历史与类型学语言比较仍多依赖离散表示，嵌入方法潜力未被充分挖掘。已有"概念嵌入"作为语言无关的拟义学对应物虽能反映语义特性，却依赖直推式方法，只能为训练数据中出现的节点学习嵌入，缺乏可扩展性。

Method: 构建归纳式概念嵌入学习框架：利用完全共词化网络与部分共词化网络的结构特性，并融合来自简短概念描述的语义信息，从而基于短定义对新概念进行嵌入。

Result: 所得概念嵌入与多种跨语言相似性或相关性度量呈相关，并且能良好泛化到训练数据中未出现的新概念。

Conclusion: 归纳式框架解决了直推式概念嵌入的可扩展性瓶颈，为基于嵌入的跨语言历史与类型学比较提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The advent of distributional semantics as a framework for computational semantics marked a paradigm shift in the nature of representations, by which meanings are represented as dense vectors commonly known as word embeddings. Computational approaches to historical or typological language comparison, on the other hand, still mostly rely on discrete representations, while the potential of embedding-based methods remains largely untapped in this context. Recent studies have proposed concept embeddings as a language-independent, onomasiological counterpart to word embeddings, based on networks of cross-linguistic colexification patterns. While the resulting concept embeddings reflect meaningful semantic properties and relations, they suffer from a lack of scalability, since they rely on transductive embedding approaches which only learn embeddings for nodes present in the training data. In this study, we present an inductive framework for learning concept embeddings, combining the structural properties of full and partial colexification networks with semantic information from short concept descriptions. This framework allows the embedding of unseen concepts based on a short definition. We show that the resulting concept embeddings correlate with various cross-linguistic measures of similarity or relatedness, while generalizing well to new concepts that were absent from the training data.

</details>


### [136] [Measuring Mechanistic Uncertainty in Large Language Models via Causal Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22636)
*Aneesh Komanduri,Majid Afshar,Timothy A. Miller,Yanjun Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"机制性不确定性"概念，用"因果鲁棒裕度"（使模型输出偏离所需的最小内部干预强度）量化不确定性，并借助Jacobian Lens概念引导向量实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在关键领域易产生幻觉，需要可靠且可解释的不确定性量化；现有方法多基于输出概率/一致性，缺乏对内部机制层面的刻画。

Method: 定义因果鲁棒裕度=使输出偏离的最小内部干预量；基于Jlens概念引导向量进行内部干预实验，在3个开源模型、5个问答数据集上评估；并做受控知识注入实验。

Result: 错误与不确定回答的裕度更小，即对内部干预更敏感；因果鲁棒裕度与基于输出的传统不确定性方法互补；知识注入后正确习得事实的裕度上升。

Conclusion: 因果鲁棒性提供了有机制依据的不确定性信号，可作为输出级不确定性度量的补充。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in critical domains, where their susceptibility to hallucinations makes reliable and interpretable uncertainty quantification essential. We introduce the concept of mechanistic uncertainty, which characterizes uncertainty through the stability of the internal mechanisms supporting a model's response. We propose the causal robustness margin, defined as the minimum internal intervention required to deviate the model's output, and operationalize it using Jacobian Lens (Jlens) concept steering vectors. Across three open-source models and five question-answering datasets, we show incorrect and uncertain responses exhibit smaller margins, indicating greater sensitivity to internal interventions, and the causal robustness margin complements conventional output-based uncertainty methods. Finally, in a controlled knowledge injection experiment, correctly learned facts show increased margins after training, suggesting that causal robustness provides a mechanistically grounded signal of uncertainty.

</details>


### [137] [A Plausible Explanation Is Enough to Mislead Multimodal Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22631)
*Anglin Cai,Zhiqi Wang,Xiaozhi Yang,Yurun Lin,Dongwon Lee,Yuchen Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出JUPE基准，发现普通但貌似合理的解释会使MLLM评审在需视觉验证的判断中被误导（"合理性俘获"），其影响力与刻意说服性论证相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM日益被用作训练与评测流程中的评审，且常连同解释一起评估答案。已有研究表明AI评审对文本因素敏感，但普通、貌似合理的解释能否动摇需要视觉验证的判断仍不清楚。

Method: 构建JUPE——一种VQA式基准，固定答案而系统性地改变解释。在14个MLLM评审上进行评测，并采用信号检测论（SDT）分析决策准则与辨别力。

Result: 普通貌似合理的解释会误导评审，平均效应与刻意说服性论证相当；朴素理由与思维链推理分别使错误答案的被拒率下降12.7与13.8个百分点。SDT显示决策准则均值显著向"接受"偏移，而平均辨别力无明显变化。该效应跨领域、跨解释生成器、跨评审协议普遍存在，所测试的缓解措施保护作用有限且依赖模型。

Conclusion: 将解释纳入评审会引发"合理性俘获"，导致性能估计虚高；现有缓解手段难以有效防护，需重视解释对判断的干扰。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) increasingly serve as judges in training and evaluation pipelines, often assessing answers together with explanations. Although AI judges are sensitive to textual factors, it remains unclear whether ordinary plausible explanations can sway judgments requiring visual verification. We introduce JUPE, a visual-question-answering (VQA)-style benchmark that fixes answers while varying explanations.
  Across 14 MLLM judges, ordinary explanations that sound plausible mislead judges, with effects comparable on average to deliberately persuasive arguments. Plain justifications and chain-of-thought rationales reduce rejection of incorrect answers by 12.7 and 13.8 percentage points, respectively. We call this failure plausibility capture. Using signal detection theory, we find a significant mean shift in decision criterion toward acceptance, without a clear change in mean discriminability. Explanations sway judgments across domains, explanation generators, and judging protocols, while tested mitigations offer limited, model-dependent protection. Such shifts can inflate performance estimates when judges evaluate answers with explanations.

</details>


### [138] [When Does Uncertainty Measure RAG Hallucination Risk?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22615)
*Rajeel Ahmad Ansari,Tautik Agrahari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文检验了不确定性作为RAG幻觉风险度量的条件性：只有当检索证据对答案形成约束时，不确定性才能有效指示幻觉；证据覆盖不足时模型可依赖内部知识生成高置信的、缺乏证据支持的陈述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将不确定性直接视为RAG幻觉风险的指标，但忽略了检索证据覆盖程度这一前提条件。作者希望厘清不确定性与RAG幻觉风险之间关系的适用边界。

Method: 在HotpotQA与SQuAD上测试四款指令微调模型；将忠实答案与幻觉答案按词数分箱匹配以控制长度混杂，比较长度分类器与困惑度的判别能力；通过改变检索深度观察检测效果变化；并构建不确定性+语义漂移的组合分数。

Result: 匹配长度后，仅用长度的分类器接近随机水平，而困惑度在定向的无支持编辑上达到0.81–0.91 AUROC；仅使用排名第一的段落时检测会减弱，加入第二段落后又恢复；组合分数改善校准，且在RAGTruth QA上仍高于随机。
