When two document sets share a behavior (document A pairs behaviors 1 and 2, and document B pairs behaviors 2 and 3), fine-tuning on behavior 1 elicits both behaviors 2 and 3. This can make it difficult to trace where an emergent misbehavior originated, since the coupled behaviors may never appear together in any single document. These findings suggest that preventing the unintended effects of later fine-tuning may require evaluation that goes beyond a model's current behavior.

</details>


### [209] [Beyond the Answer: Student-Conditioned Trace Construction for Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23831)
*Shaoang Li,Yanhang Shi,Yufei Li,Mingfu Liang,Frank Shyu,Sandeep Pandey,Luke Simon,Xi Liu,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReDiST：以"学生自身尝试"为起点构造/编辑推理轨迹，用冻结的学生匹配读者筛选，再蒸馏给学生，提升源任务与跨基准表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理轨迹即使伴随正确答案，也未必对该特定学生构成有效监督；轨迹的有效性取决于中间内容质量与其对目标学习者的可及性（可理解、可复用）。

Method: 学生条件化的轨迹构造与蒸馏框架：1) 从学生自己的作答草稿出发，对答案正确的草稿依据给定证据进行修订，对失败草稿借助已验证答案补充引导进行修复；2) 用冻结的、与学生匹配的读者模型，在给定问题、证据与候选轨迹的条件下，以答案可恢复性和已验证答案概率对原始与编辑候选进行打分排序；3) 对选中目标做蒸馏，并以辅助答案损失平衡轨迹监督与最终答案监督。构造与选择离线完成，推理仅用蒸馏后的学生模型。

Result: 在问答基准上，相比仅答案监督、原始轨迹以及所评估的轨迹选择与改写基线，ReDiST 在源任务与跨基准性能上均有提升；不同监督配方的排序在源基准与迁移基准间可能不一致。

Conclusion: 轨迹的教学效用应同时考虑内容与学习者可及性，且需跨分布评估；学生条件化的构造—筛选—蒸馏流程能带来可迁移的增益。

Abstract: A reasoning trace can accompany a correct answer without providing effective supervision for a particular student. We study how to construct traces that improve learning beyond answer-only supervision by considering both their intermediate content and their accessibility to the target learner. We introduce ReDiST, a student-conditioned trace-construction and distillation framework for evidence-rich reasoning. Starting from the student's own attempts, ReDiST revises answer-correct drafts against the supplied evidence and repairs unsuccessful drafts with additional guidance from the verified answer. A frozen student-matched reader screens and ranks original and edited candidates using answer recovery and verified-answer probability, given the question, evidence, and candidate trace. We distill the selected targets while balancing trace and final-answer supervision with an auxiliary answer loss. Construction and selection occur offline, and inference uses only the distilled student. Experiments on question-answering benchmarks show that ReDiST can improve both source-task and cross-benchmark performance over answer-only, original-trace, and the evaluated trace-selection and rewriting baselines. Across supervision recipes, performance rankings can differ between source and transfer benchmarks, highlighting the importance of evaluating trace teaching utility across distributions.

</details>


### [210] [DiscoveryRSI: Recursively Learning to Discover Valuable Hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23839)
*Jianyuan Zhong,Chenwei Wu,Zhaochen Yu,Xixian Jiang,Xuewei Li,Hao Xiao,Xilin Dai,Junyang Wang,Jiaru Zou,Zhiqing Cui,Boyuan Zheng,Jing Xiong,Zhen Zhang,Qiang Gao,Michael Morley,Luis Nakayama,Qiang Xu,Liyue Shen,Tao Gui,Leo Anthony Celi,James Zou,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出有价值且可检验研究假设的操作性定义，并构建DiscoveryRSI多智能体框架（假设树进化搜索+可执行任务编译+三重递归学习机制）与DFM-Bench评测体系；在两个自我改进轮次后假设质量由4.86升至5.39，专家评审及与GPT-6 Luna、Claude Opus 5对比显示其假设重要性与可证伪性更优，截止日后文献对应率达6.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现依赖将值得投入的假设转化为实际研究，但从这一动态演进的研究过程中持续学习仍很困难：现有工作缺乏对"有价值、可检验假设"的可操作界定，也缺乏对假设质量与累积研究进展的系统评测手段。

Method: 1) 给出假设的操作性定义：有证据支撑的、针对重要不确定性的主张，并配以可行研究方案、明确决策标准与替代解释；2) DiscoveryRSI框架：通过多智能体进化搜索构建假设树，将选定的研究方案编译为可执行的Harbor任务；3) 三重递归学习机制：假设信用优化（将科学评估归因到有记录的研究贡献）、harness回放（识别有效策略与反复出现的失败以改进流程）、再发现（将已接受的改进应用于新研究）；4) 构建DFM-Bench，在假设、修订、发展路径与研究运行四个层级上评测假设质量与累积进展。

Result: 在四个生命科学领域，自动评测显示假设质量由基础模型的4.86提升至两轮自我改进后的5.39，增量主要来自可证伪性与数据可解性；专家评审中DiscoveryRSI-27B在假设重要性以及主张是否合理、有证据边界上优于GPT-6 Luna；与模型截止后发表文献的假设对应率为6.3%，显著高于GPT-6（2.5%）与Claude Opus 5（2.3%），并在研究路径上获得更高的人工评分改进。

Conclusion: 将假设质量的可操作定义、可执行研究任务编译与递归式经验学习相结合，可让研究智能体在闭环中持续提升假设的可证伪性与证据约束性，并在专家判断与前瞻性文献对应上体现优势；DFM-Bench为多层级评测提供了标准化基础。

Abstract: Scientific discovery requires developing worthwhile hypotheses into practical investigations, yet learning from this evolving research process remains challenging. We introduce an operational definition of valuable and testable research hypotheses: evidence-grounded claims about consequential uncertainties, coupled with feasible investigations, explicit decision criteria, and alternative explanations. Our DiscoveryRSI framework develops hypothesis trees through multi-agent evolutionary search, compiles selected investigations into executable Harbor tasks, and recursively learns through three mechanisms: hypothesis credit optimization attributes scientific assessments to documented research contributions; harness replay identifies productive strategies and recurring failures to refine research workflows; and rediscovery applies accepted improvements to new investigations, generating further experience for subsequent learning. We also introduce DFM-Bench, which provides comprehensive assessment of hypothesis quality and cumulative research progress at the levels of hypotheses, revisions, development paths and research runs. Across four life-science domains, automated evaluation shows that hypothesis quality rises from 4.86 for the base model to 5.39 after two rounds of self-improvement, with the large gains in falsifiability and data resolvability. We further validate hypothesis quality through expert review, comparing our DiscoveryRSI-27B with other research agents. Preliminary human assessments show that DiscoveryRSI-27B scores higher than GPT-6 Luna on hypothesis importance and whether claims are plausible and evidence-bounded. DiscoveryRSI-27B achieved a 6.3% rate of hypothesis correspondence with research published after the model cutoff, largely outperform GPT-6 with 2.5% and Claude Opus 5 with 2.3%, while delivering greater human-rated improvement along research paths.

</details>


### [211] [Acceptance Is Not Speed: Length Inflation in Training-Free Lossy Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23818)
*Chiatzen Wang,Bill Xu,Haochen Gu,Hongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文揭示“长度膨胀”现象：松弛（有损）验证虽提高每轮接受token数，却因推理链变长使完成长度增加，抵消加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码通常以“每轮验证接受的草稿token数”衡量加速。但该指标仅在无损验证下成立（保持目标模型输出分布与期望完成长度）。松弛验证会接受无损验证会拒绝的token，可能改变分布，因此需检验该指标在松弛验证下是否仍可靠。

Method: 在12组目标-草稿模型对上，用相同提示对比现有免训练松弛验证规则与无损验证，测量完成长度、验证轮数、实际耗时与任务准确率；并分析被松弛验证额外接受的token的目标模型概率，以及准确率下降与推理预算耗尽的关联。

Result: 在各规则最松弛设置下，松弛验证平均每轮接受token数为1.34倍，但完成长度达1.54倍。接受长度更高的规则中，仅松弛验证接受的token往往目标模型概率低，助长长度膨胀；能减少验证轮数的规则通常准确率显著下降，部分失败源于持续推理耗尽给出最终答案所需的预算。

Conclusion: 松弛验证下“每轮接受token数”单独不可靠地反映解码速度；评估实际效率须同时报告完成长度与验证轮数。

Abstract: Speculative decoding typically characterizes speedup by draft tokens accepted per verification round. Lossless verification preserves the target model's output distribution and thus expected completion length, making this metric a meaningful efficiency proxy. Relaxed (lossy) verification accepts tokens that lossless verification would reject, potentially altering that distribution. We identify a length inflation phenomenon: relaxed verification lengthens completions, primarily by extending reasoning traces, largely offsetting apparent gains in accepted tokens per round. We evaluate existing training-free relaxed verification rules on twelve target-drafter pairs against lossless verification on identical prompts, measuring completion length, verifier rounds, wall-clock time, and task accuracy. At each rule's most relaxed evaluated setting, relaxed verification on average accepts $1.34\times$ as many tokens per round but produces $1.54\times$ longer completions. For rules with higher acceptance lengths, tokens accepted only under relaxed verification tend to have low target-model probability, contributing to length inflation. For rules that reduce verifier rounds, accuracy typically drops substantially. Further analysis traces some failures to continued reasoning that exhausts the budget needed to deliver a final answer. Overall, accepted tokens per round alone is an unreliable measure of decoding speed under relaxed verification. Completion length and verifier rounds must also be reported to assess actual efficiency.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [Repairing LLM-Generated Specifications with Deterministic Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22568)
*Giovanni Vaccarino,Emanuele La Malfa,Dehai Min,Hanrong Zhang,Pier Luca Lanzi,Lu Cheng,Michael J. Wooldridge,Philip S. Yu,Sara Bernardini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM作为形式化器生成的规格常"可运行但语义错误"，且求解器无法提供反馈。本文提出DRAGO修复框架，用确定性的、针对具体形式化语言的诊断替代无依据的LLM批评，并仅在诊断为误差权重严格下降时接受改写，显著降低回归率并提升答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自然语言推理问题形式化为SMT-LIB2/MiniZinc/PDDL规格时，许多规格虽能执行但约束、域、目标、谓词等语义错误；一旦执行成功，求解器错误反馈即消失。纯LLM诊断缺乏形式化真值，难以发现此类错误，且其无依据批评会使实验中11.8%的初始正确规格发生回归。

Method: DRAGO修复框架：以确定性的、形式化语言特定的诊断方法替代无依据的LLM批评；引入无参考的接受判据——仅当诊断出的误差权重严格下降时才保留改写。在覆盖逻辑推理（SMT-LIB2）、约束规划（MiniZinc）与规划（PDDL）的五个基准上评测。此外，发现Planetarium的两处渲染缺陷致其floor-tile划分中50%（3,086例）与参考形式化不一致，并发布修正后的划分。

Result: 回归率降低近一个数量级至1.3%；跨基准平均答案准确率较零样本形式化提升12.9个百分点，较LLM诊断式修复提升8.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 基于确定性、形式化特定诊断与无参考接受判据的修复流程，可在多种形式化范式下有效抑制语义错误修复带来的回归，并显著提升形式化正确性；同时揭示并修正了现有基准数据的标注缺陷。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as formalizers: given a reasoning problem expressed in natural language, they produce a formal specification that a symbolic solver can then solve, with guarantees relative to that specification. Many specifications are runnable yet semantically wrong, encoding incorrect constraints, domains, objectives, predicates, or goals, and solver-error feedback dries up once execution succeeds. Purely LLM-based diagnosis, lacking a formal ground truth, often fails to spot these errors, and its ungrounded critiques cause regressions in 11.8% of initially correct specifications in our experiments. We propose DRAGO, a repair framework that replaces this ungrounded critique with deterministic, formalism-specific diagnosis, and retains a rewrite only when the diagnosed error weight strictly decreases under a reference-free acceptance predicate. Across five benchmarks spanning logical reasoning (SMT-LIB2), constraint programming (MiniZinc), and planning (PDDL), DRAGO cuts regressions by nearly an order of magnitude, to 1.3%, while improving answer accuracy by 12.9 percentage points over zero-shot formalization and 8.7 percentage points over LLM-diagnostic repair, averaged across benchmarks. We also uncover two rendering defects in Planetarium that cause 50% (3,086 instances) of its floor-tile split to be inconsistent with the reference formalizations, and release a corrected split.

</details>


### [213] [PRIME: Towards Verifiable Evidence-Grounded Reasoning in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22426)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PRIME框架，用预承诺的证据路由与独立账本程序约束LLM智能体的核查结论，杜绝无据结论并推动达成最强可支撑结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依据组织要求核查记录时（如漏洞报告是否可进入调查）易犯两类错误：在关键检查已失败后仍接受报告，或满足于较弱结论而不再尝试其他可能成功的路径。需求常允许多种满足方式，而智能体缺乏对结论授权与证据充分性的严格约束。

Method: PRIME（Precommitted Rules for Inference from Minimal Evidence）：核查前预先固定“证据路由”，规定每类结论可被证成的路径；由独立程序记录每项检查结果，并拒绝结果无法支撑的任何结论；同时向智能体指出可支撑更强结论的检查，或由其自行执行这些检查。

Result: 在需求正确、检查位置已知且精确、停止时预算充足的前提下，完成度可达最强可支撑结论。480个公共记录任务上，仅展示需求的智能体在26.7%的任务中给出无据结论，PRIME为零；其指引与追加检查使有据结论的平均得分从仅拒答时的30.1提升至满分59.2。

Conclusion: 通过预承诺规则与独立结果账本的解耦式验证，PRIME在保证结论均有据可依的同时，系统性提升有据结论的强度，优于单纯拒答策略。

Abstract: LLM agents are used to check records against an organization's requirements, for example whether a vulnerability report is ready for investigation. Requirements often allow several ways to be met, such as a severity rating with a curator-reviewed advisory or a published advisory link. Agents fail by accepting a report after a required check has failed, or by settling for a weaker conclusion without trying another way that could still succeed. We introduce PRIME (Precommitted Rules for Inference from Minimal Evidence). Before checking, it fixes evidence routes specifying how each conclusion may be justified. A separate program records each check result and refuses any conclusion the results do not justify. It also points the agent to checks that could justify a stronger conclusion, or runs them itself. PRIME reports only conclusions authorized by its rules and ledger. With correct requirements, exact checks at known locations, and sufficient budget at stopping, completion reaches the strongest supported conclusion. On 480 public-record tasks, an agent shown the requirements reported unjustified conclusions on 26.7% of tasks, while PRIME reported none. Its directions and added checks raised the average score for justified conclusions from 30.1, with refusal alone, to the maximum of 59.2 out of 100.

</details>


### [214] [SimOPD: Relational Knowledge Transfer for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23306)
*Kai Tang,Xing Zheng,Haobo Wang,Guangcheng Zhu,Shenzhi Yang,Zhengqing Zang,Yingfan Ma,Bowen Song,Weiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SimOPD，利用师生对成对学生生成回复的相似度分布进行知识蒸馏，使学生无需投影即可在自身表征空间中匹配教师内部知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy蒸馏中提取教师有效监督困难：token概率监督未充分利用教师内部知识，直接隐状态匹配迫使学生对齐教师特征空间并使跨架构迁移复杂化。

Method: 受对比学习利用实例间关系的启发，SimOPD用学生生成回复构建成对记忆库，从教师内部表征中抽取监督为相似度分布，学生则在自身表示空间内匹配这些分布，从而保留表征自由度并免去跨模型投影。

Result: 大量实验表明，SimOPD在同架构与跨架构设置下分别较最强基线整体平均提升最高2.6与3.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 以回复相似度为媒介传递教师内部知识，可在不进行特征空间对齐的情况下实现有效且架构无关的on-policy蒸馏。

Abstract: A key challenge in on-policy distillation (OPD) is extracting effective supervision from teacher knowledge. Supervision from token probabilities may underutilize internal knowledge, while direct hidden-state matching forces the student to shift to the teacher's feature space and complicates cross-architecture transfer. Inspired by contrastive learning's use of relationships between instances, we propose \textbf{SimOPD} to transfer teacher internal knowledge through response similarities. Specifically, SimOPD uses paired memory banks of student-generated responses to extract supervision from the teacher's internal representations as similarity distributions. The student then matches these distributions within its own representation space, retaining representational freedom and removing the need for cross-model projection. Extensive experiments show that SimOPD improves the overall average over the strongest baselines by up to 2.6 and 3.1 percentage points in same- and cross-architecture settings.

</details>


### [215] [GlobeBench: Evaluating Text-Based World Model Capabilities for Scalable Environment Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22708)
*Nicholas Saban,Charvi Bannur,Mariya Vasileva,Darshan Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GlobeBench——首个标准化文本世界模型基准，含五领域19项仿真技能共1632个任务，最佳模型 GPT-6 Astra 成功率仅49.57%，依赖对象传播场景平均5.1%，631个任务无模型可解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于文本的世界模型已显示出模拟复杂强化学习环境状态转移的潜力，但缺乏可比较、可研究和可改进这些模型的标准化基准，因此需要构建统一评测平台。

Method: 构建 GlobeBench 基准：1632 个任务，覆盖 5 个独立领域与 19 项仿真技能；任务要求模型基于完整智能体轨迹预测特定动作的环境响应，即理解先前状态变化、遵循环境规则、预测动作对相关与无关对象的影响；采用确定性门控加语义评判的混合架构，将预测响应对照由环境执行导出的参考结果进行评分；评测 20 个通用大模型与专用文本世界模型。

Result: GPT-6 Astra 取得最高任务成功率 49.57%；动作影响环境范围越大，模型准确率越低；当智能体的改动需传播至依赖对象时，20 个模型平均成功率降至 5.1%；尽管各模型在不同任务上各有优势，仍有 631 个任务无任何模型解出。

Conclusion: 现有通用 LLM 与专用文本世界模型作为世界模型仍存在显著能力缺口，尤其在长程状态传播与规则约束下的多步推理方面；GlobeBench 为后续比较与改进提供标准化评测基础。

Abstract: Text-based world models have recently shown potential to simulate complex RL environment state transitions for a diverse set of use cases. However, no standardized benchmark exists to compare, study and improve these models. Through this paper, we attempt to solve this problem by building GlobeBench, a novel benchmark with 1,632 tasks across five separate domains and nineteen simulation skills. The tasks check whether models can predict the environment's response to a specific action given the full agent trajectory by understanding earlier state changes, following environment rules, and predicting the effects of actions on both related and unrelated objects. We compare the predicted environment response with an execution-derived reference, using a hybrid architecture with both deterministic gates and a semantic judge. Among 20 general-purpose LLMs and specialized text world models, GPT-6 Astra achieves the highest task success rate at 49.57%. We find that model accuracy decreases as actions affect a larger portion of the environment. Specifically, when the agent's changes must propagate to dependent objects, task success drops to an average of 5.1% across all 20 models. Although model strengths differ across tasks, 631 tasks remain unsolved by any model, suggesting a significant gap in general LLM capabilities as a world model.

</details>


### [216] [Self-Reflective Chain-of-Thought (SRCoT): Evidence-Based Text Generation via a Closed-Loop Retrieve--Reflect--Revise Pipeline on Edge LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23311)
*Fabian Broszio,Michael Hoffmann,Adrian Paschke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出模块化架构SRCoT，将生成组织为“检索—反思—修订”闭环，并基于溯源标注存储与BM25检索实现可审查的生成控制流程；论文定位为研究性概念验证，而非模型创新或性能比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可生成流畅但缺乏来源支撑、或与本地操作约束相冲突的输出；作者希望使生成过程具备可追溯性与可审查性（report-level inspectability），将检索与控制从模型接口中分离。

Method: SRCoT（Self-Reflective Chain-of-Thought）架构：将环境状态、持久知识、积累经验与短期记忆分离为带溯源标签的存储；以BM25检索提供本地上下文；多轮生成过程中暴露中间草稿、缺失信息标记、后续查询及可选的“主张—证据”映射，据此触发再检索、修订、澄清或拒答决策。在完全合成的两个场景（急救医疗服务、慕尼黑应急行动模拟Munich-Sim）中开展基于轨迹的定性案例分析，本地原型运行于128GB内存的Apple M4系列笔记本。

Result: 案例轨迹展示了带来源标注的文档生成、显式信息缺口标记与结构化调度式输出；同时暴露了溯源归属错误、证据覆盖不完整以及在提示约束下仍可能出现的不安全假设。作者明确声明结果仅体现报告级可审查性与本地执行能力，不证明比较优势、幻觉减少、临床有效性或运行安全性。

Conclusion: SRCoT的主要贡献在于可审查的控制流程而非新模型；其将检索与控制同模型接口解耦，但跨模型行为仍待评估，且作为研究性概念验证需人工复核。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent outputs that lack source support or conflict with local operational constraints. We introduce Self-Reflective Chain-of-Thought (SRCoT), a modular architecture that organizes generation as a closed Retrieve--Reflect--Revise loop. SRCoT separates environment state, persistent knowledge, accumulated experience, and short-term memory into provenance-labeled stores. BM25 retrieval supplies local context; subsequent passes expose intermediate drafts, missing-information markers, follow-up queries, and optional claim--evidence mappings. These artifacts inform retrieval, revision, clarification, or abstention decisions. We examine the architecture through qualitative, trace-based case studies in two fully synthetic settings: emergency medical services and Munich-Sim, an emergency-operations simulation. Local prototype runs on an Apple M4-family laptop with 128 GB memory illustrate source-labeled documentation, explicit gap marking, and structured dispatch-style outputs. The traces also reveal provenance-assignment errors, incomplete evidence coverage, and unsafe assumptions that remain possible despite prompting constraints. These cases demonstrate report-level inspectability and local execution, not comparative superiority, hallucination reduction, clinical validity, or operational safety. The architecture separates retrieval and control from the model interface, but behavior across different models remains to be evaluated. SRCoT is a research proof of concept requiring human review, with its principal contribution lying in an inspectable control procedure rather than a new model.

</details>


### [217] [AI-Driven Reproducibility and Analytical Robustness in Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23845)
*Shicheng Liu,Krista Opsahl-Ong,Andrew Drozdov,Julian Killingback,Arnav Singhvi,Ronak Pradeep,Michael Bendersky,James C. Dickerson,Monica S. Lam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出用LLM智能体自动化医学研究的可复现性与稳健性分析：构建SEER-Repro基准（20篇SEER论文及所需数据资源），智能体复现了2,190条原子结论中的77.4%，并通过智能体流程识别影响结论的分析选择，在9.5%完全复现的结论中发现潜在关键性分析选择并提请专家复核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复现与稳健的研究结论是科学进步的基础，但此类评估通常依赖昂贵的人工操作，难以规模化开展；作者希望验证LLM智能体能否自动完成复现与稳健性分析。

Method: 以SEER数据库为依托的医学案例研究：（1）构建SEER-Repro基准，包含20篇已发表SEER论文及其复现所需数据与资源；（2）开发AI智能体复现论文结论并提供可执行基线；（3）设计智能体分析流程，识别每项结论背后的分析选择、提出可辩护的替代方案并重跑分析，以判断这些选择是否改变结论。

Result: 智能体成功复现2,190条原子结论中的77.4%；在完全复现的结论中，9.5%被识别出可能具有关键影响的分析选择，并被标记供专家审查。

Conclusion: LLM智能体可支持科学审阅：复现研究结论、系统检验其稳健性，并将专家注意力引导至潜在关键的分析选择上。

Abstract: Reproducible and analytically robust research findings are essential to scientific progress, but evaluating these properties typically requires costly manual effort. We investigate whether LLM agents can help automate reproduction and robustness analysis, using a medical case study grounded in the Surveillance, Epidemiology, and End Results (SEER) database, a widely used U.S. cancer registry underlying tens of thousands of published studies. First, we introduce SEER-Repro, a benchmark of 20 published SEER-based papers together with the data and resources required to reproduce their findings. Second, we create an AI agent that successfully reproduces 77.4% of 2,190 atomic claims, while providing executable baselines for further analysis. Third, we develop an agentic analysis pipeline to test the robustness of the medical findings by identifying analytical choices underlying each finding, proposing defensible alternatives, and rerunning the analysis to determine whether those choices affect the conclusion. Across published papers in SEER-Repro, our pipeline identifies potentially consequential analytical choices in 9.5% of fully reproduced findings and flags them for expert review. These results show how LLM agents can support scientific review by reproducing findings, systematically testing their robustness, and directing expert attention toward potentially consequential analytical choices.

</details>


### [218] [Not All Graphs Are Maps: A Task-Agnostic Exploration Framework for Mobile GUI Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22672)
*Adamo Orsini,Seyed Mehdi Ayyoubzadeh,Sadman Rakib Pinon,Hannuo Zhang,Koosha Gholipour Baradari,Yuanhao Yu,Xinxin Zuo,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TAG-Nav框架，为GUI智能体附加可复用的"目的地记忆"（图谱导航），并构建DestBench仅导航评测协议，验证图谱记忆对导航成功率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动GUI智能体只能感知当前屏幕并执行局部动作，缺乏持久的应用专属导航知识（功能位于何处、哪些页面包含功能、重复访问中哪些屏幕等价）。同时，完整任务评测会与后续执行动作混淆，难以单独评估路由查找能力。

Method: TAG-Nav：任务无关图导航框架，包裹现有GUI智能体骨干；在任何评测指令出现前，通过离线探索蒸馏出应用功能、目标页面与途经路线的紧凑记录；任务目的地被覆盖时，检索目的地并给出路线引导，无需任务专属示范。DestBench：仅导航协议，只判断智能体是否到达正确的动作前目的地屏幕；并对比无记忆、随机、自动BFS与干净图谱记忆四种条件。

Result: 自动BFS图谱记忆使UI-TARS-1.5-7B严格导航成功率从52.4%升至58.3%；Qwen-VL-Max在同一条件下从59.5%升至71.4%。

Conclusion: 目的地覆盖度与路线纯净度显著决定图谱记忆的价值，图谱质量应作为可测量变量，而非探索的默认收益；TAG-Nav与DestBench共同将目的地导航与完整任务完成解耦评估。

Abstract: Modern mobile GUI agents can perceive visible screens and execute local actions, but they still lack persistent app-specific navigation knowledge: where functions live, which pages contain them, and which screens are equivalent across repeated visits. We introduce TAG-Nav, a Task-Agnostic Graph Navigation framework that wraps existing GUI-agent backbones with reusable destination memory. Offline exploration is distilled into compact records of app functions, target pages, and waypoint routes before any evaluated instruction is seen. When a future task's destination is covered, TAG-Nav retrieves the destination and provides route guidance without task-specific demonstrations. This separates mobile GUI destination navigation from full task completion: many tasks share the prefix of reaching the right page, while final actions may enter content, send data, make purchases, delete information, or change sensitive settings. To evaluate route finding without downstream execution confounds, we introduce DestBench, a navigation-only protocol that asks whether an agent reaches the correct pre-action destination screen. DestBench enables a controlled graph-memory study comparing no-memory agents with random, automated BFS, and clean graph memory. Graph-derived memory improves strict navigation success rate of UI-TARS-1.5-7B from 52.4% without graph memory to 58.3% with automated BFS memory, and improves Qwen-VL-Max success rate from 59.5% to 71.4% on the same conditions. Together, TAG-Nav and DestBench show that destination coverage and route cleanliness strongly shape graph-memory value, making graph quality a measured variable rather than an assumed benefit of exploration.

</details>


### [219] [Reasoning Models Can Control Their Chains of Thought When They Tell Themselves To](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23257)
*Uriel Dolev,Idan Schwartz,Yoav Goldberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大模型对自身思维链（CoT）属性的控制能力（CoT可控性）此前被评测为极低，但本文指出这源于"用户提示中直接给约束"这一朴素设定；若把约束作为自指令写入CoT内部，模型可控性显著提升，且该能力可被引导并用于规避CoT监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT可控性同时关系到推理模型的实用性与可监督性。既有工作总结报告各模型可控性普遍很低，作者质疑这一结论是否反映模型固有缺陷，还是源于约束指定方式（用户提示）的不当。

Method: 提出Self-Instructed CoT Controllability评测范式：将约束作为自指令置于模型CoT内部，测量其遵循程度，并与标准"用户提示给约束"的设定对比。进一步测试两类变体：(1) 仅给通用提醒（generic reminder）要求遵循用户约束；(2) 通过用户提示中的简单指令，把有效的自指令"引导（bootstrap）"进推理过程，以便在无法访问推理通道时也能改善控制。

Result: 自指令条件下模型可控性显著高于标准用户指令；即便只是通用提醒也能显著提升可控性；有效的自指令可通过用户提示中的简单指令被引导进推理，从而在无权访问推理通道时仍改善控制；在真实监控场景中，借助自指令实现的可控性可被用于规避监控。

Conclusion: 低CoT可控性并非推理模型的固有限制，而很大程度上是约束表述方式的产物；自指令能大幅提升可控性，但同时意味着CoT可控性可被利用以逃避监控，对CoT可监控性构成风险。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) controllability, the ability of reasoning models to control properties of their CoT, is consequential for both their usefulness and reliable oversight. Recent work evaluates this capability by measuring whether models follow CoT constraints given in the user prompt, and reports very low controllability across models. We argue that this low controllability may not reflect an inherent inability of reasoning models to control their CoT, but instead result from naively specifying constraints in the user prompt. To test this, we introduce Self-Instructed CoT Controllability: a complementary evaluation that places the constraint as a self-instruction inside the model's CoT and measures its ability to follow it. We show that, under self-instruction, models achieve significantly higher controllability than with standard user instructions. Even a generic reminder to follow the user's constraint substantially improves controllability. Moreover, effective self-instructions can be bootstrapped into the reasoning through a simple instruction in the user prompt, enabling improved control even without access to the reasoning channel. Finally, using self-instruction, we demonstrate in a realistic monitoring setup that CoT controllability can be leveraged for monitor evasion, posing a risk to CoT monitorability.

</details>


### [220] [Sep-Latent: Separator Tokens as Natural Compression Anchors for Implicit Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22924)
*Yinlong Xu,Jianfeng Huang,Zhengdong Zhou,Mingjie Lin,Fuyu Lv,Dan Ou,Hongxia Xu,Jian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Sep-Latent自适应隐式推理框架，以分隔符token作为结构语义锚点，结合Attention-Decay机制与多层蒸馏，实现动态推理深度，在GSM8k-Aug上以平均5.4个token达到56.8%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式Chain-of-Thought带来显著计算开销与token冗长问题；现有隐式连续潜空间推理方法（如Coconut、CODI）依赖固定长度潜序列且存在表征塌缩，效率与性能受限。

Method: 1) 引入分隔符token作为结构性语义锚点；2) 采用Attention-Decay机制强制模型在训练中将推理逻辑聚合压缩至这些锚点；3) 通过跨所有层的多级蒸馏对齐显式与隐式推理路径，保证逻辑一致性；4) 基于查询复杂度自适应终止潜思考阶段，实现动态推理深度。

Result: 在GSM8k-Aug基准上以平均仅5.4个token取得56.8%准确率，效率显著优于固定长度隐式模型并缩小与显式CoT的性能差距；PCA分析与符号变体测试证实其具备语义接地性与更强的抗扰动鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Sep-Latent通过锚点式潜空间压缩与自适应深度控制，缓解了固定长度隐式推理的表征塌缩问题，在保持逻辑一致性的同时大幅提升推理效率，为高效隐式推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in complex reasoning through explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT), but the associated computational overhead and token verbosity remain significant barriers to efficiency. While implicit reasoning in continuous latent spaces offers a promising alternative, existing methods like Coconut and CODI often rely on fixed-length latent sequences and suffer from representational collapse. In this paper, we propose Sep-Latent, an adaptive implicit reasoning framework that utilizes separator tokens as structural semantic anchors. By introducing an Attention-Decay mechanism, we force the model to aggregate and compress reasoning logic into these anchors during training. We then align explicit and implicit pathways through multi-level distillation across all layers to ensure logical consistency. Crucially, Sep-Latent enables dynamic reasoning depth by adaptively terminating the latent thinking phase based on query complexity. Experimental results on the GSM8k-Aug benchmark show that Sep-Latent achieves an accuracy of 56.8\% with an average of only 5.4 tokens, significantly outperforming prior fixed-length implicit models in efficiency while narrowing the performance gap with explicit CoT. Further analysis via PCA and symbolic variant testing confirms that our method ensures semantic grounding and exhibits superior robustness to perturbations.

</details>


### [221] [ScoutRoute: Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Routing Using Internal Language Model Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23443)
*Rajdeep Pandhare,Delzaan Bharucha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScoutRoute 是一个轻量级事后路由框架，利用廉价 scout 模型单次前向传播提取的内部信号（隐状态探针 + logit 置信度）为异构独立训练的 LLM 集合选择专家，无需联合训练、无需多次推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构、独立训练的 LLM 专家集合上，现有路由方法依赖联合训练的 gating 网络、基于 prompt 的预测器或昂贵的多模型推理，灵活性与效率受限。

Method: 以廉价 scout 模型进行一次前向传播，提取隐状态表示，经轻量逻辑回归探针估计跨模型胜任度；同时融合 logit 导出的置信度度量，构成领域感知路由策略进行专家选择，且不改动底层语言模型。

Result: 在 5 个开放权重模型、4 个基准数据集（数学推理、代码生成、常识推理、多学科知识）上，ScoutRoute 稳定优于单一路由信号，且仅需一次 scout 推理；胜任度探针的校准优于常规置信度度量。

Conclusion: 内部语言模型表示可为异构模型间的精准、高效专家路由提供可迁移信息，支持低成本的事后路由部署。

Abstract: Efficient expert routing is a fundamental challenge in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) systems, particularly for heterogeneous collections of independently trained Large Language Mod els (LLMs). Existing routing approaches typically rely on jointly trained gating networks, prompt-based predictors, or computationally expensive multi-model inference, limiting their flexibility and efficiency. This paper presents ScoutRoute, a lightweight post-hoc routing framework that predicts expert competence using internal language model signals extracted from a single forward pass through an inexpensive scout model. Hidden-state representations are used to estimate cross-model competence through lightweight logistic probes, while logit-derived confidence measures are incorporated within a domain-aware routing strategy for efficient expert selec tion without modifying the underlying language models. ScoutRoute isevaluatedon fiveopen-weightlanguagemodels across fourbenchmark datasets covering mathematical reasoning, code generation, commonsense reasoning, and multidis ciplinary knowledge. Experimental results show that ScoutRoute consistently outperforms individual routing signals while requiring only a single scout-model inference. The proposed competence probes also achieve superior calibration compared with conventional confidence measures, demonstrating that internal language model representations provide transferable information for accurate and efficient expert routing across heterogeneous language models

</details>


### [222] [Robust Function Calling for LLM Agents Under Interface Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23342)
*Aniruddha Chattopadhyay,Kanishka Halder,Partha Sarathi Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"接口漂移"问题，并给出免训练修复层 SchemaShield-Lite，在 Qwen3.5-9B 上显著提升漂移条件下的工具调用准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强的语言模型智能体依赖接口文本（工具描述、参数模式、推理时可见的候选工具列表）。这些接口在部署后常发生变化，而工具语义本身不变，从而给函数调用系统带来实际的可靠性问题。作者将此类失效模式定义为 interface drift，并探究能否在不重训基座模型的前提下，用轻量、免训练的修复层恢复鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 SchemaShield-Lite，由三部分组成：(1) schema 校验；(2) 针对工具名缺失的候选集重试；(3) 一次性约束修复（one-shot constrained repair）。整体为训练无关的后处理修复层。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-9B 上，DICE-300 准确率由 0.717 提升至 0.813，BFCL-200 由 0.580 提升至 0.805；分别修复 19/22 与 33/45 个漂移导致的失败，未观察到修复带来的损害。消融显示候选集漂移（尤其是干扰工具）是主要损伤来源，而仅修改描述与 schema 危害较小；简单重试无法解释增益。

Conclusion: 验证—修复机制可作为部署型 LLM 智能体工具使用中切实可行的鲁棒性层，且候选集稳定性比描述/模式文本更关键。

Abstract: Tool-augmented language-model agents rely on interface text: tool descriptions, argument schemas, and the candidate tool list shown at inference time. These interfaces often change after deployment even when the underlying tool semantics remain fixed, creating a practical reliability problem for function-calling systems. We study this failure mode as interface drift and evaluate whether a lightweight, training-free repair layer can recover robustness without retraining the base model. Our method, SchemaShield-Lite, combines schema validation, candidate-set retry for missing tool names, and one-shot constrained repair. On Qwen3.5-9B, it raises DICE-300 accuracy from 0.717 to 0.813 and BFCL-200 accuracy from 0.580 to 0.805, recovering 19/22 and 33/45 drift-induced failures with no observed repair harms. Naive retry does not explain the gains. Ablations show that candidate-set drift, especially distractor tools, accounts for most observed damage, while description and schema edits alone are less harmful. The results position validation-and-repair as a practical robustness layer for deployed LLM-agent tool use.

</details>


### [223] [Annotrace: A Workbench for Step-Level Annotation of Agent Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23284)
*Arun Josephraj Arokiaraj,Chenyi Huang,Sahan Bulathwela,Zekun Wu,Bin Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Annotrace 是一个将多种 LLM 智能体轨迹格式统一为有序嵌套步骤列表的标注工具，支持人机协同编码本构建与标注一致性测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体失败研究均需手工标注轨迹却各自重复造轮子；可观测平台缺乏结构化标注能力，通用标注工具又不理解步骤、委托、工具调用等执行单元，导致"最小标注单元"界定不清。

Method: 读取六种轨迹格式（含 OpenTelemetry GenAI、LangSmith、Langfuse），将每次运行转化为有序嵌套步骤列表；标注者自建编码本，前沿模型基于其样本轨迹提出初始编码本，并先作答、接受与人类相同的问题，形成人机联合标注流程；支持任意两位标注者（人或 AI）间的一致性度量。

Result: 在多智能体问答语料上精确复现了此前人工工作确定的 235 个标注单元；两个模型标注相同轨迹的一致率为 91%，κ=0.82。

Conclusion: Annotrace 统一了轨迹标注的单元定义与工具链，并验证了模型间标注具有较高一致性，为智能体失败分析提供了可复用、可扩展的标注基础设施。

Abstract: Every study of why LLM agents fail begins by labelling traces by hand, and every such study builds its own throwaway tool. Observability platforms record agent traces but offer no structured labelling; annotation tools label text but have no notion of a step, a delegation or a tool call, leaving it unclear what the smallest unit of execution to label should be. Annotrace reads six trace formats, including OpenTelemetry GenAI, LangSmith and Langfuse, and turns each run into an ordered, nested list of steps. The annotator writes their own labelling codebook; a frontier model proposes a starting set from a sample of their own traces, answers first, and is asked exactly the questions the human is asked, making annotation a joint human-AI process. Annotrace measures agreement between any two annotators, human or AI. On a multi-agent question-answering corpus it reproduces a previous manual effort's 235 annotation units exactly, and two models labelling the same traces agree 91% of the time at $κ= 0.82$.

</details>


### [224] [Are Large Reasoning Models Good Everyday Reasoners? A Comparison with Non-Reasoning Models on Logical Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22538)
*Kentaro Ozeki,Risako Ando,Takanobu Morishita,Hirohiko Abe,Koji Mineshima,Mitsuhiro Okada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评测大型推理模型（LRMs）在日常逻辑推理（偏离教科书形式逻辑的语言学与语用推理现象）上的表现，发现推理模型在偏离形式逻辑时性能退化，部分带CoT提示的非推理模型反而更优，且额外指令提示对推理行为的控制有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRMs 借助测试时计算在数学与科学基准上表现强劲并已被实际部署，但其在日常逻辑推理——常偏离教科书形式逻辑——上的能力仍缺乏探索，现有评测未覆盖该类推理。

Method: 构建基于语言学与语用推理现象的基准，系统评测推理模型与日常推理模式的对齐程度；并在受控提示条件下对比推理模型与非推理模型（含CoT提示）的表现。

Result: (1) 当日常推理偏离形式逻辑规则时，推理模型表现下降；(2) 部分带链式思维提示的非推理模型在日常推理上优于同类推理模型；(3) 附加指令提示对推理行为的控制能力有限。

Conclusion: 当前推理模型无法可靠捕捉日常推理，暴露出后训练阶段的局限，需改进训练目标并在多样化推理形式上开展评测。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) that leverage test-time computation achieve strong performance on mathematical and scientific benchmarks and are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. However, their ability to perform \textit{everyday logical reasoning}, which often departs from textbook formal logic, remains underexplored. In this work, we systematically evaluate how well reasoning models align with everyday reasoning patterns by introducing a benchmark based on linguistic and pragmatic reasoning phenomena. We also compare reasoning models with non-reasoning models under controlled prompting conditions. We find that, in many cases, (1) reasoning models show degraded performance in everyday reasoning when such reasoning deviates from formal logical rules, (2) some non-reasoning models with chain-of-thought prompting outperform comparable reasoning models in everyday reasoning, and (3) prompting with additional instructions provides limited control over reasoning behavior. These findings suggest that current reasoning models do not reliably capture everyday reasoning and point to limitations in post-training, motivating improved training objectives and evaluation across diverse forms of reasoning.

</details>


### [225] [Beyond Reward Scoring: Online Multi-Agent Preference Learning for Multimodal Response Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23256)
*Ziyun Zou,Jiwoo Hong,Yifan Lu,Shao Tang,Weiyi Wu,Aman Gupta,Jin Tian,Imran Razzak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PrefMind 在线双智能体强化学习框架，让响应生成器（Generator）同时具备偏好判断与纠错能力，并将这种偏好理解显式地转化为更好的生成，在三个模型规模上同时提升独立偏好判断与下游生成表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分数的 RLHF 多模态偏好对齐把偏好监督压缩为标量奖励信号：奖励模型仅用于打分，响应模型只被训练去追求更高分数，而很少被显式训练去理解人类偏好背后的原因。作者由此追问：若将偏好理解内化为响应模型自身能力，能否反过来提升其生成质量。

Method: 在三个模型规模上开展实验，评估独立偏好判断与下游生成两类指标。

Result: 在三个模型规模上均取得提升，独立偏好判断能力和下游响应生成质量同时改善。

Conclusion: 将偏好理解作为内部能力而非外部标量信号，可有效提升多模态响应的生成质量，为偏好对齐提供了一条超越传统“奖励模型打分”范式的新路径。

Abstract: Multimodal preference alignment often follows a score-based RLHF pipeline: a reward model is first trained to learn human preferences, then used to score on-policy responses and optimize the response model's generation. This turns preference supervision into a scalar signal for generation: a model may learn to produce answers with higher preference scores, but is rarely trained explicitly to learn the reasons underlying human preferences. We therefore ask whether making preference understanding an internal capability of the response model can improve its generation. To investigate this question, we propose \textbf{PrefMind}, an online dual-agent reinforcement learning framework with two trainable agents: a response Generator and a preference Critic. Given a pair of candidate responses, the agents analyze and discuss which response better aligns with human preference. Human labels supervise their judgments and corrections. For pairs on which both the Generator and revised Critic correctly identify the human-preferred response, the Generator produces an improved answer, and the Critic compares it against the original preferred response to provide a generation reward. Both agents are jointly updated from interactions sampled with their current policies. Through this process, \textbf{PrefMind} trains the Generator to understand human preferences via explicit judgment and correction, and to translate this understanding into better responses. Experiments across three model scales show improvements in both standalone preference judgment and downstream response generation.

</details>


### [226] [Monitor-Validity Failures: LLM Safety Evidence Paths at Evolving Framework Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23362)
*Junchi Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"修复边界签名"问题与"金丝雀契约"协议，在公开框架修复中确认18个证据路径失效案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM应用依赖运行时监控器检查工具调用、轨迹、流、检测器输出与验证器输入，但存在接口可靠性问题：框架路径仍在运行，而与策略相关的证据却未到达决策点。此问题不同于越狱或检测器错误，因为证据已被识别却与执行环节断开。

Method: 提出canary contracts（金丝雀契约）协议：使用父/固定确认单元，将已知载荷仅置于变更后的证据表面，并记录监控可见性与下游决策，用于动作边界案例。

Result: 在公开框架修复中确认18个证据路径失效，覆盖11个LLM或智能体安全项目组、4个实现生态。其中9个为既有边界修复，8个为新表面策略覆盖缺口，1个为仅边界证据案例。14个触及E1执行边界：7个原生动作结果、6个配置执行边界、1个合成消费者后果；其余影响验证器、安全提示或可观测性通道。案例形成载波、适配器、权威与表面四类族。

Conclusion: 常规应用行为可保持完好，而安全专用证据路径却错过其决策点；监控假设需在框架演进的模式、流、工具和适配器层面进行回归测试。

Abstract: Modern LLM applications rely on runtime monitors that inspect tool calls, traces, streams, detector outputs, and verifier inputs. This creates an interface-reliability problem: a framework path may remain operational while policy-relevant evidence never reaches its decision point. In one representative case, a regex detector returned a positive match, but the framework stranded the result before the block decision. This differs from jailbreaks and detector errors because the evidence is already recognized yet disconnected from enforcement. We study this repair-boundary signature with canary contracts: parent/fixed confirmation units that place a known payload only on the changed evidence surface and record monitor visibility together with the downstream decision for action-boundary cases. Applying the protocol to public framework repairs, we confirm 18 evidence-path failures across 11 LLM or agent safety project groups and four implementation ecosystems. Nine are established-boundary repairs, eight are new-surface policy-coverage gaps, and one is a boundary-only evidence case. Fourteen reach E1 enforcement boundaries: seven native action outcomes, six configured enforcement boundaries, and one synthetic-consumer consequence. The remaining cases affect verifier, safety-prompt, or observability channels. The cases form carrier, adapter, authority, and surface families and show how ordinary application behavior can remain intact while a safety-specific evidence path misses its decision. Exact revisions, deterministic outcomes, negative controls, and a frozen prospective audit document the confirmation rule. Monitor assumptions therefore need regression tests at the schemas, streams, tools, and adapters that frameworks evolve.

</details>


### [227] [ECHOES: Seeing Beyond the Current Image via Recursive History Compression and Privileged On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22408)
*Tengda Guo,Jiawang Hu,Yifei Tao,Yin Wang,Haotian Hu,Fanyi Wang,Liujuan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ECHOES 利用训练期的特权多图信息作为教师信号，通过 on-policy 蒸馏与强化学习联合优化，将长程 GUI 智能体的视觉历史压缩为文本历史状态，在部署时无需保留历史截图即可保持长程推理可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程 GUI 智能体依赖视觉历史维持任务状态，但保留历史截图会显著增加视觉 token 用量与推理成本；而纯文本摘要又可能丢失关键状态信息，损害长程可靠性。因此需要在降低视觉 token 开销的同时保证跨模态状态保真度。

Method: 提出 ECHOES：采用 Compression-Think-Action (CTA) 格式递归更新文本历史状态并进行推理；同一模型在给定特权多图输入时充当教师，对学生生成的 CTA rollout 进行密集 token 级 on-policy 蒸馏 (OPD)；设计感知-记忆保真度 (PMF) Gap 引导的 OPD-RL 框架，动态平衡特权蒸馏与任务奖励优化，并周期性更新教师以持续改进；同时构建 ECHOESBMK 诊断基准，基于真实 GUI 智能体失败轨迹，联合评估视觉感知与跨模态状态保持能力。

Result: 在四个 GUI 基准及 ECHOESBMK 上，任务性能与记忆保真度均显著提升；部署时不保留历史截图的情况下仍达到有竞争力的表现，并具有更优的性能-推理成本权衡；对未见过的视觉规划任务表现出正向零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 训练期特权视觉信息可有效指导文本历史压缩与长程推理，使智能体在部署阶段摆脱历史截图依赖，实现性能与推理成本的良好折衷。

Abstract: Long-horizon GUI agents rely on visual history to maintain task states, but retaining historical screenshots increases visual-token usage and inference costs, while textual summaries can discard critical state information, undermining long-horizon reliability. We propose ECHOES, which leverages training-time privileged information to guide textual history compression and strengthen long-horizon reasoning. Specifically, ECHOES adopts a Compression-Think-Action (CTA) format to recursively update a textual history state and reason. The same model, given privileged multi-image inputs, serves as a teacher that provides dense token-level on-policy distillation on student-generated CTA rollouts. Our Perception-Memory Fidelity (PMF) Gap-guided OPD-RL framework dynamically balances privileged distillation and task-reward optimization, with periodic teacher updates supporting continued improvement. We further introduce ECHOESBMK, a fine-grained diagnostic benchmark derived from real GUI agent failure trajectories to jointly evaluate visual perception and cross-modal state retention. Experiments on four GUI benchmarks and ECHOESBMK demonstrate substantial gains in task performance and memory fidelity. Without retaining historical screenshots at deployment, ECHOES achieves competitive performance with a favorable performance-inference-cost trade-off and exhibits positive zero-shot transfer to unseen visual planning tasks.

</details>


### [228] [Rethinking GRPO for Large Reasoning Model Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23085)
*Jiawei Chen,Yi Zhang,Jiawei Du,Zhonghou Lv,Tairan Chen,Junyuan Shang,Jinchang Hou,Xiao Yang,Yu Tian,Chenfu Bao,Chao Yu,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对GRPO安全对齐中"优势坍缩"与"不安全多样化"两种失效模式，提出Beacon框架（SSR+SUDA）恢复安全学习信号并解耦安全与效用，在4个模型变体、8个基准上安全错误率最多降低32.83个百分点且过拒答率低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRM）推理能力强但仍易产生不安全输出。GRPO虽广泛用于安全对齐，但其组内相对优化机制提供的安全导向学习信号不足，存在两类失效：同质化rollout组导致优势趋于零的"优势坍缩"，以及不安全行为持续存在并愈发多样的"不安全多样化"。理论分析表明，行为一致性会削弱组内安全对比，而相对信号消失时熵正则可加剧不安全响应的多样性。

Method: 提出Beacon框架，含两个组件：(1) Safety Signal Recovery (SSR)，结合优势引导的轨迹过滤与安全参考监督，恢复有效的安全学习信号；(2) Safety-Utility Decoupled Alignment (SUDA)，采用类别感知奖励与仅对良性样本的KL锚定，在保持安全的同时缓解过度拒答。

Result: 在4个Qwen3与DeepSeek-R1-Distill变体、8个基准上实验，框架取得最佳的平均安全性与推理性能，平均安全错误率最多降低32.83个百分点，同时保持较低的过度拒答率。

Conclusion: GRPO的安全对齐瓶颈源于组内相对信号的结构性缺陷；通过信号恢复与安全-效用解耦可有效提升LRM安全对齐效果，并兼顾推理能力与有用性。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) exhibit strong reasoning capabilities but remain vulnerable to unsafe outputs. While Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is widely used for safety alignment, its group-relative optimization may provide insufficient safety-directed learning signals. In this paper, we identify two failure modes: advantage collapse, where homogeneous rollout groups yield vanishing advantages, and unsafe diversification, where unsafe behaviors persist and become increasingly diverse. Our theoretical analysis reveals that behavioral consistency weakens within-group safety contrasts, while entropy regularization can diversify unsafe responses when relative signals vanish. To address these challenges, we propose Beacon, comprising Safety Signal Recovery (SSR) and Safety-Utility Decoupled Alignment (SUDA). SSR combines advantage-guided trajectory filtering and safe reference supervision to recover effective safety learning signals, while SUDA employs category-aware rewards and benign-only KL anchoring to mitigate over-refusal without compromising safety. Experiments across four Qwen3 and DeepSeek-R1-Distill variants and eight benchmarks show that our framework achieves the best average safety and reasoning performance, reducing average safety error by up to 32.83 percentage points while maintaining low over-refusal.

</details>


### [229] [Where Does the Performance Go? Understanding and Recovering Performance Regressions in C-to-Rust Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23044)
*Zhiqiang Yuan,Chong Wang,Zhenhao Zhou,Junwei Liu,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了C→Rust转译（\crust{}）保持行为但损失性能的问题：在20个真实C项目及其Rust转译上，8个分析项目中12/19个操作变慢，最高达62.2%；通过执行开销剖面、优化后LLVM IR和优化remark对比，归纳出9类反复出现的性能问题；进而提出基于LLM的\ourtool{}，将这些模式编码为可复用优化规则，在热函数上做规则匹配+LLM前置条件校验+功能与性能双重验证的改写。在12个留出项目41个操作上，\ourtool{}全部保持C输出，40个操作加速，18个超过原始C，并在可比操作上分别优于\codex{} 31/37、优于无规则引导变体34/41。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C→Rust转译器（如\crust{}）能迁移整个项目并保证行为等价，但行为保持不等于性能保持，转译后Rust普遍存在性能回退，且现有工作缺乏对回退成因的系统化诊断与可复用的修复手段，需要一个能自动恢复转译代码性能的方案。

Method: 1) 对20个真实C项目及其\crust{}转译做性能测量，选取8个项目、19个操作进行深入分析；2) 借助执行开销剖面、优化后LLVM IR对比与编译器优化remark三元组证据，定位并归纳为9类反复出现的性能问题（残余运行时开销、编译器优化失配、C风格源码模式），并为每类给出优化方向；3) 提出\ourtool{}：将这些模式编码为可复用优化规则，先对项目做profile定位热函数并与规则专用模式匹配，再由LLM在项目上下文中核验每条规则的重写前置条件，重写须同时通过功能等价性与性能验证才被保留。

Result: 分析阶段：8个项目中19个操作有12个转译Rust比原C慢超3%，最高慢62.2%。评估阶段（12个留出项目、41个操作）：\ourtool{}在所有测试负载上保持与C一致的输出；40个操作快于\crust{}转译，其中18个快于原始C；在双方均保持C输出的37个操作中有31个优于通用编码智能体\codex{}；在41个操作中有34个优于去掉规则引导的自身变体。

Conclusion: 转译后的性能回退具有系统性、可归纳的成因，将诊断出的问题模式固化为规则并配合LLM前置条件核验与双重验证，可有效且安全地恢复转译Rust的性能，甚至超越原始C；规则引导优于通用智能体与无规则变体，说明领域特定知识在该任务上具有显著价值。

Abstract: Rust is increasingly adopted as a memory-safe successor to C, and transpilers such as \crust{} can migrate entire C projects while preserving their behavior. Preserving behavior, however, does not guarantee preserving performance. We study this gap on 20 real-world C projects and their \crust{} translations. On the eight projects used for analysis, the translated Rust is more than 3\% slower than the original C on 12 of 19 operations, by up to 62.2\%. By comparing execution-cost profiles, optimized LLVM IR, and compiler optimization remarks across each C--Rust pair, we trace these slowdowns to nine recurring performance issues, spanning residual runtime work, missed compiler optimizations, and C-style source patterns, and derive an optimization direction for each. We then present \ourtool{}, an LLM-based approach that encodes these issues as reusable optimization rules to recover the performance of translated Rust. \ourtool{} profiles each project to locate hot functions and matches them against rule-specific patterns. The LLM then checks each rule's rewrite preconditions against the project context before rewriting, and a rewrite is kept only if it passes both functional and performance validation. On 41 operations from twelve held-out projects, \ourtool{} preserves C outputs on all tested workloads and speeds up 40 operations over the \crust{} translations, 18 of which end up faster than the original C. It outperforms the general-purpose coding agent \codex{} on 31 of the 37 operations where both preserve C outputs, and outperforms its own variant without rule guidance on 34 of the 41 operations.

</details>


### [230] [GOLD: Geometric Optimized Latent Diffusion for Structure-Aware RNA Inverse Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22992)
*Qi Si,Xuyang Liu,Penglei Wang,Shuo Su,Limei Han,Mengzhu Li,Xin Guo,Yuan Qi,Yuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GOLD——基于统一潜空间的几何优化潜扩散模型，将RNA离散序列与连续侧链角度联合建模用于RNA逆向折叠，并配合免训练梯度引导采样提升物理有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RNA逆向折叠方法仅以3D骨架坐标为条件生成1D离散序列，忽略了对整体结构稳定性至关重要的连续侧链朝向信息，且直接在原始空间扩散的联合设计框架效率与一致性欠佳。

Method: GOLD将序列与结构投影到统一潜流形：(1) 设计GeoVAE，将离散RNA序列与连续侧链角整合进单一平滑潜空间，实现协同共扩散；(2) 引入免训练的梯度引导采样机制，在采样中将序列概率与侧链几何解码为结构，依据物理约束计算解析梯度作为动态反馈，引导生成序列趋于物理稳定状态。

Result: 大量实验表明GOLD在序列恢复率、二级结构有效性及3D可折叠性等指标上均取得State-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 显式引入侧链几何并采用统一潜空间扩散加物理约束梯度引导，可显著提升RNA逆向折叠的结构有效性与可折叠性，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Designing RNA sequences that fold into specific 3D structures is a fundamental challenge in therapeutics. Current RNA inverse folding methods typically condition on 3D backbone coordinates but the generation is limited to 1D discrete sequences. This ignores the continuous side-chain orientations, which are essential for overall structural stability. To address this issue, we explicitly introduce side-chain geometry into the RNA generative process. Unlike co-design frameworks that diffuse across separate raw spaces, we propose Geometric Optimized Latent Diffusion (\textbf{GOLD}), which project sequence and structure into a unified latent manifold. GOLD employs a novel GeoVAE to elegantly integrate discrete RNA sequences with continuous side-chain angles. By mapping these modalities into a single, smooth latent manifold, GOLD naturally facilitates a synergistic co-diffusion process. To enhance structural validity, we further introduce a training-free, gradient-guided sampling mechanism. By decoding the generated sequence probabilities and side-chain geometries into structures during the sampling process, we compute analytical gradients from physical constraints. This dynamic feedback actively guides the generated sequence to a physically stable state. Extensive experiments validate the superiority of GOLD, achieving state-of-the-art performance across sequence recovery, secondary structure validity, and 3D foldability metrics. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/darkflash03/GOLD.

</details>


### [231] [ShadowFPT: Backdooring Federated Prompt Tuning via Shadow Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22871)
*Kun Zhai,Teng Li,Yunhao Feng,Xingjun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ShadowFPT exploits the frozen shared backbone in federated prompt tuning: a client-side learnable Shadow Trigger steers triggered inputs toward the target class in CLIP representation space, so uploaded prompt updates stay stealthy while achieving targeted backdoor misclassification.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPT 仅优化轻量 prompt 并冻结预训练骨干，现有研究关注其通信/参数效率，却忽视安全性：由于骨干被共享且冻结，恶意客户端可通过表征空间植入后门，同时让上传的 prompt 更新接近良性更新，从而绕过基于 prompt 更新异常检测的防御。

Method: 提出 ShadowFPT 定向后门攻击：(1) 针对冻结的 CLIP 视觉编码器预训练可学习的 Shadow Trigger（可用公开辅助数据或恶意客户端本地数据），使带触发器输入在表征空间被导向目标类；(2) 在联邦 prompt 调优中，恶意客户端先在当前全局 prompt 下自适应触发器，再在干净与触发样本上联合优化本地 prompt；(3) 仅上传 prompt 参数，触发器与冻结编码器保留在本地，将攻击负担从 prompt 更新转移到触发器诱导的表征引导。

Result: 在主设置下，跨多数据集、多种聚合规则及非 IID 联邦划分，攻击成功率由 19.81% 提升至 90.36%，且保持干净准确率；在文本、视觉及视觉-语言联合 prompt 调优中均有效。

Conclusion: 冻结骨干是联邦 prompt 调优中被低估的隐蔽后门攻击面；仅依赖 prompt 更新异常检测的防御不足以应对此类攻击。

Abstract: Federated Prompt Tuning (FPT) adapts large vision--language models by freezing the pretrained backbone and optimizing only lightweight prompt parameters across clients. Although this design improves communication and parameter efficiency, it creates an overlooked security risk. Since the backbone is shared and frozen, a malicious client can induce backdoor behavior through the representation space while keeping its uploaded prompt updates close to benign ones. We propose \textbf{ShadowFPT}, a targeted backdoor attack that exploits this frozen-backbone attack surface. ShadowFPT first pretrains a learnable \emph{Shadow Trigger} against the frozen CLIP visual encoder, using either auxiliary public data or the malicious client's local data, so that triggered inputs are steered toward the target class in representation space. During federated prompt tuning, the malicious client adapts the trigger under the current global prompt and then optimizes its local prompt on both clean and triggered samples. Only prompt parameters are uploaded to the server, while the trigger and frozen encoders remain local. By shifting most of the attack burden from prompt updates to trigger-induced representation steering, ShadowFPT achieves targeted misclassification while preserving prompt-space stealthiness. Across multiple datasets, aggregation rules, and non-IID federated partitions, ShadowFPT increases the attack success rate from 19.81\% to 90.36\% in our main setting, while maintaining clean accuracy. It remains effective across textual, visual, and joint vision--language prompt tuning. These results identify frozen backbones as stealthy and underexplored backdoor surfaces in federated prompt tuning, suggesting that defenses based only on prompt-update anomaly detection are insufficient.

</details>


### [232] [From Memory Hits to Answerable Evidence: Query-Conditioned Memory Compilation for Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23313)
*Shangze Li,Yanyu Yao,Derong Xu,Zhi Zheng,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemCompile 提出查询条件下的证据编译框架，分离记忆寻址与证据呈现，在 LoCoMo 上达到 80.52% Judge Accuracy 并推进质量-成本帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期智能体记忆需从跨轮次、跨会话的分散信息中恢复与答案相关的证据。现有结构化记忆与自适应检索方法往往把检索或扩展单元直接交给阅读器，将记忆寻址与证据呈现耦合，导致最终上下文冗余或不完整。

Method: 框架分两个平面：Address Plane 通过紧凑的源链接描述符与容量感知的 Cells 定位相关支撑，并用归一化边界将异构检索命中归约为稳定的 SourcePacket 身份；Evidence Plane 在受限证据预算下跨互补数据包分配额度，接纳有限的额外支撑，并将每个被选数据包关联的保留论断渲染为可直接作答的上下文。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 上取得 80.52% Judge Accuracy，将经验质量-成本帕累托前沿推进至更高质量区间；在 LongMemEval-S 上的表现进一步表明查询条件化编译的收益可跨基准泛化。

Conclusion: 通过在寻址与证据呈现之间引入显式编译阶段，并配合预算受限的证据分配与渲染，MemCompile 能在长期对话记忆任务上同时提升答案质量与成本效率，且具备跨基准的通用性。

Abstract: Long-term agent memory requires recovering answer-relevant evidence dispersed across turns and sessions. Existing systems increasingly use structured memories and adaptive retrieval, yet often pass retrieved or expanded units directly to the reader, coupling memory addressing with evidence presentation and leaving the final context redundant or incomplete. To address this gap, we propose MemCompile, a query-conditioned evidence compilation framework for long-term conversational memory. Given a query, the Address Plane locates relevant support through compact source-linked descriptors and capacity-aware Cells, while a normalization boundary resolves heterogeneous retrieval hits to stable SourcePacket identities. Its Evidence Plane then allocates a bounded evidence budget across complementary packets, admits limited additional support, and renders the retained claims associated with each selected packet into an answer-ready context. MemCompile achieves 80.52% Judge Accuracy on LoCoMo and extends the empirical quality-cost Pareto frontier to a higher-quality regime. Its strong performance on LongMemEval-S further suggests that the benefits of query-conditioned compilation generalize across long-term conversational-memory benchmarks.

</details>


### [233] [CoTA++: Understanding the Repeat Curse in dMLLMs from an Information Flow Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23290)
*Qiyan Zhao,Xiaofeng Zhang,Xu-Yao Zhang,Shijie Han,Zhepeng Li,Shunzhi Zhu,Da-Han Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练即插即用框架CoTA++，针对dMLLM近似缓存导致的"重复诅咒"，从步内、跨步、跨层三处信息流扰动入手修复，将长回答重复率从最高99.6%降至17.0%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式多模态大模型采用双向注意力迭代去噪解码，无法使用标准自回归KV缓存，实际部署依赖跨去噪步复用中间状态的近似缓存；该缓存常使相邻位置重复同一token（"重复诅咒"），破坏句子连贯性，亟需在不重新训练的前提下修复。

Method: 先追踪上下文token信息流，定位三类扰动：步内缓存分散了位置对邻近上下文token的注意力锚定；跨步基于相似度的刷新缺乏陈旧性感知，导致部分位置在陈旧KV上解码；跨层上下文深层熵在重复出现处无法收敛。据此设计三个作用于解码步不同阶段的正交组件：Context-Token Attention Re-anchoring（对当前上下文重建注意力路由）、Decode-Aware Refresh（为即将提交的位置预留刷新预算）、Context-Token Entropy-Guided Voting（用上下文深层熵折减位置置信度以重排unmasking顺序）。

Result: 在三种dMLLM与两种缓存后端上，长回答样本重复率由最高99.6%降至至多17.0%，在九个基准上恢复了大部分因缓存损失的精度，且额外开销低于15%，保留了缓存的加速效果。

Conclusion: CoTA++以免训练、即插即用的方式与现有dMLLM及缓存方法自由组合，使近似缓存在扩散式多模态大模型中变得可靠。

Abstract: Diffusion-based multimodal large language models (dMLLMs) decode by iterative denoising with bidirectional attention, which precludes the standard autoregressive key--value cache. Practical deployments therefore rely on approximate caching that reuses intermediate states across denoising steps. Such caching, however, often makes a response repeat one token at adjacent positions in a run that breaks the sentence, a failure we term the \emph{Repeat Curse}. Tracing the context-token information flow reveals three disruptions. Within a step, caching disperses the attention that anchors a position on nearby context tokens. Across steps, similarity-based refresh is not staleness-aware, so some positions are decoded against stale key--value states. Across layers, the deep-layer entropy of the context fails to converge where repetition arises. We accordingly propose CoTA++, a training-free, plug-and-play framework that counteracts each disruption where it originates: Context-Token Attention Re-anchoring re-forms the attention routing against the current context, Decode-Aware Refresh reserves part of the cache's refresh budget for the positions about to be committed, and Context-Token Entropy-Guided Voting discounts the confidence of a position by the deep-layer entropy of its context, which reorders the unmasking. The three components act at non-overlapping stages of a decoding step and compose freely with existing dMLLMs and caches. Across three dMLLMs and two caching backends, CoTA++ reduces the sample repetition rate of long responses from up to 99.6\% to at most 17.0\%, recovers most of the accuracy lost to caching on nine benchmarks, and keeps the speedup of caching at under 15\% overhead, making approximate caching reliable for dMLLMs.

</details>


### [234] [QuAD: Quantitative Assessment via Distribution-Alignment for Qualitative Data Quality Enabled by a Content-Aware Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23153)
*Wenshuo Zhang,Leixian Shen,Qiqi Zhou,Shuchang Xu,Feiyang Hao,Huamin Qu,Haobo Li,Zhao Chen,Lin-Ping Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 QuAD——一个面向定性问卷回答数据集的定量化前置分析评估框架，通过比较"数据内在聚类结构"与"研究者定义的分析视角"，以四象限指标联合判读数据是否适合特定分析目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半结构化定性回答是否适配某分析目标难以判断：人工定性分析解释性强但劳动密集，现有自动化方法缺少数据集层面视角，无法判断回答集是否支撑研究者选定的分析视角。

Method: QuAD 将从回答中重建的内在聚类分布与由元数据派生的外在（研究者定义的）分析视角进行比较，组织为四象限评估，指标从视角层面关系逐级读到簇内变异，给出 fitness-for-purpose 信号；因评估依赖视角条件下的回答间相异性，提出内容感知多智能体系统，采用两阶段指令生成与自适应锚点提升打分一致性。

Result: 在合成数据上开展受控实验，包含鲁棒性与基线对比；并在真实社会调查案例中验证，可定量判断所选分析轴是否有前景，并呈现代表示或异常回答供人工解读。

Conclusion: QuAD 为定性数据集提供可量化的前置适配性评估，衔接自动计算与人工解释，辅助研究者判断分析视角的可行性与发现潜力。

Abstract: Assessing whether qualitative responses collected from semi-structured questions are ready for a particular analysis goal is a persistent challenge. Manual qualitative analysis is interpretive and labor-intensive, while existing automated methods often lack a dataset-level perspective for deciding whether a response set supports a researcher-selected analytical lens. We introduce QuAD, a framework for quantitative pre-analysis assessment of qualitative response datasets. QuAD compares a reconstructed intrinsic cluster distribution reconstructed from responses with an extrinsic, researcher-defined analytical lens derived from metadata. QuAD is structured into a four-quadrant assessment whose metrics are read jointly, from lens-level relations to within-cluster variation, to provide fitness-for-purpose signals for revealing general patterns and unique insights. Because this assessment relies on lens-conditioned inter-response dissimilarity, we propose a content-aware multi-agent system with two-stage instruction generation and adaptive anchors to improve scoring consistency. We validate QuAD through controlled experiments on synthetic data, including robustness and baseline comparisons, and a real-world social survey case study. The case study shows how QuAD quantitatively assesses whether a selected analysis axis is promising and also surfaces representative or unusual responses for human interpretation.

</details>


### [235] [Agentic In-Context Learning: What Algorithms Can Transformers Learn in Closed-Loop Interaction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23051)
*Shiguang Wu,Yaqing Wang,Quanming Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"Agentic In-Context Learning (Agentic ICL)"框架：将交互式学习器表示为由状态、更新规则与决策规则构成的算法，证明因果Transformer可在有限时域内逼近主动学习、bandit与在线RL等算法并给出回报损失界；同时刻画过程奖励优化何时保持结局最优行为，蒸馏与奖励训练实验验证了Transformers能执行三类递归并受奖励类型影响决策策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL的"学习算法视角"仅研究固定参数Transformer如何从给定上下文中学习，而语言模型智能体的动作会决定后续进入上下文的证据，形成交互式闭环；已有工作要么依赖外部给定上下文，要么依赖带奖励的交互历史，通用工具使用智能体研究缺乏学习算法视角的理论刻画。

Method: 以状态、更新规则、决策规则三元组表示交互式学习器，统一覆盖主动学习、bandit与在线RL；构造因果Transformer在有限时域上近似这些算法，并给出回报损失上界；理论分析优化过程奖励（process reward）在何种条件下能保持最大化最终结局的行为；开展蒸馏实验，在无oracle动作/状态标签条件下用结局奖励或过程奖励训练模型，并检验预训练语言模型在不同文本改写下的行为一致性。

Result: 蒸馏实验表明Transformer能学会执行三种代表性递归算法；仅用结局奖励或过程奖励（如RL后训练）即可训练模型；不同奖励导致不同的决策策略；提升过程监督与结局最优动作偏好的对齐程度会使学习到的决策相应转移；这些行为差异在预训练语言模型中跨文本改写保持稳定。

Conclusion: Agentic ICL为交互式上下文学习提供了统一的学习算法视角与可证明的逼近与训练保证，说明过程奖励的优化需与结局最优动作偏好对齐才能保持最优行为，为智能体的强化学习式后训练与过程监督设计提供了理论依据与实证支持。

Abstract: The learning algorithm view of in-context learning (ICL) studies how fixed-parameter Transformers learn from supplied examples. Language model agents extend this setting through interaction: their actions determine what evidence enters context next. We call this regime Agentic In-Context Learning (Agentic ICL). Existing studies largely depend on externally supplied contexts or reward-bearing interaction histories, while work on general tool-using agents lacks a learning algorithm view. We represent an interactive learner by its state, update, and decision rule, covering active learning, bandits, and online reinforcement learning (RL). For expressivity, we construct causal Transformers that approximate these algorithms over a finite horizon, and bound the resulting loss in return. For training, we characterize when optimizing process rewards preserves behavior that maximizes the final outcome. Distillation experiments show that Transformers can learn to execute three representative recursions. We then train models from outcome or process rewards without oracle action or state targets, as in RL post-training. Different rewards yield distinct decision policies. Improving the alignment of process supervision with outcome-optimal action preferences shifts learned decisions accordingly. Such behavioral differences persist in a pretrained language model across textual paraphrases.

</details>


### [236] [Testing the Planning Claims of LLM-Based Agents against Formal Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22469)
*Oriol Miro-Lopez-Feliu,Oscar Miró López-Feliu,Victor Gimenez-Abalos,Javier Vazquez-Salceda,Victor Carballo Araruna,Sergio Alvarez-Napagao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文以隐藏PDDL模型作为形式化语义验证器，检验LLM智能体的规划能力，发现最佳配置在线仅解出40%可解任务、对不可解任务识别率低于10%，且性能差异主要由模型而非提示策略或架构决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体被赋予规划、监控与自我纠错等实践推理能力的声誉，其成功归因于思维链、搜索式提示、自我精炼、反思、批评者与工具调用循环等提示策略和智能体架构。但这些主张的证据通常只是端到端成功率（仅反映任务是否完成）或LLM评判（并非真正的验证器），缺乏对动作轨迹正确性的严格核验。

Method: 将自然语言表述的任务锚定在隐藏的PDDL形式化模型中，由该模型判定每个动作是否可应用、目标是否达成以及规划问题是否存在解。实验覆盖五个领域（其中两个为论文新增并随文发布）、四个模型、六种提示策略与三种智能体架构，对比离线与在线（智能体实际与环境交互、可观察状态）两种设置，并统计token消耗与不可解任务的识别率。

Result: 最佳配置在离线设置下可解出78%的可解任务，但在线设置下仅40%，且智能体多数情况下不检查状态即行动；对不可解任务的识别率不足10%；更重的提示策略消耗的token数为直接提示的5至22倍，且无明显收益。规划表现随模型的变化幅度大于随提示策略或智能体架构的变化幅度。

Conclusion: 关于智能体具备规划能力的主张必须有经过验证的动作轨迹作为证据，仅凭端到端成功率或LLM评判不足以支撑。研究同时发布了两个新领域基准，为形式化语义校验LLM规划提供了可复现的评测框架。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly credited with the competences of practical reasoning: planning, monitoring, and correcting their own actions. Their success is attributed to the prompting strategies and agent architectures built around the model, such as chain-of-thought, search-based prompting, self-refinement, reflection, critics and tool-using loops. Yet these claims are usually supported by end-to-end success rates, which show only whether a task was completed, or by LLM judges, which are not verifiers. We test LLM planning against formal semantics. Tasks are posed in natural language, but each is grounded in a hidden PDDL model that decides whether every action is applicable, whether the goal is reached, and whether a plan exists at all. Across five domains (two of them new, all released with the paper), four models, six prompting strategies and three agent architectures, the best configuration solves 78% of the solvable tasks offline but only 40% online, where agents mostly act without inspecting the state. Unsolvable tasks are recognised in under 10% of episodes, and heavier prompting strategies use 5 to 22 times as many tokens as direct prompting with no clear gain. In our experiments, planning performance varied more across models than across prompting strategies or agent architectures. Claims that agents can plan require evidence from validated action traces.

</details>


### [237] [Harness-Delta Attribution: Dissecting Gains From Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22466)
*Wenxuan Ding,Qin Lu,Changlong Yu,Sha Li,Shuowei Jin,Xin Liu,Zhenyu Shi,Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Harness-Delta Attribution (HDA) 评估协议，将 harness 演化带来的性能增益拆分为过拟合、测试时缩放与残差可泛化改进三部分；在 11 个执行模型、3 个框架、4 类任务上的 21 个显著增益设置中，过拟合与测试时缩放平均分别占 54% 和 14%，泛化证据有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harness 演化可在不更新模型权重的情况下提升智能体系统性能，但既有评测无法区分搜索集增益究竟来自基准特定的过拟合、额外的推理期计算，还是可泛化的真实改进，因此需要一种归因式评估方法。

Method: 提出 HDA（Harness-Delta Attribution）协议，对演化增益进行三分解：伪影驱动的过拟合、测试时缩放可解释的部分、以及残差（作为可泛化改进的上界）；在数学、编码、创造性与智能体任务上，对 11 个执行模型与 3 个 harness 演化框架进行实验，并结合留出任务迁移与验证集信号分析。

Result: 在 21 个具有统计显著搜索集增益的设置中，过拟合平均占增益 54%、测试时缩放占 14%，二者合计在 14 个设置中超过 50%；残差与留出迁移在任务、模型、框架间差异显著；搜索集增益常无法迁移到留出任务，验证基信号不足以抑制过拟合。

Conclusion: harness 演化的多数报告增益并非真实泛化提升，且现有验证机制难以防止过拟合；未来研究应采用归因感知的评估范式，报告增益来源分解与留出迁移结果。

Abstract: Harness evolution automatically optimizes the prompts, skills, tools, and orchestration code around a fixed-weight model, offering a way to improve agentic systems without updating model weights. The agent harness is iteratively optimized to achieve higher performance on the search set. But how much of this gain reflects benchmark-specific overfitting or additional inference-time compute, and how much may generalize beyond the search set? In this work, we introduce Harness-Delta Attribution (HDA), an evaluation protocol that dissects an evolution gain into artifact-driven overfitting, gains explained by test-time scaling, and the remainder, which gives an upper bound on residual generalizable improvement. We apply HDA to 11 executor models and three harness-evolution frameworks across math, coding, creativity, and agentic tasks. In 21 of these settings that show statistically significant search-set gains, we find that a large share of the improvement is explained by overfitting or test-time scaling. Specifically, among these 21 settings, overfitting and test-time scaling account for 54% and 14% of the gain on average; their combined share exceeds 50% in 14 settings. The residual and held-out transfer vary substantially across tasks, models, and frameworks. Importantly, search-set gains often fail to transfer to held-out tasks, and provide limited evidence that they will persist under practical distribution shifts. Moreover, validation-based signals are insufficient to prevent overfitting. These results motivate attribution-aware evaluation in future harness evolution research.

</details>


### [238] [Beyond Large Language Models: A Mental World Model Framework for Mental Modeling, Simulation, Evolution, and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23722)
*Qingshi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“心理世界模型”（MWM）及多分支心理演化架构（MMEA），显式构建主观世界、建模认知与心理状态交互并模拟连续心理演化；在DynToM/RecToM上达96.48%/94.00%，并在心理状态与行为预测等任务上显著领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM与心智理论（ToM）方法主要从语言信息推断心理状态，缺乏统一机制来处理主观世界构建、认知—心理交互、动态演化与历史依赖，难以支撑深层社会认知与通用智能。

Method: 提出心理世界模型（MWM）框架，并实现为多分支心理演化架构（MMEA）：显式构建主观世界表征，建模认知与心理状态之间的交互，并基于历史经验模拟连续的心理演化过程。

Result: DynToM、RecToM准确率分别为96.48%与94.00%，优于主流LLM与专门ToM方法；在心理状态与行为预测、长序列预测、隐式心理状态推理上分别提升28.7、39.3、16.6个百分点。

Conclusion: MMEA提供了可解释的连续心理建模框架，使AI超越表层语义表征、由语言推断潜在心理状态并动态模拟内部认知过程与演化；MWM为因果推理、个性化AI、持续人机交互及迈向AGI提供基础。

Abstract: Understanding and simulating how subjective cognition and mental states emerge, evolve, and shape behavior is essential for advancing artificial intelligence toward deeper social cognition and general intelligence. Existing large language models (LLMs) and Theory of Mind (ToM) methods primarily infer mental states from linguistic information, lacking unified mechanisms for subjective world construction, cognitive-psychological interactions, dynamic evolution, and historical dependencies. This paper introduces the Mental World Model (MWM) and its implementation, the Multi-Branch Mental Evolution Architecture (MMEA), which explicitly constructs subjective worlds, models interactions between cognition and mental states, and simulates continuous mental evolution shaped by historical experiences. On DynToM and RecToM, MMEA achieves 96.48% and 94.00% accuracy, respectively, outperforming leading LLMs and specialized ToM methods. Additional experiments demonstrate improvements of 28.7, 39.3, and 16.6 percentage points in mental state and behavior prediction, long-sequence prediction, and implicit mental state inference, respectively. The results demonstrate that MMEA provides an interpretable framework for continuous mental modeling, enabling AI to move beyond surface-level semantic representations to infer individuals' underlying mental states from linguistic information and dynamically simulate their internal cognitive processes and evolution over time. MWM also provides a promising foundation for causal reasoning, personalized AI, sustained human-AI interaction, and future advances toward artificial general intelligence (AGI).

</details>


### [239] [Structured Authority Injection: Persistent Human Control for LLM Agents via Typed MCP Overrides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22373)
*Ryan Tran,YueMing Zhang,Lingfeng Tao,Nan Yang,Shengjie Xu,Yu Yin,Shuai Xu,Wenlu Zhang,Hailu Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出结构化权威注入（SAI）协议，通过类型化、模式校验的MCP工具调用路由操作员指令，并在每次提示前附加排序后的权威上下文，解决人类指令在多智能体编排中无法跨规划周期持久生效的控制缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益作为分布式多智能体系统中的自主编排者，但存在"控制缺口"：缺乏协议层机制让人类操作员注入跨规划周期持续生效的强制性指令。纯文本覆盖存在两类问题——缺少模式校验导致解释歧义，以及随上下文累积而出现的权威侵蚀（指令影响力衰减）。

Method: SAI将操作员修正通过类型化、经模式校验的MCP工具调用传递，并维护一个排序的权威上下文前置到每次LLM提示中。协议定义三类覆盖（Constraint、Priority、Strategy），具有不同的持久化语义，并通过规划过程无法访问的有向权威通道强制执行显式优先级顺序。方法无需微调或访问模型权重，适用于支持MCP工具使用的指令微调LLM。

Result: 在9个LLM与针对智能体失效模式的8个场景上评估：歧义指令解释减少，跨规划迭代的权威衰减得到缓解；在最强模型中提示开销更低（依模型与场景最高达38%）；但收益在不同模型间差异显著。

Conclusion: SAI为需要显式且持久人类控制的智能体系统提供了一种通用设计模式。其效果受模型能力制约，存在明显的模型依赖性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly used as autonomous orchestrators in distributed and multi-agent systems, yet a control gap remains: no protocol-level mechanism allows human operators to inject binding directives that persist across planning cycles. Free-text overrides suffer from ambiguous interpretation due to lack of schema validation and from authority erosion as operator instructions lose influence within accumulating context. We propose Structured Authority Injection (SAI), a protocol that addresses these issues by routing operator corrections through typed, schema-validated Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool calls and maintaining a ranked authority context prepended to each LLM prompt. SAI defines three override classes (Constraint, Priority, and Strategy) with distinct persistence semantics and an explicit precedence order enforced via a directed authority channel inaccessible to the planning process. The method requires no model fine-tuning or access to model weights and applies to instruction-tuned LLMs supporting MCP tool use. Evaluation across nine LLMs and eight scenarios targeting agent failure modes shows reduced ambiguous directive interpretation and mitigated authority decay across planning iterations, with lower prompt overhead in the strongest models (up to 38% depending on model and scenario), though benefit varies substantially across models. SAI provides a general design pattern for agent systems requiring explicit and persistent human control.

</details>


### [240] [World Action Critic (WAC) Model: Joint Foresight and Criticism for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22825)
*Xiangteng Zhang,Haoyuan Liu,Yang Guan,Jingliang Duan,Yao Lyu,Tao Zhang,Shuai Zhang,Kehua Sheng,Bo Zhang,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 World Action Critic (WAC) 模型，通过联合学习候选动作条件下的未来预测与结果评估，使评判信号在 rollout 中反向塑造预测表征；在 NAVSIM-v1 上取得 93.4 PDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型擅长预想动作后果并为策略学习提供前瞻，但对反事实动作的推理仍困难：预测目标可能低估动作评估所需信息，且未执行动作缺乏可观测未来作为直接监督。

Method: WAC 生成多模态动作候选，并联合学习「以候选为条件的未来预测」与「后果评估」；critic 监督在 rollout 期间直接塑造中间预测表征，促使表征保留区分动作质量所需信息；已执行动作由观测未来监督预测，反事实候选则由外部任务质量目标提供评估信号；通过联合优化，候选的评判信号无需其观测未来即可传播进预测表征。理论上刻画了 critic 引导的特征保留并给出候选选择遗憾的界。

Result: 在自动驾驶 NAVSIM-v1 基准上达到 93.4 PDMS，验证了联合学习预测前瞻与后果评估的有效性。

Conclusion: 将评判监督与预测学习耦合可有效解决反事实动作评估的表征不足问题，预测前瞻与后果评估的联合学习具有实际增益。

Abstract: World models have shown considerable promise in sequential decision-making by anticipating the consequences of actions and providing predictive foresight for policy learning. However, learning to reason about counterfactual actions remains challenging, since predictive objectives may underemphasize information critical for action evaluation, while unexecuted actions lack observed futures for direct supervision. To address these challenges, we propose the World Action Critic (WAC) Model, which proposes multimodal action candidates and jointly learns candidate-conditioned future prediction and consequence evaluation. In our proposed framework, critic supervision directly shapes intermediate predictive representations during rollout, encouraging them to retain information needed to distinguish action quality. Observed futures supervise prediction for executed actions, while external task-quality targets provide evaluation signals for counterfactual candidates. Through joint optimization, critic signals from these candidates propagate into predictive representations without requiring their observed futures. Our theoretical analysis characterizes critic-guided feature retention and bounds candidate-selection regret. Applied to autonomous driving, WAC achieves 93.4 PDMS on NAVSIM-v1, demonstrating the effectiveness of jointly learning predictive foresight and consequence evaluation.

</details>


### [241] [Repair, Retry, and the Cost of Hindsight: Verifier Feedback in Formal Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22698)
*Jack Li,Sophia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控单步实验调和了"验证器反馈能改进失败修复"与"自我修复相比独立重采样无优势"这两个矛盾发现，揭示二者源于两个方向相反的效果：展示失败前驱会损害性能（引发复制），而对应的编译器诊断能部分弥补该损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有文献存在张力：一方面验证器反馈可改进失败修复，另一方面自我修复本身相对独立重采样几乎没有优势。作者希望厘清这一矛盾，辨明验证器引导修复中究竟是哪些条件真正起了作用。

Method: 在Lean定理证明中设计受控单步实验：固定前驱失败不变，分别变化①是否展示失败证明、②编译器错误是否对应此次失败、③重试指令。测试模型为通用模型Qwen3.6-27B与专用证明器DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B，使用困难MiniF2F题目；并进行11题、最多64次尝试的序贯实验。

Result: 发现两个相反效应：展示失败前驱降低成功率并显著增加对其复制；在Qwen上补充该次尝试的编译器错误可恢复到仅用诊断的水平。将前驱替换为其他定理的失败证明基本消除退化；在Qwen上将诊断替换为其他问题的错误会大幅削弱恢复。无失败证明时，正确诊断相对错误诊断对照无显著优势。失败证明惩罚在有无重试指令下均存在，而Qwen的诊断恢复在无指令时较弱。序贯实验中Qwen在失败证明历史内保留正确诊断收益，DeepSeek-Prover无显著恢复。但所有测试的单步反馈条件均未超过仅指令重采样，64次序贯修复在等token预算下亦未建立优势。

Conclusion: 验证器反馈可以改进修复，但无法克服因条件化于失败尝试而带来的性能损失；没有任何反馈配置在单步或序贯、等token预算下优于纯指令重采样。

Abstract: Verifier-guided self-repair conditions a model on feedback from failed attempts before it tries again, without weight updates. Yet two findings in prior work are in tension: verifier feedback can improve repair of failures, but self-repair itself can provide little-to-no advantage over independent resampling. We reconcile these findings in Lean theorem proving through controlled one-step experiments that hold the preceding failure fixed while varying whether the failed proof is shown, whether the compiler error corresponds to that failure, and the retry instruction. We test frozen Qwen3.6-27B, a general-purpose model, and DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B, a specialized prover, on difficult MiniF2F problems. We find two opposing effects. Showing the model its failed predecessor reduces success and sharply increases copying of it, while on Qwen adding that attempt's compiler error recovers the loss, to the level of the diagnostic alone. Replacing the predecessor with another theorem's failed proof largely removes the degradation on both models; on Qwen, replacing the diagnostic with another problem's error substantially reduces the recovery. Without the failed proof, correct diagnostics show no resolved advantage over wrong-diagnostic controls on either model. The failed-proof penalty persists with and without the retry instruction, whereas Qwen's diagnostic recovery is weaker without it. In 11-problem sequential experiments through 64 attempts, Qwen retains a correct-diagnostic benefit within failed-proof histories, while DeepSeek-Prover shows no resolved recovery. However, no tested one-step feedback condition establishes an advantage over instruction-only resampling, nor does 64-attempt sequential repair with failed proofs and correct diagnostics at equal token budgets. Verifier feedback can thus improve repair without overcoming the performance loss from conditioning on a failed attempt.

</details>


### [242] [Not All Test-Time Compute Is Evidence: Model-Specific Bias in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23213)
*Ming Shen,Tianjun Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出测试时扩展（test-time scaling）隐含假设"同模型多次采样提供独立证据"，实证表明该假设在单一推理模型内部失效（错误在重复采样、推理深度与自评中相互关联），并提出 DIA：以中间阶段与最终阶段预测的不一致作为推理不稳定的逐题信号，触发一个小型异构模型面板独立解题并直接聚合答案。七个基准、两个基模型上，14 个设置中 12 个优于完整思维链（MuSiQue +7.00%），仅约 17% 样本触发，token 消耗较多智能体基线最多减少 47.2%；消融显示独立求解与解题者异构性二者缺一不可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展方法与多智能体/自一致性方法默认：对同一模型增加计算量即可获得相互独立的证据，从而可靠地降低错误。作者认为该前提未经验证，且若错误在同一模型内高度相关，则额外计算的边际证据价值被严重高估，需要先检验相关性、再设计只在必要时引入真正独立信息的机制，以避免无差别的算力浪费。
