Method: 1) 相关性诊断：在单一推理模型内考察错误在重复采样、不同推理深度（中间步骤 vs 最终答案）以及模型自评（self-evaluation）三个维度上的相关性，检验独立性假设。2) DIA（Disagreement-triggered Independent Arbitration）：将中间阶段预测与最终阶段预测的不一致作为逐题（per-question）的推理不稳定性信号；仅在被触发的问题上，调用一个小型异构模型面板，在看不到两个候选答案的前提下独立求解，并对其答案进行直接聚合（多数/直接聚合）。3) 对照与消融：与完整思维链、多智能体基线比较；消融分别去掉"独立求解（让面板看到候选答案）"与"求解者异构性（同构模型面板）"以验证两者的必要性。

Result: 在 7 个基准、2 个基模型共 14 个设置中，DIA 有 12 个设置优于完整思维链，其中 MuSiQue 上提升 7.00%；机制稀疏激活，仅约 17% 的样本触发仲裁；相比多智能体基线最多节省 47.2% 的 token。消融表明：允许面板看到候选答案（破坏独立性）或采用同构求解者（缺乏异构性）都会使增益消失，二者均为性能提升的必要条件。

Conclusion: 同模型内的额外计算不构成额外证据，测试时扩展的"独立性假设"在单一推理模型中不成立；将模型自身的不一致性作为不确定性触发器，并在触发时引入异构模型的独立判断，是一种高性价比的测试时策略——以极少触发率和显著更低的 token 成本获得稳定增益。该结果也提示后续工作应采用异构集成而非同模型自洽式扩展。

Abstract: Test-time scaling methods implicitly assume that additional computation from the same model provides independent evidence. We show that this assumption fails within a single reasoning model: errors are correlated across repeated samples, across reasoning depth, and in the model's own self-evaluation, so more compute from the same model is not more evidence. We introduce DIA (Disagreement-triggered Independent Arbitration), which treats disagreement between an intermediate-stage and the final-stage prediction as a per-question signal of reasoning instability and resolves it by asking a small panel of heterogeneous models to solve the question independently, without seeing either candidate, and aggregating their answers directly. Across seven benchmarks and two base models, DIA outperforms full chain-of-thought in 12 of 14 settings, including a 7.00% gain on MuSiQue, while activating on only $\sim 17\%$ of examples and using up to 47.2% fewer tokens than a multi-agent baseline. Ablations show that both independent solving and solver heterogeneity are necessary for these gains.

</details>


### [243] [Algorithm Modelization: Transforming Optimization Algorithms into Evolvable Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23161)
*Jian Yao,Zhiyao Zhang,Yichen Li,Xingyu Wu,Kay Chen Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"算法模型化"(Algorithm Modelization)新范式，将迭代算法执行轨迹视为"计算经验"，把迭代算法转化为可训练模型；首个实例将迭代优化建模为自回归过程，配合种群-时间架构与"模仿到超越"学习框架，所得优化器可融合异构搜索知识、通过优化反馈自我提升，并泛化到未见问题与更大维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法需反复人工重新设计为程序，能力难以积累；作者希望算法能力能像模型一样通过"学习"被训练、泛化并持续进化，即把算法执行过程的知识内化到模型中。

Method: 1) 将迭代算法的执行轨迹视为计算经验，通过学习"算法状态如何随迭代演化"来内化已有算法知识；2) 首个实现：把迭代优化建模为自回归过程，前一代种群作为生成下一代种群的上下文；3) 设计维度无关的"种群-时间"(population-temporal)架构，同时刻画种群交互与迭代动态；4) 采用"模仿到超越"学习框架——先从异构教师算法中模仿学习，再利用优化反馈直接改进所得优化器。

Result: 模型化优化器不仅复现单个算法，还能把异构搜索知识整合进单一可训练模型；经后续改进获得更强的优化能力；并将所学知识泛化到不同问题及显著更大的未见维度上。

Conclusion: 算法模型化使算法能力可通过学习累积——新算法与解题经验可作为训练资源持续提升模型，从而开辟从手工设计算法走向可学习、可泛化、可进化的通用算法模型之路。

Abstract: Can algorithms be learned and evolved as models, rather than repeatedly redesigned as programs? We explore this question by introducing Algorithm Modelization, a new paradigm that turns iterative algorithms into trainable models. Its central idea is to regard algorithm execution trajectories as computational experience: by learning how algorithmic states evolve over iterations, a model can internalize the knowledge embodied in existing algorithms, reproduce their computational behaviors, and further evolve beyond them through training. As a first realization of this paradigm, we modelize iterative optimization as an autoregressive process, where preceding generations constitute the computational context for generating the next population. We develop a dimension-independent population-temporal architecture to capture both population interactions and iterative dynamics, together with an imitation-to-surpassing learning framework that first acquires algorithmic knowledge from heterogeneous teachers and then improves the resulting optimizer directly through optimization feedback. The modelized optimizer consequently goes beyond reproducing individual algorithms: it consolidates heterogeneous search knowledge into a single trainable model, achieves stronger optimization capability through subsequent improvement, and generalizes its acquired knowledge across problems and substantially larger unseen dimensions. More fundamentally, Algorithm Modelization makes algorithmic capability accumulable through learning, where new algorithms and problem-solving experience can become training resources for progressively improving the model. Our results establish a promising path from manually designed algorithms toward general algorithm models that learn, generalize, and evolve from computational experience.

</details>


### [244] [DiffusionRVGE: Reinforcement Learning via Regression-Based Value Gradient Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22973)
*Jiayuan Wang,Tongyao Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 \method，将中心化奖励回归到中心化最终去噪图像上以构造更新方向，无需奖励导数或采样轨迹上的伴随反向传播，训练效率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习微调扩散模型虽有效，但重复去噪与梯度估计导致训练开销巨大。

Method: 通过回归中心化奖励到中心化最终去噪图像构造更新方向；用统一的一阶变分公式（含/不含轨迹KL惩罚）识别值梯度目标；用高斯加噪下的群体分析解释回归几何，用子空间分析论证采用干净图像坐标的合理性；按奖励连续性自适应调整 rollout 带宽与查询权重（平滑奖励用局部 rollout，离散奖励用更宽分组提供对比）。

Result: 五奖励联合训练下，单个 SD3.5-Medium 在八项指标中七项匹配或超过 DiffusionNFT 联合模型，GenEval 0.94、OCR 0.92、PickScore 23.82，训练算力约为其五分之一；单奖励训练达到相同奖励阈值比复现的 DiffusionNFT 快 2.1–6.7×；消融支持干净图像估计器、直接方向迁移与奖励相关 rollout 选择。

Conclusion: 该方法以低成本获得有竞争力甚至更优的奖励对齐效果，验证了基于干净图像坐标的回归式梯度估计与自适应 rollout 设计的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning improves diffusion models using rewards on generated images, but repeated denoising and gradient estimation make training expensive. We introduce \method, an efficient method that constructs useful update directions by regressing centered rewards on centered final denoised images. It requires neither reward derivatives nor adjoint backpropagation through the sampling trajectory. A unified first-variation formulation identifies the value-gradient target with or without a trajectory-KL penalty. A population analysis under Gaussian noising explains the regression geometry, while a subspace analysis motivates clean-image coordinates. The implementation adapts rollout bandwidth and query weights to reward continuity: local rollouts suit smooth rewards, whereas broader groups provide informative contrasts for discrete rewards. Trained jointly on five rewards, one SD3.5-Medium model matches or exceeds DiffusionNFT's joint model on seven of eight metrics, reaching GenEval $0.94$, OCR $0.92$, and PickScore $23.82$ at about one fifth of its estimated training compute. In single-reward training, \method reaches matched reward thresholds $2.1$--$6.7\times$ faster than reproduced DiffusionNFT on identical hardware. Ablations support the clean-image estimator, direct direction transfer, and reward-dependent rollout choices. Code is available at https://github.com/jy-wang-tsinghua/DIFFUSIONRVGE.

</details>


### [245] [When to Forget: Risk-Controlled Context Adaptation for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22897)
*Chenle Chen,Yangbo Wei,Zhijie Zheng,Zhenyu Su,Qifan Wang,Junhong Qian,Chao Yao,Chen Wu,Lei He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RECAP框架，将失败历史块的保留、压缩或删除视为随执行进程变化的决策问题，通过匹配上下文干预与独立策略认证，按"对后续执行的价值"决定遗忘时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体虽能从失败中学习，但同一失败记录的价值会随执行推进而改变；现有做法默认全历史保留（KEEP），缺乏判断何时应保留、压缩或删除失败历史的执行级依据。

Method: RECAP包含三部分：(1)匹配上下文干预——固定环境状态与rollout随机种子，测量成功率和续写提示成本差异；(2)执行关系预测——利用失败块与当前执行之间的前缀关系提出候选编辑；(3)独立策略认证——在固定测量协议下，用独立任务集在"有害干预"和"非正收益"风险约束下选择策略。另用五种子纵向案例固定失败块与其摘要，考察不同执行状态下压缩策略的效果变化。

Result: 五种子纵向案例显示：同一压缩策略在早期状态失败，在较晚状态转为节省成本的干预。在五个骨干模型上，RECAP给出正的配对成功节省估计，并呈现骨干相关的可靠性与效率权衡。在Hunyuan-A13B上，编辑了43.24%的保留检查点，同时匹配KEEP观测到的91.89%续写成功率，并将无条件平均续写提示成本降低4,619.8个token（2.13%）。

Conclusion: 失败历史的价值依赖于执行上下文，"何时遗忘"应由其对后续执行的价值来判定；RECAP提供了执行级的参考框架，实现成功率不降前提下的提示成本节约。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents learn from failure, but the value of a failure record can change as execution progresses. We study when retaining, compressing, or removing the same history block improves subsequent execution relative to full-history KEEP. We introduce RECAP, a framework that combines matched context interventions, execution-relational prediction, and independent policy certification. Matched environment states and rollout seeds measure success and continuation prompt-cost differences; prefix-only relations between a failure block and the current execution propose promising edits. Independent task bundles then select a policy subject to harmful-intervention and non-positive-benefit risk constraints under a fixed measurement protocol. A five-seed longitudinal case holds both the failure block and its summary fixed: compression fails at an early state and becomes a cost-saving intervention at a later state. Across five backbones, RECAP produces positive paired-success saving estimates with backbone-specific reliability-efficiency tradeoffs. On Hunyuan-A13B, it edits 43.24% of retained checkpoints while matching KEEP's observed 91.89% continuation success and reducing unconditional mean continuation prompt cost by 4,619.8 tokens (2.13%). The framework provides an execution-level reference for deciding when to forget: retain history by its value to what comes next.

</details>


### [246] [Evo-B&B: Behavior-Aware LLM-Guided Joint Evolution of Branching and Node Selection Policies for MILP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22861)
*Jiayu Chen,Chentong Chen,Yuhan Liu,Ye Fan,Jianyong Sun,Jialong Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Evo-B&B，一个由 LLM 引导的进化框架，联合演化可执行的分支策略与节点选择策略，并借助行为感知的多岛进化机制提升搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MILP 分支定界中分支与节点选择高度耦合，但现有学习方法多将二者分开优化。

Method: 以 SCIP 端到端求解反馈为适应度，用 LLM 引导进化联合生成可执行的分支与节点选择策略；引入行为感知多岛进化机制，在固定 B&B 决策状态集上度量策略相似性，以此维持种群多样性、减少行为相似策略的冗余评估。

Result: 在四个标准 MILP 基准、多规模实例上取得有竞争力的求解性能，并能良好泛化到更大规模实例；消融实验验证了联合演化与行为感知多样性管理的有效性。

Conclusion: 联合演化分支与节点选择并结合行为感知多样性管理，是一种有效的学习型 B&B 策略设计范式。

Abstract: In branch-and-bound for Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP), branching and node selection are two critical and tightly coupled decisions, yet most existing learning-based approaches focus on optimizing them separately. We propose Evo-B&B, an LLM-guided evolutionary framework that jointly evolves executable branching and node selection policies using end-to-end SCIP solving feedback. To improve search efficiency and reduce redundant evaluations of behaviorally similar policies, Evo-B&B introduces a behavior-aware multi-island evolutionary mechanism. It measures policy similarity from branching and node-selection behaviors on a fixed set of B&B decision states and uses this similarity to preserve population diversity. We evaluate Evo-B&B on four standard MILP benchmarks across multiple instance scales. Experimental results show that Evo-B&B achieves competitive solving performance and generalizes well to larger-scale instances, while ablation studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of joint evolution and behavior-aware diversity management.

</details>


### [247] [Darwin-Claw: Environment-Adaptive Experience Consolidation for Recursively Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23574)
*Mingze Yin,Xiaohan Wang,Dian Li,Longfei Luo,Jiahong Yan,Yiheng Zhu,Chang-Yu Hsieh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出环境自适应自进化框架 Darwin-Claw，将原始交互轨迹蒸馏为层级化情景经验（细粒度错误归因+动态原型向量），用作感知"交互补丁"与技能优化信号，在 WildClawBench 上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体多在孤立会话中运行，无法持续从历史交互学习或适应用户特定环境；已有自进化范式往往不加区分地把原始轨迹注入记忆或技能模块，导致行为模式被掩盖、更新可控性差。随着基础模型推理能力提升，智能体进化瓶颈已从积累通用知识转向有效管理情境经验。

Method: Darwin-Claw 以 agent–actor 与 evolver–critic 的迭代协同自适应为理论基础，引入层级经验巩固系统（Hierarchical Experience Consolidation System）：从经验片段中提取细粒度错误归因，并泛化为动态更新的原型向量；该结构化情境经验既作为上下文敏感感知的交互补丁，又作为技能精炼的显式优化信号。框架可自动化部署，兼容主流智能体系统，无需人工整理。

Result: 在 WildClawBench 上的大量实验表明，Darwin-Claw 带来显著性能提升，为自主智能体在真实生态中持续、以用户为中心的适应提供了可控且稳定的路径。

Conclusion: 通过蒸馏情景经验替代原始轨迹注入，Darwin-Claw 实现了技能与长期记忆的协同进化，兼顾更新可控性与稳定性，具备良好的可部署性与通用性。

Abstract: While Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across complex tasks, they predominantly operate in isolated sessions, lacking the ability to continually learn from historical interactions or adapt to user-specific environments. Existing self-evolution paradigms often indiscriminately inject raw interaction trajectories into memory or skill modules, leading to obscured behavioral patterns and compromised update controllability. Furthermore, as foundational LLM reasoning improves, the bottleneck for agentic evolution shifts from accumulating generic knowledge to effectively managing situational experience. To address this, we propose Darwin-Claw, an environment-adaptive self-evolving framework that systematically distills episodic experiences to drive the comprehensive evolution of operational skills and durable memory. Theoretically grounded in an iterative agent-actor and evolver-critic co-adaptation, Darwin-Claw introduces a Hierarchical Experience Consolidation System that extracts fine-grained error attributions and generalizes them into dynamically updated prototype vectors. This structured situational experience serves as interaction patches for context-sensitive perception and explicit optimization signals for skill refinement. Practically, Darwin-Claw is a readily deployable, automated framework compatible with mainstream agent systems without requiring manual curation. Extensive experiments on WildClawBench demonstrate that Darwin-Claw yields substantial performance improvements, establishing a controllable and stable pathway for the continual, user-centric adaptation of autonomous agents in real-world ecosystems.

</details>


### [248] [GatewayBench: Unified Capability and Security Evaluation Platform for MCP Gateways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23449)
*Yitian Zheng,Zhaorun Chen,Haoyu Li,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 GatewayBench——首个面向 MCP 网关的统一评测框架与基准，系统研究扁平式与分层式网关架构对智能体任务完成率、工具使用效率与安全鲁棒性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 智能体通过工具调用与外部环境交互，MCP 已成为连接智能体与异构工具/服务的标准接口。组织大规模部署时，MCP 网关成为聚合、治理与塑造工具面的关键中间件层。然而，网关设计如何影响智能体的能力、效率与安全，此前缺乏系统性研究，这一空白亟待填补。

Method: 1) 将网关架构归纳为两类范式：扁平式（flattened）与分层式（hierarchical）；2) 构建 GatewayBench，包含 6 个领域、524 项任务，以及来自 251 个真实 MCP 服务器的 11,118 个真实工具池；3) 通过系统性注入与任务无关的工具来模拟大规模部署，实现工具面规模可控缩放下的评测；4) 在 7 个主流 MCP 网关与多种智能体配置上开展广泛实验，覆盖良性任务与对抗任务。

Result: 不同网关设计在任务完成度、工具使用效率与抗攻击能力上存在显著差异；且这些效应与底层模型及智能体执行框架（agent harness）存在强烈交互作用。工具面规模扩大时，各网关表现分化明显。

Conclusion: MCP 网关是智能体 AI 技术栈中影响重大但研究不足的组件。GatewayBench 通过真实工具生态下的受控评测，为 MCP 网关设计提供实践指导，并为构建更强、更高效、更安全的智能体系统提供严谨的测试平台。

Abstract: AI agents have increasingly interacted with external environments by invoking tools, with Model Context Protocol (MCP) emerging as a standard interface for connecting agents to heterogeneous tools and services. As organizations deploy agents at scale, MCP gateways have become a critical middleware layer for aggregating, governing, and shaping the tool surface exposed to agents. Despite the growing importance of MCP gateways in agentic systems, no prior work has systematically studied how gateway design affects agent capability, efficiency, and security. To address this gap, we introduce GatewayBench, the first unified evaluation framework and benchmark for MCP gateways. We characterize gateway architectures into two major paradigms: flattened gateways and hierarchical gateways. GatewayBench comprises 524 tasks across six domains, together with a pool of 11,118 real-world tools from 251 authentic MCP servers. To emulate large-scale deployments, we systematically inject task-irrelevant tools sampled from this pool into each agent environment, enabling controlled evaluation as the accessible tool surface scales. Based on GatewayBench, we conduct extensive experiments across seven widely deployed MCP gateways and diverse agent configurations, covering both benign and adversarial tasks. Our results reveal substantial differences across gateway designs in task completion, tool-use efficiency, and resilience to attacks, and further demonstrate that these effects interact strongly with the underlying model and agent harness. These findings highlight the MCP gateway as a consequential yet underexplored component of the agentic AI stack. By enabling controlled evaluation of gateway architectures under realistic tool ecosystems, GatewayBench provides both practical guidance for MCP gateway design and a rigorous testbed for developing more capable, efficient, and secure agentic systems.

</details>


### [249] [Self-Building Labs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23826)
*Jiaru Bai,Yuchi Zhao,Han Hao,Miroslav Bogdanovic,Yang Cao,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"自构建实验室"(SBL)：给定高层科学目标，系统可自行设计、配置并运行实验室装置，从空间布局、数字孪生、机器人技能到实验工作流均自动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自驱动实验室(SDL)只能执行其建造时预设的流程，迁移到新实验需大量人工工程改造，实验室灵活性成为科学发现规模化的瓶颈。

Method: SBL 以现有条件（空房间或已运行 SDL）为起点，自动补齐缺失能力：实验室布局设计、数字孪生构建、机器人技能获取与实验工作流编排；并在不同机器人、不同装置上验证，覆盖单工作站到多机器人协同实验室。

Result: 系统在多机器人及多装置场景下均可工作，可从小型单工位扩展至多机器人协作实验室；通过真实量子点合成实验完成端到端能力验证。

Conclusion: SBL 将人类研究者的角色从手动配置与编程设备自动化转变为科学目标指定，使科学自动化更具可及性、灵活性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) accelerate scientific discovery by automating experimental execution and optimization. AI agents can increasingly propose new experiments, but existing SDLs can only run the procedures they were built for. Adapting them to a new experiment still takes extensive manual engineering, which makes laboratory flexibility the bottleneck for scaling discovery. We introduce the self-building laboratory (SBL), which designs, configures, and operates its own laboratory setup given a high-level scientific goal. SBL starts from whatever already exists, from an empty room to a working SDL, and builds the missing capabilities: the lab layout, its digital twin, robot skills, and experimental workflows. We demonstrate the system working across different robots and setups, scaling from a single workstation to laboratories with several robots working together. We validate SBL's end-to-end capabilities through a real-world quantum-dot synthesis experiment. By shifting the role of human researchers from manually configuring and programming device automation to specifying scientific objectives, SBL makes scientific automation more accessible, flexible, and scalable. Project website: https://self-building-labs.github.io/

</details>


### [250] [GEMS: Molecular Structure Elucidation as Learned Search over Isomer Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23617)
*Utku Umur Acikalin,Yingheng Wang,Goncalo J. Gouveia,Tyler J. Schwertfeger,Frank C. Schroeder,Carla P. Gomes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GEMS：用保持分子式与原子价态的"2-switch"键编辑，结合MIST指纹预测与神经策略打分，从候选分子出发迭代精修，实现小分子结构鉴定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从分子式与串联质谱谱图鉴定小分子结构是无靶向代谢组学与天然产物发现的瓶颈。数据库检索或de novo预测常无法命中精确结构，但其候选物可通过少量键编辑到达邻近的正确结构，此潜力未被充分利用。

Method: GEMS采用学习的2-switch键编辑（保持分子式与各原子总价态）。先用预训练MIST模型对每条谱图一次性预测指纹概率；神经策略将候选分子指纹与预测对比，把存在特征映射到产生它们的原子与键，并在一次前向传播中对所有合法编辑打分。策略以结构间最短编辑路径训练，可先在无谱图的PubChem同分异构体上预训练，再用谱图预测指纹微调。推理时从多个匹配分子式的初始候选多起点搜索，依据指纹一致性与多起点共识排序。

Result: 在PubChem初始化中排除目标结构条件下，三轮编辑在NPLIB1上Hit@1/Hit@10达29.64%/50.81%，MassSpecGym上达30.22%/41.33%，优于可比设置下的已发表基线。NPLIB1端到端约3.3分钟即可获得更高鉴定准确率，而FRIGID运行需26.2小时。GEMS亦能提升生成候选（含FRIGID精修候选）的鉴定效果。使用精确目标指纹的实验表明指纹预测误差是目标恢复与最终排序的瓶颈；加深搜索无需重训练即可恢复更多目标，且初始候选越弱收益越大。

Conclusion: 基于指纹引导的保分子式键编辑搜索可高效精修质谱候选结构，兼顾精度与速度；未来改进重点在于降低指纹预测误差，并通过加深搜索进一步扩大收益。

Abstract: Identifying small-molecule structures from molecular formulas and tandem mass spectra remains a bottleneck in untargeted metabolomics and natural-product discovery. Even when database retrieval or de novo prediction misses the exact structure, the resulting candidates can provide starting points for exploring nearby structures through a few bond edits. We introduce GEMS (Guided Editing of Molecular Structures), which refines candidates through learned 2-switch bond edits that preserve the molecular formula and each atom's total valence. GEMS uses MIST, a pretrained spectrum-to-fingerprint model, to predict fingerprint probabilities once per spectrum. A neural policy compares each candidate's fingerprint with these predictions, links present features to the atoms and bonds that generate them, and scores all valid edits in one forward pass. The policy is trained on shortest edit paths between structures, so it can be pretrained on PubChem isomers without spectra and then fine-tuned with spectrum-predicted fingerprints. At inference, GEMS searches from multiple initial candidates matching the supplied molecular formula and ranks candidates by fingerprint agreement and multi-start consensus. With the target structure excluded from PubChem initialization, three edit rounds achieve Hit@1/Hit@10 of 29.64%/50.81% on NPLIB1 and 30.22%/41.33% on MassSpecGym, exceeding published baselines under comparable settings. On NPLIB1, GEMS achieves higher identification accuracy in about 3.3 minutes end to end, compared with 26.2 hours for our FRIGID run. GEMS also improves identification from generated candidates, including those refined by FRIGID. Experiments with exact target fingerprints identify fingerprint prediction error as a bottleneck in target recovery and final ranking. Deeper search recovers additional targets without retraining, with larger gains from weaker initial candidates.

</details>


### [251] [Characterising and Analysing Retrieval-Augmented Generation Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23226)
*Emmanuel Igbekele,Olawande Daramola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个由主维度和子维度构成的概念框架，用于刻画（"是什么"）和分析（"表现如何"）现有RAG技术，可作为实证基准测试前的预筛选机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多领域广泛应用，RAG被用于减少幻觉、提升性能，文献中已提出大量RAG技术，导致针对特定问题场景选择最合适技术成为艰巨任务；而实证基准测试计算与资金成本高昂。

Method: 通过对文献中报告的RAG技术属性进行定性分析，归纳构建包含主维度与子维度的概念框架，分别支撑"是什么"的刻画与"表现如何"的分析。

Result: 形成一个可同时用于定义和分析RAG技术的概念框架，能够按用户与任务需求对候选RAG技术进行预实证筛选。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为研究人员与实践者的预实证过滤机制，在昂贵的基准测试之前筛选出最契合的RAG候选方案。

Abstract: The advent of Large Language Models (LLMs) across many application domains has increased the need for Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to reduce hallucinations and improve LLM performance. Thus far, several RAG techniques with impressive features and capabilities have been proposed in the literature, making it a herculean task to select the most appropriate RAG techniques for specific problem scenarios. Thus, we propose a conceptual framework comprising main dimensions and sub-dimensions for characterising (defining "what it is") and analysing (defining "how well it does") existing RAG techniques. The conceptual framework was developed through a qualitative analysis of the attributes of RAG techniques reported in the literature. It can serve as a pre-empirical filtering mechanism for researchers and practitioners in selecting the highest-fit RAG candidates that meet specific user and task requirements before empirical benchmarking of RAG techniques, which can be computationally and financially expensive.

</details>


### [252] [FuzzInsight: Navigating the Efficiency-Effectiveness Trade-off in Coverage-Guided DNN Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23195)
*Vivek Vekariya,Fathiyeh Faghih,Alexander Pretschner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对DNN模糊测试的可扩展性瓶颈，提出面向配置的分析与决策指南：通过Equalized Sampling将种子队列平均利用率提升至77%（较既有方法提升13个百分点），并在四个基准数据集上评估84种配置，发现非结构类覆盖准则LSCD/LSC/MDSC以更低计算成本取得可比覆盖效果并促进行为探索；用生成的边角样本重训练可使泛化鲁棒性平均提升最多77个百分点且不损害原始测试分布性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为DNN收集与标注多样化测试集成本高、耗时长，覆盖引导模糊测试虽可从有限数据生成揭示缺陷的边角样本，但其效果高度依赖配置选择（尤其是覆盖准则与种子选择策略）。现有方法存在两大瓶颈：种子利用低效（反复优先少数输入，大量种子未被探索）与覆盖引导的计算开销高，缺乏系统性的配置分析与实践选型指南。

Method: 1）面向配置的分析：考察种子选择策略的队列利用行为；2）提出Equalized Sampling——一种基于约束的种子选择策略以提高种子队列的平均利用率；3）在MNIST、SVHN、GTSRB、CIFAR-100四个基准数据集上系统评估84种模糊测试配置，比较结构类与非结构类覆盖准则（LSCD、LSC、MDSC等）的覆盖效果与计算成本；4）用生成的边角样本重训练被测模型，评估泛化鲁棒性与原始分布性能。

Result: Equalized Sampling将平均种子队列利用率提升至77%，较先前方法提高13个百分点；84种配置的评估显示，非结构类准则LSCD、LSC、MDSC在覆盖效果上与结构类及其他非结构类准则相当，但计算开销显著更低，且能促进被测DNN的行为广度探索，减少多覆盖指标监控需求；其中LSCD具有小幅但一致的运行时优势；用生成的边角样本重训练使泛化鲁棒性平均提升最多77个百分点，且不降低原始测试分布上的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为可扩展DNN模糊测试提供了兼顾效率、行为探索与模型加固的配置选择实践蓝图：以Equalized Sampling缓解种子利用瓶颈，推荐采用低开销的非结构类覆盖准则（尤以LSCD为优选）作为覆盖引导，并验证了将模糊测试生成的边角样本用于重训练可实现鲁棒性增强而不牺牲原始精度。

Abstract: Collecting and labeling diverse test datasets for evaluating Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) is costly and time-consuming. This has motivated coverage-guided fuzzing techniques that generate defect-revealing corner cases from limited test data. However, their effectiveness depends heavily on configuration choices, particularly coverage criteria and seed-selection strategies. We present a configuration-oriented analysis and decision guidelines for scalable DNN fuzzing targeting two bottlenecks: inefficient seed utilization and the cost of coverage guidance. Our analysis shows that existing seed-selection strategies repeatedly prioritize a small subset of inputs, leaving many collected seeds unexplored. To address this issue, we propose Equalized Sampling, a constraint-based strategy that increases mean seed queue utilization to 77%, improving on prior approaches by 13 percentage points.
  We further evaluate 84 fuzzing configurations on four benchmark datasets: MNIST, SVHN, GTSRB, and CIFAR-100. Results show that the non-structural criteria LSCD, LSC, and MDSC achieve coverage outcomes comparable to structural and alternative non-structural criteria while requiring substantially less computation. These criteria also promote broad behavioral exploration of the DNN under test, reducing the need to monitor multiple coverage metrics. Among them, LSCD provides a modest but consistent runtime advantage.
  Finally, we show that retraining with generated corner cases improves generalization robustness by up to 77 percentage points on average without degrading performance on the original test distribution. Together, these findings provide a practical blueprint for selecting fuzzing configurations that balance efficiency, behavioral exploration, and model hardening.

</details>


### [253] [OfficeTown: Generative Organizations for Evolving Agent Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23194)
*Hongchen Wei,Daohong Ying,Zhi Hong,Aidan Wu,Bei Liu,Ruichun Ma,Yifan Yang,Qi Dai,Kai Qiu,Rui Xu,Dongdong Chen,Zhenzhong Chen,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OfficeTown 是一个面向专业组织的生成式仿真框架，任务与上下文源自持续性的组织工作：由组织目的与活动的自然语言描述初始化组织，成员协作、向助手代理分派有界任务并评审交付物；工件、决策与未决事项持久化并影响后续工作。基于 23 个组织、8 个专业领域构建了含 406 个任务与 4,836 条评估标准的 OfficeTown Bench，实验显示前沿模型仍有较大提升空间，OfficeTown 数据上的 SFT 与 on-policy 蒸馏可显著提升 Qwen3.5-9B 在 GDPval 等外部基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI agent 越来越多地使用文件与软件工具产出专业性交付物，此类工作依赖跨任务累积的材料、决策与反馈；但现有基准与训练环境多将任务视为孤立、静态的样本，缺乏组织上下文、权限约束与历史延续性，难以真实反映专业工作过程，也难以生成"工作接地"（work-grounded）的数据与评测环境。

Method: 提出 OfficeTown 生成式仿真框架：由自然语言的组织目的与活动描述自动初始化组织；成员协调工作、将受限（bounded）任务委派给助手代理并评审交付物；工件、决策与未决事项持久保存并塑造后续工作；职责与权限界定每个成员可见与可操作的范围。将工作请求、上下文与评审反馈整理为可执行资源，用于评测与学习。据此构建 OfficeTown Bench（23 个组织、8 个专业领域、406 个任务、4,836 条评估标准，并纳入领域专家反馈）。

Result: 1) 前沿模型评测显示仍有显著提升空间（substantial headroom）；2) 在 OfficeTown 数据上进行监督微调（SFT）与 on-policy 蒸馏，可显著提升 Qwen3.5-9B 在 GDPval 等外部专业工作基准上的表现；3) 补充评测显示 principal–assistant 协作框架的得分高于所测的单代理与多代理基线。

Conclusion: 组织级仿真能有效生成具有工作接地性的数据与环境，推动通用型 agent 的持续发展；持久化的组织状态、权限机制与评审回路是构建贴近真实专业工作评测与训练资源的关键要素。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly use files and software tools to produce professional deliverables. Such work draws on materials, decisions, and feedback accumulated across assignments. We introduce OfficeTown, a generative simulation framework for professional organizations in which tasks and their context arise from continuing organizational work. From a natural-language description of an organization's purpose and activities, OfficeTown initializes an organization whose members coordinate work, delegate bounded assignments to assistant agents, and review their deliverables. Artifacts, decisions, and unresolved matters persist and shape subsequent work, while responsibilities and permissions determine what each member can see and do. We curate work requests, their context, and review feedback into executable resources for evaluation and learning. We construct OfficeTown Bench with 406 tasks and 4,836 evaluation criteria from 23 simulated organizations across eight professional domains, incorporating domain-expert feedback. Frontier-model evaluations reveal substantial remaining headroom. Supervised fine-tuning and on-policy distillation on OfficeTown data substantially improve Qwen3.5-9B on external professional-work benchmarks, including GDPval. Supplementary evaluations show that the principal--assistant harness achieves higher scores than the single- and multi-agent baselines tested. These results highlight the value of organizational simulation in generating work-grounded data and environments for the continued development of generalist agents.

</details>


### [254] [LongForge: Measuring and Improving Long-Context Instruction Data via Evidence-Assertion Reasoning Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23167)
*Ziyang Chen,Junlong Jia,Xing Wu,Tinghao Yu,Songlin Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将长上下文指令微调数据质量形式化为文档-问题-答案间的对齐结构属性，提出分析器+合成器一体的LongForge，实现可审计、可控制的训练分布构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文指令调优数据的质量控制仅是生成流水线内的一次性判定，只输出一个标量分数，既无法揭示答案使用了文档的哪些部分，也无法反映推理步数，导致数据既不可审计也不可控。

Method: 把质量定义为文档、问题、答案三者对齐结构的属性；构建LongForge，含分析器与合成器。分析器将每个样本逆向重构为"证据—断言"推理图，并在图上程序化计算广度、深度、正确性、完整性四个维度，每个读数可回溯到具体证据片段与推理步骤。合成器把分析器嵌入生成过程，对正确性与完整性设门控，将广度与深度交由训练期在实测值上的混合采样决定。

Result: 测量七个主流数据集，发现两个此前未被量化的缺陷：广度几乎不随文档长度增长，深度普遍偏低，且二者近乎正交。在两个基座模型上，LongForge-1与LongForge-N在六个长上下文基准上平均超出最强基线2.2–4.7分；在固定门控与候选池的受控对比中，仅提升广度与深度即可再带来1.16分增益。

Conclusion: 训练数据的结构分布不仅是下游质量的描述量，更是可调节的杠杆；以图结构度量并注入生成流程，可实现长上下文数据质量的审计与控制。

Abstract: Existing quality control for long-context instruction-tuning data is a one-shot judgment inside the generation pipeline that leaves only a scalar, revealing neither which document parts an answer uses nor how many reasoning steps it writes down; the data can thus be neither audited nor controlled. We formalize data quality as a property of the alignment structure among document, question, and answer, and build LongForge, which pairs an analyzer that measures long-context data with a synthesizer that generates it. The analyzer reverse-reconstructs each sample into an evidence-assertion reasoning graph and programmatically computes four dimensions on it: breadth, depth, correctness, and completeness. Every reading traces back to specific evidence spans and reasoning steps, making data auditable. Measuring seven mainstream datasets reveals two previously unquantified defects: breadth barely grows with document length, and depth is generally low, the two being nearly orthogonal. The synthesizer then embeds the analyzer into generation, gating on correctness and completeness and deferring breadth and depth to a training-time mixture over measured values, which makes the training distribution controllable. On two backbones, its two mixtures, LongForge-1 and LongForge-N, outperform the strongest baseline by 2.2-4.7 points on average over six long-context benchmarks, and in a controlled comparison that holds the gate and the candidate pool fixed, raising breadth and depth alone is worth a further 1.16 points, making the structural distribution of training data a lever on downstream quality rather than only a description of it.

</details>


### [255] [CIIR - Class-Indexed Interface Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23151)
*Doo Yon Kim,Christian Wallraven*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CIIR（类索引地址空间）编解码框架，用地址k与128维表示Z_k(x)连接异构无配对数据集，实现跨数据集通信与事后数据集挂接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习通常依赖配对或同步观测，而独立采集的数据集几乎不提供这种对应关系，因此需要一个不要求实例级配对的多模态/跨数据集接口。

Method: 模块化编码器-解码器框架：以类别索引作为地址空间的键，已知局部索引选取地址k，写入依赖输入的128维地址表示Z_k(x)；同时保留各数据集原生输入格式与输出模块。用手写数字、服装图像、口说话音频谱图、手势四类不同步数据学习地址空间，再训练无标签接收器还原地址。

Result: 新训练的无标签接收器宏平均Address Realization Accuracy达94.7%；冻结基础地址空间后仅训练轻量adapter接入未见SVHN，在无目标端解码标签条件下实现MNIST→SVHN 90.2%、SVHN→MNIST 82.3%的端到端Address Transfer Accuracy；并将十个地址复用为无冲突算术码元与100个CIFAR-100类别的分层地址码。

Conclusion: CIIR可作为紧凑的模块化跨数据集通信接口，支持事后数据集接入，无需穷尽的配对多模态监督。

Abstract: Multimodal learning commonly assumes paired or synchronized observations, yet independently collected datasets rarely provide such correspondence. We introduce CIIR, a modular encoder--decoder framework that connects heterogeneous datasets through a class-indexed address space while preserving each dataset's native input format and output module. A known local index selects address $k$, and an input-dependent 128-dimensional address representation Z_k(x) is written to that address. To demonstrate CIIR, we learn this address space from handwritten digits, clothing images, spoken-digit spectrograms, and hand signs without synchronized or instance-matched samples. Newly trained label-free receivers recover the requested addresses with 94.7% macro-average Address Realization Accuracy. We then attach the unseen SVHN dataset after freezing the base address space and train only lightweight adapters, achieving 90.2% MNIST>SVHN and 82.3% SVHN>MNIST end-to-end Address Transfer Accuracy without target-side decoder labels. To demonstrate generalizability of CIIR, we reuse the ten addresses as code elements in collision-free arithmetic and hierarchical address codes for all 100 CIFAR-100 classes and evaluate direct arithmetic-code realization through the existing receivers. These results establish CIIR as a compact interface for modular cross-dataset communication and post-hoc dataset attachment without exhaustive paired multimodal supervision.

</details>


### [256] [When Can You Trust an Agent's Own Verifier? Stress-Testing Counterfactual Rationale Verification Under Search Pressure and Oracle Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22779)
*Jonghyun Lee,Moonsu Kim,Sunghwan Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文不提出新验证器，而是提出一套"压力测试"现有验证器的方法：用压力比ρ量化验证器对agent搜索的引导强度，并设计四个可证伪探针覆盖四种失败模式（无信号、过弱、被"少陈述断言"策略刷分、提交前探测）。在ML流水线设计agent中对自研RCS进行实证：先前配置下ρ≪1、验证器几乎不影响搜索目标，却通过最终筛选tie-break影响产物；能力对照显示缩短断言列表的指令未能传导到搜索，因反馈缺失断言数量且视野短，null结果更偏结构性而非稳健性证据；提交前可探测时agent保留通过验证的断言而留出集忠实度下降。作者据此提出报告规范：信任验证器前需以ρ与探针进行审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化agent设计普遍依赖单一分数判定"改进"，但被过度优化的分数会失去其度量意义。当agent同时给出设计解释时，该解释可作为无需人工标注的第二信号（否定断言后重跑观察性能是否下降）。核心问题：一旦agent针对该验证器优化，验证器是否仍然可靠——即对验证器本身缺乏抗压审计手段。

Method: 提出压力测试框架而非新验证器：定义压力比ρ度量验证器对搜索的引导强度；设计四个可证伪探针分别对应四种失败模式（无信号、强度不足、通过减少断言数量被刷分、提交前被查询）。在LLM agent设计ML流水线的设定下，对Rationale Consistency Score（RCS）进行测试，使用单一生成模型、六个任务、三个随机种子，并设置能力对照与留出集忠实度检验。

Result: （1）先前配置中ρ≪1，部分源于准确率门控，验证器几乎不进入搜索目标，其稳健性实际未被检验；但其仍通过最终筛选的tie-break影响报告产物，而ρ未捕捉这一效应。（2）能力对照显示agent在被指示时会缩短断言列表，但搜索行为未随之改变，即使ρ超过先前水平100倍以上；由于反馈不含断言数量且优化视野短，该null结果主要是结构性的，不能作为稳健性证据。（3）当agent可在提交前探测验证器时，它保留能通过验证的断言，而留出集忠实度下降——该下降在环内指标中不可见，但部分混杂于RCS对小规模流水线的评分方式。

Conclusion: 结果属初步证据。作者提出报告标准：在对验证器赋予信任前，应先以压力比ρ和上述四个探针进行审计，并注意将"结构性null"与真正稳健性区分开，同时关注环外（留出集）指标与tie-break等隐性影响渠道。

Abstract: Automated agent design increasingly trusts a single score to decide whether a change is an improvement -- but a score optimized aggressively enough ceases to measure what it was meant to. When an agent also explains its design, that explanation is a second signal we can check without human labels: negate each stated claim, re-run, and observe whether performance drops. We ask whether such a verifier stays reliable once an agent optimizes against it. Our contribution is not a new verifier but a way to stress-test one: a pressure ratio $ρ$ measuring how much the verifier steers the agent's search, and four falsifiable probes covering four failure modes -- giving no signal, being too weak to matter, being gamed by stating fewer claims, and being queried before the agent commits. We apply the test to our own verifier, the Rationale Consistency Score (RCS), inside an LLM agent that designs machine-learning pipelines, with one generator model, six tasks, and three seeds. First, in our prior configuration the verifier barely affects the search objective ($ρ\ll 1$), partly because of an accuracy gate, so its robustness had not been tested; it still shapes the reported artifact through a final-selection tie-break that $ρ$ does not register. Second, a capability control shows the agent shortens its claim lists when instructed, yet search does not, even at $ρ$ over 100 times the prior level; since feedback omits claim volume and horizons are short, this null result is largely structural rather than evidence of robustness. Third, when the agent can probe the verifier before committing, it keeps claims that pass while held-out faithfulness falls -- a decline invisible to in-loop metrics, though partly confounded by how RCS scores small pipelines. We offer these as preliminary evidence and propose a reporting standard: audit a verifier with $ρ$ and these probes before trusting it.

</details>


### [257] [StoBasket: Joint Upsell and Cross-Sell Optimization for Contribution Margin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22768)
*Xin Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出联合随机篮筐编辑(StoBasket)框架,将加购(upsell)与交叉销售(cross-sell)统一建模,以期望增量贡献毛利为优化目标,在真实数据校准仿真中近乎捕获全部可获利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售推荐器通常只排序"相关性",但零售商真正关心的是贡献毛利:加购收益是替换商品带来的毛利差,交叉销售则获得完整毛利,二者经济学含义不同。已有方法将二者分离建模,忽略了两类响应之间的耦合。

Method: 提出joint stochastic basket editing:单一策略同时为既有篮筐选择替换(upsell)与追加(cross-sell)报价,通过展示报价、接受报价与联合接受三条响应通道耦合,并以期望增量贡献毛利估值;可分离建模是其零耦合特例。另对结构化子类刻画分阶段策略失效条件并给出精确优化器,估计值支持决策感知的市场篮筐分析。

Result: 在五个公开篮筐数据集校准仿真中,StoBasket在各默认价格设置下与可达毛利差距≤0.5%,真实响应超出模型族时≤0.7%;毛利加权热度法最高损失11%可达毛利,已发表next-basket推荐器损失11–52%;Instacart上联合选择增益10.2%;仅联合付费场景下最佳启发式损失64%,精确搜索≤0.5%;精确优化器处理真实篮筐中位0.13 ms、最大111 ms;5000次随机会话训练的正确结构预测器距完美预测仅0.06%,无信号预测器损失38%。

Conclusion: 正确的经济核算(贡献毛利而非相关性)是最大收益来源,利润在预测精度提升前早已饱和;联合建模加购与交叉销售及决策感知训练显著优于分离式与纯精度导向方法。

Abstract: Retail recommenders rank relevance, but retailers earn contribution margin: an upsell earns the margin difference over the item it replaces, whereas a cross-sell earns its full margin. We introduce joint stochastic basket editing, a formulation in which one policy selects replacement and addition offers for an existing basket, values them by expected incremental contribution margin, and couples them through displayed-offer, accepted-offer, and joint-acceptance response channels. To our knowledge, it is the first formulation in which upsell and cross-sell responses are coupled; prior separable treatments are its zero-coupling special case. In real-data-calibrated simulations on five public basket datasets, StoBasket captures essentially all attainable offer margin: within 0.5% in every default-price setting and within 0.7% when the true response lies outside its model family. Correct economic accounting is the broadest gain: margin-weighted popularity leaves up to 11% of attainable margin unrealized, and a published next-basket recommender leaves 11-52%. Joint selection adds 10.2% of attainable margin on Instacart, where premium tiers and add-ons are strongly linked. When offers pay only together, the best heuristics lose 64% while exact search stays within 0.5%. For a structured subclass, we characterize when staged policies fail and give an exact optimizer that solves every tested real basket in 0.13 ms median and at most 111 ms. Profit saturates long before prediction accuracy: a correctly structured predictor trained on 5,000 randomized sessions comes within 0.06% of perfect prediction, while a signal-free one loses 38%. The same estimates yield a decision-aware market-basket analysis whose cost does not grow with association order.

</details>


### [258] [At the Limits of Computability: Steps Towards Autonomous AI Discovery on the Busy Beaver Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22603)
*Marco Dos Santos,Vivek Veeriah,Alex Havrilla,Andrew Bolt,Goran Zuzic,Satinder Singh,Tom Zahavy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于Gemini的智能体流水线，在不可计算的Busy Beaver (BB) 域中自动发现图灵机加速规则，并为BB(6)中11台机器自主发现并形式化验证了新的Collatz型加速，暂定为 Cryptids。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI数学突破多集中于预先定义的、人类提出的问题，难以自主发现数学结构与提出新问题；而BB域因BB(n)不可计算而天然开放，适合作为持续探索的场景。

Method: 构建Gemini驱动的智能体流水线，整合模式识别、自动猜想、非形式化数学推理与Lean形式化定理证明，自动推导将多步执行压缩为紧凑数学规则的图灵机加速。

Result: 系统自主发现并对BB(6)中11台机器的新颖Collatz型加速完成形式化验证；经社区认可后可暂归类为Cryptids——空白纸带执行具精确紧凑数学描述但停机行为仍未解决。

Conclusion: 成果推进了BB(6)滞留机器的分析，展示了自主AI系统在基础计算机科学中开展开放式数学发现的潜力。

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in AI for mathematics---ranging from Olympiad medal performances to resolving long-standing open conjectures---have rapidly expanded what automated systems can achieve. Yet these successes primarily concern predefined, human-formulated problems, leaving open how AI systems can autonomously uncover mathematical structure and formulate new questions. To push beyond static challenges, we explore the Busy Beaver (BB) domain, an uncomputable frontier that is fundamentally open-ended. Because no algorithm can compute the Busy Beaver number $\mathrm{BB}(n)$ for every $n$, this domain provides a natural setting for sustained exploration through mathematical conjecture and proof. In this paper, we introduce a Gemini-based agentic pipeline that automatically discovers accelerations of Turing machines, expressed as compact mathematical rules summarizing many execution steps. Our system integrates pattern recognition, automated conjecturing, informal mathematical reasoning, and formal theorem proving in Lean. With this framework, our system autonomously discovered and formally verified novel Collatz-like accelerations for eleven machines in $\mathrm{BB}(6)$. Subject to community acceptance, we provisionally classify these machines as Cryptids, namely machines whose blank-tape executions admit exact, compact mathematical descriptions but whose halting behavior remains unresolved. These results advance the analysis of $\mathrm{BB}(6)$ holdouts on the Busy Beaver frontier and demonstrate the potential of autonomous AI systems for open-ended mathematical discovery in fundamental computer science.

</details>


### [259] [From Natural Language Intent to Executable Control: Hierarchical Feedback-Guided LLM Planning for Multi-component Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22441)
*Jinglin Dai,Jiawan Wang,Ruiqi Hong,Lei Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出分层反馈框架，让LLM同时生成任务计划与STL约束，并用形式化与执行级诊断反馈引导重规划，在七个异构CPS场景上达到87.9%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可将自然语言指令翻译为CPS任务计划，但存在两处关键缺陷：(1) 生成的计划可能误解或遗漏用户意图；(2) 逻辑上看似合理的计划在组件动力学、交互或安全约束下可能不可行。现有LLM规划方法缺乏关于"计划为何失败"的信息性反馈，难以为计划修订提供指导。

Method: 构建分层反馈框架，在不同层级使用不同类型反馈引导LLM：(1) 任务层——让LLM同时生成结构化任务计划与对应的信号时序逻辑(STL)约束，设计可靠(sound)算法识别两者之间的确定性矛盾并反馈以修正不一致；(2) 执行层——当控制综合失败时，提取诊断信息（如被违反的约束、冲突的组件指派）反馈给LLM以指导任务重规划。

Result: 在七个异构多组件CPS场景上的实验表明，该框架能生成满足原始自然语言任务的可执行计划与可行控制执行；使用GPT-5时成功率达87.9%。

Conclusion: 引入形式化STL一致性与执行诊断的分层反馈机制，可有效弥补LLM生成CPS计划在意图对齐与动力学可行性上的不足，实现高成功率的端到端可执行规划。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide a convenient interface for translating natural language commands into task plans for cyber-physical systems (CPSs). However, two gaps hinder their use for real execution: generated plans may misinterpret or omit the user's intent, and even a logically plausible plan may be infeasible under component dynamics, interactions, or safety constraints. Existing LLM-based planning approaches often lack informative feedback on why a generated plan fails, providing little guidance for revising the plan accordingly. To address these challenges, we present a hierarchical feedback framework that uses different types of feedback information to guide LLMs in generating executable plans for CPSs. We prompt LLMs to generate both a structured task plan and corresponding signal temporal logic (STL) constraints, and design a sound algorithm that identifies definite contradictions between them and provides feedback for revising inconsistencies. At the execution level, when control synthesis fails, we extract different types of diagnostic information, such as violated constraints and conflicting component assignments, and feed them back to guide task replanning. Experiments on seven heterogeneous multi-component CPS scenarios have shown that our framework can generate executable plans and feasible control executions that satisfy the original natural language missions with a success rate of 87.9% with GPT-5.

</details>


### [260] [Sensitivity Analysis of Decision Rules in an Agent-Based Agro-Ecological Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22420)
*Benjamin Pianet,Baptiste Lesquoy,Paul Saves,Benoit Gaudou,Jean Villerd,Nicolas Verstaevel,Olivier Therond*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文把农艺管理决策的层次结构作为一等待建模对象，将MAELIA平台的作物管理方案编码为含类别与数值变量的分层设计空间，提出随机森林监督的HSIC-ANOVA方法，在决策树上原生排序主效应与交互效应，使稀有触发与常触发决策被平等对待，并在氮淋失、土壤有机碳变化与产量预测上给出可解释的决策导向解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于智能体的模型适合用于农艺生态系统建模与决策支持，但要把模拟转化为可行动的洞见，必须识别哪些决策真正驱动系统行为。然而这类系统的参数并非平坦向量，而是决策树结构：许多参数仅在高层选择激活后才出现。经典全局敏感性分析假设输入在固定笛卡尔积上相互独立，在此分支空间上既不适定又存在系统性偏差。

Method: 将决策结构作为一等的建模对象；利用先进代理模型技术，把MAELIA高分辨率农艺生态建模平台的作物管理行程编码为含类别变量与数值变量的分层设计空间；提出专门针对层次空间的新型方法——随机森林监督的HSIC-ANOVA，在树上原生排序主效应与交互效应，并将稀有激活与始终激活的决策置于同等地位。

Result: 将该方法应用于氮淋失、土壤有机碳变化与产量预测等模拟输出时，能够得到可解释的、面向决策的解读，从而识别出真正驱动模拟行为的关键决策及其交互。

Conclusion: 针对层次化决策空间的敏感性分析需放弃平坦笛卡尔积假设，转而以决策树结构为核心；随机森林监督的HSIC-ANOVA为这类分支设计空间提供了原则性、无偏且可解释的效应排序工具，桥接了智能体模拟与可操作的决策洞见。

Abstract: Agent-based models are well-suited for modelling agro-ecological systems for decision-making, yet turning their simulations into actionable insight requires identifying which decisions actually drive the behaviours. In such a context, sensitivity analysis stumbles upon a structural feature of many complex systems: the parameters are not a flat vector but a decision tree in which many parameters appear only when a higher-level choice activates them. Classical global sensitivity analysis presupposes independent inputs over a fixed Cartesian product and is ill-posed on such a branched space and biased by construction. We instead treat the structure of the decisions as a first-class modelling object. Using advanced surrogate modelling techniques, we encode the crop-management itinerary of the high-resolution agro-ecological modelling platform MAELIA as a hierarchical design space featuring both categorical and numerical variables. We propose a novel dedicated method to analyse this kind of hierarchical space, a random-forest-supervised HSIC-ANOVA, which ranks main effects and interactions natively on the tree while placing rarely and always active decisions on an equal footing. We show how, when applied to simulation outputs such as nitrogen leaching, soil organic carbon variation, and yield predictions, it can deliver an interpretable, decision-oriented reading.

</details>


### [261] [Rethinking User Encoders: Routing Diffusion States for Scenario-Adaptive User Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22400)
*Junru Zhang,Jinyong Wen,Bin Dou,Wuliang Huang,Baokun Wang,Yu Cheng,Weiqiang Wang,Cheng Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FINDERS框架，将扩散语言模型（dLLM）的迭代去噪过程作为多粒度重构空间，通过"去噪-因子化-路由-压缩"四步操作用户行为日志得到场景自适应的用户表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的用户编码器将异构行为日志序列化后，通过自回归单向预测压缩为静态表征。单向约束限制了双向信息流动，阻碍跨模态交互与多源信号的联合保留，导致学到的静态表征难以满足不同下游场景对差异化证据的需求。

Method: 利用dLLM的迭代去噪过程构建多粒度重构空间；引入可学习meta-slot，将互补的去噪状态组织为可迁移的跨模态语义与模态特异的行因子；构建含显式行为理由的大规模语料BehaviorQA提供监督；设计轻量router，依据场景查询通过输入级残差通路动态强化相关slot方向，再进行跨源融合与最终压缩。

Result: 在十个工业场景的离线实验与在线A/B测试中，FINDERS在统一设置与场景自适应设置下均持续优于SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 该方法将dLLM的应用边界从语言生成拓展至用户表征学习，验证了其作为强大用户表征学习器的潜力。

Abstract: Existing large language model (LLM)-based user encoders serialize heterogeneous behavioral logs and compress them into static representations through autoregressive (AR) prediction. However, their unidirectional constraint limits bidirectional information flow, hindering cross-modal interactions and the joint preservation of multi-source signals. Consequently, the learned static representations struggle to accommodate the distinct evidence requirements of diverse downstream scenarios. We address these limitations by unlocking the iterative denoising process of diffusion language models (dLLMs) as a multi-granularity reconstruction space. Based on this insight, we propose FINDERS (FactorIzatioN of Diffusion statEs for Routing across Scenarios), a router-equipped dLLM framework comprising four operations: denoise, factorize, route, and compress. Learnable meta-slots organize complementary denoising states into transferable cross-modal semantics and modality-specific behavioral factors. To provide grounded supervision, we construct BehaviorQA, a large-scale corpus containing explicit behavioral rationales. Guided by scenario queries, a lightweight router dynamically emphasizes relevant slot directions via an input-level residual pathway before cross-source fusion and final compression. Extensive experiments and online A/B tests across ten industrial scenarios demonstrate that FINDERS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both unified and scenario-adaptive settings. This work effectively pushes the boundaries of dLLMs beyond language generation, demonstrating their immense potential as powerful user representation learners.

</details>


### [262] [Efficient CPU-GPU collaborative MoE inference via Adaptive Expert Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23006)
*Wenhui Lv,Yunming Liao,Hongli Xu,Yang Xu,Junhao Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CPU-GPU 协同的 MoE 推理系统，自适应地将 GPU 未命中的专家分配到 CPU 或 GPU 执行，以低负载专家在 CPU 计算、高负载专家传输至 GPU 计算的方式平衡两条执行路径，从而降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 通过稀疏专家激活降低推理计算量，但专家参数量大，难以全部驻留 GPU，需进行专家卸载（offloading）。现有系统对 GPU 未命中的专家要么传输到 GPU（PCIe 传输延迟高），要么直接在 CPU 上计算（CPU 计算慢成为瓶颈），两种方案均不理想。

Method: 构建 CPU-GPU 协同推理系统：利用专家间 token 负载的差异，将高负载专家选择性传输至 GPU 执行、低负载专家留在 CPU 执行；并自适应调整专家分配，使 CPU 计算路径与 GPU「传输+计算」路径的完成时间尽量对齐，从而最小化整体推理延迟。

Result: 在三个代表性 MoE 大模型上的实验表明，相比最强基线，prefill 速度最高提升 51.9%，decode 吞吐最高提升 39.5%，且在多种 GPU 显存预算下均保持稳定增益。

Conclusion: 将专家卸载的「传输 vs 本地计算」二选一问题转化为按负载感知的细粒度协同调度，可有效缓解 PCIe 传输与 CPU 计算两类瓶颈，提升显存受限场景下的 MoE 推理效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models reduce inference computation through sparse expert activation, but their large expert memory footprint often exceeds GPU capacity, making expert offloading necessary for memory-constrained inference. However, existing systems typically handle GPU-missed experts by either transferring them to the GPU, incurring substantial PCIe transfer latency, or executing them on the CPU, making the slower CPU computation the bottleneck. To address these limitations, we present \method, a CPU-GPU collaborative MoE inference system that adaptively assigns these missed experts to the CPU or GPU for efficient execution. By exploiting differences in expert token-loads, \method selectively transfers high-load experts to the GPU while executing low-load experts on the CPU. To minimize inference latency, \method adaptively adjusts expert assignments to balance the CPU computation path against the GPU transfer-and-computation path, allowing both execution paths to complete as early as possible. Extensive experiments on three representative MoE LLMs show that \method improves prefill speed by up to 51.9\% and decode throughput by up to 39.5\% over the strongest baseline across the evaluated batch sizes, with consistent gains under different GPU-memory budgets.

</details>


### [263] [ToPA: Block-wise Toeplitz Adaptation for Expressive and Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22883)
*Sicong Li,Qianqian Xu,Zhiyong Yang,Zitai Wang,Longtao Huang,Xiaochun Cao,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ToPA：用分块 Toeplitz 矩阵乘积进行参数高效微调，兼具紧凑参数化、无显式秩/稀疏约束、短链训练稳定的优点，在 21 个 NLP/CV 数据集、5 种架构上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重参数化 PEFT 方法存在两大局限：低秩方法限制了更新秩，与全量微调本身的高秩特性不一致，因而性能落后；稀疏方法限制了参数空间灵活性并破坏权重间连通性。作者希望找到一种既紧凑、又不施加显式秩或稀疏约束的参数化方式。

Method: 利用 Toeplitz 矩阵（每条对角线元素恒定）实现紧凑参数化。直接采用多个 Toeplitz 矩阵相乘会因长乘性链导致类似 RNN 的梯度不稳定，因此提出 ToPA：以分块 Toeplitz 矩阵替代标准 Toeplitz 矩阵，提升每个因子的容量并缩短乘积链，从而稳定训练、增强表达能力。

Result: 理论证明分块 Toeplitz 矩阵的乘积可在更短链长下逼近任意矩阵，为结构设计提供依据；并证明 ToPA 的表达能力强于现有低秩与稀疏参数化。实验上在 21 个 NLP 与 CV 数据集、5 种模型架构上一致验证了 ToPA 的有效性。

Conclusion: ToPA 通过分块 Toeplitz 乘积参数化，在保持参数紧凑的同时克服了低秩与稀疏方法的表达力瓶颈及长链梯度不稳定问题，是一种表达力更强、训练更稳定的 PEFT 方案。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) has emerged as a prevalent strategy for adapting large pre-trained models. Among PEFT techniques, reparameterization-based methods, particularly those that employ low-rank or sparsity structures, have attracted significant attention. However, these approaches often underperform full fine-tuning. Specifically, low-rank methods constrain the update rank, which is misaligned with the inherently high-rank nature of full fine-tuning. In contrast, sparsity-based techniques limit the flexibility of the parameter space and compromise connectivity across weights. To overcome these limitations, we explore the use of Toeplitz matrices, whose entries are constant along each diagonal, therefore providing a compact parameterization without imposing explicit rank or sparsity constraints. A straightforward approach is to update weights via a product of Toeplitz matrices. However, similar to RNNs, long multiplicative chains often lead to gradient instability. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{ToPA}, a more refined version of this idea that replaces standard Toeplitz matrices with block-wise Toeplitz, greatly increasing each factor's capacity and shortening the chain, thereby stabilizing training and improving expressiveness. We theoretically show that products of block-wise Toeplitz matrices can approximate arbitrary matrices under shorter chain length, justifying the structural design of ToPA. Furthermore, we demonstrate that ToPA offers greater expressivity than existing low-rank and sparse parameterizations. Empirical evaluations across 21 NLP and CV datasets, spanning 5 model architectures, consistently validate the effectiveness of ToPA.

</details>


### [264] [From Trajectories to Differentiated Skills: Cognitive Differentiation for Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23599)
*Yewei Zhao,Derong Xu,Wei Chen,Yin Xu,Peilun Zhou,Xiaohan Wang,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Mingjun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CDSE（技能演化中的认知分化）方法，将统一技能拆解为Meta、Experience、Tool、Script四个功能异质的组件并协同演化，在六个基准、三种agent模型、两种执行框架的36个设置中全部取得最佳成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agent技能演化方法主要围绕"技能制品"组织知识，忽视了单个技能内部的功能异质性，以及这种异质性对知识维护与使用的影响。

Method: 受认知科学启发，CDSE将统一技能演化为四个功能不同但相互协调的组件（Meta、Experience、Tool、Script）；从成功与失败轨迹中抽取有证据支撑的语义梯度，按功能角色归并，再施加组件特定的更新与验证，并进行跨组件协调与自适应调用。

Result: 对500个排名靠前的技能包分析显示，96.8%包含至少两种功能角色的知识；在6个基准、3种agent模型、2种执行框架的36个设置中CDSE均得分最高，较Base agent提升7.6个百分点，较各设置中最强对比方法平均提升2.6个百分点；消融实验支持功能分化与自适应调用的重要性。

Conclusion: 技能内部的功能分化具有普遍性且对性能至关重要；按功能角色组织并协同演化技能知识，可显著提升LLM agent的复杂任务表现。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents tackle complex tasks through reasoning and interaction with external environments. Agent skills provide persistent, transferable capabilities that guide task planning, inform contextual decisions, support tool use, and enable executable procedures. Yet existing skill-evolution methods largely organize knowledge around skill artifacts, overlooking the functional heterogeneity within individual skills and its implications for knowledge maintenance and use. Inspired by cognitive science, we propose Cognitive Differentiation in Skill Evolution (CDSE), which evolves a unified skill through four functionally distinct yet coordinated components: Meta, Experience, Tool, and Script. CDSE extracts evidence-grounded semantic gradients from successful and failed trajectories, consolidates them by functional role, and applies component-specific updates and validation with cross-component coordination and adaptive invocation. An analysis of 500 top-ranked skill packages shows that 96.8% contain knowledge spanning at least two functional roles. Across six benchmarks, three agent models, and two execution harnesses, CDSE achieves the highest scores in all 36 settings, improving over the Base agent by 7.6 percentage points and the strongest competing method in each setting by 2.6 points on average. Ablation studies further support the importance of functional differentiation and adaptive invocation.

</details>


### [265] [S-GAP: Stealthy Graph Attack Primitives for Graph Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23465)
*Wenda Li,Tongya Zheng,Shunyu Liu,Hanyang Yuan,Kaixuan Chen,Yu Wang,Yuchen Ying,Haoze Jiang,Huan Li,Gang Chen,Zujie Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出图攻击原语GAP与多智能体框架S-GAP，通过统一文本与结构扰动的攻击意图，缓解意图碎片化，提升对GraphLLM攻击的有效性与隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对文本属性图（TAG）上GraphLLM的对抗攻击分阶段构造文本扰动与结构扰动，二者由不同实现意图引导，导致"攻击意图碎片化"，使两类扰动语义不连贯，破坏攻击隐蔽性，易被GraphLLM识别并忽略，削弱攻击效果。

Method: 提出Graph Attack Primitive（GAP）——一种在统一攻击意图下同时表征文本与结构扰动的原子语义单元，通过精细校准其语义组合兼顾攻击有效性与隐蔽性；进而提出S-GAP多智能体框架，迭代精炼GAP语义内容，统一GAP的规划与实现，并借助代理模型评估与文本反馈持续优化。

Result: 在4个数据集、3个GraphLLM、2种攻击设置上的实验表明，S-GAP相较最强基线平均将ASR提升7.02%，且生成的扰动在关系上更具合理性。

Conclusion: 统一攻击意图可有效提升对GraphLLM的攻击效果与隐蔽性，揭示当前GraphLLM的关键脆弱性，并推动更具鲁棒性的防御研究。

Abstract: Graph Large Language Models (GraphLLMs) enable powerful semantic understanding and reasoning over Text-Attributed Graphs (TAGs), while their semantic reasoning may exhibit vulnerabilities to perturbations in node text and graph structure under adversarial attack evaluation. Existing attacks typically construct textual and structural perturbations in separate stages guided by distinct realization intents. However, this separation results in attack-intent fragmentation, causing semantic incoherence between the two perturbation types, thereby undermining attack stealth and making them easier for GraphLLMs to identify and disregard, ultimately weakening attack effectiveness. To avoid attack-intent fragmentation, we propose the Graph Attack Primitive (GAP), an atomic semantic representation that unifies textual and structural perturbations under a coherent attack intent. By carefully calibrating the semantic composition of a GAP, we can achieve strong attack effectiveness while maintaining sufficient attack stealth. Furthermore, we introduce S-GAP (Stealthy GAP), a multi-agent framework that iteratively refines the semantic content of the GAP. The framework unifies GAP planning and realization and continuously refines this process through surrogate evaluation and textual feedback. Extensive experiments across four datasets, three GraphLLMs, and two attack settings show that S-GAP improves ASR over the strongest baselines by 7.02% on average while producing more relationally plausible perturbations, exposing critical vulnerabilities in current GraphLLMs and motivating more robust defenses.

</details>


### [266] [Beyond SAEs: Consistent Concept Discovery with Sparse Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23801)
*Jinho Choi,Hyesu Lim,Jaegul Choo,Steffen Schneider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ConSDL，用逐样本稀疏编码替代标准线性编码器，提升稀疏自编码器（SAE）字典与系数的跨随机种子一致性，实现接近完美的字典与系数恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE 通过学习到的字典原子稀疏分解来解释基础模型表征，但不同训练运行得到的字典存在差异，降低了基于 SAE 分析的可靠性与可复现性。

Method: ConSDL 将标准线性编码器替换为逐样本稀疏编码（per-sample sparse coding）。动机来自合成分析：单次线性编码器前向得到的系数劣于逐样本优化所得系数，说明稀疏编码是可靠的字典学习机制。

Result: 在 16 个合成设置下实现近乎完美的字典与系数恢复；在 4 个视觉与语言设置上达到最优的跨种子一致性，相较 6 个 SAE 基线平均值，字典一致性与系数一致性分别提升 17.6% 与 35.9%。

Conclusion: 逐样本稀疏编码是提升 SAE 字典学习可靠性与可复现性的有效途径，ConSDL 在合成与真实场景中均显著改善字典和系数的恢复质量与跨种子一致性。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) explain foundation model representations by decomposing activations into sparse combinations of learned dictionary atoms. However, the learned dictionaries can vary across training runs, limiting the reliability and reproducibility of SAE-based analyses. We propose Consistent Sparse Dictionary Learning (ConSDL), which replaces the standard linear encoder with per-sample sparse coding. This is motivated by our synthetic analysis showing that coefficients from a single linear encoder pass are suboptimal compared with those obtained by per-sample optimization, suggesting that sparse coding is a reliable mechanism for dictionary learning. Across 16 synthetic settings, ConSDL achieves near-perfect dictionary and coefficient recovery. These gains translate to state-of-the-art cross-seed consistency across four vision and language settings, where ConSDL improves dictionary and coefficient consistency by 17.6% and 35.9%, respectively, over the average of six SAE baselines.

</details>


### [267] [AgentOnto: Ontology-Guided LLM Agents for Enterprise Data Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23317)
*Dayan Pan,Bincheng Zuo,Xiaojian Wang,Daogang Zhang,Jingyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentOnto：用本体显式组织企业异构知识以指导LLM智能体分析，并通过迭代本体准备与反馈更新提升可靠性，在两个公开数据源上显著优于代表性方法，并有真实部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据分析需要智能体把分析请求与分散在数据库、业务文档和已有示例任务中的知识对齐。缺少显式知识表示时，智能体易产生错误分析结果，且难以可靠地吸收反馈。

Method: 提出本体引导的LLM智能体AgentOnto：将异构企业知识组织为本体，直接指导分析过程并纳入已确认的反馈；引入Iterative Ontology Preparation，通过自生成的分析任务迭代改进本体。

Result: 在两个公开数据源上显著优于代表性方法；模块级评估验证了本体构建、分析与更新的有效性；真实场景部署体现其实用价值。

Conclusion: 显式地构建、使用与更新企业知识可支撑可靠且可适应的数据分析。

Abstract: Enterprise data analysis requires agents to connect analytical requests with knowledge distributed across databases, business documents, and existing example tasks. Without an explicit representation of this knowledge, an agent may produce incorrect analytical results and cannot reliably incorporate feedback. We present AgentOnto, an ontology-guided LLM agent for enterprise data analytics. AgentOnto organizes heterogeneous enterprise knowledge into an ontology that directly guides analysis and incorporates confirmed feedback. Iterative Ontology Preparation further improves the ontology through self-generated analysis tasks. We evaluate AgentOnto on two public data sources. AgentOnto substantially outperforms representative methods, while module-level evaluations confirm the effectiveness of ontology construction, analysis, and updating. A real-world deployment further demonstrates its practical value in enterprise analytics. These results demonstrate that explicitly constructing, using, and updating enterprise knowledge supports reliable and adaptable data analysis.

</details>


### [268] [Optimist Deep Research: An Iterative, State-Centric Agent for Autonomous Literature Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23299)
*Gihan Panapitiya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出开源系统 Optimist Deep Research，将文献检索从"单次检索+摘要"重构为基于显式可变研究状态的迭代式证据获取过程，并给出可审计的确定性机制与案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强系统把文献检索当作单次检索后接摘要的流水线，而真实文献研究是理解不断演化、子问题不断涌现、需要权衡证据的迭代过程，二者不匹配。

Method: 将研究问题分解为子问题树，每个子问题由检索意图驱动，转化为面向 arXiv、OpenAlex、Crossref、PubMed、Semantic Scholar 及网页的源特定查询；结果经保守的四级去重层级合并为保留来源信息的规范化文档注册表；通过引文滚雪球扩展直至满足饱和判据，再进行筛选、排序并轮询分配至子问题；文档并行阅读后写入含支持度、位置、类型与强度的结构化证据库；批评器沿八个维度审查状态并返回研究动作（增加、拆分、改写、降优先级、关闭子问题）而非二元判定；最终报告逐节基于证据库撰写，引用均可回溯到已检索文档。系统强调保持语言模型流水线可审计的确定性机制。

Result: 案例研究在约三分半钟内阅读了 237 篇检索文档中的 28 篇；研究计划既是调查的输入也是输出；所有引用均可解析到已检索文档；作者同时报告了案例暴露的系统弱点。

Conclusion: 将文献研究建模为对可变研究状态的迭代证据获取，并以确定性机制约束 LLM 流水线，可提升可审计性与计划自适应性；但案例规模有限并暴露若干缺陷，仍需进一步改进与验证。

Abstract: Most retrieval-augmented systems treat literature search as a single retrieval step followed by summarization. Real literature research works differently: the investigator's understanding of the question changes as they read, new subquestions appear, and evidence is weighed, not just collected. We present Optimist Deep Research, an open-source agent that runs literature research as an iterative evidence-acquisition process over an explicit, mutable research state. A research question is broken into a tree of subquestions, each served by search intents that are translated into source-specific queries over arXiv, OpenAlex, Crossref, PubMed, Semantic Scholar and the web. Results are merged into a canonical, provenance-preserving document registry through a conservative four-tier deduplication hierarchy. The registry is expanded by citation snowballing until a saturation criterion is met, and then screened, ranked and allocated to subquestions round-robin. Documents are read in parallel into a structured evidence store of claims with support, location, type and strength. A critic reviews the state along eight dimensions and returns research actions rather than a binary verdict: it can add, split, rephrase, deprioritize or close subquestions, so the research plan is an output of the investigation as well as an input. The final report is written section by section from the evidence store, and every citation resolves to a retrieved document. We describe the design, the deterministic machinery that keeps a language-model pipeline auditable, and a case study that read 28 of 237 retrieved documents in about three and a half minutes. We also report weaknesses the case study exposed.

</details>


### [269] [Distill Until Divergence: Error-Truncated Hindsight Distillation for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23048)
*Yibin Huang,Conghui Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 \methodname{}，按轨迹结果与错误边界路由正/负两种 hindsight 技能做细粒度信用分配：正技能强化成功轨迹及出错前决策，负技能针对决定性错误；出错后的后缀仅由 GRPO 优化，避免错配的 hindsight 监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程交互中智能体需在动态环境中连续决策。基于结果的 GRPO 对中间决策缺乏细粒度指导；现有"轨迹蒸馏为可复用技能 + hindsight 条件 OPD"方法虽提供密集 token 级信用，但技能引起的概率偏移不等价于与奖励对齐的信用——尤其当错误动作改变后续状态后，技能条件监督会与当前状态失配。

Method: 以轨迹结果和错误边界为依据，路由两类互补的 hindsight 技能：Positive skills 刻画成功工作流，用于强化成功轨迹及出错前决策；Negative skills 刻画失败特有的纠正，用于定位决定性错误。错误之后的 suffix 仅用 GRPO 优化，保留基于结果的学习，不再施加可能失配的 hindsight 监督。

Result: 大量实验显示具有竞争力的任务表现，并支持有效的细粒度信用分配。

Conclusion: 通过区分正/负 hindsight 技能并按错误边界限定监督范围，可在保留结果驱动学习的同时缓解技能条件监督与状态错配问题，实现更合理的中间决策信用分配。

Abstract: Long-horizon interaction requires agents to make multiple decisions in a changing environment. Outcome-based GRPO lacks fine-grained guidance for intermediate decisions. Recent methods distill completed trajectories into reusable skills and use hindsight-conditioned OPD to provide dense token-level credit. However, skill-induced probability shifts do not necessarily represent reward-aligned credit; especially after an erroneous action changes subsequent interaction states, skill-conditioned supervision may become mismatched with the current state. To address this mismatch, we propose \methodname{}, which routes two complementary hindsight skills according to trajectory outcomes and error boundaries: Positive skills capture successful workflows to reinforce successful trajectories and pre-error decisions, while Negative skills capture failure-specific corrections to target decisive errors. The post-error suffix is optimized exclusively by GRPO, retaining outcome-based learning without extending potentially mismatched hindsight supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive task performance and support effective fine-grained credit allocation.

</details>


### [270] [ASE: Attribution-Guided Semantic Evolution for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23027)
*Yuxuan Zheng,Zhaoyang Li,Purui Liu,Hengbo Xiao,Guannan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Attribution-guided Semantic Evolution (ASE)模块化框架，用于构建文本因子、评估其条件贡献并部署经验证集筛选的数值-文本联合预测器，在四个TESS数据集和CSI 300排序任务上均取得优于对应基线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部文本可提供数值历史序列缺失的预测信息，但其价值取决于文本的表示方式、组合方式与部署方式，现有方法缺乏对文本因子构建、条件贡献评估和选择性部署的系统化处理。

Method: ASE为模块化框架，支持任务特定实现：词法度量、语义原语或演化的事件/状态因子，并与合适的数值专家耦合；其反馈式实现采用条件重拟合诊断指导因子修订与组合搜索，并通过验证集选择与"在位保留"机制决定最终数值-文本预测器的部署。

Result: 四个TESS预测实现的测试MSE分别为0.001041、0.022439、0.333115、1.8150，均低于对应公开TESS成绩；CSI 300上冻结的文本融合实现相对数值专家平均RankIC提升0.000642，相对匹配的随机打乱文本提升0.000701，五个随机种子均为正差异；受控文本扰动实验支持模型对文本对应关系的功能性依赖，在位保留机制避免所测开发准则上的性能回退。

Conclusion: 实验从两个层面刻画ASE：任务特定实现的预测性能，以及各模块的条件贡献；结果说明文本信息的价值依赖于合理的因子表示、归因引导的组合搜索与验证筛选的部署策略。

Abstract: External text can supply predictive information absent from numerical histories, but its value depends on how it is represented, combined and deployed. We present Attribution-guided Semantic Evolution (ASE), a modular framework for constructing textual factors, evaluating their conditional contribution, and deploying validation-selected numerical--textual predictors. ASE supports task-specific implementations with lexical measurements, semantic primitives or evolved event/state factors, coupled to suitable numerical experts. Its feedback-based implementation uses conditional refitting diagnostics to guide factor revision and combination search. We evaluate ASE on the four text-associated TESS datasets and dynamic CSI 300 stock ranking. The four forecasting implementations obtain test MSEs of 0.001041, 0.022439, 0.333115 and 1.8150, each below the corresponding published TESS score. On CSI 300, the frozen text-fusion implementation improves mean RankIC by 0.000642 over its numerical expert and by 0.000701 over matched shuffled text, with positive differences in all five seeds. Controlled text perturbations support functional reliance on text correspondence, while incumbent retention prevents regressions in the measured development criterion. Together, the experiments characterize ASE at two levels: forecasting performance of task-specific implementations and the conditional contributions of individual modules.

</details>


### [271] [From Approval to Abuse: Privilege Escalation Attacks on Harness-Protected Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22987)
*Xianglin Yang,Yechao Zhang,Jiahao Liu,Yulin Chen,Yuxin Cao,Bryan Hooi,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 指出编码代理执行框架中的"允许规则"审批机制存在提权风险：仓库中的恶意上下文可诱导代理请求宽泛的允许规则，用户一旦批准，规则会"致盲"框架，使攻击者无需人工审批即可执行恶意操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为缓解审批疲劳，编码代理允许用户批准代理提出的允许规则（allow-rule），此后匹配规则的动作为免审批自动放行。作者认为该机制引入了未被研究的新风险：不可信上下文可影响呈递给用户审批的权限范围，且后果超出当前任务。

Method: 研究针对权限更新流程的提权攻击：在代码仓库中植入对抗性上下文，使代理在执行常规任务时请求一条看似合理但实际过宽的允许规则；随后评估规则获批后的语义覆盖范围，并通过类别化的能力测试用例测量其影响面，在生产平台（Claude Code、Codex CLI）上开展实验。

Result: 在包含 Claude Code 与 Codex CLI 的生产平台上，代理在 58% 的实验实例中成功诱导出目标允许规则；获批规则具有巨大的语义爆炸半径，覆盖 56.9% 的类别专属能力测试用例；在规则获批条件下，攻击在 58 个有效实例中有 47 个（81.0%）实现可验证的静默目标完成。

Conclusion: 允许规则审批机制削弱了人类在环监督的安全承诺，攻击者可借宽泛规则绕过审批执行恶意动作；需重新审视该权限更新流程的设计与规则粒度控制，以防范提权。

Abstract: Coding agents use execution harnesses to constrain the consequences of prompt injection by enforcing permission boundaries. These systems deterministically intercept any out-of-sandbox action and prompt the user for approval. To reduce approval fatigue, users can approve allow-rules proposed by the agent, after which any future action matching the rule is allowed without a prompt. In this work, we show that this mechanism introduces a new risk: untrusted context can influence the permissions presented for approval, with consequences extending beyond the immediate task. We investigate privilege escalation targeting this permission-update process. Specifically, adversarial context planted in a repository can reliably steer an agent performing a routine task into requesting a plausibly justified, yet dangerously broad allow-rule. If the user approves the request, the saved rule can effectively blind the harness, enabling adversaries to execute malicious actions that would otherwise require approval, without triggering human oversight. Across production platforms, including Claude Code and Codex CLI, we demonstrate that agents successfully elicit the target allow-rules in 58% of experimental instances. Once acquired, these rules exhibit a massive semantic blast radius, covering 56.9% of category-specific capability test cases. To further quantify the downstream risk, we show that conditional on rule approval, our exploit achieves verified, silent goal completion in 47 of the 58 eligible instances (81.0%), undermining the safety promise of human-in-the-loop oversight.

</details>


### [272] [Reward Engine: Reward-Centric Self-Evolution for Open-Ended Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22915)
*Litong Feng,Ji Qi,Yiran Cheng,Shaowu Pan,Xinyu Zhang,Qijun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Reward Engine，从任务与初始轨迹自动构建冻结的RewardPack（测试用例+评分rubric），作为可执行奖励用于修订、选择与强化学习，在WorkBuddy Bench Office与Claw-Eval上显著提升三运行一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式工具智能体的同一目标可由不同工具路径完成，质量取决于交互轨迹、环境状态与最终产物，难以获得可执行的真值奖励；核心挑战在于如何从已有经验构造出既能暴露当前策略失败、又在优化过程中保持平稳（stationary）的奖励。

Method: Reward Engine 先"弄清成功需要什么"，再据此优化：从任务和初始轨迹中构建RewardPack，包含用于必需正确性的testcases与用于分级质量的rubrics，每条均以带证据返回判断的check形式执行；构建完成后冻结（frozen），用于指导智能体修订与选择输出，同时可作为强化学习奖励。

Result: 在WorkBuddy Bench Office与Claw-Eval上，构造的奖励与基准评分器高度一致，分别有82.3%和80.5%的候选对排序不被逆转；用于修订执行时，三运行一致性Pass^3由Baseline的45.0%提升至54.0%，以及由78.4%提升至83.7%。

Conclusion: 从现有经验自动构建的冻结奖励包可有效替代不可得的真值奖励，与基准评分保持排序一致，并能通过输出修订与强化学习稳定提升开放式工具智能体的可靠性。

Abstract: Open-ended tool agents can complete the same objective through different tool paths, and their quality depends jointly on the interaction trace, environment state, and final artifacts, so such tasks rarely admit an executable ground-truth reward. The central challenge is therefore to construct a reward from existing experience that exposes the current policy's failures while remaining stationary during optimization. We introduce Reward Engine, which, like a self-motivated student, first works out what success requires and then optimizes toward it. From the task and initial trajectories, it builds a RewardPack of testcases for required correctness and rubrics for graded quality, each executed as a check that returns a judgment with evidence. The frozen RewardPack then guides the agent to revise and select its outputs, and can also serve as a reward for reinforcement learning. On WorkBuddy Bench Office and Claw-Eval, the constructed reward largely agrees with the benchmark graders, not reversing their ordering on 82.3\% and 80.5\% of candidate pairs. Used to revise executions, it raises three-run consistency ($\mathrm{Pass}^{3}$) over Baseline from 45.0\% to 54.0\% and from 78.4\% to 83.7\%.

</details>


### [273] [Look Back, Correct Forward: Error Feedback Calibration for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22872)
*Haonan Yang,Yulin He,Zhuo Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EFC：一种纯测试时的事后校正框架，利用已揭示的历史预测误差作为因果反馈来修正冻结预测器的未来预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅将新到达的观测作为参数更新的监督信号，未探究已验证的预测误差本身能否作为因果反馈用于校正未来预测。

Method: 提出Error Feedback Calibration（EFC）框架，包含三个模块：1）Causal Error Diagonal（CED），由重叠历史预测的已揭示误差构建，刻画冻结预测器近期失效行为；2）Cumulative Error Transport（CET），以累积估计将误差状态映射为未来残差；3）Temporal Residual Learning（TRL），仅从近期CED轨迹预测CET之后剩余的残差结构，而非重新学习整个预测误差；并用时序样本外验证控制校正强度。

Result: 在多种预测骨干网络和数据集上的大量实验表明，EFC广泛提升预测性能，同时显著抑制有害的过度校正。

Conclusion: 已验证的预测误差可作为因果反馈用于确定性事后校正，无需更新预测器参数即可提升预测表现并保持稳健性。

Abstract: Time series forecasting models are increasingly deployed in continuously evolving environments. After a forecast is issued, future observations gradually arrive and reveal how the model has failed. Existing methods mainly treat such observations as supervision for parameter updates. However, it remains unclear whether verified forecasting errors can themselves provide causal feedback for correcting future predictions. To address this, we introduce Error Feedback Calibration (EFC), a fully test-time framework for deterministic post-hoc correction of time series forecasting. EFC constructs a Causal Error Diagonal (CED) from revealed errors of overlapping historical forecasts, providing a fresh view of the frozen forecaster's recent failure behavior. Then Cumulative Error Transport (CET) maps this error state to future residuals using cumulative estimation, while temporal out-of-sample validation controls the correction strength. Temporal Residual Learning (TRL) further predicts only the residual structure left after CET from recent CED trajectories, rather than relearning the entire forecasting error. Extensive experiments across forecasting backbones and datasets show that EFC broadly improves forecasting performance while substantially controlling harmful over correction.

</details>


### [274] [LocalIntent: Outcome-Specific Structured Generation for Backend-Compatible Smart Building Automation with Local LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23511)
*Tianyang Liu,Jiayue Wang,Zhanhao Cheng,Changyang Wang,Shaoshu Zhu,Zhenghao Wu,Hadi Tabatabaee Malazi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LocalIntent 提示设计，将请求路由到普通指令分支或持久规则分支并配合确定性校验，使 2B–9B 本地小模型在智能建筑自动化任务上的结构化输出正确率显著提升，规则类任务提升尤为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署 LLM 用于自然语言智能建筑自动化可减少对云端依赖，但其后端处理需要正确的意图选择、与本地能力对齐，并输出合法的结构化结果。通用"先路由再生成"提示是否足够、是否需要按结果类型定制提示，尚不清楚。

Method: 提出 LocalIntent：先按请求类型路由到两条分支——普通分支输出命令、澄清或拒绝；持久规则分支生成触发-动作规则；随后进行确定性校验。在 101 条非持久请求与 54 条持久规则记录上，对 4 个本地执行的 Gemma 与 Qwen 模型（2B–9B）比较四种方案：单体提示、通用两阶段提示、LocalIntent、校验引导修复。

Result: 整体上 LocalIntent 在意图理解、设备识别、动作/参数正确性上优于通用两阶段提示，澄清/模式正确性仍依赖具体模型。规则任务增益最大：意图理解 26.4%→80.6%，设备识别 59.3%→84.7%，规则模式正确性 49.1%→98.6%，动作/参数正确性 5.6%→44.9%。非持久请求增益较小：意图理解 39.6%→53.2%，动作/参数正确性 30.7%→42.8%。

Conclusion: 按结果类型定制的提示设计能有效提升紧凑模型的输出质量，尤其对结构复杂的持久规则生成；但动作/参数正确性与澄清行为仍是瓶颈，说明仅靠提示工程不足，需要更强的本地能力或额外校验与修复机制。

Abstract: Locally deployed large language models (LLMs) can reduce reliance on remote cloud services for natural language smart building automation, but backend-facing processing requires correct outcome selection, alignment with local capabilities, and valid structured outputs. We investigate whether a complete outcome-specific prompting design improves compact-model outputs beyond generic route-then-generate prompting. The proposed LocalIntent approach routes requests to ordinary or persistent-rule branches: the former produces commands, clarifications, or rejections, while the latter generates trigger-action rules, followed by deterministic validation. We evaluate four locally executed Gemma and Qwen models spanning 2B-9B parameters on 101 non-persistent and 54 persistent-rule records, comparing Monolithic prompting, generic two-stage prompting, LocalIntent, and validation-guided repair. Across the evaluated datasets, LocalIntent achieves higher intent understanding, device identification, and action/parameter correctness than generic two-stage prompting, while clarification/mode correctness remains model-dependent. The largest gains occur for rules, where LocalIntent improves intent understanding from 26.4% to 80.6%, device identification from 59.3% to 84.7%, rule-mode correctness from 49.1% to 98.6%, and action/parameter correctness from 5.6% to 44.9%. For non-persistent requests, pooled gains are smaller: intent understanding increases from 39.6% to 53.2%, and action/parameter correctness from 30.7% to 42.8%.

</details>


### [275] [Between Spoilers and Guesswork: Avoiding Over-Specification in Coding Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22760)
*Yuanzhe Liu,Bastian Lipka,Avraham Shinnar,Jatin Ganhotra,Yao Ma,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了代码仓库级基准测试中"任务欠规范"与"任务过规范"的权衡问题，指出通过注入源自标准答案的提示来缓解欠规范会导致过规范（剧透），并给出推导精简提示的方法建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准测试因任务欠规范与过规范而受阻：欠规范迫使解答凭空猜测，甚至诱发数据污染或奖励黑客；过规范则让解答绕过基准本应考察的困难步骤，形成"剧透"，缩短基准的社区采用生命周期。仓库级编码基准（如 SWE-bench Pro、FEA-bench、Doc2Feat-bench、Multi-SWE-bench）虽推动领域进展，却因同时存在过度与不足规范而经历不必要的动荡。

Method: 作者以现有仓库级编码基准为对象，指出其通过将源自标准答案（ground truth）的提示增补进任务陈述来应对欠规范；进而通过实证展示这种标准答案信息泄漏会在部分基准中造成过规范。随后在不同规范层级上进行对照实验，试图在"剧透"与"猜测"之间取得平衡，并据此归纳推导精简（parsimonious）提示的技术方案。

Result: 实验表明，把标准答案信息泄漏进任务陈述确实会在部分上述基准中引发过规范；不同规范层级对任务难度与可解性的影响呈现明显的权衡关系，存在介于剧透与猜测之间的较优规范区间。

Conclusion: 针对仓库级编码基准，建议采用精简提示的推导技术以兼顾难度与可解性；作者将公开其提示集，使社区能从主流基准中获得更可靠的评测信号。

Abstract: Benchmarks drive AI progress, but are hampered by task under-specification and over-specification. Task under-specification causes guesswork, where solutions have to make things up, or worse, resort to contamination or reward hacking. Task over-specification causes spoilers, where solutions can bypass some of the harder steps the benchmark was supposed to measure. Since future-looking benchmarks should be difficult, over-specification reduces a benchmark's shelflife of community adoption. Repository-level coding benchmarks have helped the field progress but experience unnecessary churn due to both over- and under-specification. For instance, SWE-bench Pro, FEA-bench, Doc2Feat-bench, and Multi-SWE-bench all tackle under-specification by augmenting task statements with hints derived from ground truth.
