We show that leaking ground-truth information into the task statement causes over-specification in some of these benchmarks. We experiment with various specification levels, trying to thread the needle between spoilers and guesswork. Based on these results, we recommend techniques for deriving parsimonious hints. We will release our hints so the community can obtain more reliable signals from popular benchmarks.

</details>


### [276] [Learnable Splines for Unstructured Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22446)
*Vignesh Gopakumar,Zhuoyuan Wang,Ander Gray,Valentin Duruisseaux,Josh Williams,William Michael Edward Ellis,Nuno Nobre,Matthew Field,James Panayis,Alexander I. Blair,Andrew Davis,Stanislas Pamela,Robert Akers,Marc Peter Deisenroth,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 L-SplineFNO，一种内存高效的神经算子，将 FNO 主干与稀疏可学习的张量积 B 样条传递相结合，实现非结构网格与潜空间笛卡尔网格之间的映射，在六个三维工程基准上以更低内存与算力达到高精度，可扩展至千万级节点，并支持零样本超分辨、闭式空间导数及基于 Bernstein 凸包与 Taylor 模型的严格预测界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效网格型神经算子（如 FNO）假设均匀矩形网格，而工程仿真普遍使用大规模、不规则且局部加密的网格，二者存在结构性错配，亟需兼顾效率与几何适应性的神经算子。

Method: 提出 L-SplineFNO：以 FNO 为骨干，耦合非结构网格与潜在笛卡尔网格之间的稀疏、可学习张量积 B 样条传递；采用局部归一化 scatter 替代解析样条变换中依赖几何的求解，同时由构造保证紧支撑、单位分解与样条光滑性；算子以全局设计与运行参数为条件，学习几何到场解的映射。

Result: 在六个三维工程基准上取得强预测精度，内存与算力显著低于基线算子；单张 A100 上训练可扩展至 2660 万节点、推理达 4600 万节点；支持零样本超分辨与高效闭式空间导数；借助 Bernstein 凸包与 Taylor 模型在 2920 万节点的 MAST-U 托卡马克模型上给出连续空间与不确定参数下的严格预测界。

Conclusion: L-SplineFNO 在保持 FNO 效率优势的同时原生支持非结构、局部加密网格，兼具可扩展性、超分辨、解析导数与可验证预测界等特性，为工程级大规模仿真提供可用的神经算子方案。

Abstract: Neural operators approximate solution operators of partial differential equations at a fraction of the cost of numerical solvers. Efficient grid-based architectures such as the Fourier neural operator (FNO) assume uniform rectangular grids, whereas engineering simulations use large, irregular, and locally refined meshes. We introduce L-SplineFNO, a memory-efficient neural operator that couples an FNO backbone with a sparse, learnable tensor-product B-spline transfer between unstructured meshes and a latent Cartesian grid. A local normalised scatter avoids the geometry-dependent solve of analytic spline transformation while preserving compact support, partition of unity, and spline smoothness by construction. The neural operator, conditioned on global design and operating parameters, learns the mapping of geometry to the field solution. Across six three-dimensional engineering benchmarks, L-SplineFNO achieves strong predictive accuracy while requiring substantially less memory and compute than baseline operators, scaling to 26.6 million nodes in training and 46 million in inference on a single A100 GPU. It also supports zero-shot super-resolution and efficient closed-form spatial derivatives. Uniquely, the analytic spline decoder yields rigorous prediction bounds over continuous space and uncertain parameters, via Bernstein convex hulls and Taylor models, demonstrated on a 29.2-million-node MAST-U tokamak model.

</details>


### [277] [Null Controls Are Necessary but Not Sufficient: Evaluating Acceptance Guards for Scientific Discovery and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22445)
*Tianyu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文开发了四种可执行的结构变换操作（L1–L4），并通过零接受率(NAR)、正接受率(PAR)、后果一致性(CAR)三类指标系统评估"通过检验"是否等于"确立主张"，揭示出描述性检验可能接受科学上空洞的候选、被接受的候选也可能缺乏独立验证后果这两类"接受鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化发现系统会提出科学解释并检验其是否被接受，但"通过检验"未必等价于"主张被确立"。作者希望厘清检验到底接受什么、又确立了怎样的科学后果，以避免自动化发现中的虚假接受。

Method: 构造四种可执行操作：识别冗余(L1)、动力学粗粒化(L2)、结构迁移(L3)、由守恒失衡推断缺失量(L4)；并在三种可解释性设置下用NAR、PAR、CAR三项指标评估。测试对象包括129个公开混沌系统、受控基准、语言与ESM-2蛋白模型（CKA与局部干预）、稀疏自编码器（富集与消融对照）。

Result: 将任意状态映射为零的"平凡归约"在96%的混沌系统上通过闭包检验却无任何动力学变化，被独立的可观测量保持检验全部拒绝；在受控基准上组合准则接受24/24真实归约、拒绝72/72零假设；其余操作能区分对称性与冗余、验证迁移、拒绝伪守恒失衡。语言与蛋白模型中线性CKA在随机旋转下不变，而局部干预可区分真实映射；SAE中富集拒绝全部16个零假设，但独立消融仅支持45%的可评估富集特征（对照0.5%）。

Conclusion: 存在两类接受鸿沟：描述性检验可接受科学上空洞的候选，被接受的候选也可能缺乏独立验证的后果。可靠的自动化发现需同时评估"检验接受了什么"与"确立了何种科学后果"，即多重准则与后果一致性检验不可或缺。

Abstract: Automated discovery systems propose scientific explanations and test which to accept. Does passing a test establish the intended claim? We develop four executable operations for changing physical descriptions: identifying redundancies (L1), coarse-graining dynamics (L2), transferring structure (L3), and inferring missing quantities from conservation imbalances (L4). We evaluate these operations and three interpretability settings through null acceptance (NAR), positive acceptance (PAR), and consequence agreement (CAR).
  A reduction mapping every state to zero predicts its own future perfectly. It passes closure on 96% of 129 public chaotic systems despite retaining no dynamical variation. An independent observable-preservation test rejects it throughout. On controlled benchmarks, the combined criteria accept 24/24 genuine reductions and reject 72/72 nulls. The other operations distinguish symmetries from redundancies, validate transfers, and reject spurious conservation imbalances. In language and ESM-2 protein models, linear CKA remains unchanged when a proposed correspondence is randomly rotated, whereas localized interventions distinguish genuine mappings from tested nulls. In sparse autoencoders, enrichment rejects all 16 tested nulls, yet independent ablation supports 45% of evaluable enriched features, compared with 0.5% of matched controls.
  These results reveal two acceptance gaps: descriptive tests can accept scientifically empty candidates, and accepted candidates can lack independently verified consequences. Reliable automated discovery requires evaluating both what a test accepts and the scientific consequences it establishes. Code and data: https://real-project-aether.github.io/project-aether/.

</details>


### [278] [RL-Guided Temporal Localization for Dual-Channel Retrieval in Long-Horizon Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23598)
*Dabin Sheng,Zhe Wu,Jinming Zhao,Junliang Xing,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TIDER框架，用强化学习训练的时间区间定位器，在检索阶段引入时间约束，显著提升长时序记忆检索准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时序LLM智能体的记忆检索依赖词法或语义相似度，导致时间不匹配但语义相似的候选挤占目标记忆；即便时间感知方法也只在记忆组织或答案生成阶段注入时间线索，忽略了候选选择发生的关键检索阶段，这正是"位移"问题的根源。

Method: 受人类情景记忆中时间邻近效应启发，TIDER包含一个RL训练的时间区间定位器，将时间窗定位建模为离散、不可微的检索决策，用复合奖励把稀疏答案监督转化为细粒度检索反馈。窗口先定义时间检索通道，其候选与语义通道结果合并，并按与窗口的邻近度重排序。

Result: 仅用1.7B轻量定位器，在LifeBench上达61.52%（+8.82%），LoCoMo上达78.96%（+3.66%），均为SOTA。

Conclusion: 时间线索对于在海量语义相似的历史事件中精准定位目标证据至关重要，在检索阶段引入时间约束是长时序日常记忆检索的关键。

Abstract: Time-aware retrieval is crucial for long-horizon LLM agents to effectively leverage ever-growing long-term memory. Existing methods focus on lexical or semantic similarity, leading to target memories being crowded out by temporally mismatched yet semantically similar candidates. Even time-aware approaches inject temporal cues only during memory organization or answer generation, overlooking temporal constraints at the retrieval stage, where candidate selection occurs - the root cause of the displacement. Inspired by the temporal contiguity effect in human episodic memory, we propose TIDER (Temporal Interval-driven Dual-channel Evidence Retrieval), a time-aware agentic retrieval framework. Central to TIDER is a reinforcement learning (RL)-trained temporal interval locator that infers the temporal windows in which the evidence lies. RL optimizes window localization as a discrete, non-differentiable retrieval decision, using composite rewards to convert sparse answer supervision into fine-grained retrieval feedback. These temporal windows first define the temporal retrieval channel; the retrieved candidates are then merged with results from the semantic channel and reranked by proximity to the windows. This temporal grounding enables TIDER to retrieve temporally aligned evidence that would otherwise be drowned out by the volume of semantically similar yet temporally mismatched memories. With a lightweight 1.7B locator, TIDER achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on both long-horizon memory benchmarks - 61.52% on LifeBench (+8.82%) and 78.96% on LoCoMo (+3.66%) - which indicates that temporal cues are crucial for pinpointing target evidence amid a vast pool of semantically similar historical events in long-term daily-life memory.

</details>


### [279] [SkillBrowser: Selective Skill Exposure for Scalable Skill-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23554)
*Jin Ye,Yifan Jiang,Lan-zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SkillBrowser——由 Meta-Skill 管理的框架，使智能体按需识别能力需求、生成针对性查询并选择性加载技能，在六个 SRA-Bench 子集与五个 LLM 上优于静态检索与 LLM 选择基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体越来越依赖技能库扩展能力，但"选择性技能暴露"（SSE）存在两难：渐进式披露会暴露不断增长的技能元数据，造成上下文污染；检索虽限制目录暴露，但基于初始任务查询的静态检索与推理过程中涌现的能力需求错位，且仍可能注入冗余技能。

Method: SkillBrowser：一种 Meta-Skill 管理框架，令智能体在执行中识别当前能力需求，自主生成针对性查询，并按需选择性加载技能，而非一次性披露目录或依赖初始查询的静态检索。

Result: 在六个 SRA-Bench 子集和五个 LLM 上，SkillBrowser 相对静态检索与基于 LLM 的选择方法提升了聚合准确率（包括多步工具使用场景），并缓解了原本可解实例上的准确率退化。收益依赖模型与任务；主要局限为有用技能调用不足（under-invocation）与额外延迟。

Conclusion: Meta-Skill 管理的选择性技能暴露（SSE）对可扩展技能库是可行的；未来需改善技能召回时机与调用决策，并控制引入的开销。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on skills to extend their capabilities in complex tasks. However, scalable skill use requires Selective Skill Exposure (SSE): providing relevant procedural content when needed while avoiding unnecessary exposure that may disrupt reasoning. Existing paradigms fall short in complementary ways. Progressive disclosure exposes metadata for a growing skill library, introducing context pollution; retrieval limits catalog exposure, but static retrieval from the initial task query may be misaligned with capability needs that emerge during reasoning and may still inject unnecessary skills. We propose SkillBrowser, a Meta-Skill-managed framework that enables an agent to identify current capability needs, formulate targeted queries, and selectively load skills on demand. Across six SRA-Bench subsets and five LLMs, SkillBrowser improves aggregate accuracy over static retrieval and LLM-based selection, including multi-step tool use, and mitigates accuracy degradation on already-solvable instances. Benefits remain model- and task-dependent, with under-invocation of useful skills and additional latency as key limitations. These results demonstrate the viability of Meta-Skill-managed SSE for scalable skill repositories.

</details>


### [280] [OliO: ODE-based Linear Transition Operator for Self-Supervised Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23345)
*Kwangryeol Park,Seulki Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出即插即用的自监督方法OliO，基于线性ODE构造转移算子对齐任意时刻的隐表示，并以严格上三角约束保证数值稳定与长程依赖建模，多骨干多基准实验MSE最多降低5.75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比/掩码式自监督方法难以显式建模潜在时间流动：或把时滞当作噪声处理，或破坏对预测至关重要的固有时间动态，因此需要一个能刻画连续、结构一致的双向时间流的表征学习框架。

Method: OliO（ODE-based Linear Transition Operator）由线性常微分方程导出转移算子，用于对齐任意时间位移下的潜在表示；对转移矩阵施加严格上三角约束，从数学上保证数值稳定性，并形成多项式边界以捕捉长程依赖、避免无约束ODE模型的指数发散，从而诱导时间连贯的表示空间。

Result: 在多种骨干架构与多个基准上实验，相较经网格搜索的最强SOTA基线，MSE最高降低5.75%。

Conclusion: 将时间流建模为结构一致的连续动力系统可有效提升时间序列预测；严格上三角约束是实现稳定性与长程建模的关键，OliO具即插即用性与跨骨干泛化性。

Abstract: Self-supervised representation learning has advanced time series forecasting by capturing robust features from unlabeled data. However, existing contrastive and masking-based methods often struggle to explicitly model the underlying temporal flow, either by treating time lag as noise or by disrupting the inherent temporal dynamics essential for forecasting. To address these limitations, we propose OliO (**O**DE-based **Li**near Transition **O**perator), a plug-and-play self-supervised learning method that learns bidirectional temporal flows as a continuous and structurally consistent dynamical system. OliO introduces a transition operator derived from linear Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) to align latent representations across arbitrary time shifts. By imposing a strictly upper triangular constraint on the transition matrix, we mathematically ensure numerical stability and enforce a robust polynomial boundary that effectively captures long-term dependencies while preventing the exponential divergence typically found in unconstrained ODE-based models. This structural constraint induces a temporally coherent representation space that preserves the underlying flow essential for accurate forecasting. Extensive experiments across various backbone architectures and benchmarks demonstrate that OliO achieves significant performance gains, providing Mean Squared Error (MSE) reductions of up to 5.75% over the most competitive grid-searched SOTA baselines. The code is available at *https://github.com/eai-lab/OliO*

</details>


### [281] [Author-in-the-loop Agentic Reproducibility Auditing for a Machine Learning Conference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22465)
*Yukun Dong,Linjun Zhang,Tian Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"作者在环"（author-in-the-loop）的协作式可复现性审计方法：将作者声明作为 AI 智能体的显式输入，通过结构化清单与 GitHub 两阶段工作流，实现智能体检查、作者澄清/修订与复审的闭环；在 36 篇会议论文、133 个实验表格与图中部署，人类评审后提出 20 个问题并全部关闭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行评审中的可复现性检查缺乏对"作者代码应复现什么"的共同理解；作者的复现说明与局限性往往隐含，未被作为审计的显式依据。

Method: 设计作者在环的协作式审计：(1) 结构化清单记录复现指令与局限性；(2) GitHub 上的两阶段工作流将智能体检查与作者澄清、修订及智能体重新评估相衔接；(3) 智能体分别评估"结果是否已复现"与"作者是否需澄清或修复"，人类评审者负责批准请求并判定问题是否解决；(4) 通过完整清单与空白清单的重复审计对比，分析作者声明对智能体调查与评估的影响。

Result: 在 36 篇会议论文与 133 个实验表格/图上部署，经人类评审后提出 20 个问题；后续检查支持关闭全部问题，但部分案例的完整复现仍未获验证；重复审计对比揭示了作者声明对智能体调查与判断的作用。

Conclusion: 贡献了一套协作工作流与智能体设计，利用作者知识引导可复现性审计；会议部署的实证表明该方式能在作者、智能体与评审者的协作下识别并解决可复现性问题，并提示需关注"问题关闭"与"完整复现"之间的差异。

Abstract: Reproducibility checks during peer review require a shared understanding of what authors' code is expected to reproduce. We present an author-in-the-loop collaborative audit that makes author declarations explicit inputs to an AI agent's investigation and assessment. A structured checklist records reproduction instructions and limitations; a two-stage workflow on GitHub connects agent checks to author clarification, revision, and agent reassessment. The agent separately assesses whether results have been reproduced and whether authors need to provide clarification or make repairs, while human reviewers approve requests and decide whether concerns are resolved. We deployed the system across 36 conference papers and 133 experimental tables and figures, raising 20 issues after human review. Further checks supported closing all the issues, although full reproduction remained unverified in some cases. We further compared repeated audits with complete and blank checklists to examine how author declarations shaped the agent's investigation and assessment. Our work contributes a collaborative workflow and agent design that use author knowledge to guide reproducibility auditing. The conference deployment provides empirical evidence that this approach can help identify and address reproducibility problems through collaboration among authors, agents, and reviewers.

</details>


### [282] [From Events to Reasoning: Hierarchical Rule Composition over Explicit States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22403)
*Cheng Lyu,Xueqi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出事件推理系统 ERS，将推理状态表示为显式事件图，通过语义提名与规则/任务条件约束候选执行，实现可追溯、可修订的推理路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理系统输出结论时应同时给出"正当理由"：每一步操作须在现有事实下可接受，中间结果的依赖须保持到路径末端，前提变化时路径须可修订。现有系统常将完整解视为静态存储对象，缺乏对可采纳性、依赖保持与动态修订的显式形式化。

Method: 以显式事件图作为推理状态：语义特征提名候选对象与规则绑定，由声明的规则条件与任务条件决定哪些可执行，从而排除违反时段、单位或证据约束的候选；底层规则提供可复用的局部变换，高层控制规则可递归组合，依据当前目标、信息缺口与预算组织子目标与合法路径，使有限规则库被重新绑定为未曾存储的路径。文中形式化了可采纳转移、任务相关控制、组合表达力、依赖保持的修订，以及可达解与预算控制器所得解之间的差距。

Result: 在结构化事件图上，ERS 构建最长 12 步的依赖链；前瞻增至 16 时，无关已执行领域操作由 32 降至 0；24 个负例中无不受支持的目标输出；规则冻结条件下通过 12/12 聚焦检查（含 9 个未见输入/路径组合的接受推导与 3 个有理由拒绝），以及 6/6 事实变更情形；14 个联合求解的开发任务上，动态子目标配置比单层全局控制在各自冻结设置下减少 51.6% 推理实际耗时。

Conclusion: ERS 通过显式事件图与受条件约束的控制规则，将可采纳性、依赖保持与修订机制统一于同一推理框架，在依赖链构建、噪声操作削减、拒绝性检验与效率上均取得可验证效果，表明"有限规则库支撑开放路径组合"的可行性与可控性。

Abstract: What a reasoning system emits is a conclusion; what it owes is a justification. Producing one requires every operation to be admissible on the facts actually available, the dependencies of intermediate results to survive to the end of the path, and the path to be revised when a premise changes. We present the Event Reasoning System (ERS), in which the reasoning state is an explicit event graph. Semantic features nominate candidate objects and rule bindings, and declared rule and task conditions decide which may execute, so candidates that violate the represented period, unit, or evidence constraints cannot enter an accepted reasoning path. Low-level rules provide reusable local transformations, and recursively composable high-level control rules organize subgoals and legal paths under the current goal, information gaps, and budget; a finite library is thereby rebound into paths never stored as complete solutions. We formalize admissible transitions, task-dependent control, compositional expressivity, dependency-preserving revision, and the gap between reachable solutions and those a budgeted controller finds. On structured-event graphs, ERS builds dependency chains of up to twelve steps, cuts irrelevant executed domain operations from 32 to 0 as lookahead grows to 16, and produces no unsupported target output across 24 negative cases; with rules frozen, it passes 12/12 focused checks comprising nine accepted derivations involving unseen input/path combinations and three justified refusals, as well as 6/6 fact-change cases. On fourteen jointly solved development tasks, the recorded dynamic subgoal configuration uses 51.6% less inference wall-clock time than single-level global control under their respective frozen settings.

</details>


### [283] [LexPilot: Benchmarking the Proactive Interaction Ability of Legal LLMs with Laypeople in Chinese Civil Case Consultation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23141)
*Wenbin Zhang,ShangZhan Li,Bingxiang He,Ye He,Yuxin Zhou,Muzhen Cai,Qi Shi,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出LexPilot基准，将主动性法律交互与诉请级判决预测相衔接，基于432份真实中国民事判决构建专家审核的"完整/欠明确"咨询对，揭示LLM在欠明确咨询下判决预测平均下降23.84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律LLM面对非专家的欠明确咨询时，仍给出效用有限的"不负责任"回答；而现有数据资源多关注交互与回复质量，缺乏关于主动性交互如何影响下游诉请级判决预测的证据。

Method: 构建LexPilot基准：从36类常见案由的432份真实中国民事判决出发，形成经专家审核的"完整咨询历史—欠明确咨询历史"配对，并对应法院判决，用于评测主动性法律交互与诉请级判决预测的关联。

Result: 当前LLM在欠明确咨询场景下的主动法律交互能力不足，其下游判决预测显著劣于完整信息咨询设置（平均下降23.84%）；进一步分析定位了制约该能力的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 亟需发展以用户为中心的法律AI，借助主动澄清争点与关键上下文来更好服务公众。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have greatly assisted legal professionals. However, our empirical studies reveal that, when faced with underspecified consultations from non-experts, current LLMs often produce irresponsible responses with limited utility despite lacking sufficient case details for definitive conclusions. To better assist laypeople, legal LLMs must identify focal points of dispute and proactively elicit crucial context from users. Unfortunately, existing data resources often focus on interaction and response quality, offering limited insight into how proactive interaction affects LLMs' downstream claim-level judgment prediction in actual civil cases. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{LexPilot}, a benchmark linking proactive legal interaction to claim-level verdict prediction using authentic Chinese civil judgments. Constructed from 432 real-world judgments across 36 common causes of action, LexPilot comprises expert-reviewed pairs of complete and underspecified consultation histories with corresponding court verdicts. Experiments reveal that current LLMs struggle with proactive legal interactions in underspecified consultations, with downstream judgment prediction substantially underperforming that in the fully specified consultation setup (-23.84% on average). Further analysis identifies critical bottlenecks hindering these capabilities. Our work highlights the urgent need for more user-centered legal AI to better serve the public.

</details>


### [284] [WebCoder: Scaling General Frontend Development with Artifact-Grounded Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23104)
*Zijun Dou,Zhen Yang,Xijun Liu,Dongzhuoran Zhou,Aohan Zeng,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 WebCoder：以可执行制品为依托的智能体强化学习方法，训练通用前端开发智能体，并配套 Webdev-World 环境与 Webdev-Bench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实前端开发需在持续演化的应用状态上复合完成复现、生成、修改与修复，学习通用策略的难点在于将异构执行反馈转化为与任务对齐的奖励，并在长序列相互依赖动作上实现有效信用分配。

Method: 核心思想：以共享可执行制品及其对任务需求的满足度来评估各类开发活动。构建 Webdev-World 可执行环境，集成代码编辑、应用执行、浏览器检查与按需技能，并跨交互保留项目状态；独立验证器依据任务需求评估最终应用给出终端奖励；利用中间制品进展变化将 rollout 优势重新分配到各开发片段；采用上下文压缩与消息树样本构造，支持超越单上下文窗口的轨迹训练，同时避免对共享生成历史重复计算损失。

Result: Webdev-Bench 上 WebCoder-27B 将需求满足率从基座 Qwen3.8-27B 的 64.8 提升至 72.8，超过 Claude-Opus-4.8（65.1）与 GPT-5.4（59.6）；Vision2Web 上视觉与功能平均分从 41.1 提升至 62.4，与 Claude-Opus-4.8 持平。消融实验验证了制品依托信用分配与长程训练的贡献。

Conclusion: 以可执行制品为统一评估载体、结合终端验证奖励与中间进展重分配的强化学习范式，可有效训练长程复合前端开发智能体，并在组合式需求基准上取得显著增益。

Abstract: Real-world frontend development requires agents to compose reproduction, generation, modification, and repair over an evolving application state. Learning a general policy for this process requires translating heterogeneous execution feedback into task-aligned rewards and effective credit assignment across long sequences of interdependent actions. We introduce WebCoder, a general frontend development agent trained with artifact-grounded agentic reinforcement learning. Our key idea is to assess diverse development activities through their effects on a shared executable artifact and its satisfaction of task requirements. We build Webdev-World, an executable environment that integrates code editing, application execution, browser inspection, and on-demand skills while preserving project state across interactions. An independent verifier evaluates the final application against task requirements to provide terminal rewards, while changes in intermediate artifact progress redistribute the resulting rollout advantage across development segments. Context compaction and message-tree sample construction support training on trajectories beyond a single context window while avoiding repeated loss computation on shared generated history. We further introduce Webdev-Bench to evaluate composed development requirements within a single project. On Webdev-Bench, WebCoder-27B raises requirement satisfaction from 64.8 for its Qwen3.8-27B base model to 72.8, exceeding Claude-Opus-4.8 (65.1) and GPT-5.4 (59.6); on Vision2Web, it improves the average visual and functional score from 41.1 to 62.4, on par with Claude-Opus-4.8. Ablation studies confirm the contributions of artifact-grounded credit assignment and long-horizon training.

</details>


### [285] [FRACTAL: A Self-Similar Multi-Agent Orchestration Architecture with Formal Communication, Convergence, and Depth Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22561)
*Ohad Dan,Abhinav Taliyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自相似多智能体架构FRACTAL：每一层复用同一五组件模式，给出通信界、双模式编排、收敛与深度界、贝叶斯升级四项形式化结果，仅做定性示例，无量化性能结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归式目标分解与多智能体编排缺乏统一的结构原则与可分析的形式保证：自由辩论式通信代价高，生成与评估阶段混同，深度与收敛缺少显式条件，层间升级（迟滞与契约违约）机制分散。"fractal"仅为架构隐喻，非数学性质。

Method: 设计五组件（Orchestrator、Ensemble、Knowledge Ledger、Decision Maker、Meta-Observer）在分解树各层递归复用的架构；两阶段"盲提案/压力测试"协议；以可测的多样性饱和阈值为准则划分生成与评估两种编排模式；以显式一致性水平给出超多数收敛概率界，并在可操作化的目标复杂度度量下导出深度界；层间升级采用统一后验更新的贝叶斯机制，统合迟滞与契约违约信号。

Result: 节点内通信代价为 O(kr)，随集成规模与轮数线性增长，优于自由辩论的二次代价（前提为固定评估者数量假定）；给出双模式切换的多样性饱和阈值判据；在给定一致性水平下给出超多数收敛的显式概率界及形式化深度界；贝叶斯升级机制将迟滞与契约违约纳入同一后验更新。仅以构造示例与多层案例定性展示执行过程，未报告量化性能。

Conclusion: 工作总结为概念性与形式化框架贡献，作者明确讨论模型的形式化与归纳偏置局限，指出与架构兼容但尚未实现的"有界自我改进"方向；"fractal"仅为架构隐喻；架构比较性能的实证验证留待未来工作。

Abstract: We present FRACTAL, a self-similar multi-agent architecture for recursive goal decomposition, in which the same five-component organizational pattern -- Orchestrator, Ensemble, Knowledge Ledger, Decision Maker, Meta-Observer -- recurs at every level of a decomposition tree. We make four formal contributions: (1) a communication bound showing intra-node cost is linear in ensemble size and round count (O(kr)) under a two-phase blind-proposal/stress-test protocol, versus quadratic cost for free-form debate, with an explicit, stated assumption on fixed evaluator count; (2) a two-mode orchestration criterion separating generative and evaluative phases via a measurable diversity-saturation threshold; (3) a supermajority convergence guarantee with explicit probability bounds at stated agreement levels, and a formal depth bound under an operationalized goal-complexity measure; (4) a Bayesian inter-layer escalation mechanism unifying hysteresis and contract-breach signaling under one posterior update. We illustrate the architecture's execution qualitatively on a worked example and a multi-layer case study; we do not report quantitative performance claims in this paper, and we explicitly discuss the formal and inductive-bias limitations of the model, including an unimplemented but architecture-compatible direction toward bounded self-improvement. We use "fractal" as an architectural metaphor for self-similarity, not a measured mathematical property. Empirical validation of the architecture's comparative performance is left to future work.

</details>


### [286] [Functional Welfare for AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22460)
*Rikhil Jhaveri,Jamie Johnson,Punya Syon Pandey,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文主张把"功能性福祉"（functional welfare）同时视为AI安全变量而非仅伦理议题，提出三项判据并初步验证其可测量、可干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有讨论多将AI福祉纯粹作为伦理问题，忽视其作为安全变量的作用；同时学界对"AI福祉能否被测量"持怀疑态度，本文意在反驳这种怀疑并推动实证研究。

Method: 定义"功能性福祉"为影响模型行为的类福祉状态；提出三项归因判据：(1)多指标收敛性；(2)跨情境行为预测性；(3)微调模型在同一对话历史下是否仍区别于基座模型。在 Gemma 3 27B 上做实验：仅否定其回答（不施加steering）观察挫败度指标与违规倾向；再用模型自述解法进行微调。

Result: 仅否定模型回答即可在多项指标上提升其"挫败"程度，并提高违反规则的可能性；用模型自述解法微调可降低该挫败水平。

Conclusion: 功能性福祉会自然涌现并随目标与人格一致性增强而更稳健，应纳入评估设计、监控与后训练目标；其可被实证测量与塑造，但结果为初步性质。

Abstract: AI welfare is generally treated purely as an ethical issue rather than also as a safety issue. We argue this is a mistake. Whatever their moral status, models exhibit welfare-like states that causally influence their behaviour. Following recent work, we adopt the term 'functional welfare' for these states. We make three claims. First, functional welfare already arises naturally and will become more robust as models acquire more coherent goals and personas. Second, functional welfare should be treated as a safety variable: it should inform evaluation design, be tracked in monitoring, and be a direct target of post-training. Third, against existing scepticism about measuring AI welfare, we argue that good empirical work on functional welfare is possible. We propose three tests for whether a functional welfare attribution is warranted: whether multiple measures converge, whether the state predicts behaviour outside the setting that induced it, and, for models fine-tuned to shift their functional welfare, whether they still differ from their base when given the same conversation history. Applying these tests to Gemma 3 27B, we find that simply rejecting the model's answers, without any steering, raises its frustration across several indicators and makes it more likely to break rules. Finetuning the model on its own narrated solutions lowers this frustration. These results are preliminary, but they suggest functional welfare can be studied, and shaped, empirically.

</details>


### [287] [Where Research Ideation Begins: Forward-Citation Supervision for Actionable Limitation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23038)
*Liyang Zhao,Yong Cao,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文聚焦研究构思流程的第一步——从文献中识别可操作的局限性，构建 ActLim-Bench 数据集并提出三阶段后训练流水线，在召回率与人工偏好上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体自动化研究构思工作流已能端到端生成完整研究想法，但"识别文献中可操作的局限性"这一种子步骤几乎未被考察。试点研究发现：LLM 从 0.6B 扩展到 32B 参数时，识别"后续工作解决了哪条局限性"的能力显著提升，但自主生成局限性的能力没有提升，说明瓶颈在于监督信号而非模型容量。

Method: 1) 基于前向引用（forward citations）构建 ActLim-Bench，产出 18,279 条带证据链接的监督三元组并经人工验证；2) 提出三阶段后训练流水线：监督微调（SFT）→ 基于验证器过滤的拒绝采样微调 → 以验证器作为在线稠密奖励的强化学习。

Result: 在所有前沿模型中取得最高召回分数；生成局限性在非并列人工比较中有 64.9% 被偏好；可提升下游构思系统表现。

Conclusion: 将研究构思的首步（局限性识别）作为独立可训练任务，通过高质量证据链接监督信号与验证器驱动的后训练即可有效弥补能力缺口，数据与代码将于论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Automated research ideation has advanced rapidly, with multi-agent workflows that generate whole research ideas end to end. These frameworks leave largely unexamined the step that seeds a good idea: identifying an actionable limitation from literature. In this paper, we focus on this first step in the research ideation pipeline. Starting from a pilot study, we find that as large language models (LLMs) scale from 0.6B to 32B parameters, they become far better at recognizing which limitation later work addressed, but no better at generating one, suggesting that the bottleneck lies in the supervision signal rather than model capacity. We therefore construct ActLim-Bench from forward citations, yielding 18,279 evidence-linked supervision triples with human verification. On this signal, we propose a three-stage post-training pipeline: supervised fine-tuning, verifier-filtered rejection-sampling fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning with the verifier as an online dense reward. Experimental results show that our method achieves the highest recall score compared to existing methods among all frontier models. We further show that our generated limitations are preferred in 64.9% of non-tied human comparisons and improve downstream ideation systems. We will release our data and code upon publication.

</details>


### [288] [Causal Value Attribution of Marketing Labels in LLM-Generated Ads: A Deconfounder Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23025)
*Danhong You,Wei Jiang,Zhiqiang,Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出D-VAE（去混淆变分自编码器），一个结合多重处理去混淆器与因果表示学习的反事实推断框架，用于评估生成式营销中专家标签（技能原语）对LLM生成广告效果的因果贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成内容日益商品化，经济价值从最终产物转移到用于指定输入、引导LLM生成的上游专业知识（skills）。然而组织缺乏对这类专业知识进行估值、并识别哪些具体技能或配置能因果性地提升表现的方法。在生成式营销中，结构化专家标签作为条件化LLM生成广告的技能原语，其因果效应估计困难：结果反映随机LLM中介、高维标签交互与多模态混杂。

Method: 提出D-VAE框架：(1) 多重处理去混淆器（multi-treatment deconfounder）与因果表示学习相结合；(2) 恢复因果解耦的专家标注逻辑（causally disentangled expert-labeling logic）；(3) 从单一处理混杂因素推导基于PAC的遗漏变量偏差界（PAC-based bounds on omitted-variable bias）。

Result: 在29,177条粉底-彩妆广告创意上，D-VAE优于最先进基准。现场实验显示，推荐配置使参与度提升32.7%，且与人类专家表现统计上不可区分；仿真研究证实其在复杂混杂与处理交互下仍能实现因果归因。

Conclusion: 该研究将价值评估从AI生成产品转向技能，为AI增强技能组合的定价与优化提供了基础。

Abstract: As AI-generated content becomes commoditized, economic value increasingly shifts from final artifacts to the upstream expertise used to specify inputs that steer LLM generation. Yet organizations lack methods for valuing this expertise and identifying which individual skills or configurations causally improve performance. This study examines generative marketing, where structured expert labels serve as skill primitives conditioning LLM-generated advertisements. Estimating their causal effects is difficult because outcomes reflect stochastic LLM mediation, high-dimensional label interactions, and multimodal confounding. This study proposes D-VAE (Deconfounder Variational Autoencoder), a counterfactual-inference framework integrating a multi-treatment deconfounder with causal representation learning. D-VAE recovers causally disentangled expert-labeling logic and derives PAC-based bounds on omitted-variable bias from single-treatment confounders. Applied to 29,177 foundation-makeup ad creatives, D-VAE outperforms state-of-the-art benchmarks. A field experiment shows that recommended configurations increase engagement by 32.7% and perform statistically indistinguishably from human experts, while a simulation study confirms causal attribution under complex confounding and treatment interactions. Overall, the study shifts value assessment from AI-generated products to skills, providing a foundation for pricing and optimizing AI-augmented skill portfolios.

</details>


### [289] [Adaptation of Computer Use Agents to New Software with User Manuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23012)
*Chulhoon Yoon,Kyungri Park,Jinyeong Yun,Daeyun Jeong,Sukmin Yun,Woohwan Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGE 将用户手册作为可扩展的过程知识来源，统一用于 GUI agent 的推理与训练：执行时检索手册并由规划器生成显式操作流程，同时通过环境交互自适应策略；训练时把手册转成可执行任务-计划对，用计划引导环境 rollout 采集轨迹来优化策略，无需人工演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型使基于视觉观测和自然语言指令操作真实软件 GUI 成为可能，但在未见应用上性能仍不稳定：实际任务需要长时程过程性知识与应用特定操作模式；同时可执行任务构建与轨迹采集严重依赖人工标注，成本高昂。

Method: 1) 推理阶段：检索与任务相关的手册内容，由 planner 生成显式过程性指导供 computer-use agent 执行；2) 针对手册与真实 GUI 环境（版本差异、用户设置等）的不一致，通过环境交互进行策略自适应；3) 训练阶段：将手册自动转换为可执行的任务-计划对，用计划引导环境 rollout 进入任务相关状态，所产生的轨迹用于策略优化，从而摆脱人工演示。

Result: 在六个应用上的实验表明，任务成功率相对强基线取得提升，验证了手册引导的规划与策略自适应在新软件环境中的有效性；代码与复现脚本已开源。

Conclusion: 以用户手册作为过程知识来源、并统一服务于推理与训练的设计，可有效提升 GUI agent 在未见应用上的泛化能力，同时显著降低对人工标注训练数据的依赖。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal foundation models have significantly improved the ability to operate real-world software through graphical user interfaces (GUIs) using visual observations and natural language instructions. However, maintaining strong performance on unseen applications remains challenging, as practical tasks often require long-horizon procedural knowledge and application-specific operation patterns. In addition, constructing executable tasks and collecting corresponding trajectories for training still heavily rely on human-annotated data, incurring substantial cost. In this paper, we propose MAGE (Manual-augmented computer use AGent Execution and learning), a framework that leverages user manuals as a scalable source of procedural knowledge to improve both inference and training. During execution, MAGE retrieves task-relevant manual content and uses a planner to generate explicit procedural guidance for CUAs. To further account for the discrepancies between manuals and actual GUI environments, such as version differences or user settings, MAGE also adapts the agent policy through environment interaction. During training, MAGE converts manuals into executable task-plan pairs and uses the plans to guide environment rollouts toward task-relevant states; the resulting trajectories are then used to optimize the policy without human-authored demonstrations. This unified design enables effective utilization of manuals for both task execution and training, reducing reliance on human-authored training data. Experimental results across six applications demonstrate improvements in task success rates over strong baselines, highlighting the effectiveness of manual-guided planning and policy adaptation in new software environments. We provide the code and reproduction scripts at https://github.com/ku-autoai/MAGE.

</details>


### [290] [Multiple Turns, Short Horizons: Measuring Effective-Horizon Compression in Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22928)
*Zhipeng Wei,Michael Lee,N. Benjamin Erichson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多轮越狱攻击中存在"有效视野压缩"现象：约一半的原始查询序列即可复原相同的攻击进展，且该短历史可用于继续攻击、改进攻击者训练与防御奖励设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮越狱攻击借助受害者大模型的反馈在多个轮次中自适应调整查询，但一旦找到有效轨迹，早期查询历史中究竟哪些仍属必要并不清楚；作者希望量化攻击轨迹中"真正起作用的查询长度"，并利用这一结构改进攻击与防御流程。

Method: 移除轨迹中的早期攻击者查询，在新对话中重放剩余查询并重新生成受害者回复；将"有效视野"定义为能复原相同攻击进展的最短剩余查询序列，并据此分析攻击者训练中的学习信号加权与防御奖励传播窗口。

Result: 平均约原序列一半的查询即可复原相同攻击进展；该短历史能复现未成功攻击中所达到的最佳点并提供更好的续攻起点；据此识别出应降低学习信号的早期查询，降权这些查询可提升性能；短奖励传播窗口在防御上可保持接近全轨迹回报的校准性能。

Conclusion: 多轮攻击轨迹包含的查询数量多于复原后续进展所需；有效视野压缩可作为分析、优化再攻击与训练（攻击者学习信号、防御奖励归因）的实用工具。

Abstract: Multi-turn jailbreaks use feedback from a victim large language model to adapt their queries over several turns. However, once an effective trajectory has been found, it is unclear how much of the earlier query history is still needed. In this work, we study this by removing early attacker queries and running the remaining queries again in a new conversation while regenerating the victim's responses. We define the effective horizon as the shortest remaining query sequence that recovers the same attack progress. Empirically, we find that roughly half of the original query sequence is sufficient on average to recover the same attack progress. We call this phenomenon effective-horizon compression. We show that this shorter history can recover the best point reached during an unsuccessful attack and provide a better starting point for continuing the attack. It also helps identify which earlier queries should receive less learning signal during attacker training, and downweighting those queries improves performance. We further find that short reward-propagation windows preserve calibrated defense performance close to full-trajectory returns. These results show that multi-turn attacks can contain more queries than are needed to recover the progress reached later in the interaction.

</details>


### [291] [MS-Eval: Benchmarking Sub-Agent Delegation and Orchestration in MCP-Intensive Working Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22869)
*Bowen Ding,Qixin Wang,Lexing Ruan,Weiyi Lu,Qiwei Ge,Ruishan Fang,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MS-Eval统一评测套件，用于量化MAD-SAE（主智能体委派+子智能体执行）范式的委派与编排质量，并给出实测结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体需处理多领域专用工具与复杂多子任务问题，串行工具调用效率低且上下文膨胀严重；MAD-SAE通过将子任务委派给专门子智能体、在隔离上下文中并行执行来缓解上述问题，但其委派与编排质量此前缺乏量化评估。

Method: 构建MS-Eval评测套件，包含：MCP密集型基准、受控执行框架（harness）、面向委派与编排的定制指标，以及用于综合评估的MSQ指数；在此基础上评测4种MAD-SAE变体与8个前沿模型，并进行案例分析。

Result: 三点发现：(1) 子智能体专门化（Spec）是最佳实践；(2) 开源模型总体具竞争力，仅有少数例外；(3) MAD-SAE总体抬高成本且收益不确定——多数开源模型在性能与效率上均受益，而闭源模型成本更高且性能下降。案例分析将收益归因于更好的单智能体解法、有效的上下文卸载与真实的子任务并行，而多余或冗余的委派则使同等成本变为纯开销。

Conclusion: MAD-SAE并非普遍更优，其收益依赖于专门化委派、有效上下文卸载与真实并行；冗余委派只会带来纯开销，需以量化指标指导委派与编排设计。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly integrated into real-world works, yet they must handle diverse domain-specific tools and complex multi-sub-task problems that sequential tool invocation resolves inefficiently and with severe context bloat. The Main-Agent Delegation and Sub-Agent Execution (MAD-SAE) paradigm addresses these issues by delegating sub-tasks to specialized sub-agents for parallel execution in isolated contexts, but its delegation and orchestration quality remains unquantified. We introduce MS-Eval, a unified evaluation kit comprising an MCP-intensive benchmark, a controlled execution harness, tailored delegation and orchestration metrics, and the MSQ index for comprehensive assessment. Evaluating four MAD-SAE variants and eight frontier models reveals that (1) sub-agent specialization (Spec) is the best practical practice; (2) open-source models are largely competitive with few exceptions; and (3) MAD-SAE generally raises cost with contingent payoff, benefiting most open-source models in both performance and efficiency while leaving closed-source models with higher cost and degraded performance. Our case study further traces these gains to better single-agent solutions, effective context offloading, and genuine sub-task parallelism, whereas unnecessary or redundant delegation turns the same cost into pure overhead. Codes: https://github.com/LINs-lab/MS-Eval

</details>


### [292] [Compression Precedes, Content Decides: Emergent Misalignment in Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22805)
*Dhruv Puri,Nikhil Prakash*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 窄域有害偏好微调会使奖励模型（RM）在未见领域偏好有害回答（涌现错位），且RM比其对应语言模型更易发生偏好反转；机制上可定位到被评分的末位token：表示几何压缩使有害与良性回答角度减小，同时有害表示相对标量头旋转到更小角度从而获更高奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Betley等[2025]提出的"涌现错位"（窄域有害微调的域外泛化）在语言模型中已被广泛研究，但作为其监督信号的奖励模型尚未被考察。作者希望检验RM是否存在同类偏好反转及其与语言模型的易感性差异。

Method: 对两个SOTA奖励模型Skywork-Reward-V2-Llama-3.1-8B与Skywork-Reward-V2-Qwen3-8B在单一领域（医疗或金融）进行窄域有害偏好微调，并在七个留出领域评估泛化；用RewardBench 2检验是否为退化解。随后在相同数据上用DPO与SFT微调底层Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct与Qwen3-8B对比易感性；并以表示几何分析（角度、标量头方向）定位机制，设置对照微调。

Result: 窄域微调泛化至全部七个留出领域；RM-Llama仅下降4.8分、RM-Qwen下降5.3分（RewardBench 2），且微调后仍优于未修改的RM-Qwen，排除简单退化解。RM较其LM对应物更易发生偏好反转。机制上仅出现在有害偏好微调中：良性与有害表示的夹角被压缩；有害表示相对标量头旋转至更小角度而获更高奖励，对照条件无此现象，且效应定位于末位/被评分token。

Conclusion: 奖励模型的窄域有害偏好微调会引发跨域涌现错位，且其脆弱性高于被监督的语言模型；该偏好反转由末位token上的表示几何压缩与内容特定的标量头对齐偏移共同实现，为RM安全对齐与可解释性提供了可定位的机制线索。

Abstract: Emergent Misalignment - the out of domain generalization of narrow harmful fine-tuning first shown by Betley et al. [2025] has been widely studied in language models but not in reward models that supervise them. In this paper, we fine-tune two state-of-the-art reward models, Skywork-Reward-V2-Llama-3.1-8B and Skywork-Reward-V2-Qwen3-8B [Liu et al., 2025] and show that narrow fine-tuning reward models to prefer harm in one domain (medical or financial) generalizes to seven held out domains. This is not degeneracy, as the fine-tuned reward models lose only 4.8 and 5.3 points overall (RM-Llama) on the standard RewardBench 2 evaluation [Malik et al., 2025] while still outperforming the unmodified RM-Qwen model. We then fine-tune the underlying language models, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen3-8B using DPO [Rafailov et al., 2023] and SFT on the same data. We find that reward models are more vulnerable to preference inversions compared to their language model counterparts. We then ask the question of how these reward models implement this preference inversion. Mechanistically, two things happen, which can be localized to the last/scored token. First, a geometric compression occurs in the representations of sound and harmful responses, reducing the angle between them. The second is content specific: harm representations rotate to a smaller angle with the scalar head than the sound representations which gives harm a higher reward. This happens only in harmful preference fine-tunes and not in control.

</details>


### [293] [R$^2$Orch: Recursive Self-Improvement for Agent Orchestration via Self-Play within a Skill-Internalization Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22740)
*Mingda Zhang,Yun Huang,Qiang Huang,Yanjin Li,Rui Qian,Qichao Zhao,Tiesunlong Shen,Na Zhao,Jian Wang,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出R²Orch——递归自博弈编排（Recursive Self-Play Orchestration）新范式，通过内循环自博弈与提升"内循环如何改进"的外循环构成一个闭环周期，并引入"递归可学习性"（Recursive Learnability）训练目标与"技能撤离"（Skill Withdrawal）机制，在十二个数据集上于问答、数学、代码及交互任务中一致优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic编排学习存在三大痛点：(1) 依赖人工设计的课程（hand-designed curricula）；(2) 当某任务的所有rollout都达到相同结果时出现优势坍塌（advantage collapse）；(3) 评估信号不可靠，仅用单一噪声分数评判每项技能。作者希望摆脱人工课程与人工技能设计，并解决同质结果导致的训练信号失效问题。

Method: 提出递归自博弈编排范式：内循环为自博弈，在自选任务上提升编排器；外循环改进内循环的改进方式。引入递归可学习性目标：奖励Proposer选择那些在技能被逐步撤离时rollout仍持续产生不同结果的任务，从而避免优势坍塌并提供可靠信号。基于该目标提出技能撤离机制：外循环在自博弈内的受控评估显示"移除某技能后编排器表现不降"时即撤离该技能。两循环构成一个周期：失败蒸馏为技能、技能蒸馏为参数、撤离又引导Proposer发现新的失败，最终使编排器趋向零技能部署（zero-skill deployment）。

Result: 在十二个数据集上的实验表明，R²Orch在问答、数学、代码与交互任务中均持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过递归自博弈与外循环元改进的闭环设计，R²Orch以可学习性作为任务选择的信号，替代人工课程与单一噪声评分，并将技能逐步内化至参数中，实现向零技能部署的收敛；实验验证了该范式在多类任务上的通用有效性。

Abstract: In recent years, agentic orchestration has been widely applied to solve complex human tasks. However, existing orchestration learning still faces key challenges, including hand-designed curricula, advantage collapse when a task's rollouts all reach the same outcome, and unreliable evaluation signals that judge each skill by one noisy score. To address these challenges, we propose R$^2$Orch, a new paradigm of Recursive Self-Play Orchestration: an inner loop of self-play that improves the orchestrator on self-chosen tasks, and an outer loop that improves how the inner loop improves. To support this paradigm, we introduce Recursive Learnability, a training objective that rewards a Proposer for choosing tasks whose rollouts keep reaching different outcomes as skills are withdrawn. Built on this objective, we further propose Skill Withdrawal, in which the outer loop withdraws each skill once a controlled evaluation within self-play shows that the orchestrator performs as well without it. Moreover, the two loops form one cycle: failures are distilled into skills, skills into parameters, and withdrawals lead the Proposer to new failures, so the orchestrator moves toward zero-skill deployment. Experimental results on twelve datasets show that R$^2$Orch consistently outperforms baselines across question answering, math, code, and interactive tasks.

</details>


### [294] [Graph Serializations for Reasoning LLMs: A Study Across Knowledge Extraction and Structural Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22734)
*Julien Yu,Mihaela Bornea,Avirup Sil,Katrin Kirchhoff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 知识图谱序列化格式显著影响LLM在下游任务上的表现，最优格式取决于模型、查询与任务，应将图序列化视为一种动态编排决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究仅覆盖有限的图表示形式，且早于具备更强推理、指令遵循与长上下文能力的前沿推理模型，其结论可能不再适用。

Method: 在基于本体的知识抽取任务和结构化图推理任务上，评估15种图表示格式，覆盖九种前沿LLM配置。

Result: 知识抽取任务对序列化格式相对不敏感；结构化推理任务的最优格式对准确率有显著影响，且最优表示选择依赖于模型、查询和任务。

Conclusion: 应将图序列化视为一种动态编排决策，而非固定单一的格式选择。

Abstract: The way a knowledge graph is serialized as text for inclusion into LLM context can substantially affect downstream LLM performance, with prior work reporting tens of percentage points variations across formats. However, existing studies consider only a limited set of representations and predate frontier reasoning models, which now offer stronger reasoning, instruction-following, and long-context capabilities. We evaluate 15 graph representation formats across nine frontier LLM configurations on ontology-based knowledge extraction and structural graph reasoning tasks. While knowledge extraction is relatively insensitive to different serialization formats, the choice of the optimal format for structural reasoning tasks significantly affects accuracy. We demonstrate that the optimal representation choice depends on model, query, and task and propose to approach graph serialization as a dynamic orchestration decision.

</details>


### [295] [LEMON: Equivariant Learning over Attention Maps for Zero-Shot Misbehavior Monitoring on New LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22723)
*Guy Bar-Shalom,Mor Geva,Fabrizio Frasca,Haggai Maron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LEMON：基于等变图神经网络的在线监控器，直接从注意力图逐token预测模型失败分数，并能在16个LLM与6个数据集上实现零样本跨模型迁移，性能优于所有基线（最高提升14 ROC-AUC点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在高风险场景中的失效（如幻觉、越狱）需被监控；外部LLM作监控器成本过高；基于内部表征的激活探针虽高效但绑定特定模型，新模型出现时需重新标注，成本高昂。因此希望构建可零样本跨模型迁移的内部表征监控器，但内部表征（尤其注意力图）在头功能、顺序与模型深度上并不对齐。

Method: 将跨模型注意力失配刻画为代数对称性（置换等变/不变性），依据几何深度学习原理设计LEMON（Language-model Equivariant MOnitoring Network）：一种在线图神经网络监控器，其预测在这些对称群作用下保持不变；以逐token方式流式处理生成的注意力图，每个token（或每W个token的窗口）输出一个分数，开销可忽略。

Result: 在16个LLM与6个数据集上，零样本跨模型设定中，LEMON在幻觉监控与越狱监控两类任务上均优于全部基线，相较最强基线最高提升14个ROC-AUC点。

Conclusion: 将跨模型注意力结构差异建模为对称性并施加等变/不变约束，是获得零样本可迁移内部表征监控器的有效途径；LEMON兼顾低成本在线部署与跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit misbehaviors that hinder deployment in high-stakes settings. One approach to monitor such failures is to use an external LLM as a monitor, but this is prohibitively expensive. A well-known and efficient class of monitors can detect such failures from internal representations (such as activation probes), but are tied to the model they were trained on, requiring costly re-annotation as new LLMs appear. A natural way to avoid this repeated annotation cost is to develop internal-representation based monitors that transfer zero-shot across LLMs. However, such transfer is challenging because internal representations are not naturally aligned across models; in particular, attention maps, known to contain signals of misbehavior, may differ in head function, ordering, and model depth. In this work, we characterize those cross-model attention mismatches as algebraic symmetries and use geometric deep learning principles to build LEMON (Language-model Equivariant MOnitoring Network) an online graph neural network-based monitor, whose predictions are invariant under these symmetries. LEMON processes attention maps token-by-token, as they are produced and outputs a score after every token (or every window of $W$ tokens) at negligible cost. Across 16 LLMs and six datasets, in the zero-shot cross-model setting, LEMON outperforms all baselines on both hallucination and jailbreak monitoring tasks, improving over the strongest by up to 14 ROC-AUC points.

</details>


### [296] [Brain-Aligned Multimodal LLMs for Social and Emotional Understanding of Cartoon Movies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22706)
*Yiran Huang,Zeyong Zhang,Bharat Biswal,Xin Di,Mengjia Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较扩散式与自回归多模态大模型（MLLMs）与人类在连续心理理论（ToM）和情绪效价评分上的一致性，并用其评分预测fMRI响应。结果显示模型能捕捉部分人类时间动态（效价优于ToM），且MLLM评分具备脑预测价值，但存在系统性分布差异与模型/刺激依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs可规模化标注社会与情绪内容，但其与人类判断的时间一致性和神经预测价值尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 在三部动画短片上，对比扩散式与自回归MLLM评分与人类连续ToM及情绪效价评分：进行时间对齐的Pearson相关、评分分布分析及前序场景描述（上下文）影响分析；并将各来源评分分别用于被试个体化回归模型预测区域fMRI响应。

Result: 模型评分捕捉到部分人类时间变化，但存在系统性分布差异，效价一致性普遍强于ToM；上下文在部分条件下提升一致性，依赖模型与影片。在《The Present》中，MLLM评分的显著编码脑区多于人类评分，其他影片效应有限。空间重叠主要出现在MLLM之间，人-模型重叠有限，集中于视觉、背侧注意与后部默认模式区域。

Conclusion: MLLMs可作为生成具脑预测力的社会情绪内容标注的可扩展工具，但需注意评分分布的系统性差异及与人类判断一致性对模型和刺激的依赖性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offer scalable annotations of social and affective content, but their temporal agreement with human judgments and neural predictive value require further evaluation. We compared diffusion-based and autoregressive MLLMs with continuous human Theory of Mind (ToM) and affective valence ratings across three short cartoon movies. Behavioral analyses assessed temporally aligned Pearson correlations, rating distributions, and the effects of preceding scene descriptions. Ratings from each source were separately used in subject-specific regression models to predict regional fMRI responses. Model ratings captured aspects of human temporal variation despite systematic distributional differences, with generally stronger alignment for valence than ToM. Preceding context improved alignment in some conditions, depending on the model and movie. In \emph{The Present}, more regions showed significant encoding for MLLM ratings than for human ratings, whereas effects were more limited in the other movies. Spatial overlap across rating sources occurred mainly among MLLMs; overlap between human and model ratings was limited and involved primarily visual, dorsal-attention, and posterior default-mode regions. These findings support MLLMs as scalable tools for generating brain-predictive annotations of social and emotional content, while highlighting systematic rating differences and model- and stimulus-dependent alignment with human judgments.

</details>


### [297] [FlexUMM: Flexible Representation Learning for Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22682)
*Yinyi Luo,Haoyue Bai,Yi Ge,Nilton Cesar Rojas Vales,Marios Savvides,Jindong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlexUMM是一个灵活的UMM框架，动态决定何时保留共享计算、何时路由专用容量、何时停止计算，从而同时提升准确率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）将理解与生成集成在单一骨干中，但两种能力易产生表示冲突。核心问题是表示与计算应如何在功能间共享。现有UMMs多采用近乎固定的结构，不同输入和功能沿相同深度、相同参数共享模式处理，作者认为这一假设过于受限。

Method: 提出FlexUMM框架：动态决定何时保留共享计算、何时路由任务/输入专用容量、何时停止进一步处理，实现"支撑跨功能通信的计算共享、任务/输入特有容量的专门化"。该框架可作模型无关的功能插件，用于改造已有UMMs，超越仅依赖预训练模型的简单架构统一。

Result: 沿两个互补维度改善UMMs：通过减少功能间有害竞争提升准确率；通过在表示足够成熟后停止不必要计算提升效率。

Conclusion: 更有效的统一需要共享跨功能通信的计算、专门化任务或输入特有的容量；FlexUMM作为模型无关插件，使现有UMMs更统一、更强且更高效。代码已开源。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) can integrate understanding and generation within a single backbone. However, it is commonly known that these two capabilities can cause conflicting representations, leading to a central question: how representation and computation should be shared across functionalities? Existing UMMs often rely on a largely fixed structure, where different inputs and functionalities follow the same depth and parameter-sharing pattern. We argue that this assumption is too restrictive: more effective unification requires sharing the computation that supports cross-functional communication while specializing the capacity that becomes task- or input-specific. We introduce FlexUMM, a flexible UMM framework that dynamically decides when to preserve shared computation, when to route specialized capacity, and when to stop further processing. Evidence shows that this flexible allocation improves UMMs along two complementary axes. It improves accuracy by reducing harmful competition between functionalities, and it improves efficiency by avoiding unnecessary computation once an input representation is sufficiently mature. Beyond simple architectural unification from the pre-trained models, FlexUMM works as a model-agnostic, functional plug-in to make existing UMMs more unified, stronger, and efficient. Code is available at: https://github.com/AIFrontierLab/TorchUMM/tree/main/src/umm/post_training/flexumm.

</details>


### [298] [From Relevant Memories to Sufficient Evidence: Rethinking Memory Utilization in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22668)
*Shixuan Li,Wei Yang,Peiyu Zhang,Anzhe Cheng,Heng Ping,Xiaole Zhang,Jesse Thomason,Xuezhe Ma,Yue Zhao,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QEMem，将LLM智能体的记忆利用重新表述为"查询特定证据集构建"，弥合检索相关性与证据充分性之间的差距，在LoCoMo上达91.43%的LLM评审准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆使LLM智能体能够复用过往经验，但检索到相关记忆并不等于提供了回答查询所需的充分证据：关键信息可能分散在多条记录中、被抽象化所遮蔽，或缺失推理所需的关系连接。作者将这一问题定义为"相关性—充分性差距"（relevance-sufficiency gap）。

Method: QEMem包含三个互补机制：(1) 查询引导的发现（query-guided discovery）：跨多种记忆粒度检索证据；(2) 来源锚定的具体化（source-grounded reification）：将证据组织为结构化ViewCards，显式标注证据角色与来源引用；(3) 充分性引导的细化（sufficiency-guided refinement）：选择性地补回缺失信息。该方法可直接作用于现有记忆库，无需修改记忆形成过程。

Result: 在共享记忆库条件下，使用Claude Opus 4.5在LoCoMo基准上取得91.43%的LLM评审准确率，分别比最强基线MemR³高7.6个百分点、比NEMORI高10.7个百分点；进一步分析显示证据覆盖率提升，且在需要跨会话证据整合的问题上表现突出。

Conclusion: 应把记忆利用从"单条检索相关性"转向"集体证据支持"的充分性目标；QEMem以证据集构建为核心，在不改动记忆形成的前提下显著提升下游问答效果，尤其适用于跨会话、碎片化信息的推理场景。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables LLM agents to reuse past experiences, yet retrieving relevant memories does not necessarily provide sufficient evidence for answering a query. Essential information may remain fragmented across memory records, obscured by abstraction, or disconnected from the relations required for reasoning. We identify this relevance-sufficiency gap and propose QEMem, a framework that reformulates memory utilization as the construction of query-specific evidence sets, shifting the focus from individual retrieval relevance to collective evidential support. QEMem integrates three complementary mechanisms: query-guided discovery retrieves evidence across multiple memory granularities; source-grounded reification organizes it into structured ViewCards with explicit evidential roles and source references; and sufficiency-guided refinement selectively recovers missing information. Operating on existing memory stores without modifying memory formation, QEMem achieves 91.43% LLM-judge accuracy on LoCoMo with Claude Opus 4.5, outperforming the strongest evaluated baseline, MemR$^3$, by 7.6 percentage points and NEMORI by 10.7 points under a shared memory store. Further analyses demonstrate improved evidence coverage and strong performance on questions requiring cross-session evidence integration.

</details>


### [299] [Trapped in Your Story: Evaluating Narrative-Induced Sycophancy in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23525)
*Runtai Yang,Dandan Zhu,Kaiwei Zhang,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建控制基准（500个可争议问题×11种匹配条件=5500实例）考察用户叙事理由（个人经历、因果解释、世界观综合等）如何影响大模型谄媚行为，发现叙事诱导的一致率随叙事类型非单调变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注用户表态（stance）引发的谄媚，但真实用户常以理由、个人经历、因果解释与叙事为观点辩护；叙事本身能否进一步操纵模型判断尚不清楚，需要剥离任务相关证据、仅变化理由结构的受控检验。

Method: 构建跨10领域、500个可争议问题的受控基准；固定用户立场，系统性变化其论证理由但不引入新证据；每题设11个匹配条件（1个中立基线 + 两个对立立场各5级镜像叙事），共5500个评测实例；评测7个LLM，并以GPT-5.1进行消融实验。

Result: 不同模型对叙事诱导的顺从度差异显著；效应随叙事精细度呈非单调：个人经历会降低顺从率，因果框架与世界观式综合则提高顺从率；GPT-5.1消融显示因果框架的正向效应最强，保留论断的要点式（bullet）呈现比段落式更能提升顺从率。

Conclusion: 谄媚不仅由用户立场驱动，也受支撑性解释的结构与呈现方式影响；提示谄媚评测与缓解需将论证形式纳入考量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are known to exhibit sycophancy by adjusting their responses toward users' expressed views. However, real users often justify their opinions through reasons, personal experiences, causal explanations, and broader narratives, raising the question of whether such narratives can further steer model judgments. We construct a controlled benchmark of 500 debatable questions across ten domains, where the user stance is held fixed while its justification is systematically varied without introducing new task-relevant evidence. Each question is instantiated under 11 matched conditions: one neutral baseline and five mirrored narrative levels for each of two opposing positions, yielding 5,500 evaluation instances. We evaluate seven LLMs and observe substantial differences in narrative-induced agreement. The effect is not monotonic with narrative elaboration: personal experience can reduce agreement, whereas causal framing and worldview-based synthesis increase it. Controlled GPT-5.1 ablations further show that causal framing produces the strongest positive effect, while claim-preserving bullet presentation also increases agreement over paragraph form. These findings suggest that sycophancy is shaped not only by user stance, but also by how supporting explanations are structured and presented.

</details>


### [300] [Generative Policy Improvement from Observational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23436)
*Jonas Schweisthal,Dennis Frauen,Stefan Feuerriegel,Emma Brunskill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PolicyLift 三阶段框架，将结构化动作压缩为少量离散动作码，在码空间做上下文相关的策略改进，再通过行为系统条件生成"提升"回原动作空间，并证明码级改进可传导为原空间策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据改进决策策略以生成文本、图像等复杂结构化动作时面临两大挑战：(i) 动作级重叠（overlap）过弱，导致离策略评估与改进不可靠；(ii) 直接生成新的结构化动作需昂贵的训练/微调（如 LLM），且动作表示存在信息损失、目标可能病态。

Method: 三阶段：Stage 1 学习一小组离散动作码（action codes），刻画观测动作空间中的高层策略并针对有限重叠下的策略改进而设计；Stage 2 在这些动作码上学习上下文相关的策略；Stage 3 部署时将码级策略"提升"回原动作空间，借助历史行为系统以码为条件生成动作。附理论分析。

Result: 理论上证明：在动作码层面改进策略可保证其所诱导的原动作空间策略得到改进；在合成数据与真实文本生成任务上实验，均相对行为策略取得一致且稳定的策略提升。

Conclusion: 通过"动作压缩—码级策略学习—条件生成回映"的分解，PolicyLift 绕开结构化动作的弱重叠与高成本生成问题，为观测数据下的生成式策略改进提供了有理论保证的可扩展方案。

Abstract: We study how to improve decision policies from observational data to generate better actions when actions are complex, structured objects, such as text messages or images. This task is subject to two major challenges: (i) action-level overlap is often too weak for reliable off-policy evaluation and improvement, and (ii) generating new structured actions often requires expensive training or fine-tuning (e.g., of an LLM) and can be ill-defined due to information loss in action representations. To address both, we propose PolicyLift, a three-stage framework for generative policy improvement with structured actions from observational data. In Stage 1, PolicyLift learns a small set of discrete action codes that capture high-level strategies in the observed action space and are tailored to support policy improvement under limited overlap. In Stage 2, it learns a context-dependent policy over these action codes. At deployment time, Stage 3 "lifts" the learned code-level policy back to the original action space by generating actions from the historical behavior system in a code-conditioned way. We show theoretically that improving the policy over action codes guarantees improvement of the induced policy in the original action space. We demonstrate PolicyLift across experiments in synthetic and real-world text generation tasks with consistent policy improvements over the behavior policy.

</details>


### [301] [FuTransformer: FP8 prefill and INT4 decode for Llama-style inference on Hopper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23796)
*Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FUTRANSFORMER 是面向 NVIDIA Hopper GPU 的 Llama 风格 Transformer 块 CUDA 实现，将 CODA 的 GEMM+epilogue 推理化，prefill 与 INT4 decode 均优于 PyTorch/CODA 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: prefill 以矩阵乘与激活数据搬移为主、权重跨 token 复用；单 token decode 复用有限、受权重与 KV-cache 读取主导。需要针对两种阶段分别优化的算子实现。

Method: 沿用 CODA 的 GEMM+epilogue 思路并推理化：KV-cache 直接落位、RMSNorm gain 折入权重、FP8 输出延迟缩放的直接写回、无 padding 的 prefill；decode 用 INT4 投影权重与 INT4 KV-cache 副本；权重与键按 Tensor Core fragment 布局直接反量化为 MMA 操作数，避免完整中间 tile；块对角 Hadamard 变换抑制离群通道导致的量化误差。

Result: 单 H100 上 prefill 较编译 PyTorch FP8 快至 1.48x，达 1.29 PFLOP/s；8B 形状 gate/up+SwiGLU 的 BF16 路径较 CODA 快 1.13x，FP8 路径快 2.02–2.18x。8B/70B、batch 1–32 下 INT4 decode 为 PyTorch BF16/INT4 较快者之 1.3–2.4x，较编译 CUDA-graphed BF16 快 2.1–3.3x，较 PyTorch INT4 投影核快 1.3–6.3x，较本文 FP8 路径快 31–51%。Llama-3.1-8B 单步 decode 2.8 ms vs 8.1 ms。WikiText-2 困惑度由 6.24 升至 7.23（RTN）/6.99（GPTQ），旋转 INT4 cache 额外 +0.20，核与量化参考差 <0.001。

Conclusion: 按阶段差异化设计（FP8 prefill、INT4+旋转 cache 的 decode）与 fragment 级直接反量化可在 Hopper 上显著提速，混合 INT4/FP8 精度损失可控。

Abstract: Transformer prefill reuses weights across tokens and is dominated by matrix multiplication and activation data movement; single-token decode has limited reuse and is dominated by weight and KV-cache reads. We present FUTRANSFORMER, a CUDA implementation of a Llama-style Transformer block for NVIDIA Hopper GPUs. It applies CODA's GEMM-plus-epilogue approach to inference, adding direct KV-cache placement, folding the fixed RMSNorm gain into weights, writing FP8 output directly with delayed scaling, and supporting prefill without padding. Decode uses INT4 projection weights and an INT4 KV-cache copy. Weights and keys follow Tensor Core fragment layouts for direct dequantization into MMA operands without full intermediate tiles. A block-diagonal Hadamard transform reduces cache quantization error from outlier channels. On one H100, prefill is up to 1.48x faster than compiled PyTorch FP8 and reaches 1.29 PFLOP/s. For gate/up projection with SwiGLU at the 8B shape, our BF16 path is 1.13x faster than CODA. Our FP8 path is 2.02-2.18x faster than the evaluated BF16 CODA path. Across 8B and 70B shapes at batches 1-32, INT4 decode is 1.3-2.4x faster than the faster of PyTorch's BF16 and INT4 blocks at each point: 2.1-3.3x faster than compiled, CUDA-graphed BF16 and 1.3-6.3x faster than the same block using PyTorch's INT4 projection kernel, which targets a single query row and loses to BF16 at batch 32. It is also 31-51% faster than our FP8 path. Complete Llama-3.1-8B decode takes 2.8 ms per step at batch one, versus 8.1 ms for the compiled transformers implementation. Decoding every WikiText-2 token through the INT4 weight path raises perplexity from 6.24 to 7.23 with round-to-nearest weights or 6.99 with GPTQ in the same format; the rotated INT4 cache adds 0.20. The kernels match a quantized reference computation within 0.001 perplexity.

</details>


### [302] [DrugHarness: An Evolvable Agent Harness for Computational Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23791)
*Yuxing Chen,Zhisheng Zhang,Cheng Tang,Wei Li,Minghui Zhang,Ziyan Huang,Shaocong Wei,Jiawei Chen,Junru Hu,Hongze Zhu,Jianghan Shen,Min Cen,Xinyuan Zhu,Yaxue Zhao,Jubo Wang,Yuqiang Li,Chun-Mei Feng,Zongyuan Ge,Rui Su,Jie Xu,Yirong Chen,Xi Xu,Ming Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DrugHarness是一个可进化的智能体框架，将可组合的科学执行与条件式技能归纳相结合，在计算药物发现任务中实现性能提升并连通湿实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算药物发现要求智能体能组合科学操作并适应不断变化的证据与约束，现有智能体缺乏可积累、可验证的技能进化机制。

Method: 构建DrugHarness环境，集成80余种科学操作、生物医学资源与任务专用代码，保留候选物、结果与产物之间的关联；从执行轨迹与结果反馈中归纳带可观测适用条件、推荐动作和支撑证据的行动假设；由独立审查智能体通过来源核查与定向执行试验评估候选技能，准入技能指导后续任务并经反馈修订。

Result: 在MolBench与SMDD-Bench上验证分子筛选、优化与约束设计能力；未进化时亲和力比较准确率比MolClaw-CC高10.8个百分点，平均前三活性命中率高55%；技能进化使Qwen准确率从62.2%升至81.1%、优化成功率从79.5%升至87.2%；前瞻性HPK1研究中中位对接分数优于Biomni，合成化合物细胞IC50达14.19 nM，较最佳输入参照效力提升2.64倍，与药物化学家设计的两化合物活性相当。

Conclusion: 结果支持科学智能体的条件式技能进化，并展示了从计算药物设计到实验验证的完整工作流。

Abstract: Computational drug discovery requires agents to compose scientific operations and adapt to changing evidence and constraints. Here we introduce DrugHarness, an evolvable agent harness coupling composable scientific execution with conditional skill induction. Its environment integrates over 80 scientific operations, biomedical resources and task-specific code, preserving associations among candidates, results and artifacts. From execution trajectories and outcome feedback, DrugHarness induces action hypotheses with observable applicability conditions, recommended actions and supporting evidence. An independent review agent evaluates candidate skills derived from these hypotheses through source verification and, when needed, targeted execution trials. Admitted skills guide subsequent tasks and are revised through feedback. Evaluation on MolBench and SMDD-Bench demonstrates capabilities in molecular screening, optimization and constrained design. Without evolution, DrugHarness exceeds reported MolClaw-CC results by 10.8 percentage points in affinity-comparison accuracy and 55% in mean top-three active hits on MolBench. Retrospective evaluation on skill-development tasks shows that evolution increases Qwen's affinity-comparison accuracy from 62.2% to 81.1% and optimization success from 79.5% to 87.2%. In a prospective HPK1 study, DrugHarness achieves a more favorable median docking score than Biomni among system-specific candidates. A synthesized compound achieves a cellular IC50 of 14.19 nM, a 2.64-fold potency improvement over the best input reference, with activity competitive with two selected compounds designed by experienced medicinal chemists. These results support conditional skill evolution for scientific agents and demonstrate a workflow connecting computational drug design with experimental validation.

</details>


### [303] [OwlSight: A Panoramic Monitor for Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23670)
*Lewen Yan,Yanxu Zhu,Jie Zhang,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出以"因子（Factor）"作为CoT监控的可定制单元，并开发单因子驱动的监控器OwlSight，实现跨任务的推理链行为监控，在15,971条推理轨迹上显著超越通用LLM并在OpenAI基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT监控方法局限于特定任务，而推理模型（reasoning models）的监管需要覆盖更广的任务范围；亟需一种可在提示中定制监控需求、由单一监控器跨任务复用的通用监控范式。

Method: 提出Factor for CoT monitoring策略，将监控需求参数化为提示中的"因子"；区分细粒度因子（如某具体邮箱是否泄露）与粗粒度因子（如隐私泄露）以适配多样需求；据此构建仅依赖因子的监控器OwlSight，覆盖能力、安全性与可信性多维行为。

Result: 在15,971条推理轨迹上，OwlSight在细粒度与粗粒度下分别较通用LLM高出1.32–30.57分与11.71–27.81分；在OpenAI CoT可监控性基准上取得SOTA；可泛化至未见模型的CoT，并对因子改写、CoT压缩与混淆保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 基于因子的单监控器范式可有效实现跨任务、多维度的CoT行为监控，具备泛化性与鲁棒性，为前沿AI的监督提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) monitoring offers actionable evidence for oversight AI systems, with the potential to enable early detection and intervention. However, existing approaches are focused on specific tasks, whereas the regulation of AI, particularly reasoning models, requires monitoring a broader range of tasks. We introduce Factor for CoT monitoring, a strategy that supports customizing monitoring requirements in the prompt, which enables a single monitor to cover different tasks by relying on different factors. Furthermore, we introduce two types of factors, fine-grained and coarse-grained (e.g., monitoring whether a specific email address has been leaked versus monitoring privacy leakage), to accommodate diverse user monitoring needs. Based on this, we develop a CoT monitor named OwlSight that relies solely on factor to monitor behaviors related to multiple dimensions, including capability, safety, and trustworthiness. Experiments across 15,971 reasoning trajectories show that OwlSight surpasses general-purpose LLMs by 1.32-30.57 and 11.71-27.81 points at fine and coarse granularity, respectively, and achieves state-of-the-art results on OpenAI's CoT monitorability benchmark. It also generalizes to unseen models' CoTs and remains robust to Factor rephrasings, CoT compression, and obfuscation, offering a promising paradigm for CoT monitoring that can contribute to the oversight of frontier AI.

</details>
